# Job

איוב

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job)

השטן משכנע את הא-ל להמיט שרשרת אסונות על אדם צדיק, וצערו של איוב מוביל לדיונים על תורת הגמול (צדיק ורע לו).

Satan convinces God to strike a righteous man with tragedy, spurring conversations about suffering.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Writings

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Miqra according to the Masorah (Segments: 1070, License: CC-BY-SA)

Versions (En):
  - The Koren Jerusalem Bible (Segments: 1070, License: CC-BY-NC)
  - The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein (Segments: 1069, License: CC-BY)


## Chapter 1

###### Job 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:1)


אִ֛ישׁ הָיָ֥ה בְאֶֽרֶץ־ע֖וּץ אִיּ֣וֹב שְׁמ֑וֹ וְהָיָ֣ה**׀** הָאִ֣ישׁ הַה֗וּא תָּ֧ם וְיָשָׁ֛ר וִירֵ֥א אֱלֹהִ֖ים וְסָ֥ר מֵרָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There was a man in the land of ῾Uż, whose name was Iyyov, and that man was perfect and upright, and one who feared God, and turned away from evil.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There was a man in the land of Utz [Aram (viz. Genesis 22:21)] by the name of Iyyov. And that man was pure and upright. And he feared G d and turned aside from evil.


###### Job 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:2)


וַיִּוָּ֥לְדוּ ל֛וֹ שִׁבְעָ֥ה בָנִ֖ים וְשָׁל֥וֹשׁ בָּנֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And there were born to him seven sons and three daughters.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there were born to him seven sons and three daughters.


###### Job 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:3)


וַיְהִ֣י מִ֠קְנֵ֠הוּ שִֽׁבְעַ֨ת אַלְפֵי־צֹ֜אן וּשְׁלֹ֧שֶׁת אַלְפֵ֣י גְמַלִּ֗ים וַחֲמֵ֨שׁ מֵא֤וֹת צֶֽמֶד־בָּקָר֙ וַחֲמֵ֣שׁ מֵא֣וֹת אֲתוֹנ֔וֹת וַעֲבֻדָּ֖ה רַבָּ֣ה מְאֹ֑ד וַֽיְהִי֙ הָאִ֣ישׁ הַה֔וּא גָּד֖וֹל מִכׇּל־בְּנֵי־קֶֽדֶם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
His possessions also were seven thousand sheep, and three thousand camels, and five hundred yoke of oxen, and five hundred she asses, and very many servants; so that this man was the greatest of all the men of the east.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And his livestock consisted of: seven thousand sheep, three thousand camels, five hundred pairs of cattle, five hundred she-asses, along with a very great amount of produce. And that man was greater than all the sons of the East [(the generation of the Tower of Bavel)].


###### Job 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:4)


וְהָלְכ֤וּ בָנָיו֙ וְעָשׂ֣וּ מִשְׁתֶּ֔ה בֵּ֖ית אִ֣ישׁ יוֹמ֑וֹ וְשָׁלְח֗וּ וְקָֽרְאוּ֙ לִשְׁלֹ֣שֶׁת אַחְיֹתֵיהֶ֔ם לֶאֱכֹ֥ל וְלִשְׁתּ֖וֹת עִמָּהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And his sons used to go and feast in the house of each one on his day; and they used to send and call for their three sisters to eat and drink with them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And his sons would go and make a feast in the house of each on his set day, and they would send and call to their three sisters to eat and drink with them.


###### Job 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:5)


וַיְהִ֡י כִּ֣י הִקִּ֩יפוּ֩ יְמֵ֨י הַמִּשְׁתֶּ֜ה וַיִּשְׁלַ֧ח אִיּ֣וֹב וַֽיְקַדְּשֵׁ֗ם וְהִשְׁכִּ֣ים בַּבֹּ֘קֶר֮ וְהֶעֱלָ֣ה עֹלוֹת֮ מִסְפַּ֣ר כֻּלָּם֒ כִּ֚י אָמַ֣ר אִיּ֔וֹב אוּלַי֙ חָטְא֣וּ בָנַ֔י וּבֵרְכ֥וּ אֱלֹהִ֖ים בִּלְבָבָ֑ם כָּ֛כָה יַעֲשֶׂ֥ה אִיּ֖וֹב כׇּל־הַיָּמִֽים׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when the days of their feasting were gone about, Iyyov sent and sanctified them, and rose up early in the morning, and offered burnt offerings according to the number of them all: for Iyyov said, It may be that my sons have sinned, and despised God in their hearts. Thus Iyyov did continually.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when the [seven] days of feasting [of each son] had completed their circuit, then Iyyov would send and invite them [to his home] and he would rise early in the morning and sacrifice burnt-offerings according to the number of all of them; for Iyyov said: "Perchance my children sinned [(in overindulgence)] and 'blessed' [(euphemism for 'cursed')] G d in their hearts." Thus did Iyyov do all of his days.


###### Job 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:6)


וַיְהִ֣י הַיּ֔וֹם וַיָּבֹ֙אוּ֙ בְּנֵ֣י הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים לְהִתְיַצֵּ֖ב עַל־יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיָּב֥וֹא גַֽם־הַשָּׂטָ֖ן בְּתוֹכָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now there was a day when the sons of God came to present themselves before the Lord, and the adversary came also among them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was on that day [(Rosh Hashanah) when the circuit began again, in the house of the eldest son)], that the Sons of G d [(the Heavenly host)] came to stand around the L rd; and Satan came also, together with them.


###### Job 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:7)


וַיֹּ֧אמֶר יְהֹוָ֛ה אֶל־הַשָּׂטָ֖ן מֵאַ֣יִן תָּבֹ֑א וַיַּ֨עַן הַשָּׂטָ֤ן אֶת־יְהֹוָה֙ וַיֹּאמַ֔ר מִשּׁ֣וּט בָּאָ֔רֶץ וּמֵֽהִתְהַלֵּ֖ךְ בָּֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord said to the adversary, From where dost thou come? Then the adversary answered the Lord, and said, From going to and fro in the earth, and from walking up and down in it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L rd said to Satan: "Where are you coming from?" And Satan answered the L rd and said: "From wandering around the earth and walking within it" [(to discriminate between the good and the evil men)].


###### Job 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:8)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר יְהֹוָה֙ אֶל־הַשָּׂטָ֔ן הֲשַׂ֥מְתָּ לִבְּךָ֖ עַל־עַבְדִּ֣י אִיּ֑וֹב כִּ֣י אֵ֤ין כָּמֹ֙הוּ֙ בָּאָ֔רֶץ אִ֣ישׁ תָּ֧ם וְיָשָׁ֛ר יְרֵ֥א אֱלֹהִ֖ים וְסָ֥ר מֵרָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord said to the adversary, Hast thou considered my servant Iyyov, that there is none like him on earth, a perfect and an upright man, one who fears God, and turns away from evil?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L rd said to Satan: "Have you set your heart against [(i.e., to incriminate)] My servant Iyyov, whose like is not found on the earth, a pure and upright man, who fears G d and turns aside from evil?"


###### Job 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:9)


וַיַּ֧עַן הַשָּׂטָ֛ן אֶת־יְהֹוָ֖ה וַיֹּאמַ֑ר הַֽחִנָּ֔ם יָרֵ֥א אִיּ֖וֹב אֱלֹהִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the adversary answered the Lord, and said, Does Iyyov fear God for nought?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Satan answered the L rd and said: "Does Iyyov fear G d for nought?


###### Job 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:10)


הֲלֹֽא־[אַ֠תָּ֠ה] (את) שַׂ֣כְתָּ בַעֲד֧וֹ וּבְעַד־בֵּית֛וֹ וּבְעַ֥ד כׇּל־אֲשֶׁר־ל֖וֹ מִסָּבִ֑יב מַעֲשֵׂ֤ה יָדָיו֙ בֵּרַ֔כְתָּ וּמִקְנֵ֖הוּ פָּרַ֥ץ בָּאָֽרֶץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hast Thou not made a hedge about him, and about his house, and about all that he has on every side? Thou hast blessed the work of his hands, and his substance is increased in the land.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Were You not a [protective] fence for him, and for his family, and for all that is his, on all sides? You have blessed his handiwork, and his livestock have flourished in the land.


###### Job 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:11)


וְאוּלָם֙ שְֽׁלַֽח־נָ֣א יָֽדְךָ֔ וְגַ֖ע בְּכׇל־אֲשֶׁר־ל֑וֹ אִם־לֹ֥א עַל־פָּנֶ֖יךָ יְבָרְכֶֽךָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But now put forth Thy hand, and touch all that he has, and he will curse Thee to Thy face.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But just send forth Your hand and strike aught that is his, and [see if] he does not 'bless' You to Your face."


###### Job 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:12)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר יְהֹוָ֜ה אֶל־הַשָּׂטָ֗ן הִנֵּ֤ה כׇל־אֲשֶׁר־לוֹ֙ בְּיָדֶ֔ךָ רַ֣ק אֵלָ֔יו אַל־תִּשְׁלַ֖ח יָדֶ֑ךָ וַיֵּצֵא֙ הַשָּׂטָ֔ן מֵעִ֖ם פְּנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord said to the adversary, Behold, all that he has is in thy power; only upon himself do not put forth thy hand. So the adversary went out from the presence of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L rd said to Satan: "All that is his is in your hands. Just do not set forth your hands against him." And Satan went forth from the presence of the L rd.


###### Job 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:13)


וַיְהִ֖י הַיּ֑וֹם וּבָנָ֨יו וּבְנֹתָ֤יו אֹֽכְלִים֙ וְשֹׁתִ֣ים יַ֔יִן בְּבֵ֖ית אֲחִיהֶ֥ם הַבְּכֽוֹר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now there was a day when his sons and his daughters were eating and drinking wine in their eldest brother’s house:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was on the day when his sons and daughters were eating and drinking wine in the house of their eldest brother


###### Job 1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:14)


וּמַלְאָ֛ךְ בָּ֥א אֶל־אִיּ֖וֹב וַיֹּאמַ֑ר הַבָּקָר֙ הָי֣וּ חֹֽרְשׁ֔וֹת וְהָאֲתֹנ֖וֹת רֹע֥וֹת עַל־יְדֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and there came a messenger to Iyyov, and said, The oxen were ploughing, and the asses were feeding beside them:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
that a messenger came to Iyyov and said: "The cattle were plowing and the she-asses were grazing in their place


###### Job 1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:15)


וַתִּפֹּ֤ל שְׁבָא֙ וַתִּקָּחֵ֔ם וְאֶת־הַנְּעָרִ֖ים הִכּ֣וּ לְפִי־חָ֑רֶב וָאִמָּ֨לְטָ֧ה רַק־אֲנִ֛י לְבַדִּ֖י לְהַגִּ֥יד לָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and men of Sheva fell upon them, and took them away; and slew the servants with the edge of the sword; and I only am escaped alone to tell thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
when [the people of] Sheva descended upon them and took them, and the youths [i.e., the herdsmen] they smote with the sword; and I alone escaped, to tell you."


###### Job 1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:16)


ע֣וֹד**׀** זֶ֣ה מְדַבֵּ֗ר וְזֶה֮ בָּ֣א וַיֹּאמַר֒ אֵ֣שׁ אֱלֹהִ֗ים נָֽפְלָה֙ מִן־הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וַתִּבְעַ֥ר בַּצֹּ֛אן וּבַנְּעָרִ֖ים וַתֹּֽאכְלֵ֑ם וָאִמָּ֨לְטָ֧ה רַק־אֲנִ֛י לְבַדִּ֖י לְהַגִּ֥יד לָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
While he was yet speaking, there came also another, and said, The fire of God has fallen from heaven, and has burned up the sheep, and the servants, and consumed them; and I only am escaped alone to tell thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
While the first was yet speaking, a second came and said: "A fire of G d fell from heaven and swept through the sheep and the youths and consumed them; and I alone escaped, to tell you."


###### Job 1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:17)


ע֣וֹד**׀** זֶ֣ה מְדַבֵּ֗ר וְזֶה֮ בָּ֣א וַיֹּאמַר֒ כַּשְׂדִּ֞ים שָׂ֣מוּ**׀** שְׁלֹשָׁ֣ה רָאשִׁ֗ים וַֽיִּפְשְׁט֤וּ עַל־הַגְּמַלִּים֙ וַיִּקָּח֔וּם וְאֶת־הַנְּעָרִ֖ים הִכּ֣וּ לְפִי־חָ֑רֶב וָאִמָּ֨לְטָ֧ה רַק־אֲנִ֛י לְבַדִּ֖י לְהַגִּ֥יד לָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
While he was yet speaking, there came also another, and said, The Kasdim formed three bands, and fell upon the camels, and have carried them away, and have slain the servants with the edge of the sword; and I only am escaped alone to tell thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
While the second was yet speaking, a third came and said: "Kasdim formed three bands and spread out against the camels and took them, and the youths they smote with the sword; and I alone escaped, to tell you."


###### Job 1:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:18)


עַ֚ד זֶ֣ה מְדַבֵּ֔ר וְזֶ֖ה בָּ֣א וַיֹּאמַ֑ר בָּנֶ֨יךָ וּבְנוֹתֶ֤יךָ אֹֽכְלִים֙ וְשֹׁתִ֣ים יַ֔יִן בְּבֵ֖ית אֲחִיהֶ֥ם הַבְּכֽוֹר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
While he was yet speaking, there came also another, and said, Thy sons and thy daughters were eating and drinking wine in their eldest brother’s house:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
While the third was yet speaking, a fourth came and said: "Your sons and daughters were eating and drinking wine in the house of their eldest brother


###### Job 1:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:19)


וְהִנֵּה֩ ר֨וּחַ גְּדוֹלָ֜ה בָּ֣אָה**׀** מֵעֵ֣בֶר הַמִּדְבָּ֗ר וַיִּגַּע֙ בְּאַרְבַּע֙ פִּנּ֣וֹת הַבַּ֔יִת וַיִּפֹּ֥ל עַל־הַנְּעָרִ֖ים וַיָּמ֑וּתוּ וָאִמָּ֨לְטָ֧ה רַק־אֲנִ֛י לְבַדִּ֖י לְהַגִּ֥יד לָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and, behold, there came a great wind from across the wilderness, and smote the four corners of the house, and it fell upon the young men, and they are dead; and I only am escaped alone to tell thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
when a great wind came from across the desert and struck the four corners of the house, and it fell on the youths and killed them; and I alone escaped, to tell you."


###### Job 1:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:20)


וַיָּ֤קׇם אִיּוֹב֙ וַיִּקְרַ֣ע אֶת־מְעִל֔וֹ וַיָּ֖גׇז אֶת־רֹאשׁ֑וֹ וַיִּפֹּ֥ל אַ֖רְצָה וַיִּשְׁתָּֽחוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Iyyov arose, and rent his coat, and shaved his head, and fell down upon the ground, and prostrated himself,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Iyyov arose and tore his garment and tore his hair from his head and fell to the ground and prostrated himself.


###### Job 1:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:21)


וַיֹּ֩אמֶר֩ עָרֹ֨ם יָצָ֜תִי מִבֶּ֣טֶן אִמִּ֗י וְעָרֹם֙ אָשׁ֣וּב שָׁ֔מָּה יְהֹוָ֣ה נָתַ֔ן וַֽיהֹוָ֖ה לָקָ֑ח יְהִ֛י שֵׁ֥ם יְהֹוָ֖ה מְבֹרָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and said, Naked I came out of my mother’s womb, and naked I shall return there: the Lord gave, and the Lord has taken away; blessed be the name of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said: "Just as I came naked from the womb of my mother [(the earth)], so I will return there naked. The L rd gives and the L rd takes. May the name of the L rd be blessed."


###### Job 1:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/1:22)


בְּכׇל־זֹ֖את לֹא־חָטָ֣א אִיּ֑וֹב וְלֹא־נָתַ֥ן תִּפְלָ֖ה לֵאלֹהִֽים׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In all this Iyyov sinned not, nor did he lay reproach on God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
With all this, Iyyov did not sin and did not impute wrong to G d.



## Chapter 2

###### Job 2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:1)


וַיְהִ֣י הַיּ֔וֹם וַיָּבֹ֙אוּ֙ בְּנֵ֣י הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים לְהִתְיַצֵּ֖ב עַל־יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיָּב֤וֹא גַֽם־הַשָּׂטָן֙ בְּתֹכָ֔ם לְהִתְיַצֵּ֖ב עַל־יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Again there was a day when the sons of God came to present themselves before the Lord, and the adversary came also among them to present himself before the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was on the day that the sons of G d came to stand before the L rd, that Satan came also with them to stand before the L rd.


###### Job 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:2)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר יְהֹוָה֙ אֶל־הַשָּׂטָ֔ן אֵ֥י מִזֶּ֖ה תָּבֹ֑א וַיַּ֨עַן הַשָּׂטָ֤ן אֶת־יְהֹוָה֙ וַיֹּאמַ֔ר מִשֻּׁ֣ט בָּאָ֔רֶץ וּמֵֽהִתְהַלֵּ֖ךְ בָּֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord said to the adversary, From where dost thou come? And the adversary answered the Lord, and said, From going to and fro in the earth, and from walking up and down in it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L rd said to Satan: "Where are you coming from?" And Satan answered the L rd and said: "From wandering around the earth and walking within it."


###### Job 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:3)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר יְהֹוָ֜ה אֶל־הַשָּׂטָ֗ן הֲשַׂ֣מְתָּ לִבְּךָ֮ אֶל־עַבְדִּ֣י אִיּוֹב֒ כִּי֩ אֵ֨ין כָּמֹ֜הוּ בָּאָ֗רֶץ אִ֣ישׁ תָּ֧ם וְיָשָׁ֛ר יְרֵ֥א אֱלֹהִ֖ים וְסָ֣ר מֵרָ֑ע וְעֹדֶ֙נּוּ֙ מַחֲזִ֣יק בְּתֻמָּת֔וֹ וַתְּסִיתֵ֥נִי ב֖וֹ לְבַלְּע֥וֹ חִנָּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord said to the adversary, Hast thou considered my servant Iyyov, that there is none like him on earth, a perfect and an upright man, one that fears God and turns away from evil? and still he holds fast to his integrity, although thou didst move me against him, to destroy him without cause.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L rd said to Satan: "Have you set your heart against My servant Iyyov, whose like is not found on the earth, a pure and upright man, who fears G d and turns aside from evil, who still persists in his purity, though you have incited Me against him to destroy him for nought?"


###### Job 2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:4)


וַיַּ֧עַן הַשָּׂטָ֛ן אֶת־יְהֹוָ֖ה וַיֹּאמַ֑ר ע֣וֹר בְּעַד־ע֗וֹר וְכֹל֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר לָאִ֔ישׁ יִתֵּ֖ן בְּעַ֥ד נַפְשֽׁוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the adversary answered the Lord, and said, Skin covers skin! for all that a man has he will give for his life.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Satan answered the L rd and said: "Skin for skin [i.e., a lesser limb for a greater], and all that a man possesses will he give for his life" [(and since Iyyov knows that he is liable for his life, he willingly makes the exchange.)]


###### Job 2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:5)


אוּלָם֙ שְֽׁלַֽח־נָ֣א יָֽדְךָ֔ וְגַ֥ע אֶל־עַצְמ֖וֹ וְאֶל־בְּשָׂר֑וֹ אִם־לֹ֥א אֶל־פָּנֶ֖יךָ יְבָרְכֶֽךָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But put forth thy hand now, and touch his bone and his flesh, and he will curse Thee to Thy face.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But just stretch out Your hand and smite himself and his flesh, [and see] if he will not 'bless' You to Your face."


###### Job 2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:6)


וַיֹּ֧אמֶר יְהֹוָ֛ה אֶל־הַשָּׂטָ֖ן הִנּ֣וֹ בְיָדֶ֑ךָ אַ֖ךְ אֶת־נַפְשׁ֥וֹ שְׁמֹֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord said to the adversary, Behold, he is in thy hand; but save his life.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L rd said to Satan: "He is in your hands; just take care to heed his soul" [(that it not leave his body)].


###### Job 2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:7)


וַיֵּצֵא֙ הַשָּׂטָ֔ן מֵאֵ֖ת פְּנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיַּ֤ךְ אֶת־אִיּוֹב֙ בִּשְׁחִ֣ין רָ֔ע מִכַּ֥ף רַגְל֖וֹ (עד) [וְעַ֥ד] קׇדְקֳדֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So the adversary went forth from the presence of the Lord, and smote Iyyov with vile sores from the sole of his foot to his crown.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Satan went forth from the presence of the L rd, and he smote Iyyov with severe boils, from the soles of his feet to his crown.


###### Job 2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:8)


וַיִּֽקַּֽח־ל֣וֹ חֶ֔רֶשׂ לְהִתְגָּרֵ֖ד בּ֑וֹ וְה֖וּא יֹשֵׁ֥ב בְּתוֹךְ־הָאֵֽפֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he took him a potsherd with which to scrape himself; and he sat down among the ashes.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he took a shard to scratch himself with, and he sat among the ashes.


###### Job 2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:9)


וַתֹּ֤אמֶר לוֹ֙ אִשְׁתּ֔וֹ עֹדְךָ֖ מַחֲזִ֣יק בְּתֻמָּתֶ֑ךָ בָּרֵ֥ךְ אֱלֹהִ֖ים וָמֻֽת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then his wife said to him, Dost thou still retain thy integrity? curse God, and die.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And his wife said to him: "Are you still persisting in your purity? 'Bless' G d and die."


###### Job 2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:10)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלֶ֗יהָ כְּדַבֵּ֞ר אַחַ֤ת הַנְּבָלוֹת֙ תְּדַבֵּ֔רִי גַּ֣ם אֶת־הַטּ֗וֹב נְקַבֵּל֙ מֵאֵ֣ת הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים וְאֶת־הָרָ֖ע לֹ֣א נְקַבֵּ֑ל בְּכׇל־זֹ֛את לֹא־חָטָ֥א אִיּ֖וֹב בִּשְׂפָתָֽיו׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But he said to her, Thou speakest as one of the foolish women speaks. What? shall we receive good at the hand of God, and shall we not receive evil? In all this Iyyov did not sin with his lips.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said to her: "You speak as one of the lowly ones. [(Is it not enough that He created us, but)] shall we also receive good from G d and not receive bad?" With all this, Iyyov did not sin with his lips [(but he did sin in his heart)].


###### Job 2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:11)


וַֽיִּשְׁמְע֞וּ שְׁלֹ֣שֶׁת**׀** רֵעֵ֣י אִיּ֗וֹב אֵ֣ת כׇּל־הָרָעָ֣ה הַזֹּאת֮ הַבָּ֣אָה עָלָיו֒ וַיָּבֹ֙אוּ֙ אִ֣ישׁ מִמְּקֹמ֔וֹ אֱלִיפַ֤ז הַתֵּֽימָנִי֙ וּבִלְדַּ֣ד הַשּׁוּחִ֔י וְצוֹפַ֖ר הַנַּֽעֲמָתִ֑י וַיִּוָּעֲד֣וּ יַחְדָּ֔ו לָב֥וֹא לָנֽוּד־ל֖וֹ וּֽלְנַחֲמֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now when Iyyov’s three friends heard of all this evil that was come upon him, they came every one from his own place; Elifaz the Temanite, and Bildad the Shuĥite, and Żofar the Na῾amatite: for they had made an appointment together to come to mourn with him and to comfort him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the three friends [i.e., lovers] of Iyyov heard of all this evil that had come upon him, and they came, each from his place: Elifaz the Temanite, Bildad the Shuchite, and Tzofar the Na'amathite. And they gathered together to come to bemoan him and to console him.


###### Job 2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:12)


וַיִּשְׂא֨וּ אֶת־עֵינֵיהֶ֤ם מֵֽרָחוֹק֙ וְלֹ֣א הִכִּירֻ֔הוּ וַיִּשְׂא֥וּ קוֹלָ֖ם וַיִּבְכּ֑וּ וַֽיִּקְרְעוּ֙ אִ֣ישׁ מְעִל֔וֹ וַיִּזְרְק֥וּ עָפָ֛ר עַל־רָאשֵׁיהֶ֖ם הַשָּׁמָֽיְמָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when they lifted up their eyes afar off, and knew him not, they lifted up their voice, and wept; and they rent every one his coat, and sprinkled dust upon their heads toward heaven.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they lifted their eyes from afar and they did not recognize him [(his features having become transfigured by his afflictions)]. And they lifted their voices and cried. And each of them tore his garment. And they cast dust above their heads, heavenwards [(in mourning)].


###### Job 2:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/2:13)


וַיֵּשְׁב֤וּ אִתּוֹ֙ לָאָ֔רֶץ שִׁבְעַ֥ת יָמִ֖ים וְשִׁבְעַ֣ת לֵיל֑וֹת וְאֵין־דֹּבֵ֤ר אֵלָיו֙ דָּבָ֔ר כִּ֣י רָא֔וּ כִּי־גָדַ֥ל הַכְּאֵ֖ב מְאֹֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they sat down with him on the ground for seven days and seven nights, and none spoke a word to him: for they saw that his suffering was very great.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they sat with him upon the ground seven days and seven nights, and no one spoke a word to him, for they saw that he was sorely grieved.



## Chapter 3

###### Job 3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:1)


אַֽחֲרֵי־כֵ֗ן פָּתַ֤ח אִיּוֹב֙ אֶת־פִּ֔יהוּ וַיְקַלֵּ֖ל אֶת־יוֹמֽוֹ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
After this Iyyov opened his mouth, and cursed his day.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
After that Iyyov opened his mouth and cursed his day [(the day of his birth)].


###### Job 3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:2)


וַיַּ֥עַן אִיּ֗וֹב וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Iyyov spoke, and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Iyyov raised his voice and said:


###### Job 3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:3)


יֹ֣אבַד י֭וֹם אִוָּ֣לֶד בּ֑וֹ וְהַלַּ֥יְלָה אָ֝מַ֗ר הֹ֣רָה גָֽבֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Oh that the day had perished wherein I was born, and the night which said, There is a man child conceived.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would that [the] day were lost on which I was to be born, and the night [when it was] said: "A man [(my father)] has caused [(my mother)] to conceive."


###### Job 3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:4)


הַיּ֥וֹם הַה֗וּא יְֽהִ֫י־חֹ֥שֶׁךְ אַֽל־יִדְרְשֵׁ֣הוּ אֱל֣וֹהַּ מִמַּ֑עַל וְאַל־תּוֹפַ֖ע עָלָ֣יו נְהָרָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let that day be darkness; let not God inquire after it from above, nor let the light shine upon it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Let that day [(every year)] be darkness. Let G d on high not be solicitous of it [(for good)], and let no light shine upon it.


###### Job 3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:5)


יִגְאָלֻ֡הוּ חֹ֣שֶׁךְ וְ֭צַלְמָוֶת תִּשְׁכׇּן־עָלָ֣יו עֲנָנָ֑ה יְ֝בַעֲתֻ֗הוּ כִּֽמְרִ֥ירֵי יֽוֹם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let darkness and the shadow of death stain it; let a cloud dwell upon it; let the blackness of the day terrify it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Let it be sullied by darkness and the shadow of death; let it be shrouded in darkness; let the demons of day confound it.


###### Job 3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:6)


הַלַּ֥יְלָה הַהוּא֮ יִקָּחֵ֢ה֫וּ אֹ֥פֶל אַל־יִ֭חַדְּ בִּימֵ֣י שָׁנָ֑ה בְּמִסְפַּ֥ר יְ֝רָחִ֗ים אַל־יָבֹֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As for that night, let darkness seize upon it; let it not rejoice among the days of the year, let it not come into the number of the months.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Let that night be seized by thick darkness; let it not rejoice with the days of the year; let it not enter into the reckoning of months.


###### Job 3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:7)


הִנֵּ֤ה הַלַּ֣יְלָה הַ֭הוּא יְהִ֣י גַלְמ֑וּד אַל־תָּב֖וֹא רְנָנָ֣ה בֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Lo, let that night be solitary, let no joyful cry be heard in it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Let that night be unfrequented; let no rejoicing enter into it.


###### Job 3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:8)


יִקְּבֻ֥הוּ אֹֽרְרֵי־י֑וֹם הָ֝עֲתִידִ֗ים עֹרֵ֥ר לִוְיָתָֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let them curse it who curse the day, who are ready to arouse livyatan.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Let the cursers of [their] day curse it, those destined to be childless [in] their union.


###### Job 3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:9)


יֶחְשְׁכוּ֮ כּוֹכְבֵ֢י נִ֫שְׁפּ֥וֹ יְקַו־לְא֥וֹר וָאַ֑יִן וְאַל־יִ֝רְאֶ֗ה בְּעַפְעַפֵּי־שָֽׁחַר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let the stars of its dusk be dark; let it look for light, but have none; and let it not see the eyelids of the morn:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Let the stars of its night be darkened; let it hope for light and not find it; and let it not see the first light of day.


###### Job 3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:10)


כִּ֤י לֹ֣א סָ֭גַר דַּלְתֵ֣י בִטְנִ֑י וַיַּסְתֵּ֥ר עָ֝מָ֗ל מֵעֵינָֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
because it did not shut up the doors of my mother’s womb nor hide trouble from my eyes.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For He did not close the walls of my stomach [i.e., the womb] and hide toil from my eyes.


###### Job 3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:11)


לָ֤מָּה לֹּ֣א מֵרֶ֣חֶם אָמ֑וּת מִבֶּ֖טֶן יָצָ֣אתִי וְאֶגְוָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Why did I not die from the womb? why did I not perish when I came out of the belly?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Why did I not die when out of the womb? Why [did I] not perish when leaving the stomach?


###### Job 3:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:12)


מַ֭דּוּעַ קִדְּמ֣וּנִי בִרְכָּ֑יִם וּמַה־שָּׁ֝דַ֗יִם כִּ֣י אִינָֽק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Why did the knees receive me? or why the breasts that I should suck?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Why did [my mother's] knees receive me, and why [her] breasts, that I should suck?


###### Job 3:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:13)


כִּֽי־עַ֭תָּה שָׁכַ֣בְתִּי וְאֶשְׁק֑וֹט יָ֝שַׁ֗נְתִּי אָ֤ז**׀** יָנ֬וּחַֽ לִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For now should I have lain still and been quiet, I should have slept: then had I been at rest,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For now I could have been lying still [(in the grave)], sleeping and at rest.


###### Job 3:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:14)


עִם־מְ֭לָכִים וְיֹ֣עֲצֵי אָ֑רֶץ הַבֹּנִ֖ים חֳרָב֣וֹת לָֽמוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
with kings and counsellors of the earth, who built desolate places for themselves;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
With kings and counselors of the earth, who build up waste places for their names' sake.


###### Job 3:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:15)


א֣וֹ עִם־שָׂ֭רִים זָהָ֣ב לָהֶ֑ם הַֽמְמַלְאִ֖ים בָּתֵּיהֶ֣ם כָּֽסֶף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
or with princes that had gold, who filled their houses with silver:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Or with princes possessed of gold, who fill their houses with silver.


###### Job 3:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:16)


א֤וֹ כְנֵ֣פֶל טָ֭מוּן לֹ֣א אֶהְיֶ֑ה כְּ֝עֹלְלִ֗ים לֹא־רָ֥אוּ אֽוֹר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
or as an hidden untimely birth I had not been; as infants that never saw light.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Or [why could I not have been] a huddled-up abortion, like fetuses who never saw the light?


###### Job 3:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:17)


שָׁ֣ם רְ֭שָׁעִים חָ֣דְלוּ רֹ֑גֶז וְשָׁ֥ם יָ֝נ֗וּחוּ יְגִ֣יעֵי כֹֽחַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There the wicked cease from troubling; and there the weary are at rest.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There [(in the grave)] the wicked cease from wrath, and there the weary rest.


###### Job 3:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:18)


יַ֭חַד אֲסִירִ֣ים שַׁאֲנָ֑נוּ לֹ֥א שָׁ֝מְע֗וּ ק֣וֹל נֹגֵֽשׂ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There the prisoners are at ease together; they hear not the voice of the slave driver.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The prisoners there are all at peace; they do not hear the oppressor's voice.


###### Job 3:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:19)


קָטֹ֣ן וְ֭גָדוֹל שָׁ֣ם ה֑וּא וְ֝עֶ֗בֶד חׇפְשִׁ֥י מֵאֲדֹנָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The small and great are there; and the servant is free from his master.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(True and enduring)] smallness or greatness are [recognized] there [(and not in this world)], and the slave is free of his master.


###### Job 3:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:20)


לָ֤מָּה יִתֵּ֣ן לְעָמֵ֣ל א֑וֹר וְ֝חַיִּ֗ים לְמָ֣רֵי נָֽפֶשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Why is light given to him that is in misery, and life to the bitter in soul;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Why should light be given to the [sorrow-] burdened, and life to the bitter in soul,


###### Job 3:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:21)


הַֽמְחַכִּ֣ים לַמָּ֣וֶת וְאֵינֶ֑נּוּ וַֽ֝יַּחְפְּרֻ֗הוּ מִמַּטְמוֹנִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who long for death, but it does not come; and dig for it more than for hidden treasures;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
who hope for death, but find it not, though they dig for it more than for treasure,


###### Job 3:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:22)


הַשְּׂמֵחִ֥ים אֱלֵי־גִ֑יל יָ֝שִׂ֗ישׂוּ כִּ֣י יִמְצְאוּ־קָֽבֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who rejoice exceedingly, and are glad, when they can find a grave?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
who [would] regale in joy, the joy of finding a grave


###### Job 3:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:23)


לְ֭גֶבֶר אֲשֶׁר־דַּרְכּ֣וֹ נִסְתָּ֑רָה וַיָּ֖סֶךְ אֱל֣וֹהַּ בַּעֲדֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Why is light given to a man whose way is hidden, and from whom God has screened himself?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
for the man [(i.e., himself)] whose way [(i.e., all the good that he has done)] is hidden [from Him] and who has been fenced off by G d?


###### Job 3:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:24)


כִּֽי־לִפְנֵ֣י לַ֭חְמִי אַנְחָתִ֣י תָבֹ֑א וַֽיִּתְּכ֥וּ כַ֝מַּ֗יִם שַׁאֲגֹתָֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For my sighing comes before I eat, and my roarings are poured out like the waters.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For my groaning comes before my bread and my cries stream forth like water.


###### Job 3:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:25)


כִּ֤י פַ֣חַד פָּ֭חַדְתִּי וַיֶּאֱתָיֵ֑נִי וַאֲשֶׁ֥ר יָ֝גֹ֗רְתִּי יָ֣בֹא לִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the thing which I had feared is come upon me, and that which I was afraid of is come to me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For what I feared [(for my children, viz. 1:5)] has come upon me; what I dreaded has befallen me.


###### Job 3:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/3:26)


לֹ֤א שָׁלַ֨וְתִּי**׀** וְלֹ֖א שָׁקַ֥טְתִּי וְֽלֹא־נָ֗חְתִּי וַיָּ֥בֹא רֹֽגֶז׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I had no repose, nor had I rest, nor was I quiet; yet trouble came.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I was never at peace; I was never still; I was never at rest — and the terror came!



## Chapter 4

###### Job 4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:1)


וַ֭יַּעַן אֱלִיפַ֥ז הַֽתֵּימָנִ֗י וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Elifaz the Temanite answered and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Elifaz the Temanite [(the son of Esav [who, being reared by Isaac, attained to the Holy Spirit])] raised his voice and said:


###### Job 4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:2)


הֲנִסָּ֬ה דָבָ֣ר אֵלֶ֣יךָ תִּלְאֶ֑ה וַעְצֹ֥ר בְּ֝מִלִּ֗ין מִ֣י יוּכָֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If one ventures a word to thee, wilt thou be grieved? but who can withhold himself from speaking?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do you tire with one trial? Who, then, can refrain from rebuttal?


###### Job 4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:3)


הִ֭נֵּה יִסַּ֣רְתָּ רַבִּ֑ים וְיָדַ֖יִם רָפ֣וֹת תְּחַזֵּֽק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, thou hast instructed many, and thou hast strengthened the weak hands.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Did you not reprove many [who inveighed against the Almighty] and did you not strengthen the hands of those who were weak [in bearing up under the attribute of justice]?


###### Job 4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:4)


כּ֭וֹשֵׁל יְקִימ֣וּן מִלֶּ֑יךָ וּבִרְכַּ֖יִם כֹּרְע֣וֹת תְּאַמֵּֽץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thy words have upheld him that was falling, and thou hast strengthened the feeble knees.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Your words would raise the stumbling and you would strengthen the buckling knees.


###### Job 4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:5)


כִּ֤י עַתָּ֨ה**׀** תָּב֣וֹא אֵלֶ֣יךָ וַתֵּ֑לֶא תִּגַּ֥ע עָ֝דֶ֗יךָ וַתִּבָּהֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But now it is come upon thee, and thou art weary; it touches thee, and thou art troubled.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now that it [(affliction)] comes upon you, do you weary? It touches you and you are confounded?


###### Job 4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:6)


הֲלֹ֣א יִ֭רְאָתְךָ כִּסְלָתֶ֑ךָ תִּ֝קְוָתְךָ֗ וְתֹ֣ם דְּרָכֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Is not thy fear of God thy confidence, and thy hope the integrity of thy ways?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Is it not [now evident that all] your fear [of the L rd] was folly, as was your hope [of good] and the uprightness of your ways?


###### Job 4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:7)


זְכׇר־נָ֗א מִ֤י ה֣וּא נָקִ֣י אָבָ֑ד וְ֝אֵיפֹ֗ה יְשָׁרִ֥ים נִכְחָֽדוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Recall, now, who that was innocent ever perished? or where were the upright cut off?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Remember now: Who is the clean man that has gone lost, and where are the righteous who have been cut off,


###### Job 4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:8)


כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר רָ֭אִיתִי חֹ֣רְשֵׁי אָ֑וֶן וְזֹרְעֵ֖י עָמָ֣ל יִקְצְרֻֽהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Even as I have seen, they that plough iniquity, and sow wickedness, reap the same.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
as I have seen the plowers of iniquity and the sowers of wrong reap it [(the fruits of their evil)]?


###### Job 4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:9)


מִנִּשְׁמַ֣ת אֱל֣וֹהַּ יֹאבֵ֑דוּ וּמֵר֖וּחַ אַפּ֣וֹ יִכְלֽוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
By the breath of God they perish, and by the blast of his anger they are consumed.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
By the breath [(the tempestuous wrath)] of G d they go lost; they are cut off by the wind of His nostril. [(The allusion is to the generation of the flood.)]


###### Job 4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:10)


שַׁאֲגַ֣ת אַ֭רְיֵה וְק֣וֹל שָׁ֑חַל וְשִׁנֵּ֖י כְפִירִ֣ים נִתָּֽעוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The roar of the lion, and the cry of the fierce lion, and the teeth of the young lions, are broken.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Though theirs is] the roar of the [great] lion, and the voice of the lion, and the teeth of the young lions, they are confounded.


###### Job 4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:11)


לַ֭יִשׁ אֹבֵ֣ד מִבְּלִי־טָ֑רֶף וּבְנֵ֥י לָ֝בִ֗יא יִתְפָּרָֽדוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The old lion perishes for lack of prey, and the lion’s whelps are scattered abroad.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The lion goes lost, without his prey, and the lion's children are scattered [(over all the earth [an allusion to the generation of the Tower of Bavel])].


###### Job 4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:12)


וְ֭אֵלַי דָּבָ֣ר יְגֻנָּ֑ב וַתִּקַּ֥ח אׇ֝זְנִ֗י שֵׁ֣מֶץ מֶֽנְהֽוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now a word came stealthily to me, and my ear took fright at it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Now [(concerning you)] a thing [(a prophecy)] stole itself to me [(the Holy Spirit not coming openly to the gentile prophets)]; my ear caught a trace of it.


###### Job 4:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:13)


בִּ֭שְׂעִפִּים מֵחֶזְיֹנ֣וֹת לָ֑יְלָה בִּנְפֹ֥ל תַּ֝רְדֵּמָ֗ה עַל־אֲנָשִֽׁים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In thoughts from the visions of the night, when deep sleep falls,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the thoughts of the visions of the night, when deep sleep falls upon men.


###### Job 4:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:14)


פַּ֣חַד קְ֭רָאַנִי וּרְעָדָ֑ה וְרֹ֖ב עַצְמוֹתַ֣י הִפְחִֽיד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
fear came upon me, and trembling, which made all my bones to shake.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Fear possessed me and trembling, and it affrighted the bulk of my bones.


###### Job 4:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:15)


וְ֭רוּחַ עַל־פָּנַ֣י יַחֲלֹ֑ף תְּ֝סַמֵּ֗ר שַׂעֲרַ֥ת בְּשָׂרִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then a spirit passed before my face; it made the hair of my flesh to bristle up:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a spirit [(an angel)] passed before my face; it stood the hair of my flesh on end.


###### Job 4:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:16)


יַעֲמֹ֤ד**׀** וְֽלֹא־אַכִּ֬יר מַרְאֵ֗הוּ תְּ֭מוּנָה לְנֶ֣גֶד עֵינָ֑י דְּמָמָ֖ה וָק֣וֹל אֶשְׁמָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
it stood still, but I could not discern its form: a shape was before my eyes: there was silence, and I heard a voice saying,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
It stood, but I could not delineate its appearance, [though] its visage was before my eyes; a soft voice I heard;


###### Job 4:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:17)


הַ֭אֱנוֹשׁ מֵאֱל֣וֹהַּ יִצְדָּ֑ק אִ֥ם מֵ֝עֹשֵׂ֗הוּ יִטְהַר־גָּֽבֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Shall mortal man be more just than God? shall a man be more pure than his maker?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
"Will a mortal [presume to] be more righteous than G d? Will a man be purer than his Maker?


###### Job 4:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:18)


הֵ֣ן בַּ֭עֲבָדָיו לֹ֣א יַאֲמִ֑ין וּ֝בְמַלְאָכָ֗יו יָשִׂ֥ים תׇּהֳלָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, he puts no trust in his servants; and his angels he charges with folly:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In His very servants [(Moses and Aaron)] He places no faith and to His angels [Moses and Aaron (viz. Numbers 20:16)] He ascribes wrong [viz. Ibid. 12].


###### Job 4:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:19)


אַ֤ף**׀** שֹׁכְנֵ֬י בָֽתֵּי־חֹ֗מֶר אֲשֶׁר־בֶּעָפָ֥ר יְסוֹדָ֑ם יְ֝דַכְּא֗וּם לִפְנֵי־עָֽשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
how much more those who dwell in houses of clay, whose foundation is in the dust, who are crushed before the moth?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
How much more so [is He exalted above] the inhabitants of houses of clay [i.e., graves], whose origin is in the dust, who are crushed before the worms [(which devour them)]!


###### Job 4:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:20)


מִבֹּ֣קֶר לָעֶ֣רֶב יֻכַּ֑תּוּ מִבְּלִ֥י מֵ֝שִׂ֗ים לָנֶ֥צַח יֹאבֵֽדוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Between morning and evening they are destroyed: they perish for ever without anyone paying heed.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
From morning to night [i.e., in one day] they are broken, without taking [it to heart to repent], and they go lost for all time.


###### Job 4:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/4:21)


הֲלֹא־נִסַּ֣ע יִתְרָ֣ם בָּ֑ם יָ֝מ֗וּתוּ וְלֹ֣א בְחׇכְמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Is not their excellency which was in them gone away? they die; for they are without wisdom.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Is their primacy [(their pride and their wisdom)] not swallowed up in them? They die — and not in wisdom."



## Chapter 5

###### Job 5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:1)


קְֽרָא־נָ֭א הֲיֵ֣שׁ עוֹנֶ֑ךָּ וְאֶל־מִ֖י מִקְּדֹשִׁ֣ים תִּפְנֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Call now, is there any that will answer thee? and to which of the holy ones wilt thou turn?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(reversion to the reproof [after the prophecy])] Cry out now. Will anyone answer you? And to which of the holy ones [(who decreed this upon you)] will you turn [to war with]?


###### Job 5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:2)


כִּֽי־לֶ֭אֱוִיל יַהֲרׇג־כָּ֑עַשׂ וּ֝פֹתֶ֗ה תָּמִ֥ית קִנְאָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For anger kills the foolish man, and envy slays the simpleton.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For anger kills a fool [(like you. [Had you kept quiet, the attribute of mercy might have "returned" to you.])], and wrath slays the simpleton.


###### Job 5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:3)


אֲֽנִי־רָ֭אִיתִי אֱוִ֣יל מַשְׁרִ֑ישׁ וָאֶקּ֖וֹב נָוֵ֣הוּ פִתְאֹֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I have seen the foolish taking root: and suddenly I cursed his dwelling, saying,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[When] I saw the [wicked] fool take root, I cursed his abode at once:


###### Job 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:4)


יִרְחֲק֣וּ בָנָ֣יו מִיֶּ֑שַׁע וְיִֽדַּכְּא֥וּ בַ֝שַּׁ֗עַר וְאֵ֣ין מַצִּֽיל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let his children be far from safety, and let them be crushed in the gate, with none to rescue them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
"Let his children be far from help; let them be ground in the gate, with none to come to the rescue."


###### Job 5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:5)


אֲשֶׁ֤ר קְצִיר֨וֹ**׀** רָ֘עֵ֤ב יֹאכֵ֗ל וְאֶֽל־מִצִּנִּ֥ים יִקָּחֵ֑הוּ וְשָׁאַ֖ף צַמִּ֣ים חֵילָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let the hungry eat up his harvest, and take it to the thorn hedges, and let the thirsty swallow up their substance.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[When the wicked one dies,] the hungry one [(who was despoiled by him in his lifetime)] will eat his harvest, and he [(the judge)] will take it for [the survivors] of his weapons, and the thirsty [i.e., the despoiled] will recover their wealth.


###### Job 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:6)


כִּ֤י**׀** לֹא־יֵצֵ֣א מֵעָפָ֣ר אָ֑וֶן וּ֝מֵאֲדָמָ֗ה לֹא־יִצְמַ֥ח עָמָֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For affliction does not come out of the dust, nor does trouble spring out of the ground;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For affliction does not spring from the dust [i.e., in vain] and distress does not sprout from the earth.


###### Job 5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:7)


כִּֽי־אָ֭דָם לְעָמָ֣ל יוּלָּ֑ד וּבְנֵי־רֶ֝֗שֶׁף יַגְבִּ֥יהוּ עֽוּף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but man is born to trouble, as the sparks fly upward.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For man is born to [suffer] distress [for his sins], [unlike] the flaming ones [(the angels)], who fly on high [(above such things)].


###### Job 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:8)


אוּלָ֗ם אֲ֭נִי אֶדְרֹ֣שׁ אֶל־אֵ֑ל וְאֶל־אֱ֝לֹהִ֗ים אָשִׂ֥ים דִּבְרָתִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But I would seek to God, and to God I would commit my cause:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But [(were I in your position)], I would beseech the L rd and I would place my words [of supplication] with G d,


###### Job 5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:9)


עֹשֶׂ֣ה גְ֭דֹלוֹת וְאֵ֣ין חֵ֑קֶר נִ֝פְלָא֗וֹת עַד־אֵ֥ין מִסְפָּֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who does great things and unsearchable; marvellous things without number:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
who does great things which cannot be fathomed; wonders without end.


###### Job 5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:10)


הַנֹּתֵ֣ן מָ֭טָר עַל־פְּנֵי־אָ֑רֶץ וְשֹׁ֥לֵֽחַ מַ֝֗יִם עַל־פְּנֵ֥י חוּצֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who gives rain upon the earth, and sends water upon the fields:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He bestows rain upon the earth [(Eretz Yisrael)] and sends water [(through an intermediary)] to the outlying regions,


###### Job 5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:11)


לָשׂ֣וּם שְׁפָלִ֣ים לְמָר֑וֹם וְ֝קֹדְרִ֗ים שָׂ֣גְבוּ יֶֽשַׁע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to set up on high those who are low; that those who mourn may be exalted to safety.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
to place the lowly on high [(against the intrigues of the price-gougers)]. And those [whose faces were] darkened [by hunger] have taken strength [in His] salvation.


###### Job 5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:12)


מֵ֭פֵר מַחְשְׁב֣וֹת עֲרוּמִ֑ים וְֽלֹא־תַעֲשֶׂ֥נָה יְ֝דֵיהֶ֗ם תֻּֽשִׁיָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He frustrates the devices of the crafty, so that their hands cannot perform their policy.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He sets to nought the thoughts of the subtle ones, and their hands do not execute their counsels.


###### Job 5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:13)


לֹכֵ֣ד חֲכָמִ֣ים בְּעׇרְמָ֑ם וַעֲצַ֖ת נִפְתָּלִ֣ים נִמְהָֽרָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He catches the wise in their own craftiness; and the rash counsel of the crooked.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He ensnares the wise in their [own] subtleties, and the counsel of the perverse hastens [to their destruction].


###### Job 5:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:14)


יוֹמָ֥ם יְפַגְּשׁוּ־חֹ֑שֶׁךְ וְ֝כַלַּ֗יְלָה יְֽמַשְׁשׁ֥וּ בַֽצׇּהֳרָֽיִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They meet with darkness in the daytime, and grope in the noonday as in the night.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the light of day, they will encounter darkness [(the foiling of their plans)], and, as in the night, they will feel about in the afternoon.


###### Job 5:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:15)


וַיֹּ֣שַׁע מֵ֭חֶרֶב מִפִּיהֶ֑ם וּמִיַּ֖ד חָזָ֣ק אֶבְיֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But he saves from the sword, from the enemy’s mouth; and the poor from the hand of the mighty.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He will rescue [(their intended victims)] from the sword — from their mouths [(which sought to swallow them)] — and the lowly one from the hand of the strong.


###### Job 5:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:16)


וַתְּהִ֣י לַדַּ֣ל תִּקְוָ֑ה וְ֝עֹלָ֗תָה קָ֣פְצָה פִּֽיהָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So the poor has hope, and iniquity stops her mouth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the poor one shall realize [his] hope, and wrongdoing shall close its mouth.


###### Job 5:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:17)


הִנֵּ֤ה אַשְׁרֵ֣י אֱ֭נוֹשׁ יוֹכִחֶ֣נּוּ אֱל֑וֹהַּ וּמוּסַ֥ר שַׁ֝דַּ֗י אַל־תִּמְאָֽס׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, happy is the man whom God corrects: therefore do not despise the chastening of the Almighty:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Happy is the man whom G d chastises [(and whom He thus heals), so] do not spurn the reproof of the Almighty.


###### Job 5:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:18)


כִּ֤י ה֣וּא יַכְאִ֣יב וְיֶחְבָּ֑שׁ יִ֝מְחַ֗ץ וְיָדָ֥ו תִּרְפֶּֽינָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for he makes sore, and binds up: he wounds, but his hands make whole.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For [wherewith] He wounds, He binds up, and with the hands He strikes with He heals.


###### Job 5:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:19)


בְּשֵׁ֣שׁ צָ֭רוֹת יַצִּילֶ֑ךָּ וּבְשֶׁ֓בַע**׀** לֹֽא־יִגַּ֖ע בְּךָ֣ רָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He will deliver thee in six troubles: and in seven no evil shall touch thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
From [(the following)] six woes He shall save you [(by the afflictions which you have suffered)], and in the seventh, evil shall not reach you.


###### Job 5:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:20)


בְּֽ֭רָעָב פָּדְךָ֣ מִמָּ֑וֶת וּ֝בְמִלְחָמָ֗ה מִ֣ידֵי חָֽרֶב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In famine he will redeem thee from death: and in war from the power of the sword.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In hunger he will redeem you from death, and in war, from the hands of the sword.


###### Job 5:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:21)


בְּשׁ֣וֹט לָ֭שׁוֹן תֵּחָבֵ֑א וְֽלֹא־תִירָ֥א מִ֝שֹּׁ֗ד כִּ֣י יָבֽוֹא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt be hid from the scourge of the tongue: nor shalt thou be afraid of destruction when it comes.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When the tongue [of slander] is unleashed, you will be hidden, and you will not fear destruction when it comes.


###### Job 5:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:22)


לְשֹׁ֣ד וּלְכָפָ֣ן תִּשְׂחָ֑ק וּֽמֵחַיַּ֥ת הָ֝אָ֗רֶץ אַל־תִּירָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
At destruction and famine thou shalt laugh: nor shalt thou be afraid of the beasts of the earth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You will laugh at despoiling and hoarding, and you will not fear the "beasts of the earth" [(robbers)].


###### Job 5:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:23)


כִּ֤י עִם־אַבְנֵ֣י הַשָּׂדֶ֣ה בְרִיתֶ֑ךָ וְחַיַּ֥ת הַ֝שָּׂדֶ֗ה הׇשְׁלְמָה־לָּֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For thou shalt be in league with the stones of the field: and the wild beasts shall be at peace with thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But with the avnei hasadeh [(a man-like creature)] will be your covenant, and the chayath hasadeh [(a kind of creature)] will be at peace with you.


###### Job 5:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:24)


וְֽ֭יָדַעְתָּ כִּֽי־שָׁל֣וֹם אׇהֳלֶ֑ךָ וּֽפָקַדְתָּ֥ נָ֝וְךָ֗ וְלֹ֣א תֶחֱטָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt know that thy tent is at peace; and thou shalt visit thy habitation, and shalt miss nothing.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [(wherever you are)] you will know that there is peace in your tent, and you will survey your abode and find nothing lacking [(this being "the seventh" [verse 19])].


###### Job 5:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:25)


וְֽ֭יָדַעְתָּ כִּֽי־רַ֣ב זַרְעֶ֑ךָ וְ֝צֶאֱצָאֶ֗יךָ כְּעֵ֣שֶׂב הָאָֽרֶץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt know also that thy seed shall be great, and thy offspring as the grass of the earth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you will know that your children will be many, and your offspring as the grass of the earth.


###### Job 5:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:26)


תָּב֣וֹא בְכֶ֣לַח אֱלֵי־קָ֑בֶר כַּעֲל֖וֹת גָּדִ֣ישׁ בְּעִתּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt come to thy grave with a rich harvest, like a full sheaf of corn which comes up in its season.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You will come to the grave in a ripe old age, as the harvest is taken up in its time.


###### Job 5:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/5:27)


הִנֵּה־זֹ֖את חֲקַרְנ֥וּהָ כֶּֽן־הִ֑יא שְׁ֝מָעֶ֗נָּה וְאַתָּ֥ה דַֽע־לָֽךְ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Lo this, we have searched it, so it is; hear it, and know it, for thy good.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
We have proved this. It is true. Pay heed to it. And you, know what you have to do.



## Chapter 6

###### Job 6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:1)


וַיַּ֥עַן אִיּ֗וֹב וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Iyyov answered and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Iyyov raised his voice and said [(in response to 5:2)]:


###### Job 6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:2)


ל֗וּ שָׁק֣וֹל יִשָּׁקֵ֣ל כַּעְשִׂ֑י (והיתי) [וְ֝הַוָּתִ֗י] בְּֽמֹאזְנַ֥יִם יִשְׂאוּ־יָֽחַד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Oh that my vexation were thoroughly weighed, and my calamity laid in the balances!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If my anger and my breaking were weighed, if they were placed together in the balance [scale],


###### Job 6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:3)


כִּֽי־עַתָּ֗ה מֵח֣וֹל יַמִּ֣ים יִכְבָּ֑ד עַל־כֵּ֝֗ן דְּבָרַ֥י לָֽעוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For now it would be heavier than the sand of the sea: therefore my words are stammering.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
it [(my breaking)] would outweigh [(my anger)] more than [would the weight of] the sand of the seas; therefore, I rant as I do.


###### Job 6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:4)


כִּ֤י חִצֵּ֪י שַׁדַּ֡י עִמָּדִ֗י אֲשֶׁ֣ר חֲ֭מָתָם שֹׁתָ֣ה רוּחִ֑י בִּעוּתֵ֖י אֱל֣וֹהַּ יַעַרְכֽוּנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the arrows of the Almighty are within me, the poison of which my spirit drinks up: the terrors of God array themselves against me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the arrows of the Almighty are lodged with me, whose venom my spirit drinks. The terrors of G d beleaguer me.


###### Job 6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:5)


הֲיִֽנְהַק־פֶּ֥רֶא עֲלֵי־דֶ֑שֶׁא אִ֥ם יִגְעֶה־שּׁ֝֗וֹר עַל־בְּלִילֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Does the wild ass bray when he has grass? or does the ox low over his fodder?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Does the wild ass bray [(in complaint)] over his grass? Does the ox low over his fodder?


###### Job 6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:6)


הֲיֵאָכֵ֣ל תָּ֭פֵל מִבְּלִי־מֶ֑לַח אִם־יֶשׁ־טַ֝֗עַם בְּרִ֣יר חַלָּמֽוּת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Can that which is unsavoury be eaten without salt? or is there any taste in the white of an egg?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Can one eat bland food [(your reproof)] unsalted? Is there any taste in the spittle [that comes from eating] strong food?


###### Job 6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:7)


מֵאֲנָ֣ה לִנְגּ֣וֹעַ נַפְשִׁ֑י הֵ֝֗מָּה כִּדְוֵ֥י לַחְמִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My soul refuses to touch them; they are to me as loathsome food.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
What my soul recoiled at touching are [now] the spreads of my bread [i.e., my table].


###### Job 6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:8)


מִֽי־יִ֭תֵּן תָּב֣וֹא שֶׁאֱלָתִ֑י וְ֝תִקְוָתִ֗י יִתֵּ֥ן אֱלֽוֹהַּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Oh that I might have my request; and that God would grant me the thing that I long for!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would that my request were granted, and that G d fulfilled my hope —


###### Job 6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:9)


וְיֹאֵ֣ל אֱ֭לוֹהַּ וִידַכְּאֵ֑נִי יַתֵּ֥ר יָ֝ד֗וֹ וִיבַצְּעֵֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Even that it would please God to trample on me; that he would let loose his hand, and cut me off!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
that G d will and stamp me out, that He strengthen His hand [i.e., His blow] and have done with me!


###### Job 6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:10)


וּ֥תְהִי־ע֨וֹד**׀** נֶ֘חָ֤מָתִ֗י וַאֲסַלְּדָ֣ה בְ֭חִילָה לֹ֣א יַחְמ֑וֹל כִּי־לֹ֥א כִ֝חַ֗דְתִּי אִמְרֵ֥י קָדֽוֹשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then should I yet have comfort; yea, I would exalt in pain: let him not spare; for I have not denied the words of the Holy One.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And let my consolation further be — (and I shall shudderingly implore that He not pity [and cut me off])] — that I have not destroyed words of holiness [i.e., that I have fulfilled His mitzvoth and will find peace in the world to come].


###### Job 6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:11)


מַה־כֹּחִ֥י כִֽי־אֲיַחֵ֑ל וּמַה־קִּ֝צִּ֗י כִּֽי־אַאֲרִ֥יךְ נַפְשִֽׁי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
What is my strength, that I should hope? and what is my end, that I should be patient?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
What strength is [left] in me that I should wait [(for healing or for death)] and how [important] is my end that I should stretch out my will [against protestation]?


###### Job 6:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:12)


אִם־כֹּ֣חַ אֲבָנִ֣ים כֹּחִ֑י אִֽם־בְּשָׂרִ֥י נָחֽוּשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Is my strength the strength of stones? or is my flesh of brass?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Is my strength the strength of stones? Is my flesh copper?


###### Job 6:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:13)


הַאִ֬ם אֵ֣ין עֶזְרָתִ֣י בִ֑י וְ֝תֻשִׁיָּ֗ה נִדְּחָ֥ה מִמֶּֽנִּי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Is not my help in me? and is sound policy driven quite from me?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Is even my succor [(the consolation of my friends)] absent from me? Counsel is cast away from me [(for they have risen against me to vex me)].


###### Job 6:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:14)


לַמָּ֣ס מֵרֵעֵ֣הוּ חָ֑סֶד וְיִרְאַ֖ת שַׁדַּ֣י יַעֲזֽוֹב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
To him that is afflicted love is due from his friend; or else he forsakes the fear of the Almighty.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[You have become] as one who withholds lovingkindness from his friend and abandons the fear of the Almighty.


###### Job 6:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:15)


אַ֭חַי בָּגְד֣וּ כְמוֹ־נָ֑חַל כַּאֲפִ֖יק נְחָלִ֣ים יַעֲבֹֽרוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My brethren have dealt deceitfully like a wadi, and like the water courses which disappear;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My friends have betrayed me like a stream; like a water course they have passed on [(from friendship)].


###### Job 6:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:16)


הַקֹּדְרִ֥ים מִנִּי־קָ֑רַח עָ֝לֵ֗ימוֹ יִתְעַלֶּם־שָֽׁלֶג׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
which are black by reason of the ice, and in which the snow dissolves:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(the "betrayals" of a stream:)] [Its waters are] darkened with ice; and snow [(falling on the ice)] is hidden over them [(so that their prospective drinkers are "betrayed")].


###### Job 6:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:17)


בְּעֵ֣ת יְזֹרְב֣וּ נִצְמָ֑תוּ בְּ֝חֻמּ֗וֹ נִדְעֲכ֥וּ מִמְּקוֹמָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
when the time comes they grow warm, they vanish: when it is hot, they are consumed out of their place.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When they are warmed [(by the sun)], they shrivel; in his heat, they leap from their place.


###### Job 6:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:18)


יִ֭לָּ֣פְתוּ אׇרְח֣וֹת דַּרְכָּ֑ם יַעֲל֖וּ בַתֹּ֣הוּ וְיֹאבֵֽדוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The caravans turn this way and that in their course: they go up into the waste, and perish.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They cling to their wonted ways; they vanish into nothingness and are lost.


###### Job 6:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:19)


הִ֭בִּיטוּ אׇרְח֣וֹת תֵּמָ֑א הֲלִיכֹ֥ת שְׁ֝בָ֗א קִוּוּ־לָֽמוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The caravans of Tema looked, the companies of Sheva waited for them –


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The caravans of Tema looked out [for them]; the travelers of Sheva made a line for them.


###### Job 6:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:20)


בֹּ֥שׁוּ כִֽי־בָטָ֑ח בָּ֥אוּ עָ֝דֶ֗יהָ וַיֶּחְפָּֽרוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
they were confounded because they had hoped; they came there, and were abashed.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They who came trusting [to drink] were shamed. They came to it [(the stream)] and were shamed.


###### Job 6:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:21)


כִּֽי־עַ֭תָּה הֱיִ֣יתֶם לֹ֑א תִּֽרְא֥וּ חֲ֝תַ֗ת וַתִּירָֽאוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For now you are like it; you see terror, and are afraid.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Now you [(my "friends")] have been like it. You saw [my] breaking and you were afraid [to speak the truth].


###### Job 6:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:22)


הֲֽכִי־אָ֭מַרְתִּי הָ֣בוּ לִ֑י וּ֝מִכֹּחֲכֶ֗ם שִׁחֲד֥וּ בַעֲדִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Did I say, Bring me something? or, Give a ransom for me out of your substance?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Did I say, "Give me something," and "Pay a bribe for me from your labors" [i.e., your money]?


###### Job 6:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:23)


וּמַלְּט֥וּנִי מִיַּד־צָ֑ר וּמִיַּ֖ד עָרִיצִ֣ים תִּפְדּֽוּנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
or, Deliver me from the enemy’s hand? or, Redeem me from the hand of tyrants?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
and "Save me from the hand of the oppressors," and "Redeem me from the hand of tyrants"?


###### Job 6:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:24)


ה֭וֹרוּנִי וַאֲנִ֣י אַחֲרִ֑ישׁ וּמַה־שָּׁ֝גִ֗יתִי הָבִ֥ינוּ לִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Teach me, and I will hold my peace: and cause me to understand wherein I have erred.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Teach me and I will keep still. Make me understand where I have gone astray.


###### Job 6:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:25)


מַה־נִּמְרְצ֥וּ אִמְרֵי־יֹ֑שֶׁר וּמַה־יּוֹכִ֖יחַ הוֹכֵ֣חַ מִכֶּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
How forcible are right words! but what does your reproof seek to reprove?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
How cogent are just words, but what does a reprover among you prove!


###### Job 6:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:26)


הַלְהוֹכַ֣ח מִלִּ֣ים תַּחְשֹׁ֑בוּ וּ֝לְר֗וּחַ אִמְרֵ֥י נֹאָֽשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do you think your words to be a sound argument, but the sayings of a desperate man to be mere wind?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would you think to elucidate things? [Your] futile words are like the wind!


###### Job 6:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:27)


אַף־עַל־יָת֥וֹם תַּפִּ֑ילוּ וְ֝תִכְר֗וּ עַֽל־רֵיעֲכֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
would you even overthrow an orphan, or seek to undermine your friend?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[By your argumentation] you cast wrath upon an orphan [i.e., an unfortunate such as I], and you dig a pit against your friend.


###### Job 6:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:28)


וְ֭עַתָּה הוֹאִ֣ילוּ פְנוּ־בִ֑י וְעַל־פְּ֝נֵיכֶ֗ם אִם־אֲכַזֵּֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now therefore, be good enough to turn towards me: for surely I will not lie to your face.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, if you will, pay heed to me, and see if I lie to your faces.


###### Job 6:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:29)


שֽׁוּבוּ־נָ֭א אַל־תְּהִ֣י עַוְלָ֑ה (ושבי) [וְשׁ֥וּבוּ] ע֝֗וֹד צִדְקִי־בָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Return, I pray you, let there be no unfairness: and turn back again: my cause is just.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Return now [(to search things out)] that there be no wrong, and return again [to probe the matter], and my righteousness [(will be found)] in it.


###### Job 6:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/6:30)


הֲיֵשׁ־בִּלְשׁוֹנִ֥י עַוְלָ֑ה אִם־חִ֝כִּ֗י לֹא־יָבִ֥ין הַוּֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Is there injustice in my tongue? cannot my taste discern crafty devices?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Is there wrong in my tongue? Does my palate not sense deception?



## Chapter 7

###### Job 7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:1)


הֲלֹֽא־צָבָ֣א לֶֽאֱנ֣וֹשׁ (על) [עֲלֵי־]אָ֑רֶץ וְכִימֵ֖י שָׂכִ֣יר יָמָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Has not a man hard service upon earth? and are not his days like the days of a hireling?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Is there not a term for a mortal upon the earth? Are his days not those of a hired laborer?


###### Job 7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:2)


כְּעֶ֥בֶד יִשְׁאַף־צֵ֑ל וּ֝כְשָׂכִ֗יר יְקַוֶּ֥ה פׇעֳלֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As a servant earnestly desires the shadow, and as a hireling looks for the reward of his work:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As a servant who longs for the shadow [of night], and as a hired laborer, who looks forward to the wages of his labor,


###### Job 7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:3)


כֵּ֤ן הׇנְחַ֣לְתִּי לִ֭י יַרְחֵי־שָׁ֑וְא וְלֵיל֥וֹת עָ֝מָ֗ל מִנּוּ־לִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
so I am allotted months of emptiness, and wearisome nights are appointed to me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
so have I inherited months in vain [(having longed for repose, but not having found it)], and nights of toil have they appointed for me [(in Heaven)].


###### Job 7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:4)


אִם־שָׁכַ֗בְתִּי וְאָמַ֗רְתִּי מָתַ֣י אָ֭קוּם וּמִדַּד־עָ֑רֶב וְשָׂבַ֖עְתִּי נְדֻדִ֣ים עֲדֵי־נָֽשֶׁף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When I lie down, I say, When shall I arise, and the night be gone? and I am full of tossings to and fro until the dawning of the day.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When I lie down, I say, "When will I rise and the night move on?" and I am sated with "movings" until dusk.


###### Job 7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:5)


לָ֘בַ֤שׁ בְּשָׂרִ֣י רִ֭מָּה (וגיש) [וְג֣וּשׁ] עָפָ֑ר עוֹרִ֥י רָ֝גַ֗ע וַיִּמָּאֵֽס׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My flesh is clothed with worms; my skin is a clod of earth: it curdles and decays.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[For] my flesh has clothed itself in worms and encrustation; my skin has split and become putrescent.


###### Job 7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:6)


יָמַ֣י קַ֭לּוּ מִנִּי־אָ֑רֶג וַ֝יִּכְל֗וּ בְּאֶ֣פֶס תִּקְוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My days are swifter than a weaver’s shuttle, and are spent without hope.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My days [of good] are swifter than a weaver's shuttle and will end without hope.


###### Job 7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:7)


זְ֭כֹר כִּי־ר֣וּחַ חַיָּ֑י לֹא־תָשׁ֥וּב עֵ֝ינִ֗י לִרְא֥וֹת טֽוֹב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
O remember that my life is a breath: my eye shall no more see good.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Remember [G d] that my life is like the wind; my eyes will not return to see good [(a denial of the resurrection)].


###### Job 7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:8)


לֹא־תְ֭שׁוּרֵנִי עֵ֣ין רֹ֑אִי עֵינֶ֖יךָ בִּ֣י וְאֵינֶֽנִּי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The eye of him who sees me shall see me no more: while Thy eyes are upon me, I am gone.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The eye that sees me now will not see me again. If You [merely] set Your eyes upon me, I would no longer be here [(Why, then, do You torture me so?)]


###### Job 7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:9)


כָּלָ֣ה עָ֭נָן וַיֵּלַ֑ךְ כֵּ֥ן יוֹרֵ֥ד שְׁ֝א֗וֹל לֹ֣א יַעֲלֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As the cloud is consumed and vanishes away: so he who goes down to the grave shall come up no more.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Just as the cloud dissolves and is gone, so he who descends to the grave will not rise.


###### Job 7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:10)


לֹא־יָשׁ֣וּב ע֣וֹד לְבֵית֑וֹ וְלֹא־יַכִּירֶ֖נּוּ ע֣וֹד מְקֹמֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall return no more to his house, nor shall his place know him any more.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He will return no more to his home. [The people of] his place will recognize him no more.


###### Job 7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:11)


גַּם־אֲנִי֮ לֹ֤א אֶחֱשׇׂ֫ךְ־פִּ֥י אֲֽ֭דַבְּרָה בְּצַ֣ר רוּחִ֑י אָ֝שִׂ֗יחָה בְּמַ֣ר נַפְשִֽׁי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore I will not restrain my mouth; I will speak in the anguish of my spirit; I will complain in the bitterness of my soul.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Therefore, [(because You do not leave off tormenting me)], I, too, will not spare my mouth [(from contending with you)]. I will speak in the affliction of my spirit. I will make my plaint in the bitterness of my soul.


###### Job 7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:12)


הֲֽיָם־אָ֭נִי אִם־תַּנִּ֑ין כִּֽי־תָשִׂ֖ים עָלַ֣י מִשְׁמָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Am I a sea, or a sea monster, that Thou settest a watch over me?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Am I the ocean or a sea-serpent that You have placed a guard [(Satan)] over me [that my soul not leave me? (viz. 2:6)]


###### Job 7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:13)


כִּֽי־אָ֭מַרְתִּי תְּנַחֲמֵ֣נִי עַרְשִׂ֑י יִשָּׂ֥א בְ֝שִׂיחִ֗י מִשְׁכָּבִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When I say, My bed shall comfort me, my couch shall ease my complaint;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If I said, "My bed will console me, my couch will bear my plaint" [i.e., it will relieve my afflictions and lessen my complaint],


###### Job 7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:14)


וְחִתַּתַּ֥נִי בַחֲלֹמ֑וֹת וּֽמֵחֶזְיֹנ֥וֹת תְּבַעֲתַֽנִּי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then Thou dost scare me with dreams, and dost terrify me through visions:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
then You broke me with dreams and terrified me with visions.


###### Job 7:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:15)


וַתִּבְחַ֣ר מַחֲנָ֣ק נַפְשִׁ֑י מָ֝֗וֶת מֵעַצְמוֹתָֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
so that my soul chooses strangling, and death rather than these my bones.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My soul chooses strangulation [above life]; death, above my bones.


###### Job 7:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:16)


מָ֭אַסְתִּי לֹא־לְעֹלָ֣ם אֶחְיֶ֑ה חֲדַ֥ל מִ֝מֶּ֗נִּי כִּי־הֶ֥בֶל יָמָֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I loathe it; I would not live always: let me alone; for my days are emptiness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I have sickened [of my life]! I will not live forever. Quit me [of suffering], for my days are vanity.


###### Job 7:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:17)


מָֽה־אֱ֭נוֹשׁ כִּ֣י תְגַדְּלֶ֑נּוּ וְכִֽי־תָשִׁ֖ית אֵלָ֣יו לִבֶּֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
What is man, that Thou shouldst magnify him? and that Thou shouldst set thy heart upon him?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
What is man that You should aggrandize him by giving heart to him?


###### Job 7:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:18)


וַתִּפְקְדֶ֥נּוּ לִבְקָרִ֑ים לִ֝רְגָעִ֗ים תִּבְחָנֶֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and that Thou shouldst remember him every morning, and try him every moment?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
That You should remember him each morning and examine him each moment?


###### Job 7:19
