[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:19)


כַּ֭מָּה לֹא־תִשְׁעֶ֣ה מִמֶּ֑נִּי לֹֽא־תַ֝רְפֵּ֗נִי עַד־בִּלְעִ֥י רֻקִּֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
How long wilt Thou not depart from me, nor let me alone till I swallow down my spittle?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
How [long it is] that You do not quit me. You do not leave me long enough to swallow my spittle.


###### Job 7:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:20)


חָטָ֡אתִי מָ֤ה אֶפְעַ֨ל**׀** לָךְ֮ נֹצֵ֢ר הָ֫אָדָ֥ם לָ֤מָה שַׂמְתַּ֣נִי לְמִפְגָּ֣ע לָ֑ךְ וָאֶהְיֶ֖ה עָלַ֣י לְמַשָּֽׂא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If I have sinned, what do I do to Thee, O Thou preserver of men? why hast Thou set me as thy target, so that I am a burden to myself?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if I sinned, what can I do to You? Creator of man, why did You make me, to offend You and to be a burden to You?


###### Job 7:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/7:21)


וּמֶ֤ה**׀** לֹא־תִשָּׂ֣א פִשְׁעִי֮ וְתַעֲבִ֢יר אֶת־עֲוֺ֫נִ֥י כִּֽי־עַ֭תָּה לֶעָפָ֣ר אֶשְׁכָּ֑ב וְשִׁ֖חַרְתַּ֣נִי וְאֵינֶֽנִּי׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And why dost Thou not pardon my transgression, and take away my iniquity? for now I shall sleep in the dust; and Thou shalt seek me, but I shall not be.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And why do You not forgive my offense and remove my transgression? For I will soon be in the earth, and You will look for me, and I will not be there.



## Chapter 8

###### Job 8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:1)


וַ֭יַּעַן בִּלְדַּ֥ד הַשּׁוּחִ֗י וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then answered Bildad the Shuĥite, and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Bildad the Shuchite raised his voice and said:


###### Job 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:2)


עַד־אָ֥ן תְּמַלֶּל־אֵ֑לֶּה וְר֥וּחַ כַּ֝בִּ֗יר אִמְרֵי־פִֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
How long wilt thou speak these things? and how long shall the words of thy mouth be like a strong wind?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
How long will you speak thus? A great wind are the words of your mouth.


###### Job 8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:3)


הַ֭אֵל יְעַוֵּ֣ת מִשְׁפָּ֑ט וְאִם־שַׁ֝דַּ֗י יְעַוֵּת־צֶֽדֶק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Does God pervert judgment? or does the Almighty pervert justice?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will G d pervert judgment [(and not punish the wicked)]?


###### Job 8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:4)


אִם־בָּנֶ֥יךָ חָֽטְאוּ־ל֑וֹ וַֽ֝יְשַׁלְּחֵ֗ם בְּיַד־פִּשְׁעָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If thy children have sinned against him, he has cast them out for their transgression.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When your sons sinned against Him, He drove them [(from the world)] through their offense.


###### Job 8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:5)


אִם־אַ֭תָּה תְּשַׁחֵ֣ר אֶל־אֵ֑ל וְאֶל־שַׁ֝דַּ֗י תִּתְחַנָּֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If thou wilt seek to God, and make thy supplication to the Almighty;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If you seek out the L rd and make supplication of the Almighty,


###### Job 8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:6)


אִם־זַ֥ךְ וְיָשָׁ֗ר אָ֥֫תָּה כִּֽי־עַ֭תָּה יָעִ֣יר עָלֶ֑יךָ וְ֝שִׁלַּ֗ם נְוַ֣ת צִדְקֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
if thou be pure and upright; surely now he will rouse himself for thee, and make the habitation of thy righteousness prosperous.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If you are pure and upright, He will now awaken [your merit] for you and make complete the habitation of your righteousness.


###### Job 8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:7)


וְהָיָ֣ה רֵאשִׁיתְךָ֣ מִצְעָ֑ר וְ֝אַחֲרִֽיתְךָ֗ יִשְׂגֶּ֥ה מְאֹֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Though thy beginning was small, yet thy end will be very great.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And your former [good] will be [accounted] small, for your latter [good] will be extremely great.


###### Job 8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:8)


כִּֽי־שְׁאַל־נָ֭א לְדֹ֣ר רִישׁ֑וֹן וְ֝כוֹנֵ֗ן לְחֵ֣קֶר אֲבוֹתָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For inquire, I pray thee, of the former age, and attend assiduously to what their fathers have sought out:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For inquire now about the past generation and ready yourself to [understand] the findings of their ancestors.


###### Job 8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:9)


כִּֽי־תְמ֣וֹל אֲ֭נַחְנוּ וְלֹ֣א נֵדָ֑ע כִּ֤י צֵ֖ל יָמֵ֣ינוּ עֲלֵי־אָֽרֶץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
(for we are but of yesterday, and know nothing, because our days upon earth are a shadow:)


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For it is [as if] we [were born] yesterday and knew nothing; for as a [passing] shadow are our days upon the earth.


###### Job 8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:10)


הֲלֹא־הֵ֣ם י֭וֹרוּךָ יֹ֣אמְרוּ לָ֑ךְ וּ֝מִלִּבָּ֗ם יוֹצִ֥אוּ מִלִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
shall not they teach thee, and tell thee, and utter words out of their heart?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will they not teach you and tell you [what they have acquired] and bring forth [their own] words [of wisdom] from their hearts?


###### Job 8:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:11)


הֲיִֽגְאֶה־גֹּ֭מֶא בְּלֹ֣א בִצָּ֑ה יִשְׂגֶּה־אָ֥חוּ בְלִי־מָֽיִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Can the rush shoot up without mire? can the reed grass grow without water?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(And this is what they will tell you:)] Will the rush grow without mire? Will the reed grass grow without water?


###### Job 8:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:12)


עֹדֶ֣נּוּ בְ֭אִבּוֹ לֹ֣א יִקָּטֵ֑ף וְלִפְנֵ֖י כׇל־חָצִ֣יר יִיבָֽשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Whilst it is yet in its greenness, and not cut down, it withers before any other herb.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As long as it preserves its moistness it will not be cut off; but [without it] it will dry up before any other herb.


###### Job 8:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:13)


כֵּ֗ן אׇ֭רְחוֹת כׇּל־שֹׁ֣כְחֵי אֵ֑ל וְתִקְוַ֖ת חָנֵ֣ף תֹּאבֵֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So are the paths of all who forget God; and the hypocrite’s hope shall perish,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
So is the way of all who forget the L rd [(to prosper so long as their measure is not yet full)], but [ultimately] the hope of the wicked one will go lost.


###### Job 8:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:14)


אֲשֶׁר־יָק֥וֹט כִּסְל֑וֹ וּבֵ֥ית עַ֝כָּבִ֗ישׁ מִבְטַחֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
whose hope shall be cut off, and whose trust shall be a spider’s web.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
His confidence will be cut off; as [fragile] as the house [i.e., the web] of the spider will be his trust.


###### Job 8:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:15)


יִשָּׁעֵ֣ן עַל־בֵּ֭יתוֹ וְלֹ֣א יַעֲמֹ֑ד יַחֲזִ֥יק בּ֝֗וֹ וְלֹ֣א יָקֽוּם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall lean upon his house, but it shall not stand: he shall hold it fast, but it shall not endure.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He will lean on his house, but it will not stand; he will grab on to it, but it will not hold.


###### Job 8:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:16)


רָטֹ֣ב ה֭וּא לִפְנֵי־שָׁ֑מֶשׁ וְעַ֥ל גַּ֝נָּת֗וֹ יֹנַקְתּ֥וֹ תֵצֵֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He is green before the sun, and his branch shoots forth over his garden wall.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He is moist before the [stroke of the] sun and over his garden his shoot emerges.


###### Job 8:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:17)


עַל־גַּ֭ל שׇׁרָשָׁ֣יו יְסֻבָּ֑כוּ בֵּ֖ית אֲבָנִ֣ים יֶחֱזֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
His roots are wrapped about the stone heap: he selects for himself a place of stones.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
On a mound his roots entwine and he sees a house of stone [(a strong site, which he builds up as a stronghold)].


###### Job 8:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:18)


אִם־יְבַלְּעֶ֥נּוּ מִמְּקֹמ֑וֹ וְכִ֥חֶשׁ בּ֝֗וֹ לֹ֣א רְאִיתִֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if one destroy him from his place, then it shall deny him, saying, I have not seen thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But when He swallows him from his place, it denies him, [saying, as it were:] "I never saw you."


###### Job 8:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:19)


הֶן־ה֭וּא מְשׂ֣וֹשׂ דַּרְכּ֑וֹ וּ֝מֵעָפָ֗ר אַחֵ֥ר יִצְמָֽחוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, this is the joy of his way, and out of the earth shall others spring.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
This, then is the "joy" of his [(the wicked one's)] way. And from [men who until now were as lowly as] the earth, another will sprout [(to appropriate all of his greatness)].


###### Job 8:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:20)


הֶן־אֵ֭ל לֹ֣א יִמְאַס־תָּ֑ם וְלֹֽא־יַ֝חֲזִ֗יק בְּיַד־מְרֵעִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, God will not cast away an innocent man, nor will he uphold evildoers.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For G d will not despise the innocent one, and He will not uphold evildoers [(your foes)].


###### Job 8:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:21)


עַד־יְמַלֵּ֣ה שְׂח֣וֹק פִּ֑יךָ וּשְׂפָתֶ֥יךָ תְרוּעָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He will yet fill thy mouth with laughing, and thy lips with rejoicing.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Until [(He will again have mercy upon you)] and He will fill your mouth with laughter and your lips with shouts [of joy].


###### Job 8:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/8:22)


שֹׂנְאֶ֥יךָ יִלְבְּשׁוּ־בֹ֑שֶׁת וְאֹ֖הֶל רְשָׁעִ֣ים אֵינֶֽנּוּ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They who hate thee shall be clothed with shame; and the tent of the wicked shall be no more.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Your foes will be clothed with shame and the tent of the wicked will be no more.



## Chapter 9

###### Job 9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:1)


וַיַּ֥עַן אִיּ֗וֹב וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Iyyov answered and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Iyyov raised his voice and said:


###### Job 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:2)


אׇ֭מְנָם יָדַ֣עְתִּי כִי־כֵ֑ן וּמַה־יִּצְדַּ֖ק אֱנ֣וֹשׁ עִם־אֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Truly I know it is so: but how should a man be just before God?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Indeed, I, too, know that it is so. And how would a man be right with G d?


###### Job 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:3)


אִם־יַ֭חְפֹּץ לָרִ֣יב עִמּ֑וֹ לֹֽא־יַ֝עֲנֶ֗נּוּ אַחַ֥ת מִנִּי־אָֽלֶף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If one will contend with him, he cannot answer him one of a thousand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If He chooses to dispute with him, he could not give Him even one answer to a thousand questions.


###### Job 9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:4)


חֲכַ֣ם לֵ֭בָב וְאַמִּ֣יץ כֹּ֑חַ מִֽי־הִקְשָׁ֥ה אֵ֝לָ֗יו וַיִּשְׁלָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He is wise in heart, and mighty in strength: who has hardened himself against him, and prospered?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Who is] wise of heart [to dispute] and grand in strength [to exact punishment]? Who ever hardened [his heart] against Him and remained whole?


###### Job 9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:5)


הַמַּעְתִּ֣יק הָ֭רִים וְלֹ֣א יָדָ֑עוּ אֲשֶׁ֖ר הֲפָכָ֣ם בְּאַפּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
he who removes mountains, and they know not: when he overturns them in his anger:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He moves mountains, but they [the people] do not know until He overturns them in His wrath.


###### Job 9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:6)


הַמַּרְגִּ֣יז אֶ֭רֶץ מִמְּקוֹמָ֑הּ וְ֝עַמּוּדֶ֗יהָ יִתְפַּלָּצֽוּן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who shakes the earth out of her place, and its pillars tremble:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He shakes the earth from its place [(by looking at it)] and its pillars quake.


###### Job 9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:7)


הָאֹמֵ֣ר לַ֭חֶרֶס וְלֹ֣א יִזְרָ֑ח וּבְעַ֖ד כּוֹכָבִ֣ים יַחְתֹּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who commands the sun, and it rises not; and seals up the stars.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He commands the sun and it does not shine, and He seals up [the light of] the stars.


###### Job 9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:8)


נֹטֶ֣ה שָׁמַ֣יִם לְבַדּ֑וֹ וְ֝דוֹרֵ֗ךְ עַל־בָּ֥מֳתֵי יָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He alone spreads out the heavens, and treads upon the waves of the sea:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He stretches out the heavens alone and treads down the breakers of the sea.


###### Job 9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:9)


עֹֽשֶׂה־עָ֭שׁ כְּסִ֥יל וְכִימָ֗ה וְחַדְרֵ֥י תֵמָֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who makes the Bear, Orion, and Pleiades, and the chambers of the south wind:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He makes the Bear, Orion, and Pleiades, and the chamber of the south wind.


###### Job 9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:10)


עֹשֶׂ֣ה גְ֭דֹלוֹת עַד־אֵ֣ין חֵ֑קֶר וְ֝נִפְלָא֗וֹת עַד־אֵ֥ין מִסְפָּֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who does great things past finding out; yea, and wonders without number.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He does great things, which cannot be fathomed, and wonders without end.


###### Job 9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:11)


הֵ֤ן יַעֲבֹ֣ר עָ֭לַי וְלֹ֣א אֶרְאֶ֑ה וְ֝יַחֲלֹ֗ף וְֽלֹא־אָבִ֥ין לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Lo, he goes by me, and I see him not: he passes on also, but I perceive him not.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Were He to pass before me, I would not see Him. He would pass by and I would not perceive Him.


###### Job 9:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:12)


הֵ֣ן יַ֭חְתֹּף מִ֣י יְשִׁיבֶ֑נּוּ מִי־יֹאמַ֥ר אֵ֝לָ֗יו מַֽה־תַּעֲשֶֽׂה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, he snatches away, who can hinder him? who will say to him, What doest Thou?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He [smites] suddenly. Who can turn Him back? Who can say to Him: "What are You doing?"


###### Job 9:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:13)


אֱ֭לוֹהַּ לֹא־יָשִׁ֣יב אַפּ֑וֹ תַּחְתָּ֥ו שָׁ֝חֲח֗וּ עֹ֣זְרֵי רָֽהַב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
God will not withdraw his anger: the helpers of Rahav stoop under him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
G d does not turn back His wrath [before any man]. Beneath Him are bowed down the [would-be] helpers of the proud [(Egypt)].


###### Job 9:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:14)


אַ֭ף כִּֽי־אָנֹכִ֣י אֶעֱנֶ֑נּוּ אֶבְחֲרָ֖ה דְבָרַ֣י עִמּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
How much less shall I answer him, and choose out my words to reason with him?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
How much less, then, can one such as I remonstrate with Him! [How can] I choose [to place] my words before Him?


###### Job 9:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:15)


אֲשֶׁ֣ר אִם־צָ֭דַקְתִּי לֹ֣א אֶעֱנֶ֑ה לִ֝מְשֹׁפְטִ֗י אֶתְחַנָּֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Whom, though I were righteous, yet would I not answer, but I would make supplication to him who contends with me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Even if I am right, I cannot remonstrate with Him. Can I appeal to Him who has passed judgment upon me?


###### Job 9:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:16)


אִם־קָרָ֥אתִי וַֽיַּעֲנֵ֑נִי לֹֽא־אַ֝אֲמִ֗ין כִּֽי־יַאֲזִ֥ין קוֹלִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If I had called, and he had answered me, yet would I not believe that he had hearkened to my voice.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Even if I call [Him] and He answer me, I cannot believe [(in my great fear of Him)] that He will hearken to my voice.


###### Job 9:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:17)


אֲשֶׁר־בִּשְׂעָרָ֥ה יְשׁוּפֵ֑נִי וְהִרְבָּ֖ה פְצָעַ֣י חִנָּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For he crushes me with a tempest, and multiplies my wounds without cause:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He crushes me in the tempest and multiplies my wounds without cause.


###### Job 9:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:18)


לֹֽא־יִ֭תְּנֵנִי הָשֵׁ֣ב רוּחִ֑י כִּ֥י יַ֝שְׂבִּעַ֗נִי מַמְּרֹרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
he will not let me take my breath, but fills me with bitterness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He does not allow me to restore my spirit, for He sates me with bitterness.


###### Job 9:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:19)


אִם־לְכֹ֣חַ אַמִּ֣יץ הִנֵּ֑ה וְאִם־לְ֝מִשְׁפָּ֗ט מִ֣י יוֹעִידֵֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If I speak of strength, lo, he is strong: and if of judgment, who shall set me a time to plead?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If [He comes against me] with strength, He is [all-] powerful, and if with judgment, who will represent me before Him?


###### Job 9:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:20)


אִם־אֶ֭צְדָּק פִּ֣י יַרְשִׁיעֵ֑נִי תׇּֽם־אָ֝֗נִי וַֽיַּעְקְשֵֽׁנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Though I were just, my own mouth would condemn me: though I were innocent, he would prove me perverse.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Even] if I am right, my mouth will incriminate me; I am innocent and my mouth will make me crooked.


###### Job 9:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:21)


תׇּֽם־אָ֭נִי לֹֽא־אֵדַ֥ע נַפְשִׁ֗י אֶמְאַ֥ס חַיָּֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Though I were innocent, yet would I not know myself: I would despise my life.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I am innocent, but I do not know how to find rest. I despise my life.


###### Job 9:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:22)


אַחַ֗ת הִ֥֫יא עַל־כֵּ֥ן אָמַ֑רְתִּי תָּ֥ם וְ֝רָשָׁ֗ע ה֣וּא מְכַלֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore I said, It is all one: he destroys the innocent and the wicked.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There is [only] one [(thing in the world — the Satan's scourge)]; that is why I have said He destroys the innocent and the wicked alike.


###### Job 9:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:23)


אִם־שׁ֭וֹט יָמִ֣ית פִּתְאֹ֑ם לְמַסַּ֖ת נְקִיִּ֣ם יִלְעָֽג׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If the scourge slay suddenly, he would mock at the trial of the innocent.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If the scourge [(of Satan's tongue)] kills [the righteous one] suddenly, he [Satan] will mock the melting of the clean [(that he has slain)].


###### Job 9:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:24)


אֶ֤רֶץ**׀** נִתְּנָ֬ה בְֽיַד־רָשָׁ֗ע פְּנֵֽי־שֹׁפְטֶ֥יהָ יְכַסֶּ֑ה אִם־לֹ֖א אֵפ֣וֹא מִי־הֽוּא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The earth is given into the hand of the wicked: he covers the faces of its judges; if not he, then who is it?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The earth has been given into the hands of the wicked one [(Satan)]. He covers the face of its judges [(from perceiving the truth)]. And if it is not [as I say], who is it [that destroys the innocent]?


###### Job 9:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:25)


וְיָמַ֣י קַ֭לּוּ מִנִּי־רָ֑ץ בָּ֝רְח֗וּ לֹא־רָא֥וּ טוֹבָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now my days are swifter than a runner: they flee away, they see no good.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And my days [(along with those of the other innocent ones)] have been quicker than the runner. They have fled. They have seen no good.


###### Job 9:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:26)


חָ֭לְפוּ עִם־אֳנִיּ֣וֹת אֵבֶ֑ה כְּ֝נֶ֗שֶׁר יָט֥וּשׂ עֲלֵי־אֹֽכֶל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They pass away like the swift ships: like the vulture that swoops on the prey.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They have sped on with the boats of Eveh, as an eagle that swoops down upon its prey.


###### Job 9:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:27)


אִם־אׇ֭מְרִי אֶשְׁכְּחָ֣ה שִׂיחִ֑י אֶעֶזְבָ֖ה פָנַ֣י וְאַבְלִֽיגָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If I say, I will forget my complaint, I will leave off my sad countenance, and comfort myself:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If I say: I will forget my plaint, I will abandon my anger, I will contain myself,


###### Job 9:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:28)


יָגֹ֥רְתִּי כׇל־עַצְּבֹתָ֑י יָ֝דַ֗עְתִּי כִּי־לֹ֥א תְנַקֵּֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I am afraid of all my pains, I know that Thou wilt not hold me innocent.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I will collect all of my grief [within myself and not cry out] — [even then] I know that You will not acquit me.


###### Job 9:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:29)


אָנֹכִ֥י אֶרְשָׁ֑ע לָמָּה־זֶּ֝֗ה הֶ֣בֶל אִיגָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I shall be condemned; why then do I labour in vain?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I would be incriminated. Why, then, should I toil in vain?


###### Job 9:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:30)


אִם־הִתְרָחַ֥צְתִּי (במו) [בְמֵי־]שָׁ֑לֶג וַ֝הֲזִכּ֗וֹתִי בְּבֹ֣ר כַּפָּֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If I wash myself with snow water, and make my hands never so clean;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Though I washed in waters of snow and cleansed my palms in purity,


###### Job 9:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:31)


אָ֭ז בַּשַּׁ֣חַת תִּטְבְּלֵ֑נִי וְ֝תִעֲב֗וּנִי שַׂלְמוֹתָֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
yet shalt Thou plunge me in the ditch, and my own clothes shall abhor me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You would plunge me into the pit [of filth] (i.e., guilt)], and my [very] clothes would despise me.


###### Job 9:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:32)


כִּֽי־לֹא־אִ֣ישׁ כָּמ֣וֹנִי אֶעֱנֶ֑נּוּ נָב֥וֹא יַ֝חְדָּ֗ו בַּמִּשְׁפָּֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For he is not a man, as I am, that I should answer him, and we should come together in judgment.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For He is not a man as I am that I can answer Him: "Let us come together in disputation."


###### Job 9:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:33)


לֹ֣א יֵשׁ־בֵּינֵ֣ינוּ מוֹכִ֑יחַ יָשֵׁ֖ת יָד֣וֹ עַל־שְׁנֵֽינוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There is no umpire between us, who might lay his hand upon us both.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There is no arbiter between us to lay his hand [(i.e., to impose his decision)] upon us both in disputation.


###### Job 9:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:34)


יָסֵ֣ר מֵעָלַ֣י שִׁבְט֑וֹ וְ֝אֵמָת֗וֹ אַֽל־תְּבַעֲתַֽנִּי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let him take his rod away from me, and let not his fear terrify me:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If He removed His rod from me, and His terror would not confound me,


###### Job 9:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/9:35)


אֲֽ֭דַבְּרָה וְלֹ֣א אִירָאֶ֑נּוּ כִּ֥י לֹא־כֵ֥ן אָ֝נֹכִ֗י עִמָּדִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for then I would speak, and not fear him; for I am not so in myself.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I would speak and not fear Him, for I am not so [(i.e., I am not guilty)] in my eyes.



## Chapter 10

###### Job 10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:1)


נָֽקְטָ֥ה נַפְשִׁ֗י בְּחַ֫יָּ֥י אֶעֶזְבָ֣ה עָלַ֣י שִׂיחִ֑י אֲ֝דַבְּרָ֗ה בְּמַ֣ר נַפְשִֽׁי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My soul is weary of my life; I will give free utterance to my complaint; I will speak in the bitterness of my soul.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My soul wrangles against my life. I shall burden myself with my plaint. I shall speak in the bitterness of my soul.


###### Job 10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:2)


אֹמַ֣ר אֶל־אֱ֭לוֹהַּ אַל־תַּרְשִׁיעֵ֑נִי ה֝וֹדִיעֵ֗נִי עַ֣ל מַה־תְּרִיבֵֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I will say to God, Do not condemn me; inform why Thou dost contend with me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I say to G d: Do not brand me as an evildoer. Tell me what You accuse me of.


###### Job 10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:3)


הֲט֤וֹב לְךָ֨**׀** כִּֽי־תַעֲשֹׁ֗ק כִּֽי־תִ֭מְאַס יְגִ֣יעַ כַּפֶּ֑יךָ וְעַל־עֲצַ֖ת רְשָׁעִ֣ים הוֹפָֽעְתָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Is it good to Thee that Thou shouldst oppress, that Thou shouldst despise the work of Thy hands, and shine upon the counsel of the wicked?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Is it pleasing to You that You rob [(the righteous of their righteousness)], that You despise the labor of Your hands, and that You shine upon the counsel of the wicked?


###### Job 10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:4)


הַעֵינֵ֣י בָשָׂ֣ר לָ֑ךְ אִם־כִּרְא֖וֹת אֱנ֣וֹשׁ תִּרְאֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hast Thou eyes of flesh? or seest Thou as man sees?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do You have eyes of flesh [(that You do not distinguish between the righteous and the wicked)] Do You see [only] as mortals do?


###### Job 10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:5)


הֲכִימֵ֣י אֱנ֣וֹשׁ יָמֶ֑יךָ אִם־שְׁ֝נוֹתֶ֗יךָ כִּ֣ימֵי גָֽבֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Are Thy days as the days of man? are Thy years as man’s days,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Are Your days the days of a mortal? Are Your years as the years of a man


###### Job 10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:6)


כִּֽי־תְבַקֵּ֥שׁ לַעֲוֺנִ֑י וּֽלְחַטָּאתִ֥י תִדְרֽוֹשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that Thou inquirest after my iniquity, and searchest after my sin;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
that You seek out my transgression and search out my sin?


###### Job 10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:7)


עַֽל־דַּ֭עְתְּךָ כִּֽי־לֹ֣א אֶרְשָׁ֑ע וְאֵ֖ין מִיָּדְךָ֣ מַצִּֽיל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
although Thou knowest that I am not wicked; and there is none that can deliver out of Thy hand?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You know that I will not be incriminated — but there is no escape from Your hand.


###### Job 10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:8)


יָדֶ֣יךָ עִ֭צְּבוּנִי וַֽיַּעֲשׂ֑וּנִי יַ֥חַד סָ֝בִ֗יב וַֽתְּבַלְּעֵֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thy hands have made me and fashioned me together round about; yet Thou dost destroy me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Your hands moulded me and made me, and now [Your hosts (the worms)] come round [me] together and You would swallow me up.


###### Job 10:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:9)


זְכׇר־נָ֭א כִּי־כַחֹ֣מֶר עֲשִׂיתָ֑נִי וְֽאֶל־עָפָ֥ר תְּשִׁיבֵֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Remember, I beseech Thee, that Thou hast made me like clay; and wilt Thou bring me back to dust?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Remember, now, that You fashioned me as clay, and would You [now] return me to the earth?


###### Job 10:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:10)


הֲלֹ֣א כֶ֭חָלָב תַּתִּיכֵ֑נִי וְ֝כַגְּבִנָּ֗ה תַּקְפִּיאֵֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hast Thou not poured me out like milk, and curdled me like cheese?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Did You not rarefy me as milk [(in the sperm)] and solidify me as cheese?


###### Job 10:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:11)


ע֣וֹר וּ֭בָשָׂר תַּלְבִּישֵׁ֑נִי וּֽבַעֲצָמ֥וֹת וְ֝גִידִ֗ים תְּשֹׂכְכֵֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou hast clothed me with skin and flesh, and hast knit me together with bones and sinews.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You clothed me in skin and flesh, and You covered me with bones and sinews.


###### Job 10:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:12)


חַיִּ֣ים וָ֭חֶסֶד עָשִׂ֣יתָ עִמָּדִ֑י וּ֝פְקֻדָּתְךָ֗ שָֽׁמְרָ֥ה רוּחִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou hast granted me life and favour, and Thy providence has preserved my spirit.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Life, through lovingkindness, You bequeathed to me, and Your charge guarded my spirit.


###### Job 10:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:13)


וְ֭אֵלֶּה צָפַ֣נְתָּ בִלְבָבֶ֑ךָ יָ֝דַ֗עְתִּי כִּי־זֹ֥את עִמָּֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yet these things hast Thou hidden in thy heart: I know that this is with Thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And have You hidden [these things] in Your heart [(in order to destroy me)]? I know that this [(all that I have mentioned)] is with You [(and that You have not forgotten)].


###### Job 10:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:14)


אִם־חָטָ֥אתִי וּשְׁמַרְתָּ֑נִי וּ֝מֵעֲוֺנִ֗י לֹ֣א תְנַקֵּֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If I sin, then Thou markest me, and Thou wilt not acquit me from my iniquity.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If I have sinned, You have guarded me [and overlooked nothing], and You would not cleanse me of my transgression.


###### Job 10:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:15)


אִם־רָשַׁ֡עְתִּי אַלְלַ֬י לִ֗י וְ֭צָדַקְתִּי לֹא־אֶשָּׂ֣א רֹאשִׁ֑י שְׂבַ֥ע קָ֝ל֗וֹן וּרְאֵ֥ה עׇנְיִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If I be wicked, woe is me; and if I be righteous, yet will I not lift up my head; for I am filled with disgrace, and I see my affliction.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If I am wicked, woe unto me! And if I am righteous [(what good is it?)] I cannot hold up my head, sated with shame [as I am] and beholding my affliction.


###### Job 10:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:16)


וְ֭יִגְאֶה כַּשַּׁ֣חַל תְּצוּדֵ֑נִי וְ֝תָשֹׁ֗ב תִּתְפַּלָּא־בִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If my head is lifted up proudly, Thou huntest me like a lion: and again Thou dost work wonders against me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[My offense] has grown so great [in Your eyes] that You hunt me as a lion. And You return [day after day], [ever more] exacting [with me].


###### Job 10:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:17)


תְּחַדֵּ֬שׁ עֵדֶ֨יךָ**׀** נֶגְדִּ֗י וְתֶ֣רֶב כַּ֭עַשְׂךָ עִמָּדִ֑י חֲלִיפ֖וֹת וְצָבָ֣א עִמִּֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou renewest Thy witnesses against me, and increasest Thy indignation upon me; Thou dost bring fresh armies against me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You renew Your witnesses against me [(to bring fresh woes upon me)] and You increase Your anger against me. Changes [(of afflictions)] and set times [for each] are with me.


###### Job 10:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:18)


וְלָ֣מָּה מֵ֭רֶחֶם הֹצֵאתָ֑נִי אֶ֝גְוַ֗ע וְעַ֣יִן לֹֽא־תִרְאֵֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Why then hast Thou brought me forth out of the womb? Oh, that I had perished, and no eye had seen me!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Why did You take me from the womb? Would that I had died and no eye had seen me!


###### Job 10:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:19)


כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹא־הָיִ֣יתִי אֶהְיֶ֑ה מִ֝בֶּ֗טֶן לַקֶּ֥בֶר אוּבָֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I should have been as though I had not been; I should have been carried from the womb to the grave.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then it would have been as if I had not been. Would that I had been brought from the stomach to the grave!


###### Job 10:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:20)


הֲלֹֽא־מְעַ֣ט יָמַ֣י (יחדל) [וַחֲדָ֑ל] (ישית) [וְשִׁ֥ית] מִ֝מֶּ֗נִּי וְאַבְלִ֥יגָה מְּעָֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Are not my days few? cease then, and let me alone, that I may take comfort a little,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Are my days not few? Desist! Betake Yourself and let me come to myself a little


###### Job 10:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:21)


בְּטֶ֣רֶם אֵ֭לֵךְ וְלֹ֣א אָשׁ֑וּב אֶל־אֶ֖רֶץ חֹ֣שֶׁךְ וְצַלְמָֽוֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Before I go whence I shall not return, to the land of darkness and the shadow of death,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
before I go — not to return — to the land of darkness and the shadow of death.


###### Job 10:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/10:22)


אֶ֤רֶץ עֵפָ֨תָה**׀** כְּמ֥וֹ אֹ֗פֶל צַ֭לְמָוֶת וְלֹ֥א סְדָרִ֗ים וַתֹּ֥פַע כְּמוֹ־אֹֽפֶל׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A land of gloom, as darkness itself; and of the shadow of death, without any order, and where the light is as darkness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A land of darkness — like pitch darkness. It is the shadow of death and there are no [diurnal] orders there; and even the light there is like pitch darkness.



## Chapter 11

###### Job 11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:1)


וַ֭יַּעַן צֹפַ֥ר הַֽנַּעֲמָתִ֗י וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then answered Żofar the Na῾amatite, and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Tzofar the Na'amathite raised his voice and said:


###### Job 11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:2)


הֲרֹ֣ב דְּ֭בָרִים לֹ֣א יֵעָנֶ֑ה וְאִם־אִ֖ישׁ שְׂפָתַ֣יִם יִצְדָּֽק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Should not the multitude of words be answered? and should a man full of talk be accounted in the right?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will the multiplier of words not be answered? Will the man of glib speech [(on that account)] be justified?


###### Job 11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:3)


בַּ֭דֶּיךָ מְתִ֣ים יַחֲרִ֑ישׁוּ וַ֝תִּלְעַ֗ג וְאֵ֣ין מַכְלִֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Should thy babble make men hold their peace? and when thou mockest, shall no man make thee ashamed?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will your fabrications silence men, and will you mock [the L rd] because no one shames [you]?


###### Job 11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:4)


וַ֭תֹּאמֶר זַ֣ךְ לִקְחִ֑י וּ֝בַ֗ר הָיִ֥יתִי בְעֵינֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For thou hast said, My doctrine is pure, and I am clean in Thy eyes.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You said: "My ways are clean, and I was pure in Your eyes."


###### Job 11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:5)


וְֽאוּלָ֗ם מִֽי־יִתֵּ֣ן אֱל֣וֹהַּ דַּבֵּ֑ר וְיִפְתַּ֖ח שְׂפָתָ֣יו עִמָּֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But oh that God would speak, and open his lips against thee:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But would that G d spoke and opened His lips [to contend] with you.


###### Job 11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:6)


וְיַגֶּד־לְךָ֨**׀** תַּ֥עֲלֻמ֣וֹת חׇכְמָה֮ כִּֽי־כִפְלַ֢יִם לְֽת֫וּשִׁיָּ֥ה וְדַ֡ע כִּֽי־יַשֶּׁ֥ה לְךָ֥ אֱ֝ל֗וֹהַּ מֵעֲוֺנֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and that he would tell thee the secrets of wisdom, for wisdom is manifold! Know therefore that God exacts of thee less than thy iniquity deserves.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He would tell you the secrets of wisdom — that there is double to the Wisdom [(the Torah, which you have not fulfilled)]. And know that G d has lent you [but a part of the punishment due you] for your sins.


###### Job 11:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:7)


הַחֵ֣קֶר אֱל֣וֹהַּ תִּמְצָ֑א אִ֤ם עַד־תַּכְלִ֖ית שַׁדַּ֣י תִּמְצָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Canst thou find out the deep things of God? canst thou find out the purpose of the Almighty?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would you find out the probings of G d [(that you feel you have fulfilled everything)]? Would you find out the far reaches of the Almighty?


###### Job 11:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:8)


גׇּבְהֵ֣י שָׁ֭מַיִם מַה־תִּפְעָ֑ל עֲמֻקָּ֥ה מִ֝שְּׁא֗וֹל מַה־תֵּדָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It is as high as heaven; what canst thou do? deeper than She᾽ol; what canst thou know?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Something as high as] the heights of heaven — what would you do [with it]? [Something] deeper than the depths — what would you know of it?


###### Job 11:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:9)


אֲרֻכָּ֣ה מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִדָּ֑הּ וּ֝רְחָבָ֗ה מִנִּי־יָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Its measure is longer than the earth, and broader than the sea.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Longer than the earth is its measure and broader than the sea.


###### Job 11:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:10)


אִם־יַחֲלֹ֥ף וְיַסְגִּ֑יר וְ֝יַקְהִ֗יל וּמִ֣י יְשִׁיבֶֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If he pass by, or shut up, or call to judgment, then who can hinder him?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If He pass by and close [one up in afflictions], and he gather his entire retinue [to protest it], who can controvert Him?


###### Job 11:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:11)


כִּי־ה֭וּא יָדַ֣ע מְתֵי־שָׁ֑וְא וַיַּרְא־אָ֝֗וֶן וְלֹ֣א יִתְבּוֹנָֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For he knows vain men; and when he sees iniquity, shall he then take no notice of it?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For He knows men of falsehood, and He sees [their] iniquity [(though in His long-suffering, it may seem to them)] that He does not perceive [it].


###### Job 11:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:12)


וְאִ֣ישׁ נָ֭בוּב יִלָּבֵ֑ב וְעַ֥יִר פֶּ֝֗רֶא אָדָ֥ם יִוָּלֵֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the stupid man shall become wise, when a wild ass’s colt, shall be turned into man!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [even] the hollow [(wayward)] man can gain a heart [(to repent)], and [one who was ignorant like] a wild ass can [(teach himself to)] be born a man.


###### Job 11:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:13)


אִם־אַ֭תָּה הֲכִינ֣וֹתָ לִבֶּ֑ךָ וּפָרַשְׂתָּ֖ אֵלָ֣יו כַּפֶּֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If thou prepare thy heart, and stretch out thy hands towards him;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If you would prepare your heart [(after the afflictions)] and spread out your palms to Him —


###### Job 11:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:14)


אִם־אָ֣וֶן בְּ֭יָדְךָ הַרְחִיקֵ֑הוּ וְאַל־תַּשְׁכֵּ֖ן בְּאֹהָלֶ֣יךָ עַוְלָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
if iniquity be in thy hand, put it far away, and let not wickedness dwell in thy tents.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If there is iniquity in your hand remove it far from you and let no wrong dwell in your tent —


###### Job 11:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:15)


כִּי־אָ֤ז**׀** תִּשָּׂ֣א פָנֶ֣יךָ מִמּ֑וּם וְהָיִ֥יתָ מֻ֝צָ֗ק וְלֹ֣א תִירָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Surely then thou shalt lift up thy face without blemish; and thou shalt be steadfast, and shalt not fear:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then you would lift your face without blemish [(for you would have removed all your blemishes)] and you would be steadfast and not fear.


###### Job 11:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:16)


כִּֽי־אַ֭תָּה עָמָ֣ל תִּשְׁכָּ֑ח כְּמַ֖יִם עָבְר֣וּ תִזְכֹּֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
because thou shalt forget thy misery, and remember it as waters that pass away:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For you will forget [your] trials and [remember of them only] as [much as one remembers of] passing waters.


###### Job 11:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:17)


וּֽ֭מִצׇּהֳרַיִם יָק֣וּם חָ֑לֶד תָּ֝עֻ֗פָה כַּבֹּ֥קֶר תִּהְיֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and thy eye shall be clearer than the noonday; its darkness shall be like the morning.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a time [of light] brighter than the afternoon will arise for you. [Your] darkness will turn into morning.


###### Job 11:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:18)


וּֽ֭בָטַחְתָּ כִּֽי־יֵ֣שׁ תִּקְוָ֑ה וְ֝חָפַרְתָּ֗ לָבֶ֥טַח תִּשְׁכָּֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt be secure, because there is hope; yea, thou shalt look about thee, and thou shalt take thy rest in safety.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And rest secure, for there is hope; [as if] you had dug [a protective trench] rest secure.


###### Job 11:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:19)


וְֽ֭רָבַצְתָּ וְאֵ֣ין מַחֲרִ֑יד וְחִלּ֖וּ פָנֶ֣יךָ רַבִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Also thou shalt lie down, and none shall make thee afraid; for many shall entreat thy favour.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [when] you lie down, no one will affright [you]. And great ones will solicit your favor.


###### Job 11:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/11:20)


וְעֵינֵ֥י רְשָׁעִ֗ים תִּ֫כְלֶ֥ינָה וּ֭מָנוֹס אָבַ֣ד מִנְהֶ֑ם וְ֝תִקְוָתָ֗ם מַֽפַּֽח־נָֽפֶשׁ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the eyes of the wicked shall fail, and they shall not escape, and their hope shall turn to despair.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But the hope of the wicked [(your foes)] will be defeated. Refuge will be lost to them. And their hope [for you will be] dejection of spirit [for them].



## Chapter 12

###### Job 12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:1)


וַיַּ֥עַן אִיּ֗וֹב וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Iyyov answered and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Iyyov raised his voice and said:


###### Job 12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:2)


אׇ֭מְנָם כִּ֣י אַתֶּם־עָ֑ם וְ֝עִמָּכֶ֗ם תָּמ֥וּת חׇכְמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
No doubt but you are the people, and wisdom shall die with you.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
True, you are the people [and its princes] and wisdom will perish with you.


###### Job 12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:3)


גַּם־לִ֤י לֵבָ֨ב**׀** כְּֽמוֹכֶ֗ם לֹא־נֹפֵ֣ל אָנֹכִ֣י מִכֶּ֑ם וְאֶת־מִי־אֵ֥ין כְּמוֹ־אֵֽלֶּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But I have understanding as well as you; I am not inferior to you; yea, who knows not such things as these?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[But] I, too, have a heart as you do. I am not lesser than you; and who does not [know these things just] as these [i.e., just as you do]?


###### Job 12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:4)


שְׂחֹ֤ק לְרֵעֵ֨הוּ**׀** אֶהְיֶ֗ה קֹרֵ֣א לֶ֭אֱלוֹהַּ וַֽיַּעֲנֵ֑הוּ שְׂ֝ח֗וֹק צַדִּ֥יק תָּמִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I am as one who is become a laughing stock to his friend; that called upon God and he answered him; the just, the innocent man is a laughing stock.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I am [one] who has become a laughingstock to his friend, and calls out to G d, who answers him [(that in return for this He will give him)] the true laughter of the righteous one.


###### Job 12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:5)


לַפִּ֣יד בּ֭וּז לְעַשְׁתּ֣וּת שַׁאֲנָ֑ן נָ֝כ֗וֹן לְמ֣וֹעֲדֵי רָֽגֶל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Contempt is ready in the thought of him that is at ease, for those that are ruined, that slip with their feet.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(And I also know, just as you, that)] the torch of [the] shame [of Gehinnom awaits] him who is at ease in [his] thoughts, [saying: "I shall be at peace," and that] he is prepared [to join the ranks of] "those whose foot slips."


###### Job 12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:6)


יִשְׁלָ֤יוּ אֹהָלִ֨ים**׀** לְשֹׁ֥דְדִ֗ים וּֽ֭בַטֻּחוֹת לְמַרְגִּ֣יזֵי אֵ֑ל לַאֲשֶׁ֤ר הֵבִ֖יא אֱל֣וֹהַּ בְּיָדֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The tents of robbers prosper, and they who provoke God are secure; they who bring their God in their hand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(And I also know, just as you, that for many days [until their ultimate destruction])] the tents of robbers are at peace, and those who anger G d dwell secure, [and that with all] that G d has brought [to the evildoer, the "hour" plays into] his hand.


###### Job 12:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:7)


וְֽאוּלָ֗ם שְׁאַל־נָ֣א בְהֵמ֣וֹת וְתֹרֶ֑ךָּ וְע֥וֹף הַ֝שָּׁמַ֗יִם וְיַגֶּד־לָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But ask now the beasts, and they shall teach thee; and the birds of the sky, and they shall tell thee:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Ask [even] the beasts and they will teach [these things to] you, and the birds of heaven will tell you.


###### Job 12:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:8)


א֤וֹ שִׂ֣יחַ לָאָ֣רֶץ וְתֹרֶ֑ךָּ וִיסַפְּר֥וּ לְ֝ךָ֗ דְּגֵ֣י הַיָּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
or speak to the earth, and it shall teach thee: and the fishes of the sea shall declare to thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Or speak to the earth, and it will teach you; and the fish of the sea will tell you.


###### Job 12:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:9)


מִ֭י לֹא־יָדַ֣ע בְּכׇל־אֵ֑לֶּה כִּ֥י יַד־יְ֝הֹוָ֗ה עָ֣שְׂתָה זֹּֽאת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Who knows not among all these that the hand of the Lord has done this;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who would not know by all these that the hand of the L rd has wrought this?


###### Job 12:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:10)


אֲשֶׁ֣ר בְּ֭יָדוֹ נֶ֣פֶשׁ כׇּל־חָ֑י וְ֝ר֗וּחַ כׇּל־בְּשַׂר־אִֽישׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
in whose hand is the soul of every living thing, and the breath of all mankind?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
That in His hand is the spirit of all living things and the spirit of all mortal flesh?


###### Job 12:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:11)


הֲלֹא־אֹ֭זֶן מִלִּ֣ין תִּבְחָ֑ן וְ֝חֵ֗ךְ אֹ֣כֶל יִטְעַם־לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Does not the ear try words as the palate tastes food?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will not the ear [which hears what He has wrought] not discern [the implications of these] words just as the palate tastes food?


###### Job 12:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:12)


בִּישִׁישִׁ֥ים חׇכְמָ֑ה וְאֹ֖רֶךְ יָמִ֣ים תְּבוּנָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
With aged men is wisdom; and length of days brings understanding.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There is wisdom in the old and understanding in the long-lived


###### Job 12:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:13)


עִ֭מּוֹ חׇכְמָ֣ה וּגְבוּרָ֑ה ל֝֗וֹ עֵצָ֥ה וּתְבוּנָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
With him is wisdom and might, he has counsel and understanding.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[to know that] with Him is wisdom and strength; with Him is counsel and understanding.


###### Job 12:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:14)


הֵ֣ן יַ֭הֲרוֹס וְלֹ֣א יִבָּנֶ֑ה יִסְגֹּ֥ר עַל־אִ֝֗ישׁ וְלֹ֣א יִפָּתֵֽחַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, he breaks down, and it cannot be built again: he shuts up a man, and there can be no opening.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When He destroys, none can rebuild. When He imprisons a man, none can free him.


###### Job 12:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:15)


הֵ֤ן יַעְצֹ֣ר בַּמַּ֣יִם וְיִבָ֑שׁוּ וִ֝ישַׁלְּחֵ֗ם וְיַ֖הַפְכוּ־אָֽרֶץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, he withholds the waters, and they dry up: then he sends them out, and they overwhelm the earth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When He holds back the waters, the land is dry; when He sends them forth [(as He did in the generation of Enosh, when He flooded a third of the world)], they overturn the earth.


###### Job 12:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:16)


עִ֭מּוֹ עֹ֣ז וְתוּשִׁיָּ֑ה ל֝֗וֹ שֹׁגֵ֥ג וּמַשְׁגֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
With him is strength and sound wisdom: the deceived and the deceiver are his.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He is the source of strength and weakness. His is the strayer and the one who causes [men] to stray [i.e., the Satan].


###### Job 12:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:17)


מוֹלִ֣יךְ יוֹעֲצִ֣ים שׁוֹלָ֑ל וְֽשֹׁפְטִ֥ים יְהוֹלֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He leads counsellors away bereft of counsel, and makes judges fools.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He leads counselors into folly and makes judges foolish.


###### Job 12:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:18)


מוּסַ֣ר מְלָכִ֣ים פִּתֵּ֑חַ וַיֶּאְסֹ֥ר אֵ֝ז֗וֹר בְּמׇתְנֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He looses the bond of kings, and binds their loins with a girdle.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He unties the bonds of kings [e.g., Nevuchadnezzar] just as He buckles the belt on their thighs [(to "gird" them into kingship)].


###### Job 12:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:19)


מוֹלִ֣יךְ כֹּהֲנִ֣ים שׁוֹלָ֑ל וְאֵ֖תָנִ֣ים יְסַלֵּֽף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He leads priests away bereft of dignity, and overthrows the mighty.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He leads princes away with folly and twists the mighty.


###### Job 12:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:20)


מֵסִ֣יר שָׂ֭פָה לְנֶאֱמָנִ֑ים וְטַ֖עַם זְקֵנִ֣ים יִקָּֽח׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He removes the speech of the trusty, and takes away the understanding of the aged.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He [sometimes] removes speech [even] from the faithful [(viz., e.g., Genesis 15:6, followed by 8] and takes the acumen of the elders.


###### Job 12:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:21)


שׁוֹפֵ֣ךְ בּ֭וּז עַל־נְדִיבִ֑ים וּמְזִ֖יחַ אֲפִיקִ֣ים רִפָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He pours contempt upon princes, and weakens the belt of the mighty.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He spills contempt upon the lordly and loosens the buckler of the mighty.


###### Job 12:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:22)


מְגַלֶּ֣ה עֲ֭מֻקוֹת מִנִּי־חֹ֑שֶׁךְ וַיֹּצֵ֖א לָא֣וֹר צַלְמָֽוֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He uncovers deep things out of darkness, and brings out to light the shadow of death.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He reveals [to man] what is profounder than darkness and brings shadows to light.


###### Job 12:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:23)


מַשְׂגִּ֣יא לַ֭גּוֹיִם וַֽיְאַבְּדֵ֑ם שֹׁטֵ֥חַ לַ֝גּוֹיִ֗ם וַיַּנְחֵֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He makes nations great and destroys them: he disperses nations, and guides them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He raises nations and destroys them [(through their pride)], spreads nations out [(over the earth)] and leads them [(into exile)].


###### Job 12:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:24)


מֵסִ֗יר לֵ֭ב רָאשֵׁ֣י עַם־הָאָ֑רֶץ וַ֝יַּתְעֵ֗ם בְּתֹ֣הוּ לֹא־דָֽרֶךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He takes away the heart of the chiefs of the people of the land, and causes them to wander in a wilderness where there is no way.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He removes the wisdom of the leaders of the people, and they lead them into a void that is not a path.


###### Job 12:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/12:25)


יְמַֽשְׁשׁוּ־חֹ֥שֶׁךְ וְלֹא־א֑וֹר וַ֝יַּתְעֵ֗ם כַּשִּׁכּֽוֹר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They grope in the dark without light, and he makes them stagger like a drunken man.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They feel in the darkness and not in light and He leads them astray as drunkards.



## Chapter 13

###### Job 13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:1)


הֶן־כֹּ֭ל רָאֲתָ֣ה עֵינִ֑י שָֽׁמְעָ֥ה אׇ֝זְנִ֗י וַתָּ֥בֶן לָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Lo, my eye has seen all this, my ear has heard and understood it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All of these things my eyes have seen and my ears have heard, and they have understood.


###### Job 13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:2)


כְּֽ֭דַעְתְּכֶם יָדַ֣עְתִּי גַם־אָ֑נִי לֹֽא־נֹפֵ֖ל אָנֹכִ֣י מִכֶּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
What you know, I know also: I am not inferior to you.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As well as you know, I know. I, too, am no less than you.


###### Job 13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:3)


אוּלָ֗ם אֲ֭נִי אֶל־שַׁדַּ֣י אֲדַבֵּ֑ר וְהוֹכֵ֖חַ אֶל־אֵ֣ל אֶחְפָּֽץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yet, I would speak to the Almighty, and I desire to reason with God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But I would speak with the Almighty and my desire is to dispute with the L rd.


###### Job 13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:4)


וְֽאוּלָ֗ם אַתֶּ֥ם טֹפְלֵי־שָׁ֑קֶר רֹפְאֵ֖י אֱלִ֣ל כֻּלְּכֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But you are forgers of lies, you are all physicians of no value.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But you are compounders of falsehood; you are all healers of vanity.


###### Job 13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:5)


מִי־יִ֭תֵּן הַחֲרֵ֣שׁ תַּחֲרִישׁ֑וּן וּתְהִ֖י לָכֶ֣ם לְחׇכְמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
O that you would altogether keep silent! and it should be your wisdom.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would that you kept still so that you would be accounted wise!


###### Job 13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:6)


שִׁמְעוּ־נָ֥א תוֹכַחְתִּ֑י וְרִב֖וֹת שְׂפָתַ֣י הַקְשִֽׁיבוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hear now my reasoning, and hearken to the pleadings of my lips.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Hear, now, my dispute, and give ear to the arguments of my lips.


###### Job 13:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:7)


הַ֭לְאֵל תְּדַבְּר֣וּ עַוְלָ֑ה וְ֝ל֗וֹ תְּֽדַבְּר֥וּ רְמִיָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Will you speak wickedly for God? and talk deceitfully for him?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will you speak wrong on behalf of G d? Will you speak deceit on His behalf?


###### Job 13:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:8)


הֲפָנָ֥יו תִּשָּׂא֑וּן אִם־לָאֵ֥ל תְּרִיבֽוּן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Will you show him partiality? will you contend on behalf of God?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will you be partial for Him? Will you argue [deceitfully] for G d?


###### Job 13:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:9)


הֲ֭טוֹב כִּֽי־יַחְקֹ֣ר אֶתְכֶ֑ם אִם־כְּהָתֵ֥ל בֶּ֝אֱנ֗וֹשׁ תְּהָתֵ֥לּוּ בֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Will it be well for you if he should search you out? or as one man deceives another, will you deceive him?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will it be good when He probes you [and find you liars]? Will you mock Him as one mocks a man [(saying: "For Your honor we have made ourselves liars"?)]


###### Job 13:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:10)


הוֹכֵ֣חַ יוֹכִ֣יחַ אֶתְכֶ֑ם אִם־בַּ֝סֵּ֗תֶר פָּנִ֥ים תִּשָּׂאֽוּן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He will surely reprove you, if you secretly show partiality.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He will reprove you [(for your partiality)]. Will you be partial to Him in secret [i.e., when you stand before Him in judgment for your partiality]?


###### Job 13:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:11)


הֲלֹ֣א שְׂ֭אֵתוֹ תְּבַעֵ֣ת אֶתְכֶ֑ם וּ֝פַחְדּ֗וֹ יִפֹּ֥ל עֲלֵיכֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Shall not his majesty make you afraid? and his dread fall upon you?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will His majesty not confound you and His fright not fall upon you?


###### Job 13:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:12)


זִֽ֭כְרֹנֵיכֶם מִשְׁלֵי־אֵ֑פֶר לְגַבֵּי־חֹ֝֗מֶר גַּבֵּיכֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Your remembrances are like ashes; your bodies are like bodies of clay.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Your memories are like [the memory of] dust; your heights are like dust to Him.


###### Job 13:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:13)


הַחֲרִ֣ישׁוּ מִ֭מֶּנִּי וַאֲדַבְּרָה־אָ֑נִי וְיַעֲבֹ֖ר עָלַ֣י מָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hold your peace, let me alone, that I may speak, and let come on me what will.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Leave off speaking against me, and let me speak, and let there come upon me what may.


###### Job 13:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:14)


עַל־מָ֤ה**׀** אֶשָּׂ֣א בְשָׂרִ֣י בְשִׁנָּ֑י וְ֝נַפְשִׁ֗י אָשִׂ֥ים בְּכַפִּֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For what? I will take my flesh in my teeth, and take my life in my hand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Why should I hold my flesh in my teeth [(to force myself to silence)] and place [i.e., offer up] my life in my palm?


###### Job 13:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:15)


הֵ֣ן יִ֭קְטְלֵנִי (לא) [ל֣וֹ] אֲיַחֵ֑ל אַךְ־דְּ֝רָכַ֗י אֶל־פָּנָ֥יו אוֹכִֽיחַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Though he slay me, yet will I trust in him: but I will maintain my own ways before him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Though He slay me, I will trust in Him, but I would argue my ways to His face [(to know whereof He accuses me)].


###### Job 13:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:16)


גַּם־הוּא־לִ֥י לִישׁוּעָ֑ה כִּי־לֹ֥א לְ֝פָנָ֗יו חָנֵ֥ף יָבֽוֹא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
This also shall be my salvation: for a hypocrite shall not come before him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(And as I am wholehearted with Him)], He, likewise, will be my salvation; for a flatterer [(such as you)] will not come before Him.


###### Job 13:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:17)


שִׁמְע֣וּ שָׁ֭מוֹעַ מִלָּתִ֑י וְ֝אַחֲוָתִ֗י בְּאׇזְנֵיכֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hear diligently my speech, and let my declaration be in your ears.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Hear, then, my words, and let my speech enter your ears.


###### Job 13:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:18)


הִנֵּה־נָ֭א עָרַ֣כְתִּי מִשְׁפָּ֑ט יָ֝דַ֗עְתִּי כִּי־אֲנִ֥י אֶצְדָּֽק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold now, I have ordered my cause; I know that I shall be vindicated.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I have prepared my defense; I know that I will be vindicated.


###### Job 13:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:19)


מִי־ה֭וּא יָרִ֣יב עִמָּדִ֑י כִּֽי־עַתָּ֖ה אַחֲרִ֣ישׁ וְאֶגְוָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Who is he that will contend with me? for then I would hold my tongue and die.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who would dispute with me? If I kept quiet now, I would expire.


###### Job 13:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:20)


אַךְ־שְׁ֭תַּיִם אַל־תַּ֣עַשׂ עִמָּדִ֑י אָ֥ז מִ֝פָּנֶ֗יךָ לֹ֣א אֶסָּתֵֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Only do not two things to me: then will I not hide myself from Thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Just two things do not do to me, and then I will not hide from Your face:


###### Job 13:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:21)


כַּ֭פְּךָ מֵעָלַ֣י הַרְחַ֑ק וְ֝אֵ֥מָתְךָ֗ אַֽל־תְּבַעֲתַֽנִּי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Withdraw Thy hand far from me: and let not Thy terror make me afraid.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Keep Your compulsion far from me, and let Your terror not confound me.


###### Job 13:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:22)


וּ֭קְרָא וְאָנֹכִ֣י אֶעֱנֶ֑ה אֽוֹ־אֲ֝דַבֵּ֗ר וַהֲשִׁיבֵֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then call Thou, and I will answer: or let me speak, and Thou answer me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then call and I will answer, or I shall speak and You answer me.


###### Job 13:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:23)


כַּמָּ֣ה לִ֭י עֲוֺנ֣וֹת וְחַטָּא֑וֹת פִּֽשְׁעִ֥י וְ֝חַטָּאתִ֗י הֹדִיעֵֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
How many are my iniquities and sins? let me know my transgression and my sin.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
How many are my transgressions and my sins? Make known to me my offenses and my sins.


###### Job 13:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:24)


לָֽמָּה־פָנֶ֥יךָ תַסְתִּ֑יר וְתַחְשְׁבֵ֖נִי לְאוֹיֵ֣ב לָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Why dost Thou hide Thy face, and hold me for Thy enemy?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Why do You hide Your face [(from seeing My uprightness)] and reckon me Your foe?


###### Job 13:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:25)


הֶעָלֶ֣ה נִדָּ֣ף תַּעֲר֑וֹץ וְאֶת־קַ֖שׁ יָבֵ֣שׁ תִּרְדֹּֽף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Wilt Thou chase a driven leaf? and wilt Thou pursue the dry stubble?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will You affright a shriveled leaf? Will You pursue dry stubble?


###### Job 13:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:26)


כִּֽי־תִכְתֹּ֣ב עָלַ֣י מְרֹר֑וֹת וְ֝תוֹרִישֵׁ֗נִי עֲוֺנ֥וֹת נְעוּרָֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For Thou writest bitter things against me, and makest me inherit the iniquities of my youth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For You write down my contrariness against me [(and not the good that I have done)] and You make me inherit the sins of my youth.


###### Job 13:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:27)


וְתָ֘שֵׂ֤ם בַּסַּ֨ד**׀** רַגְלַ֗י וְתִשְׁמ֥וֹר כׇּל־אׇרְחוֹתָ֑י עַל־שׇׁרְשֵׁ֥י רַ֝גְלַ֗י תִּתְחַקֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou puttest my feet also in the stocks, and lookest narrowly at all my paths; Thou settest Thy print upon the heels of my feet.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And You put my feet in stocks and guard all my ways. You engrave my [very] footsteps for Yourself [("Here he stepped and thus he did.")]


###### Job 13:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/13:28)


וְ֭הוּא כְּרָקָ֣ב יִבְלֶ֑ה כְּ֝בֶ֗גֶד אֲכָ֣לוֹ עָֽשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he is consumed like rottenness; like a garment eaten by moths.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But is it [(the body)] not like decaying rottenness, like a moth-eaten garment? [i.e., it is not to Your honor to pursue it so.]



## Chapter 14

###### Job 14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:1)


אָ֭דָם יְל֣וּד אִשָּׁ֑ה קְצַ֥ר יָ֝מִ֗ים וּֽשְׂבַֽע־רֹֽגֶז׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Man that is born of a woman is of few days, and full of trouble.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Is not] man born of woman, short of days and full of vexation?


###### Job 14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:2)


כְּצִ֣יץ יָ֭צָא וַיִּמָּ֑ל וַיִּבְרַ֥ח כַּ֝צֵּ֗ל וְלֹ֣א יַעֲמֽוֹד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He comes forth like a flower, and is cut down: he flees also like a shadow, and does not endure.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As a blossom that blows and is cut off, fleeting as a shadow that does not stand.


###### Job 14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:3)


אַף־עַל־זֶ֭ה פָּקַ֣חְתָּ עֵינֶ֑ךָ וְאֹ֘תִ֤י תָבִ֖יא בְמִשְׁפָּ֣ט עִמָּֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And dost Thou open Thy eyes upon such a one, and bringest me into judgment with Thee?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
On this, too, You have cast Your eye [in judgment], and [even] me You have brought into judgment with You [(for my sins)].


###### Job 14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:4)


מִֽי־יִתֵּ֣ן טָ֭הוֹר מִטָּמֵ֗א לֹ֣א אֶחָֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Who can bring a clean thing out of an unclean? not one.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Can pure [(one wholly free of sin)] emerge from impure? — not one!


###### Job 14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:5)


אִ֥ם־חֲרוּצִ֨ים**׀** יָמָ֗יו מִֽסְפַּר־חֳדָשָׁ֥יו אִתָּ֑ךְ חֻקָּ֥ו עָ֝שִׂ֗יתָ וְלֹ֣א יַעֲבֹֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Seeing his days are determined, the number of his months is with Thee, Thou hast appointed his bounds that he cannot pass;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(Is a man not simply requited for his sins)] if his days are circumscribed [(unto death)], if the number of his months is [fixed] with You? You have set his limit, which he cannot pass.


###### Job 14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:6)


שְׁעֵ֣ה מֵעָלָ֣יו וְיֶחְדָּ֑ל עַד־יִ֝רְצֶ֗ה כְּשָׂכִ֥יר יוֹמֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
turn from him, that he may rest, till he shall accomplish, as a hireling, his day.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Leave off [afflicting] him, then, and let it [(his pain)] cease, until as a hired laborer [(who desires the end of his working day)], he desires his day [of death]. [(For there is more finality in his passing than in that of all other things:)]


###### Job 14:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:7)


כִּ֤י יֵ֥שׁ לָעֵ֗ץ תִּ֫קְוָ֥ה אִֽם־יִ֭כָּרֵת וְע֣וֹד יַחֲלִ֑יף וְ֝יֹנַקְתּ֗וֹ לֹ֣א תֶחְדָּֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For there is hope of a tree, if it be cut down, that it will sprout again, and that its tender branch will not cease.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For a tree has hope, though it is cut off. It will renew itself, and its young branches will not cease.


###### Job 14:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:8)


אִם־יַזְקִ֣ין בָּאָ֣רֶץ שׇׁרְשׁ֑וֹ וּ֝בֶעָפָ֗ר יָמ֥וּת גִּזְעֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Though its root grow old in the earth, and its stock die in the ground;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Though its root grow old in the earth and its stock die in the dust,


###### Job 14:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:9)


מֵרֵ֣יחַ מַ֣יִם יַפְרִ֑חַ וְעָשָׂ֖ה קָצִ֣יר כְּמוֹ־נָֽטַע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
yet through the scent of water it will bud, and bring forth boughs like a plant.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
the [very] scent of water will cause it to bloom, and it will sprout branches like a sapling.


###### Job 14:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:10)


וְגֶ֣בֶר יָ֭מוּת וַֽיֶּחֱלָ֑שׁ וַיִּגְוַ֖ע אָדָ֣ם וְאַיּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But man dies, and is laid low; yea, man perishes, and where is he?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But when a man dies, he is sapped. When a man expires, where is he?


###### Job 14:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:11)


אָֽזְלוּ־מַ֭יִם מִנִּי־יָ֑ם וְ֝נָהָ֗ר יֶחֱרַ֥ב וְיָבֵֽשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The waters fail from the sea, and the river is parched, and dries up:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When the waters leave the sea, will the river [(which comes from the sea)] not be parched, and dry up?


###### Job 14:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:12)


וְאִ֥ישׁ שָׁכַ֗ב וְֽלֹא־יָ֫ק֥וּם עַד־בִּלְתִּ֣י שָׁ֭מַיִם לֹ֣א יָקִ֑יצוּ וְלֹֽא־יֵ֝עֹ֗רוּ מִשְּׁנָתָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but man lies down, and does not rise: till the heavens be no more, they shall not awake, nor be raised out of their sleep.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
So a man will lie down and not rise. Until the heavens are worn away, they [(men)] will not awaken and they will not rise up from their sleep.


###### Job 14:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:13)


מִ֤י יִתֵּ֨ן**׀** בִּשְׁא֬וֹל תַּצְפִּנֵ֗נִי תַּ֭סְתִּירֵנִי עַד־שׁ֣וּב אַפֶּ֑ךָ תָּ֤שִֽׁית־לִ֖י חֹ֣ק וְתִזְכְּרֵֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
O that Thou wouldst hide me in She᾽ol, that Thou wouldst keep me secret, until Thy wrath is past, that Thou wouldst appoint me a set time, and remember me!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would that [(when You were angry with me)] You hid me in the grave, concealed me until Your wrath were turned, set me a time and remembered me.


###### Job 14:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:14)


אִם־יָמ֥וּת גֶּ֗בֶר הֲיִֽ֫חְיֶ֥ה כׇּל־יְמֵ֣י צְבָאִ֣י אֲיַחֵ֑ל עַד־בּ֝֗וֹא חֲלִיפָתִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If a man die, shall he live again? all the days of my service I should wait, until my reward should come.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When a man dies, will he live [again]? Then all the days of my time [on this earth] I would hope [to live] until [the time of] my passing came.


###### Job 14:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:15)


תִּ֭קְרָא וְאָנֹכִ֣י אֶעֱנֶ֑ךָּ לְֽמַעֲשֵׂ֖ה יָדֶ֣יךָ תִכְסֹֽף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shouldst call, and I would answer Thee: Thou shouldst have a desire to the work of Thy hands.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Call [me (to reprove me)] and I will answer You. Desire the work of Your hands.


###### Job 14:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:16)


כִּֽי־עַ֭תָּה צְעָדַ֣י תִּסְפּ֑וֹר לֹֽא־תִ֝שְׁמֹ֗ר עַל־חַטָּאתִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For now Thou numberest my steps: dost Thou not watch for my sin?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But now You count [all of] my steps and You do not delay [(the payment)] for my sin.


###### Job 14:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:17)


חָתֻ֣ם בִּצְר֣וֹר פִּשְׁעִ֑י וַ֝תִּטְפֹּ֗ל עַל־עֲוֺנִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My transgression is sealed up in a bag, and Thou dost daub my iniquity with wax.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My offense is tied in a knot [(that it not go lost)] and it is joined to my transgression.


###### Job 14:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:18)


וְ֭אוּלָם הַר־נוֹפֵ֣ל יִבּ֑וֹל וְ֝צ֗וּר יֶעְתַּ֥ק מִמְּקֹמֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And surely the mountain falling crumbles away, and the rock is removed out of its place.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Even] a fallen mountain will [crumble and] produce vegetation, as will a rock dislodged from its place.


###### Job 14:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:19)


אֲבָנִ֤ים**׀** שָׁ֥חֲקוּ מַ֗יִם תִּשְׁטֹֽף־סְפִיחֶ֥יהָ עֲפַר־אָ֑רֶץ וְתִקְוַ֖ת אֱנ֣וֹשׁ הֶאֱבַֽדְתָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The waters wear the stones: the torrents wash away the dust of the earth; so Thou destroyest the hope of man.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Stones worn down by water — its crumblings are flooded over [to become] dust of the earth [(from which vegetation later sprouts)]. But the hope of man is [completely] lost [with his death].


###### Job 14:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:20)


תִּתְקְפֵ֣הוּ לָ֭נֶצַח וַֽיַּהֲלֹ֑ךְ מְשַׁנֶּ֥ה פָ֝נָ֗יו וַֽתְּשַׁלְּחֵֽהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou prevailest for ever against him, and he passes: Thou changest his countenance, and sendest him away.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You overpower him for eternity and he passes on. You change his face and send him away.


###### Job 14:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:21)


יִכְבְּד֣וּ בָ֭נָיו וְלֹ֣א יֵדָ֑ע וְ֝יִצְעֲר֗וּ וְֽלֹא־יָבִ֥ין לָֽמוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
His sons come to honour, and he knows it not; and they are brought low, but he perceives them not.
