**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If his sons are "weighted" [(with wealth)], he will not know it; if they are reduced, he will not perceive it.


###### Job 14:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/14:22)


אַךְ־בְּ֭שָׂרוֹ עָלָ֣יו יִכְאָ֑ב וְ֝נַפְשׁ֗וֹ עָלָ֥יו תֶּאֱבָֽל׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Only when his flesh is on him does he feel pain, and while his soul is within him does he mourn.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Only the flesh upon him [(fed on by the maggots)] will [(smart in)] pain, and his soul will mourn over him.



## Chapter 15

###### Job 15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:1)


וַ֭יַּעַן אֱלִיפַ֥ז הַֽתֵּימָנִ֗י וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then answered Elifaz the Temanite, and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Elifaz the Temanite raised his voice and said:


###### Job 15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:2)


הֶחָכָ֗ם יַעֲנֶ֥ה דַעַת־ר֑וּחַ וִימַלֵּ֖א קָדִ֣ים בִּטְנֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Should a wise man utter windy knowledge, and fill his belly with the east wind?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will the wise man answer vain thoughts and fill his stomach with the east wind?


###### Job 15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:3)


הוֹכֵ֣חַ בְּ֭דָבָר לֹ֣א יִסְכּ֑וֹן וּ֝מִלִּ֗ים לֹא־יוֹעִ֥יל בָּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Should he reason with unprofitable talk? or with speeches wherewith he can do no good?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To debate what is of no benefit and [to spout] words which are of no avail?


###### Job 15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:4)


אַף־אַ֭תָּה תָּפֵ֣ר יִרְאָ֑ה וְתִגְרַ֥ע שִׂ֝יחָ֗ה לִפְנֵי־אֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yea, thou castest off fear, and thou dost slight the prayer that is made before God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
How much more so do you [(who are exceedingly wise)] abolish fear [of G d] and increase complaint before G d!


###### Job 15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:5)


כִּ֤י יְאַלֵּ֣ף עֲוֺנְךָ֣ פִ֑יךָ וְ֝תִבְחַ֗ר לְשׁ֣וֹן עֲרוּמִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For thy mouth utters thy iniquity, and thou choosest the tongue of the crafty.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For your sin [i.e., your evil inclination] teaches your mouth [to speak in this manner]; you should have chosen the tongue of the subtle [in wisdom].


###### Job 15:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:6)


יַרְשִׁיעֲךָ֣ פִ֣יךָ וְלֹא־אָ֑נִי וּ֝שְׂפָתֶ֗יךָ יַעֲנוּ־בָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thy own mouth condemns thee, and not I: yea, thy own lips testify against thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Answer my question and] let your [own] mouth condemn you, and not I, and let your [own] lips testify against you:


###### Job 15:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:7)


הֲרִאישׁ֣וֹן אָ֭דָם תִּוָּלֵ֑ד וְלִפְנֵ֖י גְבָע֣וֹת חוֹלָֽלְתָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Art thou the first man that was born? or wast thou made before the hills?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Were you born first, [before] Adam? Were you created before the hills?


###### Job 15:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:8)


הַבְס֣וֹד אֱל֣וֹהַּ תִּשְׁמָ֑ע וְתִגְרַ֖ע אֵלֶ֣יךָ חׇכְמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Dost thou listen in to the council of God, and keep wisdom to thyself?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Have you been privy to the secrets of G d and drawn down [all of] wisdom to yourself?


###### Job 15:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:9)


מַה־יָּ֭דַעְתָּ וְלֹ֣א נֵדָ֑ע תָּ֝בִ֗ין וְֽלֹא־עִמָּ֥נוּ הֽוּא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
What knowst thou, that we know not? what understandest thou, which is not in us?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
What do you know that we do not? What do you understand that is not with us?


###### Job 15:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:10)


גַּם־שָׂ֣ב גַּם־יָשִׁ֣ישׁ בָּ֑נוּ כַּבִּ֖יר מֵאָבִ֣יךָ יָמִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
With us are both the greyheaded and very aged men, much older than thy father.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There is also a grey one, also an elder, among us, of more [days] than your father.


###### Job 15:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:11)


הַמְעַ֣ט מִ֭מְּךָ תַּנְחוּמ֣וֹת אֵ֑ל וְ֝דָבָ֗ר לָאַ֥ט עִמָּֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Are the consolations of God too small for thee? or the word which he spoke softly with thee?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Are the consolations of G d [i.e., your earlier good] too little for you, and His graciousness to you?


###### Job 15:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:12)


מַה־יִּקָּחֲךָ֥ לִבֶּ֑ךָ וּֽמַה־יִּרְזְמ֥וּן עֵינֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Why does thy heart carry thee away? and why do thy eyes wink?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
What does your heart teach you and what do your eyes insinuate


###### Job 15:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:13)


כִּֽי־תָשִׁ֣יב אֶל־אֵ֣ל רוּחֶ֑ךָ וְהֹצֵ֖אתָ מִפִּ֣יךָ מִלִּֽין׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that thou turnest thy spirit against God, and lettest such words go out of thy mouth?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
that you turn your spirit against the L rd and spout words [against Him]?


###### Job 15:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:14)


מָה־אֱנ֥וֹשׁ כִּֽי־יִזְכֶּ֑ה וְכִי־יִ֝צְדַּ֗ק יְל֣וּד אִשָּֽׁה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
What is man, that he should be clean? and one born of a woman, that he should be righteous?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
What is [(the power of)] man that he can be pure [(before the L rd)]? Can one born of woman be righteous?


###### Job 15:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:15)


הֵ֣ן בִּ֭קְדֹשָׁו לֹ֣א יַאֲמִ֑ין וְ֝שָׁמַ֗יִם לֹא־זַכּ֥וּ בְעֵינָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, he puts no trust in his holy ones; and the heavens are not clean in his sight:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In His angels He puts no permanence and the [hosts of] heaven are not pure in His eyes.


###### Job 15:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:16)


אַ֭ף כִּי־נִתְעָ֥ב וְֽנֶאֱלָ֑ח אִישׁ־שֹׁתֶ֖ה כַמַּ֣יִם עַוְלָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
how much less one who is abominable and filthy? man, who drinks iniquity like water?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
How much more so man, who is despised and abhorrent, who drinks wrong as he does water!


###### Job 15:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:17)


אֲחַוְךָ֥ שְֽׁמַֽע־לִ֑י וְזֶֽה־חָ֝זִ֗יתִי וַאֲסַפֵּֽרָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I will tell thee, hear me; and that which I have seen I will declare;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I will tell you something; listen to me. This is what I have seen, and I will tell it to you:


###### Job 15:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:18)


אֲשֶׁר־חֲכָמִ֥ים יַגִּ֑ידוּ וְלֹ֥א כִ֝חֲד֗וּ מֵאֲבוֹתָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
which wise men have told from their fathers, and have not hidden it:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The wise tell [i.e., they confess their offenses] and do not conceal [their sins] from their fathers.


###### Job 15:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:19)


לָהֶ֣ם לְ֭בַדָּם נִתְּנָ֣ה הָאָ֑רֶץ וְלֹא־עָ֖בַר זָ֣ר בְּתוֹכָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to whom alone the earth was given, and no stranger passed among them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To them alone [(Judah and Reuven, by their respective confessions)] was the earth given and no stranger intruded among them.


###### Job 15:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:20)


כׇּל־יְמֵ֣י רָ֭שָׁע ה֣וּא מִתְחוֹלֵ֑ל וּמִסְפַּ֥ר שָׁ֝נִ֗ים נִצְפְּנ֥וּ לֶעָרִֽיץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The wicked man travails with pain all his days, through the number of years stored up for the tyrant.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[But] all the days of the wicked one [(who does not repent and confess)], he laments, [fearing] the number of years [of punishment] laid up for the tyrant.


###### Job 15:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:21)


קוֹל־פְּחָדִ֥ים בְּאׇזְנָ֑יו בַּ֝שָּׁל֗וֹם שׁוֹדֵ֥ד יְבוֹאֶֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A dreadful sound is in his ears: in prosperity the destroyer shall come upon him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The voice of fear is in his ears [(saying: "The time of my recompense has come")], and in the midst of peace robbers will come upon him.


###### Job 15:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:22)


לֹא־יַאֲמִ֣ין שׁ֭וּב מִנִּי־חֹ֑שֶׁךְ (וצפו) [וְצָפ֖וּי] ה֣וּא אֱלֵי־חָֽרֶב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He does not believe that he will return out of darkness, and he is waited for by the sword.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(In the abundance of his sins)] he will not believe [that] he can return from the darkness [of Gehinnom], and he is consigned to the sword


###### Job 15:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:23)


נֹ֘דֵ֤ד ה֣וּא לַלֶּ֣חֶם אַיֵּ֑ה יָדַ֓ע**׀** כִּי־נָכ֖וֹן בְּיָד֣וֹ יֽוֹם־חֹֽשֶׁךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He wanders abroad for bread, saying, Where is it? he knows that the day of darkness is ready at his hand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
and to wander in search of bread. He knows [for a certainty] that the day of darkness is readied for him.


###### Job 15:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:24)


יְֽ֭בַעֲתֻהוּ צַ֣ר וּמְצוּקָ֑ה תִּ֝תְקְפֵ֗הוּ כְּמֶ֤לֶךְ**׀** עָתִ֬יד לַכִּידֽוֹר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Trouble and anguish make him afraid; they prevail against him, as a king ready to the battle.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Woe and affliction will confound him. He will be overpowered by it like the king [(Sancherev)] consigned to the fire [of Gehinnom (viz. Isaiah 30:33)].


###### Job 15:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:25)


כִּֽי־נָטָ֣ה אֶל־אֵ֣ל יָד֑וֹ וְאֶל־שַׁ֝דַּ֗י יִתְגַּבָּֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For he stretches out his hand against God, and behaves proudly against the Almighty.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For he has stretched forth his hand against G d and strengthened himself against the Almighty.


###### Job 15:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:26)


יָר֣וּץ אֵלָ֣יו בְּצַוָּ֑אר בַּ֝עֲבִ֗י גַּבֵּ֥י מָגִנָּֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He runs upon him with neck armour, with the thick bosses of his bucklers:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He has run against Him [(to anger Him)] with outstretched neck, [trusting] in the thickness [and] the height of his shields [i.e., trusting in his strength].


###### Job 15:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:27)


כִּֽי־כִסָּ֣ה פָנָ֣יו בְּחֶלְבּ֑וֹ וַיַּ֖עַשׂ פִּימָ֣ה עֲלֵי־כָֽסֶל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
because he has covered his face with his fat, and has put collops of fat on his flanks.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For he has covered his face with his fat and has made "mouths" [i.e., folds of fat] upon his ribs.


###### Job 15:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:28)


וַיִּשְׁכּ֤וֹן**׀** עָ֘רִ֤ים נִכְחָד֗וֹת בָּ֭תִּים לֹא־יֵ֣שְׁבוּ לָ֑מוֹ אֲשֶׁ֖ר הִתְעַתְּד֣וּ לְגַלִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he dwells in desolate cities, and in houses which no man inhabits, which are ready to become heaps.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [in his pride] he has inhabited waste cities, houses unfit for habitation, which had been relegated to the rubbish heaps.


###### Job 15:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:29)


לֹֽא־יֶ֭עְשַׁר וְלֹא־יָק֣וּם חֵיל֑וֹ וְלֹא־יִטֶּ֖ה לָאָ֣רֶץ מִנְלָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall not be rich, neither shall his substance continue, neither shall their produce bend to the earth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[But] he will not remain wealthy and his riches shall not endure, and their reversion will not turn [from heaven] to earth [i.e., their desires will not be gratified].


###### Job 15:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:30)


לֹֽא־יָס֨וּר**׀** מִנִּי־חֹ֗שֶׁךְ יֹ֭נַקְתּוֹ תְּיַבֵּ֣שׁ שַׁלְהָ֑בֶת וְ֝יָס֗וּר בְּר֣וּחַ פִּֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall not depart out of darkness; the flame shall dry up his branches, and by the breath of his mouth shall he go away.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He will not be removed from darkness. The flame will dry up his young branch, and he will be removed [from the world] by the breath of His mouth.


###### Job 15:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:31)


אַל־יַאֲמֵ֣ן בַּשָּׁ֣ו נִתְעָ֑ה כִּי־שָׁ֝֗וְא תִּהְיֶ֥ה תְמוּרָתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let him not trust in vanity, deceiving himself: for vanity shall be his recompense.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Let him who has wandered in vanity not trust [to good], for vanity will be his exchange.


###### Job 15:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:32)


בְּֽלֹא־י֭וֹמוֹ תִּמָּלֵ֑א וְ֝כִפָּת֗וֹ לֹ֣א רַעֲנָֽנָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It shall be accomplished before his time, and his branch shall not be green.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Before his time, it [(his "exchange")] will be full [i.e., it will have exhausted itself], and his dome [i.e., his branches] will not be green.


###### Job 15:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:33)


יַחְמֹ֣ס כַּגֶּ֣פֶן בִּסְר֑וֹ וְיַשְׁלֵ֥ךְ כַּ֝זַּ֗יִת נִצָּתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall shake off his unripe grape like the vine, and shall cast off his flower like the olive.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He will cast off his unripe grapes [i.e., his children] like the vine, and he will shed his bloom like the olive.


###### Job 15:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:34)


כִּֽי־עֲדַ֣ת חָנֵ֣ף גַּלְמ֑וּד וְ֝אֵ֗שׁ אָכְלָ֥ה אׇהֳלֵי־שֹֽׁחַד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the company of hypocrites shall be desolate, and fire shall consume the tents of bribery.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the company of the flatterer will be solitary and the fire [(G d's wrath)] will consume the tents of graft.


###### Job 15:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/15:35)


הָרֹ֣ה עָ֭מָל וְיָ֣לֹֽד אָ֑וֶן וּ֝בִטְנָ֗ם תָּכִ֥ין מִרְמָֽה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They conceive mischief, and bring forth vanity, and their belly prepares deceit.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They conceive wrong and beget vanity and their belly spawns deceit.



## Chapter 16

###### Job 16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:1)


וַיַּ֥עַן אִיּ֗וֹב וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Iyyov answered and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Iyyov raised his voice and said:


###### Job 16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:2)


שָׁמַ֣עְתִּי כְאֵ֣לֶּה רַבּ֑וֹת מְנַחֲמֵ֖י עָמָ֣ל כֻּלְּכֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I have heard many such things: miserable comforters are you all.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I have heard such words many times. You are all counselors of deceit.


###### Job 16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:3)


הֲקֵ֥ץ לְדִבְרֵי־ר֑וּחַ א֥וֹ מַה־יַּ֝מְרִֽיצְךָ֗ כִּ֣י תַֽעֲנֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Shall windy words have an end? or what provokes thee that thou answerest?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Is there an end to words of vanity? What [would] impel you to answer [cogently]?


###### Job 16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:4)


גַּ֤ם**׀** אָנֹכִי֮ כָּכֶ֢ם אֲדַ֫בֵּ֥רָה ל֤וּ יֵ֪שׁ נַפְשְׁכֶ֡ם תַּ֤חַת נַפְשִׁ֗י אַחְבִּ֣ירָה עֲלֵיכֶ֣ם בְּמִלִּ֑ים וְאָנִ֥יעָה עֲ֝לֵיכֶ֗ם בְּמ֣וֹ רֹאשִֽׁי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Would I also speak as you do? if your soul were in my soul’s stead, would I join words together against you, and shake my head at you?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I could also speak as you if you were [being afflicted] instead of me. I could [also] join you with words and nod my head [in lamentation] over you.


###### Job 16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:5)


אֲאַמִּצְכֶ֥ם בְּמוֹ־פִ֑י וְנִ֖יד שְׂפָתַ֣י יַחְשֹֽׂךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I would strengthen you with my mouth, and the moving of my lips should assuage your grief.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I could strengthen you with my mouth [(as you do to me)], and the plaint of my lips would cease.


###### Job 16:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:6)


אִֽם־אֲ֭דַבְּרָה לֹא־יֵחָשֵׂ֣ךְ כְּאֵבִ֑י וְ֝אַחְדְּלָ֗ה מַה־מִּנִּ֥י יַהֲלֹֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Though I speak, my grief is not assuaged: and though I forbear, what am I eased?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[But now,] if I speak, my pain will not cease, and if I cease from speech, what [(of my pain)] would leave me?


###### Job 16:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:7)


אַךְ־עַתָּ֥ה הֶלְאָ֑נִי הֲ֝שִׁמּ֗וֹתָ כׇּל־עֲדָתִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But now it has made me weary: Thou hast made desolate all my company.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But now, it has wearied me. You have stunned all of my household [(into silence)].


###### Job 16:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:8)


וַֽ֭תִּקְמְטֵנִי לְעֵ֣ד הָיָ֑ה וַיָּ֥קׇם בִּ֥י כַ֝חֲשִׁ֗י בְּפָנַ֥י יַעֲנֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Thou hast filled me with wrinkles, which is a witness against me: and my leanness rising up against me bears witness to my face.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You have shriveled me up as testimony [to my sin]. My leanness has risen against me [(in testimony)]; it testifies in my face.


###### Job 16:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:9)


אַפּ֤וֹ טָרַ֨ף**׀** וַֽיִּשְׂטְמֵ֗נִי חָרַ֣ק עָלַ֣י בְּשִׁנָּ֑יו צָרִ֓י**׀** יִלְטֹ֖שׁ עֵינָ֣יו לִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He tears me in his wrath, and hates me: he gnashes at me with his teeth; my enemy sharpens his eyes upon me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
His wrath has torn me and He has hated me. He has gnashed His teeth against me and my oppressor [(Satan)] sharpens his eyes against me.


###### Job 16:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:10)


פָּעֲר֬וּ עָלַ֨י**׀** בְּפִיהֶ֗ם בְּ֭חֶרְפָּה הִכּ֣וּ לְחָיָ֑י יַ֝֗חַד עָלַ֥י יִתְמַלָּאֽוּן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They have gaped upon me with their mouth; they have smitten me upon the cheek scornfully; they gather themselves together against me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They have opened their mouths against me. They have smitten my cheeks with shame. They have gathered together against me.


###### Job 16:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:11)


יַסְגִּירֵ֣נִי אֵ֭ל אֶ֣ל עֲוִ֑יל וְעַל־יְדֵ֖י רְשָׁעִ֣ים יִרְטֵֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
God has delivered me to the ungodly, and turned me over into the hands of the wicked.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
G d has handed me over to a mocker, and by the hands of the wicked He "heals" me.


###### Job 16:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:12)


שָׁ֘לֵ֤ו הָיִ֨יתִי**׀** וַֽיְפַרְפְּרֵ֗נִי וְאָחַ֣ז בְּ֭עׇרְפִּי וַֽיְפַצְפְּצֵ֑נִי וַיְקִימֵ֥נִי ל֝֗וֹ לְמַטָּרָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I was at ease, but he broke me asunder: he has also taken me by my neck, and shaken me to pieces, and set me up for his target.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I was at peace and he [(the mocker)] crumbled me. He seized my neck and smashed me, and he set me up as a target for him.


###### Job 16:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:13)


יָ֘סֹ֤בּוּ עָלַ֨י**׀** רַבָּ֗יו יְפַלַּ֣ח כִּ֭לְיוֹתַי וְלֹ֣א יַחְמֹ֑ל יִשְׁפֹּ֥ךְ לָ֝אָ֗רֶץ מְרֵרָתִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
His archers compass me round about, he cleaves my reins asunder, and does not spare; he pours out my gall upon the ground.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
His archers surrounded me. He splits my innards and he has no mercy. He spills my gall to the ground.


###### Job 16:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:14)


יִפְרְצֵ֣נִי פֶ֭רֶץ עַל־פְּנֵי־פָ֑רֶץ יָרֻ֖ץ עָלַ֣י כְּגִבּֽוֹר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He breaks me with breach upon breach, he runs upon me like a giant.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He multiplies wound upon wound. He runs upon me like a warrior.


###### Job 16:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:15)


שַׂ֣ק תָּ֭פַרְתִּי עֲלֵ֣י גִלְדִּ֑י וְעֹלַ֖לְתִּי בֶעָפָ֣ר קַרְנִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I have sewn sackcloth upon my skin, and have laid my horn in the dust.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I sewed sackcloth for my scab, and I sullied my [face's] radiance with ashes.


###### Job 16:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:16)


פָּנַ֣י (חמרמרה) [חֳ֭מַרְמְרוּ] מִנִּי־בֶ֑כִי וְעַ֖ל עַפְעַפַּ֣י צַלְמָֽוֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My face is scalded with weeping, and on my eyelids is the shadow of death;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My face became creased from crying, and death-shadows descended on my lids.


###### Job 16:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:17)


עַ֭ל לֹא־חָמָ֣ס בְּכַפָּ֑י וּֽתְפִלָּתִ֥י זַכָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
though no injustice is in my hands; and my prayer is pure.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[And all this befell me] for no violence in my palms; and my prayer was pure [(I did not curse my friend nor desire ill for him)].


###### Job 16:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:18)


אֶ֭רֶץ אַל־תְּכַסִּ֣י דָמִ֑י וְֽאַל־יְהִ֥י מָ֝ק֗וֹם לְזַעֲקָתִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
O earth, cover not my blood, and let my cry have no pause.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Earth, cover my blood, and let there be no place [therein to silence] my outcries [(from ascending to heaven)].


###### Job 16:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:19)


גַּם־עַ֭תָּה הִנֵּה־בַשָּׁמַ֣יִם עֵדִ֑י וְ֝שָׂהֲדִ֗י בַּמְּרֹמִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Even now, behold, my witness is in heaven, and my testimony is on high.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Even now my Witness [(who knows my ways)] is in heaven and my testimony is in the heights.


###### Job 16:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:20)


מְלִיצַ֥י רֵעָ֑י אֶל־אֱ֝ל֗וֹהַּ דָּלְפָ֥ה עֵינִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My friends scorn me: but my eye pours out tears to God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(You, who should have been)] my intercessors, my friends — my eyes stream to G d.


###### Job 16:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:21)


וְיוֹכַ֣ח לְגֶ֣בֶר עִם־אֱל֑וֹהַּ וּֽבֶן־אָדָ֥ם לְרֵעֵֽהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
O that a man might plead his cause with God, as the son of man with his fellow!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would that [He granted me that] a man could dispute with G d as a man [disputes] with his neighbor.


###### Job 16:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/16:22)


כִּֽי־שְׁנ֣וֹת מִסְפָּ֣ר יֶאֱתָ֑יוּ וְאֹ֖רַח לֹא־אָשׁ֣וּב אֶהֱלֹֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For when a few years are come, then I shall go the way whence I shall not return.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the years numbered [for my life] are coming [to an end], when I will travel the road of no return.



## Chapter 17

###### Job 17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:1)


רוּחִ֣י חֻ֭בָּלָה יָמַ֥י נִזְעָ֗כוּ קְבָרִ֥ים לִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My spirit is broken, my days are extinct, the grave is ready for me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My spirit has been destroyed. My days have leapt away. Graves await me


###### Job 17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:2)


אִם־לֹ֣א הֲ֭תֻלִים עִמָּדִ֑י וּ֝בְהַמְּרוֹתָ֗ם תָּלַ֥ן עֵינִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Are there not mockers with me? and does not my eye dwell on their provocation?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
even without mockers against me and my eyes constantly looking on at their taunting.


###### Job 17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:3)


שִֽׂימָה־נָּ֭א עׇרְבֵ֣נִי עִמָּ֑ךְ מִ֥י ה֝֗וּא לְיָדִ֥י יִתָּקֵֽעַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Give now a pledge, be surety for me with thyself: who else is there that will strike hands with me?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Give [heart] to me, I pray You. Give me surety [(by "handshake") to dispute] with You. [For] who is there [among them] to shake my hand [(in friendship)]?


###### Job 17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:4)


כִּֽי־לִ֭בָּם צָפַ֣נְתָּ מִשָּׂ֑כֶל עַל־כֵּ֝֗ן לֹ֣א תְרֹמֵֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For thou hast hid their heart from understanding: therefore shalt thou not exalt them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For You have hid their hearts from wisdom, wherefore You will not be exalted [through them].


###### Job 17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:5)


לְ֭חֵלֶק יַגִּ֣יד רֵעִ֑ים וְעֵינֵ֖י בָנָ֣יו תִּכְלֶֽנָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They are as one who invites friends to have a share, whilst the eyes of his children fail.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Friends will flatter each other and [(as a result)] the eyes of their sons will fail.


###### Job 17:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:6)


וְֽ֭הִצִּיגַנִי לִמְשֹׁ֣ל עַמִּ֑ים וְתֹ֖פֶת לְפָנִ֣ים אֶהְיֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He has made me also a byword of the peoples; and I shall be a horror to every face.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(This would be my dispute with the L rd:)] He has set me up as a parable for the peoples. I am become a mockery to [their] faces.


###### Job 17:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:7)


וַתֵּ֣כַהּ מִכַּ֣עַשׂ עֵינִ֑י וִיצֻרַ֖י כַּצֵּ֣ל כֻּלָּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My eye also is dim by reason of vexation, and all my members are as a shadow.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My eye is dimmed with anger and all of my limbs are like shadows.


###### Job 17:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:8)


יָשֹׁ֣מּוּ יְשָׁרִ֣ים עַל־זֹ֑את וְ֝נָקִ֗י עַל־חָנֵ֥ף יִתְעֹרָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Upright men are astonished at this, and the innocent stirs up himself against the godless.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Let the upright be astounded over this, and let the clean one [(hearing of these mockeries)] be aroused against the flatterer.


###### Job 17:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:9)


וְיֹאחֵ֣ז צַדִּ֣יק דַּרְכּ֑וֹ וּֽטְהׇר־יָ֝דַ֗יִם יֹסִ֥יף אֹֽמֶץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yet the righteous holds on his way, and he that has clean hands grows stronger and stronger.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And let the righteous one hold fast to his way [(to oppose the flatterers)] and [let] the pure of hand add courage.


###### Job 17:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:10)


וְֽאוּלָ֗ם כֻּלָּ֣ם תָּ֭שֻׁבוּ וּבֹ֣אוּ נָ֑א וְלֹֽא־אֶמְצָ֖א בָכֶ֣ם חָכָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But as for you all, return, and come now: for I cannot find one wise man among you.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But all of you [(my friends)] turn about and come now [(to learn the truth)]; but I find no wise one among you [(in your mocking of my plaint)].


###### Job 17:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:11)


יָמַ֣י עָ֭בְרוּ זִמֹּתַ֣י נִתְּק֑וּ מ֖וֹרָשֵׁ֣י לְבָבִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My days are past, my purposes are broken off, even the thoughts of my heart.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My days [(of good)] have passed; my thoughts [(my expectations of continued good)] have been cut off, the bequests of my heart.


###### Job 17:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:12)


לַ֭יְלָה לְי֣וֹם יָשִׂ֑ימוּ א֝֗וֹר קָר֥וֹב מִפְּנֵי־חֹֽשֶׁךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They change the night into day, saying, The light is near when the darkness is at hand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They [(their mockeries)] change day to night. The light [of the day seems] short [to me] because of the darkness [(of my afflictions)].


###### Job 17:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:13)


אִם־אֲ֭קַוֶּה שְׁא֣וֹל בֵּיתִ֑י בַּ֝חֹ֗שֶׁךְ רִפַּ֥דְתִּי יְצוּעָֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If I look to She᾽ol as my home: if I spread my couch in the darkness:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Since I hope for the pit as my home, I have spread my bed in darkness [i.e., the grave].


###### Job 17:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:14)


לַשַּׁ֣חַת קָ֭רָאתִי אָ֣בִי אָ֑תָּה אִמִּ֥י וַ֝אֲחֹתִ֗י לָרִמָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
if I say to the pit, Thou art my father; to the worm. Thou art my mother and my sister;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To the pit I have called: "You are my father"; "my mother" and "my sister" to the worms.


###### Job 17:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:15)


וְ֭אַיֵּה אֵפ֣וֹ תִקְוָתִ֑י וְ֝תִקְוָתִ֗י מִ֣י יְשׁוּרֶֽנָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
where then is my hope? as for my hope, who shall see it?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Where is [(the realization of)] my hope, and who will see my hope [(to hurry it to me)]?


###### Job 17:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/17:16)


בַּדֵּ֣י שְׁאֹ֣ל תֵּרַ֑דְנָה אִם־יַ֖חַד עַל־עָפָ֣ר נָֽחַת׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Will they go down with me to the bars of She᾽ol? shall we descend together to the dust?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would that they [(my limbs)] which [are destined to] descend to the pit would [(soon)] lie on the dust [in death]!



## Chapter 18

###### Job 18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:1)


וַ֭יַּעַן בִּלְדַּ֥ד הַשֻּׁחִ֗י וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then answered Bildad the Shuĥite, and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Bildad the Shuchite raised his voice and said:


###### Job 18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:2)


עַד־אָ֤נָה**׀** תְּשִׂימ֣וּן קִנְצֵ֣י לְמִלִּ֑ין תָּ֝בִ֗ינוּ וְאַחַ֥ר נְדַבֵּֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
How long will you make a perversion of words? consider, and afterwards we will speak.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
How long will you [(the friends)] put an end to words? [i.e., How long will you interrupt the words of Iyyov?]


###### Job 18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:3)


מַ֭דּוּעַ נֶחְשַׁ֣בְנוּ כַבְּהֵמָ֑ה נִ֝טְמִ֗ינוּ בְּעֵינֵיכֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Why are we counted as beasts, and reputed vile in your sight?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Why should we be looked upon as beasts? We are obtuse in your eyes.


###### Job 18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:4)


טֹרֵ֥ף נַפְשׁ֗וֹ בְּאַ֫פּ֥וֹ הַֽ֭לְמַעַנְךָ תֵּעָ֣זַב אָ֑רֶץ וְיֶעְתַּק־צ֝֗וּר מִמְּקֹמֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He tears himself in his anger. Shall the earth be forsaken for thee? or shall the rock be removed out of his place?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[You Iyyov,] who tears his spirit in his anger, shall the [ordinance of the] earth be abandoned for your sake, and the Rock be removed from His place [i.e., from His ordinance]?


###### Job 18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:5)


גַּ֤ם א֣וֹר רְשָׁעִ֣ים יִדְעָ֑ךְ וְלֹא־יִ֝גַּ֗הּ שְׁבִ֣יב אִשּֽׁוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yea, the light of the wicked shall be put out, and the spark of his fire shall not shine.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Verily, the light of the wicked will dart away, and the spark of his fire will not light.


###### Job 18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:6)


א֭וֹר חָשַׁ֣ךְ בְּאׇהֳל֑וֹ וְ֝נֵר֗וֹ עָלָ֥יו יִדְעָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The light shall be dark in his tent, and his candle over him shall be put out.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The light of his tent will grow dark, and the light of his lamp will dart away.


###### Job 18:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:7)


יֵ֭צְרוּ צַעֲדֵ֣י אוֹנ֑וֹ וְֽתַשְׁלִיכֵ֥הוּ עֲצָתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The steps of his strength shall be straitened, and his own counsel shall cast him down.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The steps of his might will be constricted, and his counsel will cast him away.


###### Job 18:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:8)


כִּֽי־שֻׁלַּ֣ח בְּרֶ֣שֶׁת בְּרַגְלָ֑יו וְעַל־שְׂ֝בָכָ֗ה יִתְהַלָּֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For he is cast into a net by his own feet, and he walks on a pitfall.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For he will be sent into the net with his [own] feet, and he will walk upon the lattice-work [i.e., the net].


###### Job 18:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:9)


יֹאחֵ֣ז בְּעָקֵ֣ב פָּ֑ח יַחֲזֵ֖ק עָלָ֣יו צַמִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The trap shall take him by the heel, and the snare shall prevail against him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The trap will hold him by the heel, and thirst will grow upon him.


###### Job 18:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:10)


טָמ֣וּן בָּאָ֣רֶץ חַבְל֑וֹ וּ֝מַלְכֻּדְתּ֗וֹ עֲלֵ֣י נָתִֽיב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The noose is laid for him in the ground, and a trap for him in the way.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
His rope [i.e., the rope which will snare him] is hidden in the ground; his trap lies upon the path.


###### Job 18:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:11)


סָ֭בִיב בִּעֲתֻ֣הוּ בַלָּה֑וֹת וֶהֱפִיצֻ֥הוּ לְרַגְלָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Terrors shall make him afraid on every side, and shall chase him at his heels:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Around him, demons affright him, and they beat him [down] to his feet.


###### Job 18:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:12)


יְהִי־רָעֵ֥ב אֹנ֑וֹ וְ֝אֵ֗יד נָכ֥וֹן לְצַלְעֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
his first born shall he plagued with hunger, and his wife beset with calamity.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
His son will hunger and calamity awaits his wife.


###### Job 18:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:13)


יֹ֭אכַל בַּדֵּ֣י עוֹר֑וֹ יֹאכַ֥ל בַּ֝דָּ֗יו בְּכ֣וֹר מָֽוֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He will devour the limbs of his skin: the firstborn doomed to die will devour his own limbs.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He will consume the branches of his skin [(his other sons and daughters)]. The prince of death will consume his branches.


###### Job 18:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:14)


יִנָּתֵ֣ק מֵ֭אׇהֳלוֹ מִבְטַח֑וֹ וְ֝תַצְעִדֵ֗הוּ לְמֶ֣לֶךְ בַּלָּהֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall be cut off from the tent of his security; and shall he brought to the king of terrors.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He will be cut off from his tent [i.e., his wife], who trusts to him, and she will dispatch him to the king of the demons.


###### Job 18:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:15)


תִּשְׁכּ֣וֹן בְּ֭אׇהֳלוֹ מִבְּלִיל֑וֹ יְזֹרֶ֖ה עַל־נָוֵ֣הוּ גׇפְרִֽית׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In his tent dwells that which is none of his: brimstone is scattered on his habitation.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
She will live in his tent [(a widow)] without him, [and, in the end,] his habitation will be overspread with brimstone.


###### Job 18:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:16)


מִ֭תַּחַת שׇׁרָשָׁ֣יו יִבָ֑שׁוּ וּ֝מִמַּ֗עַל יִמַּ֥ל קְצִירֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
His roots shall be dried up beneath, and above shall his branch wither.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Beneath, his roots [i.e., what took root] will dry up, and above, his boughs will be cut away [(without his deriving any benefit from them)].


###### Job 18:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:17)


זִֽכְרוֹ־אָ֭בַד מִנִּי־אָ֑רֶץ וְלֹא־שֵׁ֥ם ל֝֗וֹ עַל־פְּנֵי־חֽוּץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
His remembrance shall perish from the earth, and he shall have no name in the street.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
His remembrance will vanish from the land; he will have no name in the thoroughfares.


###### Job 18:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:18)


יֶ֭הְדְּפֻהוּ מֵא֣וֹר אֶל־חֹ֑שֶׁךְ וּֽמִתֵּבֵ֥ל יְנִדֻּֽהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall be driven from light into darkness, and chased out of the world.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(From heaven)] they will thrust him from light into darkness, and they will remove him from the world.


###### Job 18:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:19)


לֹ֘א־נִ֤ין ל֣וֹ וְלֹא־נֶ֣כֶד בְּעַמּ֑וֹ וְאֵ֥ין שָׂ֝רִ֗יד בִּמְגוּרָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall have neither son nor offspring among his people, nor any remaining in his dwellings.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There will remain to him neither a son nor a grandson among his people, and no trace [of him will remain] in his dwelling places.


###### Job 18:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:20)


עַל־י֭וֹמוֹ נָשַׁ֣מּוּ אַחֲרֹנִ֑ים וְ֝קַדְמֹנִ֗ים אָ֣חֲזוּ שָֽׂעַר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They who come after him shall be astonished at his day, as they who went before were affrighted.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The later ones will be astounded at his day [(of destruction)], and the early ones [(who knew him previously)] will be seized [as with] a tempest.


###### Job 18:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/18:21)


אַךְ־אֵ֭לֶּה מִשְׁכְּנ֣וֹת עַוָּ֑ל וְ֝זֶ֗ה מְק֣וֹם לֹֽא־יָדַֽע־אֵֽל׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Surely such are the dwellings of the wicked, and this is the place of him who knows not God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
This alone [is the lot of] the abodes of iniquity, and this [is the lot of] the place that G d did not know [i.e., that He rejected].



## Chapter 19

###### Job 19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:1)


וַיַּ֥עַן אִיּ֗וֹב וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Iyyov answered and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Iyyov raised his voice and said:


###### Job 19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:2)


עַד־אָ֭נָה תּוֹגְי֣וּן נַפְשִׁ֑י וּֽתְדַכּאוּנַ֥נִי בְמִלִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
How long will you vex my soul, and break me in pieces with words?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
How long will you grieve my soul and oppress me with words?


###### Job 19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:3)


זֶ֤ה עֶ֣שֶׂר פְּ֭עָמִים תַּכְלִימ֑וּנִי לֹֽא־תֵ֝בֹ֗שׁוּ תַּהְכְּרוּ־לִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These ten times have you reproached me: you are not ashamed that you slight me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You have now shamed me ten times. You have not been ashamed to estrange yourselves from me.


###### Job 19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:4)


וְאַף־אׇמְנָ֥ם שָׁגִ֑יתִי אִ֝תִּ֗י תָּלִ֥ין מְשׁוּגָתִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And be it indeed that I have erred, my error remains with myself.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And even if I did go astray, with me [alone] my erring rests [i.e., I alone am smitten for it].


###### Job 19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:5)


אִם־אׇ֭מְנָם עָלַ֣י תַּגְדִּ֑ילוּ וְתוֹכִ֥יחוּ עָ֝לַ֗י חֶרְפָּתִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If indeed you will magnify yourselves against me, and plead against me my reproach:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If, indeed, [I erred], you [only] magnify [my transgression] and throw up my shame to my face.


###### Job 19:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:6)


דְּֽעוּ־אֵ֭פוֹ כִּֽי־אֱל֣וֹהַּ עִוְּתָ֑נִי וּ֝מְצוּד֗וֹ עָלַ֥י הִקִּֽיף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
know now that God has overthrown me, and has compassed me with his net.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Know, then, that G d has perverted [my verdict] and that He has cast His net around me.


###### Job 19:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:7)


הֵ֤ן אֶצְעַ֣ק חָ֭מָס וְלֹ֣א אֵעָנֶ֑ה אֲ֝שַׁוַּ֗ע וְאֵ֣ין מִשְׁפָּֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, I cry out, Violence, but I am not heard: I cry aloud, but there is no justice.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I cry out against the injustice [done me], and I am not answered. I scream and there is no justice.


###### Job 19:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:8)


אׇרְחִ֣י גָ֭דַר וְלֹ֣א אֶעֱב֑וֹר וְעַ֥ל נְ֝תִיבוֹתַ֗י חֹ֣שֶׁךְ יָשִֽׂים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He has fenced up my way so that I cannot pass, and he has set darkness in my paths.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He has barred my way and I cannot cross, and He has placed darkness upon my paths.


###### Job 19:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:9)


כְּ֭בוֹדִי מֵעָלַ֣י הִפְשִׁ֑יט וַ֝יָּ֗סַר עֲטֶ֣רֶת רֹאשִֽׁי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He has stripped me of my glory, and taken the crown from my head.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He has stripped me of my honor [i.e., my clothing] and removed the crown of my head.


###### Job 19:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:10)


יִתְּצֵ֣נִי סָ֭בִיב וָאֵלַ֑ךְ וַיַּסַּ֥ע כָּ֝עֵ֗ץ תִּקְוָתִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He has destroyed me on every side, and I am gone: and my hope he has uprooted like a tree.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He has smashed me on all sides, and I have gone, and He has uprooted my hope as a tree.


###### Job 19:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:11)


וַיַּ֣חַר עָלַ֣י אַפּ֑וֹ וַיַּחְשְׁבֵ֖נִי ל֣וֹ כְצָרָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He has also kindled his wrath against me, and he counts me to him as one of his enemies.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He has vented His wrath upon me, accounting me as [one of] His foes.


###### Job 19:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:12)


יַ֤חַד**׀** יָ֘בֹ֤אוּ גְדוּדָ֗יו וַיָּסֹ֣לּוּ עָלַ֣י דַּרְכָּ֑ם וַיַּחֲנ֖וּ סָבִ֣יב לְאׇהֳלִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
His troops come together, and raise up their way against me, and encamp round about my tent.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All of His troops [i.e., His afflictions] come together and pave their way against me, and they camp around my tent.


###### Job 19:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:13)


אַ֭חַי מֵעָלַ֣י הִרְחִ֑יק וְ֝יֹדְעַ֗י אַךְ־זָ֥רוּ מִמֶּֽנִּי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He has put my brethren far from me, and my acquaintances are wholly estranged from me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He has distanced my brothers from me, and those who knew me have become estranged from me.


###### Job 19:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:14)


חָדְל֥וּ קְרוֹבָ֑י וּֽמְיֻדָּעַ֥י שְׁכֵחֽוּנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My kinsfolk have failed, and my familiar friends have forgotten me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My close ones are gone, and my familiars have forgotten me.


###### Job 19:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:15)


גָּ֘רֵ֤י בֵיתִ֣י וְ֭אַמְהֹתַי לְזָ֣ר תַּחְשְׁבֻ֑נִי נׇ֝כְרִ֗י הָיִ֥יתִי בְעֵינֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They who dwell in my house, and my womenservants, count me for a stranger: I am an alien in their sight.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The dwellers in my household and my maidservants have considered me a stranger. I have become a foreigner in their eyes.


###### Job 19:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:16)


לְעַבְדִּ֣י קָ֭רָאתִי וְלֹ֣א יַעֲנֶ֑ה בְּמוֹ־פִ֝֗י אֶתְחַנֶּן־לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I call my servant, and he gives me no answer; though I entreat him with my mouth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I call my servant and he does not answer, [though] I implore him with my mouth.


###### Job 19:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:17)


ר֭וּחִי זָ֣רָה לְאִשְׁתִּ֑י וְ֝חַנֹּתִ֗י לִבְנֵ֥י בִטְנִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My breath is repulsive to my wife, and my cries to my own children.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My spirit is strange to my wife, and I implore [(those I have reared in my house as if they were)] the sons of my body.


###### Job 19:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:18)


גַּם־עֲ֭וִילִים מָ֣אֲסוּ בִ֑י אָ֝ק֗וּמָה וַיְדַבְּרוּ־בִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yea, young children despise me; I arise, and they speak against me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Even youths despise me. [Though] I rise [(to honor them)], they speak [contemptuously] of me.


###### Job 19:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:19)


תִּ֭עֲבוּנִי כׇּל־מְתֵ֣י סוֹדִ֑י וְזֶה־אָ֝הַ֗בְתִּי נֶהְפְּכוּ־בִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
All my trusted friends abhor me: and they whom I love are turned against me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All the men of my counsel have abominated me, and these [(the friends)] that I have loved have turned against me.


###### Job 19:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:20)


בְּעוֹרִ֣י וּ֭בִבְשָׂרִי דָּבְקָ֣ה עַצְמִ֑י וָ֝אֶתְמַלְּטָ֗ה בְּע֣וֹר שִׁנָּֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My bone cleaves to my skin and to my flesh, and I am escaped with the skin of my teeth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(I have become so emaciated that)] my bones have stuck to my skin and to my flesh, and I have escaped [only] with the skin of my teeth [i.e., only my gums have escaped boils and worms].


###### Job 19:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:21)


חׇנֻּ֬נִי חׇנֻּ֣נִי אַתֶּ֣ם רֵעָ֑י כִּ֥י יַד־אֱ֝ל֗וֹהַּ נָ֣גְעָה בִּֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Have pity upon me, have pity upon me, O my friends; for the hand of God has touched me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Pity me, pity me, you, my friends, for the hand of G d has struck me.


###### Job 19:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:22)


לָ֭מָּה תִּרְדְּפֻ֣נִי כְמוֹ־אֵ֑ל וּ֝מִבְּשָׂרִ֗י לֹ֣א תִשְׂבָּֽעוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Why do you, like God, persecute me, and are not satisfied with my flesh?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Why do you pursue me as G d [does], and why are you not satisfied with [the afflictions of] my flesh?


###### Job 19:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:23)


מִי־יִתֵּ֣ן אֵ֭פוֹ וְיִכָּתְב֣וּן מִלָּ֑י מִי־יִתֵּ֖ן בַּסֵּ֣פֶר וְיֻחָֽקוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Oh that my words were now written! oh that they were inscribed in a book!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would that my words were written down! Would that they were inscribed in a book!


###### Job 19:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:24)


בְּעֵט־בַּרְזֶ֥ל וְעֹפָ֑רֶת לָ֝עַ֗ד בַּצּ֥וּר יֵחָצְבֽוּן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that with an iron pen and lead they were engraved in the rock for ever!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Would that] with a pen of iron and with lead they were eternally etched in rock!


###### Job 19:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:25)


וַאֲנִ֣י יָ֭דַעְתִּי גֹּ֣אֲלִי חָ֑י וְ֝אַחֲר֗וֹן עַל־עָפָ֥ר יָקֽוּם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For I know that my avenger lives, and that he who outlives all things, will rise when I shall be dust.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(reversion to 22)] I know that my Redeemer lives [(and He will requite you for this)] and He will endure after all [men] upon the earth.


###### Job 19:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:26)


וְאַחַ֣ר ע֭וֹרִי נִקְּפוּ־זֹ֑את וּ֝מִבְּשָׂרִ֗י אֶחֱזֶ֥ה אֱלֽוֹהַּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But whilst I am still in my flesh, though it be after my skin is torn from my body, I would see God:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(But they pay no heed to my Redeemer, but)] after [the afflictions of] my skin, they have thus tormented [me], and from my flesh I see Judgment.


###### Job 19:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:27)


אֲשֶׁ֤ר אֲנִ֨י**׀** אֶחֱזֶה־לִּ֗י וְעֵינַ֣י רָא֣וּ וְלֹא־זָ֑ר כָּל֖וּ כִלְיֹתַ֣י בְּחֵקִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that I might see him for myself: that my eyes might behold, and not another: in longing for that my reins are consumed within me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
What I am aware of within me, and [what] my eyes have seen and not [those of] a stranger is that my kidneys have become consumed in my innards.


###### Job 19:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:28)


כִּ֣י תֹ֭אמְרוּ מַה־נִּרְדׇּף־ל֑וֹ וְשֹׁ֥רֶשׁ דָּ֝בָ֗ר נִמְצָא־בִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if you should say, Do we indeed persecute him? and is not the root of the matter found in me?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would it enter your minds to say, "Why should we pursue him?" and [to attempt to discover] the root of the matter [i.e., the cause of the suffering] that I am undergoing?


###### Job 19:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/19:29)


גּ֤וּרוּ לָכֶ֨ם**׀** מִפְּנֵי־חֶ֗רֶב כִּֽי־חֵ֭מָה עֲוֺנ֣וֹת חָ֑רֶב לְמַ֖עַן תֵּדְע֣וּן (שדין) [שַׁדּֽוּן]׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
be afraid of the sword: for wrath brings the punishments of the sword, that you may know that there is judgment.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Fear you, then, the sword; for [unjustified] wrath is [among] the sins [punishable by] the sword; and you will know that there is judgment [in the world].



## Chapter 20

###### Job 20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:1)


וַ֭יַּעַן צֹפַ֥ר הַֽנַּעֲמָתִ֗י וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then answered Żofar the Na῾amatite, and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Tzofar the Na'amathite raised his voice and said:


###### Job 20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:2)


לָ֭כֵן שְׂעִפַּ֣י יְשִׁיב֑וּנִי וּ֝בַעֲב֗וּר ח֣וּשִׁי בִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore my thoughts answer me, even because of my agitation that is in me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Therefore, my thoughts will make answer for me, for [I have placed] my silence within me.


###### Job 20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:3)


מוּסַ֣ר כְּלִמָּתִ֣י אֶשְׁמָ֑ע וְ֝ר֗וּחַ מִֽבִּינָתִ֥י יַעֲנֵֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I have heard the censure which insults me, and the spirit of my understanding causes me to answer.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I hear [from you] the afflictions of my shame [i.e., your shaming of me], and a spirit from my understanding will answer for me.


###### Job 20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:4)


הֲזֹ֣את יָ֭דַעְתָּ מִנִּי־עַ֑ד מִנִּ֤י שִׂ֖ים אָדָ֣ם עֲלֵי־אָֽרֶץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Knowst thou not this of old, since man was placed upon earth,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Don't you know this always to have been so, from the time man was put upon the earth —


###### Job 20:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:5)


כִּ֤י רִנְנַ֣ת רְ֭שָׁעִים מִקָּר֑וֹב וְשִׂמְחַ֖ת חָנֵ֣ף עֲדֵי־רָֽגַע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that the triumphing of the wicked is short, and the joy of the hypocrite but for a moment?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
that the rejoicing of the wicked is short-lived and the joy of the flatterer but momentary?


###### Job 20:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:6)


אִם־יַעֲלֶ֣ה לַשָּׁמַ֣יִם שִׂיא֑וֹ וְ֝רֹאשׁ֗וֹ לָעָ֥ב יַגִּֽיעַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Though his excellency mount up to the heavens, and his head reach the clouds;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Though his height ascends to the heavens and his head reaches the clouds,


###### Job 20:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:7)


כְּֽ֭גֶלְלוֹ לָנֶ֣צַח יֹאבֵ֑ד רֹ֝אָ֗יו יֹאמְר֥וּ אַיּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
yet he shall perish for ever like his own dung: they who have seen him shall say, Where is he?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
he will go lost eternally, like his dung. The beholders [(of his former success)] will say: "Where is he?"


###### Job 20:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:8)


כַּחֲל֣וֹם יָ֭עוּף וְלֹ֣א יִמְצָא֑וּהוּ וְ֝יֻדַּ֗ד כְּחֶזְי֥וֹן לָֽיְלָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall fly away like a dream, and shall not be found: yea, he shall be chased away like a vision of the night.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As a dream he will vanish and they will not find him, and he will depart as a vision of the night.


###### Job 20:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:9)


עַ֣יִן שְׁ֭זָפַתּוּ וְלֹ֣א תוֹסִ֑יף וְלֹֽא־ע֝֗וֹד תְּשׁוּרֶ֥נּוּ מְקוֹמֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The eye which saw him shall see him no more; nor shall his place any more behold him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The eye that has seen him will not continue [to do so], and [the people of] his place will see him no more.


###### Job 20:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:10)


בָּ֭נָיו יְרַצּ֣וּ דַלִּ֑ים וְ֝יָדָ֗יו תָּשֵׁ֥בְנָה אוֹנֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
His children shall seek to please the poor, and his hands shall give back that which he has robbed.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
His children will conciliate the poor [(whom he has despoiled)] or his [own] hands will return [the plunder of] his strength.


###### Job 20:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:11)


עַ֭צְמוֹתָיו מָלְא֣וּ עֲלוּמָ֑ו וְ֝עִמּ֗וֹ עַל־עָפָ֥ר תִּשְׁכָּֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
His bones are full of his youth; yet shall it lie down with him in the dust.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
His bones are full of [the strength of] his youth, and with him on the dust will it lie [(in his death)].


###### Job 20:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:12)


אִם־תַּמְתִּ֣יק בְּפִ֣יו רָעָ֑ה יַ֝כְחִידֶ֗נָּה תַּ֣חַת לְשֹׁנֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Though wickedness be sweet in his mouth, though he hide it under his tongue;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(This is the way of the wicked one:)] If evil [devising] is sweet in his mouth [(and he is prevented from executing it)], he hides it under his tongue.


###### Job 20:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:13)


יַחְמֹ֣ל עָ֭לֶיהָ וְלֹ֣א יַעַזְבֶ֑נָּה וְ֝יִמְנָעֶ֗נָּה בְּת֣וֹךְ חִכּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
though he spare it, and forsake it not; but keep it still within his mouth:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He is solicitous of it and will not let it go, and he holds it back in his palate.


###### Job 20:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:14)


לַ֭חְמוֹ בְּמֵעָ֣יו נֶהְפָּ֑ךְ מְרוֹרַ֖ת פְּתָנִ֣ים בְּקִרְבּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
yet his food in his bowels is turned, it is the gall of asps within him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(On the day of his downfall)] the food turns around in his stomach — the gall of serpents is within him!


###### Job 20:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:15)


חַ֣יִל בָּ֭לַע וַיְקִאֶ֑נּוּ מִ֝בִּטְנ֗וֹ יֹרִשֶׁ֥נּוּ אֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He has swallowed down riches, and he shall vomit them up again: God shall cast them out of his belly.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[If] he had swallowed wealth, he will regurgitate it; G d will drive it from his stomach


###### Job 20:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:16)


רֹאשׁ־פְּתָנִ֥ים יִינָ֑ק תַּ֝הַרְגֵ֗הוּ לְשׁ֣וֹן אֶפְעֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall suck the poison of asps: the viper’s tongue shall slay him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
as it he had sucked the head of serpents. The tongue of an adder will kill him.


###### Job 20:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:17)


אַל־יֵ֥רֶא בִפְלַגּ֑וֹת נַהֲרֵ֥י נַ֝חֲלֵ֗י דְּבַ֣שׁ וְחֶמְאָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall not see the rivers, the floods, the streams of honey and curd.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He will not see the streams [of Gan Eden], the flowing rivers of honey and butter.


###### Job 20:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:18)


מֵשִׁ֣יב יָ֭גָע וְלֹ֣א יִבְלָ֑ע כְּחֵ֥יל תְּ֝מוּרָת֗וֹ וְלֹ֣א יַעֲלֹֽס׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
That which he laboured for he shall restore, and shall not swallow it down: from the riches of his trading he will have no enjoyment.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He will return the toil [of the poor, that he has stolen], and he will not retain it. When his booty reaches its height, [he will be undone and] he will not rejoice [in it].


###### Job 20:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:19)


כִּֽי־רִ֭צַּץ עָזַ֣ב דַּלִּ֑ים בַּ֥יִת גָּ֝זַ֗ל וְלֹ֣א יִבְנֵֽהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Because he has oppressed and has forsaken the poor; he has violently taken away a house which he did not build;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
After having broken the poor, he left [them] (and was "dispatched" from the world, and they come and claim their property)]. He stole a house, but he will not build [i.e., inhabit] it.


###### Job 20:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:20)


כִּ֤י**׀** לֹא־יָדַ֣ע שָׁלֵ֣ו בְּבִטְנ֑וֹ בַּ֝חֲמוּד֗וֹ לֹ֣א יְמַלֵּֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
because his greed knew no rest, he will not save anything over of that which he coveted.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Because his stomach knew no peace, [(always desiring more theft to fill it)], therefore, he will not escape with his "embonpoint" [(but will wither away)].


###### Job 20:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:21)


אֵין־שָׂרִ֥יד לְאׇכְל֑וֹ עַל־כֵּ֝֗ן לֹא־יָחִ֥יל טוּבֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
None of his food shall be left; therefore shall his goods not prosper.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He left nothing over from his meal [(for the poor)]; therefore, his good will not prosper.


###### Job 20:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:22)


בִּמְלֹ֣אות שִׂ֭פְקוֹ יֵ֣צֶר ל֑וֹ כׇּל־יַ֖ד עָמֵ֣ל תְּבֹאֶֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In the fullness of his sufficiency he shall be in straits: all the force of misery shall come upon him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When he attains to the fulfillment of his desire, he will be aggrieved. Every laboring hand [i.e., every poor man whom he robbed] will come against him.


###### Job 20:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:23)


יְהִ֤י**׀** לְמַלֵּ֬א בִטְנ֗וֹ יְֽשַׁלַּח־בּ֭וֹ חֲר֣וֹן אַפּ֑וֹ וְיַמְטֵ֥ר עָ֝לֵ֗ימוֹ בִּלְחוּמֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When he is about to fill his belly, God shall cast the fury of his wrath upon him, and shall rain it upon him into his flesh.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[His wrath] will be [sufficient] to fill his stomach. He will vent the fury of His wrath upon him, and He will rain down [fire and brimstone] upon him in warring with him.


###### Job 20:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:24)


יִ֭בְרַח מִנֵּ֣שֶׁק בַּרְזֶ֑ל תַּ֝חְלְפֵ֗הוּ קֶ֣שֶׁת נְחוּשָֽׁה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall flee from the iron weapon, and the bow of brass shall strike him through.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When he runs from iron weapons, a copper bow will pierce him through.


###### Job 20:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:25)


שָׁלַף֮ וַיֵּצֵ֢א מִגֵּ֫וָ֥ה וּ֭בָרָק מִֽמְּרֹרָת֥וֹ יַהֲלֹ֗ךְ עָלָ֥יו אֵמִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It is drawn, and comes out of his body; yea, the glittering blade comes out of his gall: terrors are upon him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When [the sword] is drawn and it leaves its scabbard, its flash, in its awesomeness, will cast terrors upon him.


###### Job 20:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:26)


כׇּל־חֹשֶׁךְ֮ טָמ֢וּן לִצְפּ֫וּנָ֥יו תְּ֭אׇכְלֵהוּ אֵ֣שׁ לֹא־נֻפָּ֑ח יֵ֖רַע שָׂרִ֣יד בְּאׇהֳלֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Utter darkness is laid up for his treasures: a fire not blown shall consume him; it shall go ill with him that is left in his tent.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All [forms of] darkness are hidden for what lies in store for him. He will be consumed by an unfanned fire [(the fire of Gehinnom)]. Evil will befall what remains in his tent.


###### Job 20:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:27)


יְגַלּ֣וּ שָׁמַ֣יִם עֲוֺנ֑וֹ וְ֝אֶ֗רֶץ מִתְקוֹמָ֘מָ֥ה לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The heaven shall reveal his iniquity; and the earth shall rise up against him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The heavens will reveal his transgression, and the earth will rise up against him.


###### Job 20:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:28)


יִ֭גֶל יְב֣וּל בֵּית֑וֹ נִ֝גָּר֗וֹת בְּי֣וֹם אַפּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The increase of his house shall depart, and his goods shall flow away in the day of his wrath.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The produce of his house will go lost. [His treasures will be] swept away on the day of His wrath.


###### Job 20:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/20:29)


זֶ֤ה**׀** חֵלֶק־אָדָ֣ם רָ֭שָׁע מֵאֱלֹהִ֑ים וְנַחֲלַ֖ת אִמְר֣וֹ מֵאֵֽל׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
This is the portion of a wicked man from God, and the heritage appointed to him by God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
This is the portion of the wicked man and the inheritance foretold for him by the Almighty.



## Chapter 21

###### Job 21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:1)


וַיַּ֥עַן אִיּ֗וֹב וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Iyyov answered and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Iyyov raised his voice and said:


###### Job 21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:2)


שִׁמְע֣וּ שָׁ֭מוֹעַ מִלָּתִ֑י וּתְהִי־זֹ֝֗את תַּנְח֥וּמֹֽתֵיכֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hear diligently my speech, and let this be your consolations.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Hear, now, my words, and let this [(your listening)] be your consolation [to me].


###### Job 21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:3)


שָׂ֭אוּנִי וְאָנֹכִ֣י אֲדַבֵּ֑ר וְאַחַ֖ר דַּבְּרִ֣י תַלְעִֽיג׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Suffer me that I may speak; and after I have spoken, mock on.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Bear with me, and I will speak; and after I speak, you may mock.


###### Job 21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:4)


הֶ֭אָנֹכִי לְאָדָ֣ם שִׂיחִ֑י וְאִם־מַ֝דּ֗וּעַ לֹֽא־תִקְצַ֥ר רוּחִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As for me, is my complaint to man? why should I not be impatient?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Is it to a man [like myself] that I am speaking [(that he should hear my plaint)]? Why, then, should my spirit not be short [(from crying out in my affliction)]?


###### Job 21:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:5)


פְּנוּ־אֵלַ֥י וְהָשַׁ֑מּוּ וְשִׂ֖ימוּ יָ֣ד עַל־פֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Turn to me, and be astonished, and lay your hand upon your mouth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Turn to me [to hear] and be amazed, and put your hand upon your mouth [(for you will not be able to answer)].


###### Job 21:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:6)


וְאִם־זָכַ֥רְתִּי וְנִבְהָ֑לְתִּי וְאָחַ֥ז בְּ֝שָׂרִ֗י פַּלָּצֽוּת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Even when I remember, I am afraid, and trembling takes holds of my flesh.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Now that I remember [what] I wish to ask viz. verse 7)], I am confounded, and my flesh is seized with trembling.


###### Job 21:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:7)


מַ֭דּוּעַ רְשָׁעִ֣ים יִֽחְי֑וּ עָ֝תְק֗וּ גַּם־גָּ֥בְרוּ חָֽיִל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Why do the wicked live, become old, yea, grow mighty in power?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Why should the wicked live, grow old, and increase their wealth?


###### Job 21:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:8)


זַרְעָ֤ם נָכ֣וֹן לִפְנֵיהֶ֣ם עִמָּ֑ם וְ֝צֶאֱצָאֵיהֶ֗ם לְעֵינֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Their seed is established in their sight with them, and their offspring before their eyes.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Their children are well established before them, together with them, and their offspring before their eyes.


###### Job 21:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:9)


בָּתֵּיהֶ֣ם שָׁל֣וֹם מִפָּ֑חַד וְלֹ֤א שֵׁ֖בֶט אֱל֣וֹהַּ עֲלֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Their houses are safe without fear, nor is the rod of God upon them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Their houses are at peace from fear and the rod of the L rd has not come against them.


###### Job 21:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:10)


שׁוֹר֣וֹ עִ֭בַּר וְלֹ֣א יַגְעִ֑ל תְּפַלֵּ֥ט פָּ֝רָת֗וֹ וְלֹ֣א תְשַׁכֵּֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Their bull genders, and does not fail; their cow calves, and does not cast her calf.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
His bull inseminates [the cow] and there is no rejection [(of the sperm without conception)]; his cow emits [the fetus] and does not miscarry.


###### Job 21:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:11)


יְשַׁלְּח֣וּ כַ֭צֹּאן עֲוִילֵיהֶ֑ם וְ֝יַלְדֵיהֶ֗ם יְרַקֵּדֽוּן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They send forth their little ones like a flock, and their children dance.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They send their young ones forth like sheep, and their children dance about.


###### Job 21:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:12)


יִ֭שְׂאוּ כְּתֹ֣ף וְכִנּ֑וֹר וְ֝יִשְׂמְח֗וּ לְק֣וֹל עוּגָֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They take the timbrel and lyre, and rejoice at the sound of the pipe.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They sound the timbrel and the lyre and they rejoice to the sound of the pipe.


###### Job 21:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:13)


(יבלו) [יְכַלּ֣וּ] בַטּ֣וֹב יְמֵיהֶ֑ם וּ֝בְרֶ֗גַע שְׁא֣וֹל יֵחָֽתּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They spend their days in wealth, and in a moment go down to She᾽ol.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They spend their days in good, and [(when their day of death arrives)], in a moment they descend to the pit [(without afflictions)].


###### Job 21:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:14)


וַיֹּאמְר֣וּ לָ֭אֵל ס֣וּר מִמֶּ֑נּוּ וְדַ֥עַת דְּ֝רָכֶ֗יךָ לֹ֣א חָפָֽצְנוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore they say to God, Depart from us; for we desire not the knowledge of Thy ways.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they say to G d: "Get away from us; we do not want to know Your ways."


###### Job 21:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:15)


מַה־שַּׁדַּ֥י כִּֽי־נַעַבְדֶ֑נּוּ וּמַה־נּ֝וֹעִ֗יל כִּ֣י נִפְגַּע־בּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
What is the Almighty, that we should serve him? and what profit should we have, if we pray to him?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
"What is the Almighty that we should serve Him, and what will it avail us if we pray to Him?" [i.e., we have no need of Him]


###### Job 21:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:16)


הֵ֤ן לֹ֣א בְיָדָ֣ם טוּבָ֑ם עֲצַ֥ת רְ֝שָׁעִ֗ים רָ֣חֲקָה מֶֽנִּי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Lo, is their good not in their hand? let the counsel of the wicked be far from me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Is their good not in their hands? — But the counsel of the wicked is far from me [(and I will not emulate their ways!)]


###### Job 21:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:17)


כַּמָּ֤ה**׀** נֵר־רְשָׁ֘עִ֤ים יִדְעָ֗ךְ וְיָבֹ֣א עָלֵ֣ימוֹ אֵידָ֑ם חֲ֝בָלִ֗ים יְחַלֵּ֥ק בְּאַפּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
How often is the candle of the wicked put out? and when does their calamity come upon them? does he distribute sorrows to them in his anger?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(reversion to verse 7)] How long [will this persist]? Let the lamp of the wicked be quenched and their doom come down upon them! Let Him give them [their] lot in His wrath!


###### Job 21:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:18)


יִֽהְי֗וּ כְּתֶ֥בֶן לִפְנֵי־ר֑וּחַ וּ֝כְמֹ֗ץ גְּנָבַ֥תּוּ סוּפָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
are they as stubble before the wind, and as chaff that the storm carries away?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Let them be as stubble in the wind and as chaff stolen by the tempest.


###### Job 21:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:19)


אֱל֗וֹהַּ יִצְפֹּן־לְבָנָ֥יו אוֹנ֑וֹ יְשַׁלֵּ֖ם אֵלָ֣יו וְיֵדָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
God lays up his iniquity for the children: let him rather reward him, that he may know it!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Should G d lay away [punishment for] his wickedness with his sons [(and he die in peace?)] Let it [(his punishment)] be paid to him [directly], and let [the world] know [that G d judges men].


###### Job 21:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:20)


יִרְא֣וּ עֵינָ֣ו כִּיד֑וֹ וּמֵחֲמַ֖ת שַׁדַּ֣י יִשְׁתֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let his eyes see his own destruction, and let him drink of the wrath of the Almighty!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Let his eyes see his [own] destruction, and let him [(himself)] drink of the fury of the Almighty!


###### Job 21:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:21)


כִּ֤י מַה־חֶפְצ֣וֹ בְּבֵית֣וֹ אַחֲרָ֑יו וּמִסְפַּ֖ר חֳדָשָׁ֣יו חֻצָּֽצוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For what pleasure has he in his house after him, when the number of his months is already complete?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For what care should he take of his household after him, seeing that the allotment of his months has been realized [i.e., seeing that he has lived his full life span]?


###### Job 21:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:22)


הַלְאֵ֥ל יְלַמֶּד־דָּ֑עַת וְ֝ה֗וּא רָמִ֥ים יִשְׁפּֽוֹט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Shall any teach God knowledge? seeing he judges those who are high.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
