Should one [(i.e., should I)] teach G d knowledge [(to judge truly)]? — But He judges [in His] exaltedness [(and pays no heed to "mundane" considerations)].


###### Job 21:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:23)


זֶ֗ה יָ֭מוּת בְּעֶ֣צֶם תֻּמּ֑וֹ כֻּ֝לּ֗וֹ שַׁלְאֲנַ֥ן וְשָׁלֵֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
One dies in his full strength, being wholly at ease and quiet.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
This [wicked one] dies at the height of fulfillment, all calm and peaceful.


###### Job 21:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:24)


עֲ֭טִינָיו מָלְא֣וּ חָלָ֑ב וּמֹ֖חַ עַצְמוֹתָ֣יו יְשֻׁקֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
His vessels are full of milk, and his bones are moistened with marrow.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
His receptacles are full of milk and the marrow of his bones [is fully] irrigated.


###### Job 21:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:25)


וְזֶ֗ה יָ֭מוּת בְּנֶ֣פֶשׁ מָרָ֑ה וְלֹֽא־אָ֝כַ֗ל בַּטּוֹבָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And another dies in the bitterness of his soul, and has never eaten of good things.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And this [righteous] one dies with a bitter spirit, never having tasted good.


###### Job 21:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:26)


יַ֭חַד עַל־עָפָ֣ר יִשְׁכָּ֑בוּ וְ֝רִמָּ֗ה תְּכַסֶּ֥ה עֲלֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They shall lie down alike in the dust, and the worm shall cover them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They lie together in the ground, and the worm covers them up [(and none can tell the difference between them)].


###### Job 21:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:27)


הֵ֣ן יָ֭דַעְתִּי מַחְשְׁבֽוֹתֵיכֶ֑ם וּ֝מְזִמּ֗וֹת עָלַ֥י תַּחְמֹֽסוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, I know your thoughts, and the devices which you wrongfully imagine against me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Now I know your thoughts and your evil devisings against me.


###### Job 21:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:28)


כִּ֤י תֹאמְר֗וּ אַיֵּ֥ה בֵית־נָדִ֑יב וְ֝אַיֵּ֗ה אֹ֤הֶל**׀** מִשְׁכְּנ֬וֹת רְשָׁעִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For you say, Where is the house of the prince? and where is the tent in which the wicked dwelt?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For you would say: "Where is the house of the [(formerly)] lordly one, [i.e., Iyyov], and where is the tent-dwelling of the wicked?"


###### Job 21:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:29)


הֲלֹ֣א שְׁ֭אֶלְתֶּם ע֣וֹבְרֵי דָ֑רֶךְ וְ֝אֹתֹתָ֗ם לֹ֣א תְנַכֵּֽרוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Have you not asked those who go by the way? and do not falsify their tokens;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Have you not inquired of the passers-by, whose signs you do not deny?


###### Job 21:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:30)


כִּ֤י לְי֣וֹם אֵ֭יד יֵחָ֣שֶׂךְ רָ֑ע לְי֖וֹם עֲבָר֣וֹת יוּבָֽלוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that the wicked man is reserved for the day of destruction; that they shall be led forth to the day of wrath.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[And have you not been told] that for the day of [his] destruction evil is withheld from the wicked one, for the day when wrath will be brought [down upon him]?


###### Job 21:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:31)


מִֽי־יַגִּ֣יד עַל־פָּנָ֣יו דַּרְכּ֑וֹ וְהוּא־עָ֝שָׂ֗ה מִ֣י יְשַׁלֶּם־לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But who shall declare his way to his face? and who shall repay him for what he has done?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who will [dare] tell [i.e., dispute] His way to His face? Once He has done, who shall requite Him,


###### Job 21:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:32)


וְ֭הוּא לִקְבָר֣וֹת יוּבָ֑ל וְֽעַל־גָּדִ֥ישׁ יִשְׁקֽוֹד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For he is brought to the grave, and watch is kept over his tomb.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
when he [(the one who would do so)] will be brought to the grave and will be hastened to the mound?


###### Job 21:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:33)


מָֽתְקוּ־ל֗וֹ רִגְבֵ֫י־נָ֥חַל וְ֭אַחֲרָיו כׇּל־אָדָ֣ם יִמְשׁ֑וֹךְ וּ֝לְפָנָ֗יו אֵ֣ין מִסְפָּֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The clods of the valley shall be sweet to him, and every man shall draw after him, and there are innumerable going before him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The river clods will be sweet to him, and all men will trail after him [to death] as [all men] before Him, without number.


###### Job 21:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/21:34)


וְ֭אֵיךְ תְּנַחֲמ֣וּנִי הָ֑בֶל וּ֝תְשׁ֥וּבֹתֵיכֶ֗ם נִשְׁאַר־מָֽעַל׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Why then do you comfort me in vain, seeing in your answers there remains falsehood?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And why would you console me with vanity? And your answers remain deceit!



## Chapter 22

###### Job 22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:1)


וַ֭יַּעַן אֱלִיפַ֥ז הַֽתֵּמָנִ֗י וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Elifaz the Temanite answered and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Elifaz the Temanite raised his voice and said:


###### Job 22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:2)


הַלְאֵ֥ל יִסְכׇּן־גָּ֑בֶר כִּֽי־יִסְכֹּ֖ן עָלֵ֣ימוֹ מַשְׂכִּֽיל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Can a man be profitable to God? Surely he that is wise is profitable to himself!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Does a man benefit G d when he teaches wisdom concerning them [(men)]?


###### Job 22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:3)


הַחֵ֣פֶץ לְ֭שַׁדַּי כִּ֣י תִצְדָּ֑ק וְאִם־בֶּ֝֗צַע כִּֽי־תַתֵּ֥ם דְּרָכֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Is it any pleasure to the Almighty, that thou art righteous? or is it gain to him, that thou makest thy ways perfect?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Does the Almighty need your vindication [(in dispute with Him)]? Does He profit in the perfection of your ways?


###### Job 22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:4)


הֲֽ֭מִיִּרְאָ֣תְךָ יֹכִיחֶ֑ךָ יָב֥וֹא עִ֝מְּךָ֗ בַּמִּשְׁפָּֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
will he reprove thee for fear of thee? will he enter with thee into judgment?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Is it from fear of you that He should debate you, that He should enter into judgment with you?


###### Job 22:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:5)


הֲלֹ֣א רָעָתְךָ֣ רַבָּ֑ה וְאֵֽין־קֵ֝֗ץ לַעֲוֺנֹתֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Is not thy wickedness great? and thy iniquities infinite?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Does He not [know that] your evil is great, that there is no end to your sins?


###### Job 22:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:6)


כִּֽי־תַחְבֹּ֣ל אַחֶ֣יךָ חִנָּ֑ם וּבִגְדֵ֖י עֲרוּמִּ֣ים תַּפְשִֽׁיט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For thou hast taken pledges from thy brother for nought, and stripped the naked of their clothing.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For you have demanded security of your brothers — for nought — and you have stripped the naked of their clothing [i.e., you have taken their only garment so that they are naked].


###### Job 22:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:7)


לֹא־מַ֭יִם עָיֵ֣ף תַּשְׁקֶ֑ה וּ֝מֵרָעֵ֗ב תִּֽמְנַֽע־לָֽחֶם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou has not given water to the weary to drink, and thou hast withheld bread from the hungry.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You did not give water to the weary, and you withheld bread from the hungry.


###### Job 22:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:8)


וְאִ֣ישׁ זְ֭רוֹעַ ל֣וֹ הָאָ֑רֶץ וּנְשׂ֥וּא פָ֝נִ֗ים יֵ֣שֶׁב בָּֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But like the mighty man, who has the earth; and the honourable man who dwells in it,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Should a [(once)] mighty man [(i.e., you)] inherit the earth? Should a [(former)] dignitary endure in it?


###### Job 22:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:9)


אַ֭לְמָנוֹת שִׁלַּ֣חְתָּ רֵיקָ֑ם וּזְרֹע֖וֹת יְתֹמִ֣ים יְדֻכָּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
thou hast sent widows away empty, and the arms of the fatherless have been broken.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You drove out widows empty-handed and you [(thus)] crushed the arms of the orphans.


###### Job 22:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:10)


עַל־כֵּ֭ן סְבִיבוֹתֶ֣יךָ פַחִ֑ים וִ֝יבַהֶלְךָ֗ פַּ֣חַד פִּתְאֹֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore snares are round about thee, and sudden fear troubles thee;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
That is why there are traps around you and sudden fear confounds you.


###### Job 22:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:11)


אוֹ־חֹ֥שֶׁךְ לֹֽא־תִרְאֶ֑ה וְֽשִׁפְעַת־מַ֥יִם תְּכַסֶּֽךָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
or darkness, that thou canst not see; and abundance of waters cover thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Or [would you think that your deeds were done] in darkness [(and not observed by G d)]? Do not see [it so]! Or [would you think that] an abundance of water [(in the heavens)] conceals you?


###### Job 22:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:12)


הֲֽלֹא־אֱ֭לוֹהַּ גֹּ֣בַהּ שָׁמָ֑יִם וּרְאֵ֤ה רֹ֖אשׁ כּוֹכָבִ֣ים כִּי־רָֽמּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Is not God in the height of heaven? and behold the height of the stars, how high they are!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Would you say:] "Is not G d in the heights of the heavens, viewing the tops of the stars in their height?"


###### Job 22:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:13)


וְֽ֭אָמַרְתָּ מַה־יָּ֣דַֽע אֵ֑ל הַבְעַ֖ד עֲרָפֶ֣ל יִשְׁפּֽוֹט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou sayst, What does God know? does he judge through the dark cloud?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
wherefore you say: "What can G d know [of what transpires below]?


###### Job 22:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:14)


עָבִ֣ים סֵֽתֶר־ל֭וֹ וְלֹ֣א יִרְאֶ֑ה וְח֥וּג שָׁ֝מַ֗יִם יִתְהַלָּֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thick clouds are a covering to him, so that he sees not; and he walks in the circuit of heaven.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
"Do the clouds not hide things from Him and keep Him from seeing? Does He not walk upon the arc of the heavens?"


###### Job 22:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:15)


הַאֹ֣רַח עוֹלָ֣ם תִּשְׁמ֑וֹר אֲשֶׁ֖ר דָּרְכ֣וּ מְתֵי־אָֽוֶן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hast thou marked the old way which wicked men have trodden?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do you remember the way of yore trodden by men of iniquity,


###### Job 22:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:16)


אֲשֶֽׁר־קֻמְּט֥וּ וְלֹא־עֵ֑ת נָ֝הָ֗ר יוּצַ֥ק יְסוֹדָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who were cut down out of time, whose foundation was overflown with a flood:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
who were cut off [from the world] before their time, a flood [(that of Noach or the fire and brimstone of Sodom)] inundating their foundations,


###### Job 22:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:17)


הָאֹמְרִ֣ים לָ֭אֵל ס֣וּר מִמֶּ֑נּוּ וּמַה־יִּפְעַ֖ל שַׁדַּ֣י לָֽמוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who said to God, Depart from us: and what can the Almighty do for them?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
who said of G d: "He has removed Himself from us," and [(who questioned)] what the Almighty could do to them?


###### Job 22:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:18)


וְה֤וּא מִלֵּ֣א בָתֵּיהֶ֣ם ט֑וֹב וַעֲצַ֥ת רְ֝שָׁעִ֗ים רָ֣חֲקָה מֶֽנִּי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yet he filled their houses with good things: but the counsel of the wicked is far from me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
— when He had filled their houses with good! — But the counsel of the wicked is far from me.


###### Job 22:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:19)


יִרְא֣וּ צַדִּיקִ֣ים וְיִשְׂמָ֑חוּ וְ֝נָקִ֗י יִלְעַג־לָֽמוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The righteous see it, and are glad: and the innocent laugh them to scorn, saying,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Let the righteous see [the downfall of the wicked] and rejoice, and let the clean one mock them.


###### Job 22:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:20)


אִם־לֹ֣א נִכְחַ֣ד קִימָ֑נוּ וְ֝יִתְרָ֗ם אָ֣כְלָה אֵֽשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Surely our adversaries are cut off, and the remnant of them the fire consumes.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(reversion to verse 15)] Was its [(that generation's)] height not cut off and their pride consumed in fire?


###### Job 22:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:21)


הַסְכֶּן־נָ֣א עִמּ֣וֹ וּשְׁלָ֑ם בָּ֝הֶ֗ם תְּֽבוֹאַתְךָ֥ טוֹבָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Acquaint now thyself with him, and be at peace: thereby good shall come to thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Learn, then, [to be wholehearted] with Him, and you will be at peace. Through them [(these things)] good will come to you.


###### Job 22:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:22)


קַח־נָ֣א מִפִּ֣יו תּוֹרָ֑ה וְשִׂ֥ים אֲ֝מָרָ֗יו בִּלְבָבֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Receive, I pray thee, Tora from his mouth, and lay up his words in thy heart.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Take, now, Torah from His mouth, and place His words in your heart.


###### Job 22:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:23)


אִם־תָּשׁ֣וּב עַד־שַׁ֭דַּי תִּבָּנֶ֑ה תַּֽרְחִ֥יק עַ֝וְלָ֗ה מֵאׇהֳלֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If thou return to the Almighty, thou shalt be built up, thou shalt put away iniquity far from thy tent.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When you return to the Almighty you will be rebuilt — but remove iniquity from your tents.


###### Job 22:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:24)


וְשִׁית־עַל־עָפָ֥ר בָּ֑צֶר וּכְצ֖וּר נְחָלִ֣ים אוֹפִֽיר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt hold gold as dust, and the gold of Ofir as the stones of the brooks.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you will store up [wealth] upon the earth, and in the valley-rock [the gold of] Ophir.


###### Job 22:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:25)


וְהָיָ֣ה שַׁדַּ֣י בְּצָרֶ֑יךָ וְכֶ֖סֶף תּוֹעָפ֣וֹת לָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yea, the Almighty shall be thy gold, and precious silver to thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And your stored-up wealth will be great, and hoards of silver will be yours.


###### Job 22:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:26)


כִּי־אָ֭ז עַל־שַׁדַּ֣י תִּתְעַנָּ֑ג וְתִשָּׂ֖א אֶל־אֱל֣וֹהַּ פָּנֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For then shalt thou have thy delight in the Almighty, and shall lift up thy face to God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then you will find pleasure in the Almighty and you will lift your face [(without shame) in supplication] to the L rd.


###### Job 22:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:27)


תַּעְתִּ֣יר אֵ֭לָיו וְיִשְׁמָעֶ֑ךָּ וּנְדָרֶ֥יךָ תְשַׁלֵּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt make thy prayer to him, and he shall hear thee, and thou shalt pay thy vows.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You will pray to Him and He will hear you, and you will pay your vows [(i.e., your offerings in fulfillment of your vows will be acceptable to Him.)]


###### Job 22:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:28)


וְֽתִגְזַר־אֹ֭מֶר וְיָ֣קׇם־לָ֑ךְ וְעַל־דְּ֝רָכֶ֗יךָ נָ֣גַהּ אֽוֹר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt also decree a thing, and it shall be established unto thee: and the light shall shine upon thy ways.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you will make a resolution and it will avail you, and on your ways light will shine.


###### Job 22:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:29)


כִּֽי־הִ֭שְׁפִּילוּ וַתֹּ֣אמֶר גֵּוָ֑ה וְשַׁ֖ח עֵינַ֣יִם יוֹשִֽׁעַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When men have humbled thee, and thou sayst, There is lifting up, then he shall save the humble person.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If they [(the men of your generation)] are lowly, by your word [they will be] upraised, and one whose eyes are lowered [in suffering for his sin], He will save [by your intercession].


###### Job 22:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/22:30)


יְמַלֵּ֥ט אִֽי־נָקִ֑י וְ֝נִמְלַ֗ט בְּבֹ֣ר כַּפֶּֽיךָ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He delivers the innocent man: and thou shalt be delivered by the pureness of thy hands.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He will deliver him who is unclean, and he will be saved, through the cleanliness of your hands.



## Chapter 23

###### Job 23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:1)


וַיַּ֥עַן אִיּ֗וֹב וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Iyyov answered and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Iyyov raised his voice and said:


###### Job 23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:2)


גַּם־הַ֭יּוֹם מְרִ֣י שִׂחִ֑י יָ֝דִ֗י כָּבְדָ֥ה עַל־אַנְחָתִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Even today my complaint is bitter: my stroke is heavy because of my groaning.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Today, too, [(after all these consolations)], my plaint is bitter, [for] my wound is greater than my sigh.


###### Job 23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:3)


מִֽי־יִתֵּ֣ן יָ֭דַעְתִּי וְאֶמְצָאֵ֑הוּ אָ֝ב֗וֹא עַד־תְּכוּנָתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Oh that I knew where I might find him! that I might come even to his seat!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would that I knew where to find Him; I would come to His abode.


###### Job 23:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:4)


אֶעֶרְכָ֣ה לְפָנָ֣יו מִשְׁפָּ֑ט וּ֝פִ֗י אֲמַלֵּ֥א תוֹכָחֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I would order my cause before him, and fill my mouth with arguments.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I would arrange my judgment [i.e., my plea] before Him, and I would fill my mouth with remonstrance.


###### Job 23:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:5)


אֵ֭דְעָה מִלִּ֣ים יַעֲנֵ֑נִי וְ֝אָבִ֗ינָה מַה־יֹּ֥אמַר לִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I would know the words which he would answer me, and understand what he would say to me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(And even if He would not allow me to speak, perhaps He would speak to me and)] I would know [with what] words He would answer me, and I would understand what He would tell me.


###### Job 23:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:6)


הַבְּרׇב־כֹּ֭חַ יָרִ֣יב עִמָּדִ֑י לֹ֥א אַךְ־ה֝֗וּא יָשִׂ֥ם בִּֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Would he contend with me in his great power? No; but he would give heed to me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would He contend with me with overpowering force? No, only [the "quarrel" of my sins (and not that of false libels)] would He wage with me.


###### Job 23:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:7)


שָׁ֗ם יָ֭שָׁר נוֹכָ֣ח עִמּ֑וֹ וַאֲפַלְּטָ֥ה לָ֝נֶ֗צַח מִשֹּׁפְטִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There the upright might reason with him; so should I be delivered for ever from my judge.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There [(in the place of our quarrel)] my uprightness will be apparent before Him, and I will escape forever from My judge.


###### Job 23:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:8)


הֵ֤ן קֶ֣דֶם אֶהֱלֹ֣ךְ וְאֵינֶ֑נּוּ וְ֝אָח֗וֹר וְֽלֹא־אָבִ֥ין לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, I go forward, but he is not there; and backward, but I cannot perceive him:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(reversion to verse 3)] But if I go east, He is not there, and if west, I cannot apprehend Him.


###### Job 23:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:9)


שְׂמֹ֣אול בַּעֲשֹׂת֣וֹ וְלֹא־אָ֑חַז יַעְטֹ֥ף יָ֝מִ֗ין וְלֹ֣א אֶרְאֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
on the left hand, where he works, but I cannot behold him: he hides himself on the right hand, that I cannot see him:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When He made the north, I did not see [Him (i.e., He did not establish His abode there)]. He has concealed the south [from me] and I do not see [Him there].


###### Job 23:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:10)


כִּֽי־יָ֭דַע דֶּ֣רֶךְ עִמָּדִ֑י בְּ֝חָנַ֗נִי כַּזָּהָ֥ב אֵצֵֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for he knows the way that I take: when he has tried me, I shall come forth like gold.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For He knows the [uprightness of the] way I have trod; He has examined me, and I have come out as gold [(Therefore, He is "avoiding" me)].


###### Job 23:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:11)


בַּ֭אֲשֻׁרוֹ אָחֲזָ֣ה רַגְלִ֑י דַּרְכּ֖וֹ שָׁמַ֣רְתִּי וְלֹא־אָֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My foot has held fast to his steps; his way have I kept, and not turned aside.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My foot has held to His path; I have heeded His way and not strayed [from it].


###### Job 23:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:12)


מִצְוַ֣ת שְׂ֭פָתָיו וְלֹ֣א אָמִ֑ישׁ מֵ֝חֻקִּ֗י צָפַ֥נְתִּי אִמְרֵי־פִֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Nor have I gone back from the commandment of his lips, I have treasured the words of his mouth more than my necessary food.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[I have heeded] the commandment of His lips and I have not cast it aside. More than my daily bread have I treasured the words of His mouth.


###### Job 23:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:13)


וְה֣וּא בְ֭אֶחָד וּמִ֣י יְשִׁיבֶ֑נּוּ וְנַפְשׁ֖וֹ אִוְּתָ֣ה וַיָּֽעַשׂ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But he is unchangeable, and who can turn him? and what his soul desires, even that he does.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But He is one [(in the world and knows the thoughts of all men)], so who can answer Him? [Therefore,] whatever He wills, He does.


###### Job 23:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:14)


כִּ֭י יַשְׁלִ֣ים חֻקִּ֑י וְכָהֵ֖נָּה רַבּ֣וֹת עִמּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For he performs the thing that is appointed for me and many such things are with him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[I know] that [He will not leave off until] He completes my "allotment" of afflictions], for many such things are with Him [i.e., for He has often wrought thus].


###### Job 23:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:15)


עַל־כֵּ֭ן מִפָּנָ֣יו אֶבָּהֵ֑ל אֶ֝תְבּוֹנֵ֗ן וְאֶפְחַ֥ד מִמֶּֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore am I terrified at his presence: when I consider, I am afraid of him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Therefore, [(because He does not recompense a man according to his ways)], I am confounded before Him. I reflect [upon His ways] and am affrighted of Him.


###### Job 23:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:16)


וְ֭אֵל הֵרַ֣ךְ לִבִּ֑י וְ֝שַׁדַּ֗י הִבְהִילָֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For God makes my heart faint, and the Almighty has affrighted me:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
G d has caused my heart to quake. The Almighty has confounded me


###### Job 23:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/23:17)


כִּֽי־לֹ֣א נִ֭צְמַתִּי מִפְּנֵי־חֹ֑שֶׁךְ וּ֝מִפָּנַ֗י כִּסָּה־אֹֽפֶל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
because I was not cut off before the darkness, nor did he cover the thick darkness from my face.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
in that I have not been cut off [(by death)] before the [(onset of the)] darkness, and [in that] the darkness was [not] concealed from me [before my death].



## Chapter 24

###### Job 24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:1)


מַדּ֗וּעַ מִ֭שַּׁדַּי לֹא־נִצְפְּנ֣וּ עִתִּ֑ים וְ֝יֹדְעָ֗ו לֹא־חָ֥זוּ יָמָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Why are not times of judgment treasured up by the Almighty? and why do those who know him never see his days?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Why are the times [i.e., the time of a man's death] not hidden from the Almighty [(so that he could die before punishment overtook him)]? And [why] do His knowers not see [i.e., not know] His ways [i.e., Why can they not account for His ways (as in the following:)]?


###### Job 24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:2)


גְּבֻל֥וֹת יַשִּׂ֑יגוּ עֵ֥דֶר גָּ֝זְל֗וּ וַיִּרְעֽוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Some remove the landmarks; they violently take away flocks, and feed them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They [the evildoers] annex the boundaries [of their neighbors]. They steal the flock and graze it.


###### Job 24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:3)


חֲמ֣וֹר יְתוֹמִ֣ים יִנְהָ֑גוּ יַ֝חְבְּל֗וּ שׁ֣וֹר אַלְמָנָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They drive away the ass of the fatherless, they take the widow’s ox for a pledge.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They lead away the ass of the orphans. They take the widow's ox as a pledge.


###### Job 24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:4)


יַטּ֣וּ אֶבְיֹנִ֣ים מִדָּ֑רֶךְ יַ֥חַד חֻ֝בְּא֗וּ עֲנִיֵּי־אָֽרֶץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They turn the needy out of the way: the poor of the earth hide themselves together;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They repel the paupers from the road; the poor of the earth [(in fear of them)] hide together [from them].


###### Job 24:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:5)


הֵ֤ן פְּרָאִ֨ים**׀** בַּ֥מִּדְבָּ֗ר יָצְא֣וּ בְּ֭פׇעֳלָם מְשַׁחֲרֵ֣י לַטָּ֑רֶף עֲרָבָ֥ה ל֥וֹ לֶ֝֗חֶם לַנְּעָרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
behold, like wild asses in the desert, they go forth to their work; rising betimes to work for food: the wilderness yields food for them for their children;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They are [like the] wild asses of the desert. They go out on their foray; they stalk their prey. The wasteland is food for their youths [(who accompany them)].


###### Job 24:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:6)


בַּ֭שָּׂדֶה בְּלִיל֣וֹ (יקצירו) [יִקְצ֑וֹרוּ] וְכֶ֖רֶם רָשָׁ֣ע יְלַקֵּֽשׁוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
they reap his corn in the field; and they glean the vineyard of the wicked;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They harvest [for themselves] the produce in the field [of another], and the(se) wicked (ones) crop [for themselves] the vineyard [of another].


###### Job 24:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:7)


עָר֣וֹם יָ֭לִינוּ מִבְּלִ֣י לְב֑וּשׁ וְאֵ֥ין כְּ֝ס֗וּת בַּקָּרָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
they lie all night naked without clothing, and they have no covering in the cold;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They make [(their victims)] lie naked [at night], without clothing, and for want of cover embrace the rock.


###### Job 24:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:8)


מִזֶּ֣רֶם הָרִ֣ים יִרְטָ֑בוּ וּֽמִבְּלִ֥י מַ֝חְסֶ֗ה חִבְּקוּ־צֽוּר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
they are wet with the showers of the mountains, and embrace the rock for want of a shelter.


###### Job 24:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:9)


יִ֭גְזְלוּ מִשֹּׁ֣ד יָת֑וֹם וְֽעַל־עָנִ֥י יַחְבֹּֽלוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These pluck the fatherless from the breast, and take a pledge of the poor,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They rob from the orphan's breast [i.e., from his source of sustenance] and take pledges from the poor.


###### Job 24:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:10)


עָר֣וֹם הִ֭לְּכוּ בְּלִ֣י לְב֑וּשׁ וּ֝רְעֵבִ֗ים נָ֣שְׂאוּ עֹֽמֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who go naked without clothing; and they that carry the sheaf are hungry;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They cause [him] to go naked, without a garment, and from the hungry they take the sheaves [of leket (viz. Leviticus 23:22)].


###### Job 24:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:11)


בֵּין־שׁוּרֹתָ֥ם יַצְהִ֑ירוּ יְקָבִ֥ים דָּ֝רְכ֗וּ וַיִּצְמָֽאוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
they also make the oil within the olive rows of the wicked; they tread the winepresses, and suffer thirst.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Between their rows [of olives] they [(the owners)] make oil; they tread the winepresses — yet they go thirsty [(for the wicked steal their oil and wine)].


###### Job 24:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:12)


מֵ֘עִ֤יר מְתִ֨ים**׀** יִנְאָ֗קוּ וְנֶֽפֶשׁ־חֲלָלִ֥ים תְּשַׁוֵּ֑עַ וֶ֝אֱל֗וֹהַּ לֹֽא־יָשִׂ֥ים תִּפְלָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Men groan from out of the city, and the soul of the wounded cries out: yet God lays no blame on them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The people [(whom they ravish)] scream from the city, and the slain cry out, and G d [(apparently)] does not deem it wrong [(for He does not punish the wicked)].


###### Job 24:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:13)


הֵ֤מָּה**׀** הָיוּ֮ בְּֽמֹרְדֵ֫י־א֥וֹר לֹא־הִכִּ֥ירוּ דְרָכָ֑יו וְלֹ֥א יָ֝שְׁב֗וּ בִּנְתִיבֹתָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They are of those who rebel against the light; they know not its ways, nor abide in its paths.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They [(these wicked ones)] are the rain-rebels [(the generation of the flood, who rebelled against G d, saying that they did not need Him even for rain [(which ascended from the ground [viz. Genesis 2:6])]. They did not know His ways and did not sit in His paths.


###### Job 24:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:14)


לָא֡וֹר יָ֘ק֤וּם רוֹצֵ֗חַ יִֽקְטׇל־עָנִ֥י וְאֶבְי֑וֹן וּ֝בַלַּ֗יְלָה יְהִ֣י כַגַּנָּֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The murderer rising with the light kills the poor and needy, and in the night he is like a thief.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If the murderer wakes at daylight, he slays the poor and the needy, and [if] at night, he is like a thief [(breaking into houses)].


###### Job 24:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:15)


וְעֵ֤ין נֹאֵ֨ף**׀** שָׁ֤מְרָֽה־נֶ֣שֶׁף לֵ֭אמֹר לֹא־תְשׁוּרֵ֣נִי עָ֑יִן וְסֵ֖תֶר פָּנִ֣ים יָשִֽׂים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The eye also of the adulterer waits for the twilight, saying, No eye shall see me: and he disguises his face.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the eye of the adulterer [among them] awaits the night, saying: "No eye will see me," and in the dark he sets his face [to transgress].


###### Job 24:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:16)


חָתַ֥ר בַּחֹ֗שֶׁךְ בָּ֫תִּ֥ים יוֹמָ֥ם חִתְּמוּ־לָ֗מוֹ לֹא־יָ֥דְעוּ אֽוֹר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In the dark they dig through houses; by day they shut themselves up: they know not the light.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He [(the wicked one who is not bold enough to operate by day)] tunnels under houses in the darkness. They are sealed [in their houses] in the daytime; they do not know the light.


###### Job 24:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:17)


כִּ֤י יַחְדָּ֨ו**׀** בֹּ֣קֶר לָ֣מוֹ צַלְמָ֑וֶת כִּֽי־יַ֝כִּ֗יר בַּלְה֥וֹת צַלְמָֽוֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the morning is to them even as deep darkness: for he was acquainted with the terrors of deep darkness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For night was one with [(i.e., the same as)] morning to them, for he [i.e., each one of them] recognized the terrors [i.e., the demons] of night [(and was not afraid of them)].


###### Job 24:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:18)


קַל־ה֤וּא**׀** עַל־פְּנֵי־מַ֗יִם תְּקֻלַּ֣ל חֶלְקָתָ֣ם בָּאָ֑רֶץ לֹא־יִ֝פְנֶ֗ה דֶּ֣רֶךְ כְּרָמִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He was swift upon the waters; their portion is cursed in the earth: no treader turns towards their vineyards.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(When Noah and Methuselah reproved them, warning them against the flood, they would say:)] "It is easy [to escape (by boat)] over the face of the waters. Let their portion [(that of the righteous)] in the land be cursed!" [And] he [(each one of the wicked)] would not turn [i.e., follow in] the way of the "vineyards" [(the righteous))].


###### Job 24:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:19)


צִיָּ֤ה גַם־חֹ֗ם יִגְזְל֥וּ מֵֽימֵי־שֶׁ֗לֶג שְׁא֣וֹל חָטָֽאוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Drought and heat consume the snow waters: so does She᾽ol those who have sinned.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Dryness and heat they steal [(Because of the wickedness of the generation of the flood, certain natural ordinances were suspended)], [and they steal even] snow-water [because] they have sinned [unto] Sheol.


###### Job 24:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:20)


יִשְׁכָּ֘חֵ֤הוּ רֶ֨חֶם**׀** מְתָ֘ק֤וֹ רִמָּ֗ה ע֥וֹד לֹֽא־יִזָּכֵ֑ר וַתִּשָּׁבֵ֖ר כָּעֵ֣ץ עַוְלָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The womb shall forget him; the worm shall feed sweetly on him; he shall be no more remembered; and wickedness shall be broken like a tree.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[His] womb [i.e., his mother] will forget him. He is sweet to the worm. He will no longer be remembered, and [(the man of wrong)] is broken as [the breaking of] a stick.


###### Job 24:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:21)


רֹעֶ֣ה עֲ֭קָרָה לֹ֣א תֵלֵ֑ד וְ֝אַלְמָנָ֗ה לֹ֣א יְיֵטִֽיב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He preys on the barren childless woman: and does not good to the widow.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(He marries two wives: one, for children; the other, for lust-gratification, whom he gives a potion to drink so that she cannot conceive and retains her beauty)]. He pampers the barren one who cannot bear, and the "widow" he does not provide for.


###### Job 24:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:22)


וּמָשַׁ֣ךְ אַבִּירִ֣ים בְּכֹח֑וֹ יָ֝ק֗וּם וְֽלֹא־יַאֲמִ֥ין בַּחַיִּֽין׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he draws the mighty away with his power: he rises up, and no man is sure of life.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He pulls the tyrants with His strength [(from the grace period He granted them, to destruction.)] [One day (the day of the flood)] he rises, and does not [thereafter] believe in life!


###### Job 24:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:23)


יִתֶּן־ל֣וֹ לָ֭בֶטַח וְיִשָּׁעֵ֑ן וְ֝עֵינֵ֗יהוּ עַל־דַּרְכֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Though it be given him to be in safety, whereon he rests; yet his eyes are upon their ways.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He [(G d)] grants him security, and he relies [(on his wealth, until the day of his destruction)], and His eyes are [fixed] on their ways.


###### Job 24:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:24)


ר֤וֹמּוּ מְּעַ֨ט**׀** וְֽאֵינֶ֗נּוּ וְֽהֻמְּכ֗וּ כַּכֹּ֥ל יִקָּפְצ֑וּן וּכְרֹ֖אשׁ שִׁבֹּ֣לֶת יִמָּֽלוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Go up a little, and he is no more: go down at all and they are shut up: they wither like the top of an ear of corn.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They are removed [from the world] in a moment and are no more [(without having suffered)], and they are snuffed out like all [things] that are whisked away [at harvest time], and like the head of a stalk they are cut off.


###### Job 24:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/24:25)


וְאִם־לֹ֣א אֵ֭פוֹ מִ֣י יַכְזִיבֵ֑נִי וְיָשֵׂ֥ם לְ֝אַ֗ל מִלָּתִֽי׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if it be not so now, who will make me a liar, and make my speech nothing worth?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if [it is] not [as I say], where is the man, and who is he, that would refute me and put to nought my words [(i.e., my questioning as to why He grants grace periods to the wicked, whereas His lovers, such as I, He does not do away with, but makes them linger on in suffering)]?



## Chapter 25

###### Job 25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/25:1)


וַ֭יַּעַן בִּלְדַּ֥ד הַשֻּׁחִ֗י וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then answered Bildad the Shuĥite, and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Bildad the Shuchite raised his voice and said [(response to 23:4)]:


###### Job 25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/25:2)


הַמְשֵׁ֣ל וָפַ֣חַד עִמּ֑וֹ עֹשֶׂ֥ה שָׁ֝ל֗וֹם בִּמְרוֹמָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Dominion and fear are with him, he makes peace in his high places.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Governance and fear is with Him: He makes peace in His heights.


###### Job 25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/25:3)


הֲיֵ֣שׁ מִ֭סְפָּר לִגְדוּדָ֑יו וְעַל־מִ֝֗י לֹא־יָק֥וּם אוֹרֵֽהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Is there any number to his armies? and upon whom does not his light arise?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Is there any number to His angelic hosts, and on whom will His light not shine?


###### Job 25:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/25:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/25:4)


וּמַה־יִּצְדַּ֣ק אֱנ֣וֹשׁ עִם־אֵ֑ל וּמַה־יִּ֝זְכֶּ֗ה יְל֣וּד אִשָּֽׁה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
How then can man be justified with God? or how can he be clean that is born of a woman?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
How, then, can a man be accounted righteous before G d? [(response to 23:10)] And how would one born of woman be accounted meritorious?


###### Job 25:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/25:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/25:5)


הֵ֣ן עַד־יָ֭רֵחַ וְלֹ֣א יַאֲהִ֑יל וְ֝כוֹכָבִ֗ים לֹא־זַכּ֥וּ בְעֵינָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold even the moon has no brightness; and the stars are not pure in his sight.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He removes [the light of] the moon and it does not shine, and the stars are not righteous in His eyes [(viz. Isaiah 24:23)].


###### Job 25:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/25:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/25:6)


אַ֭ף כִּֽי־אֱנ֣וֹשׁ רִמָּ֑ה וּבֶן־אָ֝דָ֗ם תּוֹלֵעָֽה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
How much less man, that is a worm? and the son of man, who is a maggot?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
How much more so a mortal, [who is destined for] the worm, and the son of man, [who is destined for] the maggot!



## Chapter 26

###### Job 26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:1)


וַיַּ֥עַן אִיּ֗וֹב וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Iyyov answered and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Iyyov raised his voice and said:


###### Job 26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:2)


מֶה־עָזַ֥רְתָּ לְלֹא־כֹ֑חַ ה֝וֹשַׁ֗עְתָּ זְר֣וֹעַ לֹא־עֹֽז׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
How hast thou helped him that is without power? how hast thou saved the arm that has no strength?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
How have you helped him who has no strength? [How have] you helped him who has no power [in his] arm?


###### Job 26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:3)


מַה־יָּ֭עַצְתָּ לְלֹ֣א חׇכְמָ֑ה וְ֝תֻשִׁיָּ֗ה לָרֹ֥ב הוֹדָֽעְתָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
How hast thou counselled him that has no wisdom? and how hast thou plentifully declared sound wisdom?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
What have you counseled without wisdom? Have you imparted an abundance of knowledge?


###### Job 26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:4)


אֶת־מִ֭י הִגַּ֣דְתָּ מִלִּ֑ין וְנִשְׁמַת־מִ֝֗י יָצְאָ֥ה מִמֶּֽךָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
To whom hast thou uttered words? and whose spirit came from thee?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To whom have you told [these] things, and whose breath has issued forth from you? [i.e., Doesn't everybody know this?]


###### Job 26:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:5)


הָרְפָאִ֥ים יְחוֹלָ֑לוּ מִתַּ֥חַת מַ֝֗יִם וְשֹׁכְנֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The shades tremble; the waters beneath with the inhabitants thereof.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The shades [i.e., Gehinnom (with seven hollows)] which were hollowed out [in the depths], water beneath [them], and their dwellers


###### Job 26:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:6)


עָר֣וֹם שְׁא֣וֹל נֶגְדּ֑וֹ וְאֵ֥ין כְּ֝ס֗וּת לָאֲבַדּֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She᾽ol is naked before him, and Avaddon has no covering.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
— Sheol is bared before Him, and there is no covering to hell.


###### Job 26:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:7)


נֹטֶ֣ה צָפ֣וֹן עַל־תֹּ֑הוּ תֹּ֥לֶה אֶ֝֗רֶץ עַל־בְּלִי־מָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He stretches out the north over the empty place, and hangs the earth upon nothing.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He props the north on a void; He suspends the earth on nothingness.


###### Job 26:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:8)


צֹרֵֽר־מַ֥יִם בְּעָבָ֑יו וְלֹֽא־נִבְקַ֖ע עָנָ֣ן תַּחְתָּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He binds up the waters in his thick clouds; and the cloud is not rent under them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He ties the waters in His thick clouds, and the clouds do not burst beneath them.


###### Job 26:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:9)


מְאַחֵ֥ז פְּנֵֽי־כִסֵּ֑ה פַּרְשֵׁ֖ז עָלָ֣יו עֲנָנֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He closes in the face of his throne, and spreads his cloud upon it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He closes in [(with partitions)] the face of His throne, and spreads His cloud upon it [viz. Ezekiel 1:22]


###### Job 26:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:10)


חֹֽק־חָ֭ג עַל־פְּנֵי־מָ֑יִם עַד־תַּכְלִ֖ית א֣וֹר עִם־חֹֽשֶׁךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He has compassed the waters with bounds, at the boundary between light and darkness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He has circumscribed a statute [(the sand)] around the face of the waters, [to endure] until the end of light and darkness.


###### Job 26:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:11)


עַמּוּדֵ֣י שָׁמַ֣יִם יְרוֹפָ֑פוּ וְ֝יִתְמְה֗וּ מִגַּעֲרָתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The pillars of heaven tremble and are astonished at his reproof.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The pillars of heaven trembled [(at the creation)], and they were stunned [still] by His shout.


###### Job 26:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:12)


בְּ֭כֹחוֹ רָגַ֣ע הַיָּ֑ם (ובתובנתו) [וּ֝בִתְבוּנָת֗וֹ] מָ֣חַץ רָֽהַב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He stirs up the sea with his power, and by his understanding he smites through Rahav.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In His strength He split the sea, and in His understanding He smote the [Egyptian] host.


###### Job 26:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:13)


בְּ֭רוּחוֹ שָׁמַ֣יִם שִׁפְרָ֑ה חֹלְלָ֥ה יָ֝ד֗וֹ נָחָ֥שׁ בָּרִֽחַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
By his wind the heavens were made fair; his hand slew the slant serpent.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He tented the heavens with His breath, and His hand formed the [sea-] spanning Leviathan [(Pharaoh)].


###### Job 26:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/26:14)


הֶן־אֵ֤לֶּה**׀** קְצ֬וֹת דְּרָכָ֗ו וּמַה־שֵּׁ֣מֶץ דָּ֭בָר נִשְׁמַע־בּ֑וֹ וְרַ֥עַם גְּ֝בוּרֹתָ֗ו מִ֣י יִתְבּוֹנָֽן׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Lo, these are parts of his ways, and how terrifying is the thing that is heard of him; but the thunder of his power who can understand?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
These are but a few of His deeds. And what vestige of a thing [can a man] understand of it? Who, then, will comprehend the thunder of His mighty [deeds]?



## Chapter 27

###### Job 27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:1)


וַיֹּ֣סֶף אִ֭יּוֹב שְׂאֵ֥ת מְשָׁל֗וֹ וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Iyyov continued his discourse, and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Iyyov continued — raising his voice in parable — and he said:


###### Job 27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:2)


חַי־אֵ֭ל הֵסִ֣יר מִשְׁפָּטִ֑י וְ֝שַׁדַּ֗י הֵמַ֥ר נַפְשִֽׁי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As God lives, who has taken away my right; and the Almighty, who has embittered my soul;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[I swear] As G d lives, He has cast aside my judgment, and, [as] the Almighty [lives], He has embittered my spirit.


###### Job 27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:3)


כִּי־כׇל־ע֣וֹד נִשְׁמָתִ֣י בִ֑י וְר֖וּחַ אֱל֣וֹהַּ בְּאַפִּֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
all the while my breath is in me, and the spirit of God is in my nostrils;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For as long as my spirit is in me and the breath of G d is in my nostrils,


###### Job 27:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:4)


אִם־תְּדַבֵּ֣רְנָה שְׂפָתַ֣י עַוְלָ֑ה וּ֝לְשׁוֹנִ֗י אִם־יֶהְגֶּ֥ה רְמִיָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
my lips shall not speak wickedness, nor my tongue utter deceit.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[may this oath be upon me] if my lips speak wrongly or my tongue utters deceit.


###### Job 27:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:5)


חָלִ֣ילָה לִּי֮ אִם־אַצְדִּ֢יק אֶ֫תְכֶ֥ם עַד־אֶגְוָ֑ע לֹֽא־אָסִ֖יר תֻּמָּתִ֣י מִמֶּֽנִּי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Far be it from me that I should justify you: till I die I will not put away my integrity from me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[G d] forbid that I should justify you [(in your incrimination of me)]. Until I die, I shall not disavow my innocence.


###### Job 27:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:6)


בְּצִדְקָתִ֣י הֶ֭חֱזַקְתִּי וְלֹ֣א אַרְפֶּ֑הָ לֹא־יֶחֱרַ֥ף לְ֝בָבִ֗י מִיָּמָֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My righteousness I hold fast, and I will not let it go: my heart shall not reproach me as long as I live.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I have persisted in my righteousness and not grown slack. My heart never reproached [me for my conduct] all of my days.


###### Job 27:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:7)


יְהִ֣י כְ֭רָשָׁע אֹ֣יְבִ֑י וּמִתְקוֹמְמִ֥י כְעַוָּֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let my enemy be as the wicked, and he who rises up against me as the unrighteous.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Perforce,] it is my foe who is the evildoer, and he who rises against me, the wrongdoer.


###### Job 27:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:8)


כִּ֤י מַה־תִּקְוַ֣ת חָ֭נֵף כִּ֣י יִבְצָ֑ע כִּ֤י יֵ֖שֶׁל אֱל֣וֹהַּ נַפְשֽׁוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For what is the hope of the hypocrite, though he has gained, when God takes away his soul?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For what is the hope of the flatterer who robs, when G d casts away his soul?


###### Job 27:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:9)


הֲֽ֭צַעֲקָתוֹ יִשְׁמַ֥ע׀אֵ֑ל כִּֽי־תָב֖וֹא עָלָ֣יו צָרָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Will God hear his cry when trouble comes upon him?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will G d hear his outcry when trouble comes upon him?


###### Job 27:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:10)


אִם־עַל־שַׁדַּ֥י יִתְעַנָּ֑ג יִקְרָ֖א אֱל֣וֹהַּ בְּכׇל־עֵֽת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Will he delight himself in the Almighty? will he always call upon God?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will he delight in the Almighty? Will he call upon G d at all times?


###### Job 27:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:11)


אוֹרֶ֣ה אֶתְכֶ֣ם בְּיַד־אֵ֑ל אֲשֶׁ֥ר עִם־שַׁ֝דַּ֗י לֹ֣א אֲכַחֵֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I will teach you concerning the hand of God: that which is with the Almighty will I not conceal.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I will teach you what [(mode of conduct)] is in the hand of G d. I will not conceal what resides with the Almighty.


###### Job 27:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:12)


הֵן־אַתֶּ֣ם כֻּלְּכֶ֣ם חֲזִיתֶ֑ם וְלָמָּה־זֶּ֝֗ה הֶ֣בֶל תֶּהְבָּֽלוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, all you yourselves have seen it; why then do you thus altogether breathe emptiness?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In truth, all of you have seen [viz. verse 13, etc.]. Why, then, do you pursue vanity [(being evildoers and flatterers)]?


###### Job 27:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:13)


זֶ֤ה**׀** חֵלֶק־אָדָ֖ם רָשָׁ֥ע׀עִם־אֵ֑ל וְֽנַחֲלַ֥ת עָ֝רִיצִ֗ים מִשַּׁדַּ֥י יִקָּֽחוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
This is the portion of a wicked man with God, and the heritage of oppressors, which they shall receive of the Almighty.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
This [(as you have seen)] is the portion of the wicked man, which is reposited with G d, and the inheritance that the tyrants will take from the Almighty.


###### Job 27:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:14)


אִם־יִרְבּ֣וּ בָנָ֣יו לְמוֹ־חָ֑רֶב וְ֝צֶאֱצָאָ֗יו לֹ֣א יִשְׂבְּעוּ־לָֽחֶם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If his children be multiplied, it is for the sword: and his offspring shall not be satisfied with bread.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Though his sons increase, they are for the sword, and his descendants will not be sated with bread.


###### Job 27:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:15)


שְׂ֭רִידָו בַּמָּ֣וֶת יִקָּבֵ֑רוּ וְ֝אַלְמְנֹתָ֗יו לֹ֣א תִבְכֶּֽינָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Those that remain of him shall be buried by some other death: and his widows shall not weep.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Those that are left to him, in death [(after terrible afflictions)] will be buried, and his widows will not mourn them, [(seeing their death as a "reprieve")].


###### Job 27:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:16)


אִם־יִצְבֹּ֣ר כֶּעָפָ֣ר כָּ֑סֶף וְ֝כַחֹ֗מֶר יָכִ֥ין מַלְבּֽוּשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Though he heap up silver as the dust, and prepare raiment as the clay;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He may amass silver as dust and store up raiment as clay.


###### Job 27:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:17)


יָ֭כִין וְצַדִּ֣יק יִלְבָּ֑שׁ וְ֝כֶ֗סֶף נָקִ֥י יַחֲלֹֽק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
he may prepare it, but the just shall put it on, and the innocent shall divide the silver.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He may store it up, but the righteous one will wear it, and He will apportion the silver [to] the innocent one.


###### Job 27:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:18)


בָּנָ֣ה כָעָ֣שׁ בֵּית֑וֹ וּ֝כְסֻכָּ֗ה עָשָׂ֥ה נֹצֵֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He builds his house like the moth, and as a booth which the watchman makes.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He has built his house [(to come down)] as that of a moth and as a booth made by a watcher.


###### Job 27:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:19)


עָשִׁ֣יר יִ֭שְׁכַּב וְלֹ֣א יֵאָסֵ֑ף עֵינָ֖יו פָּקַ֣ח וְאֵינֶֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
A rich man shall he lie down, as though his wealth could not be summed up: he opens his eyes, and it is gone.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He dies rich and [sometimes] he is not [even] gathered up [for burial]. He opens his eyes and he is gone.


###### Job 27:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:20)


תַּשִּׂיגֵ֣הוּ כַ֭מַּיִם בַּלָּה֑וֹת לַ֝֗יְלָה גְּנָבַ֥תּוּ סוּפָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Terrors overtake him like waters; a tempest steals him away in the night.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Terrors seize him as a torrent. At night he is spirited away by a whirlwind.


###### Job 27:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:21)


יִשָּׂאֵ֣הוּ קָדִ֣ים וְיֵלַ֑ךְ וִ֝ישָׂעֲרֵ֗הוּ מִמְּקֹמֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The east wind carries him away, and he departs: and it hurts him out of his place.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The east wind lifts him up and he goes, and it sweeps him from his place.


###### Job 27:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:22)


וְיַשְׁלֵ֣ךְ עָ֭לָיו וְלֹ֣א יַחְמֹ֑ל מִ֝יָּד֗וֹ בָּר֥וֹחַ יִבְרָֽח׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It hurls itself at him, and does not spare: he would fain flee out of its hand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Men] heap [evil] upon him and have no mercy. [His former friends] flee from him.


###### Job 27:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/27:23)


יִשְׂפֹּ֣ק עָלֵ֣ימוֹ כַפֵּ֑ימוֹ וְיִשְׁרֹ֥ק עָ֝לָ֗יו מִמְּקֹמֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Men shall clap their hands at him, and shall hiss him out of his place.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Men smite their palms over him [(in dismay at his downfall)], and they whistle over him in his place.



## Chapter 28

###### Job 28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:1)


כִּ֤י יֵ֣שׁ לַכֶּ֣סֶף מוֹצָ֑א וּ֝מָק֗וֹם לַזָּהָ֥ב יָזֹֽקּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Surely there is a mine for silver, and a place for gold where they refine it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[reversion to 27:6 (… for why should I be wicked? Not for silver and gold)] for there is a source [(a beginning)] for silver and a place where gold is refined [and "finished,] but (verse 20): "Whence does wisdom come, etc."? (i.e., it has no beginning and no end, and it is to that that I have devoted my life)].


###### Job 28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:2)


בַּ֭רְזֶל מֵעָפָ֣ר יֻקָּ֑ח וְ֝אֶ֗בֶן יָצ֥וּק נְחוּשָֽׁה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Iron is taken out of the earth, and brass is molten out of the stone.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Iron is drawn from the earth and a stone gives forth copper. [(All things have a beginning and an end, for the world will end and all that is in it.)]


###### Job 28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:3)


קֵ֤ץ**׀** שָׂ֤ם לַחֹ֗שֶׁךְ וּֽלְכׇל־תַּ֭כְלִית ה֣וּא חוֹקֵ֑ר אֶ֖בֶן אֹ֣פֶל וְצַלְמָֽוֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He puts an end to darkness, and searches out all perfection: the stones of darkness, and the shadow of death.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He has set an end for the [onset of] darkness [i.e., oblivion], and He searches out every end, the stone [i.e., the source] of blackness and the shadow of death.


###### Job 28:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:4)


פָּ֤רַץ נַ֨חַל**׀** מֵֽעִם־גָּ֗ר הַֽנִּשְׁכָּחִ֥ים מִנִּי־רָ֑גֶל דַּ֖לּוּ מֵאֱנ֣וֹשׁ נָֽעוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He breaks open a watercourse in place far from inhabitants, forgotten by foot-travellers: they are dried up; they are gone away from men.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A stream [(of fire and brimstone)] burst forth from its source [(upon Sodom and Gomorrah)], who [(i.e., the people of Sodom)] caused the [code of the] wayfarer to be forgotten.


###### Job 28:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:5)


אֶ֗רֶץ מִמֶּ֥נָּה יֵֽצֵא־לָ֑חֶם וְ֝תַחְתֶּ֗יהָ נֶהְפַּ֥ךְ כְּמוֹ־אֵֽשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As for the earth, out of it comes bread: and under it is turned up as it were fire.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A land from which bread had issued forth — its place was overturned, as if [consumed by] fire.


###### Job 28:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:6)


מְקוֹם־סַפִּ֥יר אֲבָנֶ֑יהָ וְעַפְרֹ֖ת זָהָ֣ב לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The stones of it are the place of sapphires: and it has dust of gold.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A place of sapphire were its stones, and dusts of gold were there.


###### Job 28:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:7)


נָ֭תִיב לֹא־יְדָ֣עוֹ עָ֑יִט וְלֹ֥א שְׁ֝זָפַ֗תּוּ עֵ֣ין אַיָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There is a path which no bird of prey knows, and which the falcon’s eye has not seen:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[And now it is] a path unknown [i.e., unfrequented] by brigands and unseen by the falcon's eye [i.e., not scouted out by the spies],


###### Job 28:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:8)


לֹֽא־הִדְרִיכ֥וּהוּ בְנֵי־שָׁ֑חַץ לֹֽא־עָדָ֖ה עָלָ֣יו שָֽׁחַל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
the lion’s whelps have not trodden it, nor the fierce lion passed by it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
untrodden by the haughty [beasts] and not crossed by the lion.


###### Job 28:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:9)


בַּ֭חַלָּמִישׁ שָׁלַ֣ח יָד֑וֹ הָפַ֖ךְ מִשֹּׁ֣רֶשׁ הָרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He puts forth his hand upon the rock; he overturns the mountains by the roots.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He sent His hand against the flint. He overturned mountains from their roots.


###### Job 28:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:10)


בַּ֭צּוּרוֹת יְאֹרִ֣ים בִּקֵּ֑עַ וְכׇל־יְ֝קָ֗ר רָאֲתָ֥ה עֵינֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He cuts out channels among the rocks; and his eye sees every precious thing.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(reversion to verse 1)] He cleft rocks [as the source of] rivers, and His eye [fore-] saw [the sources of] all precious [things for man].


###### Job 28:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:11)


מִ֭בְּכִי נְהָר֣וֹת חִבֵּ֑שׁ וְ֝תַעֲלֻמָ֗הּ יֹ֣צִא אֽוֹר׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He binds the floods so that they do not trickle; and the thing that is hidden he brings forth to light.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
From Bechi [(a source of a kind)] He girded rivers, and from Ta'alumah [(a window in heaven)] He brings forth light. [(In sum, all things have a source.)]


###### Job 28:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:12)


וְֽ֭הַחׇכְמָה מֵאַ֣יִן תִּמָּצֵ֑א וְאֵ֥י זֶ֝֗ה מְק֣וֹם בִּינָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But where shall wisdom be found? and where is the place of understanding?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But whence is wisdom [i.e., Torah] brought forth? And where is the place of understanding?


###### Job 28:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:13)


לֹא־יָדַ֣ע אֱנ֣וֹשׁ עֶרְכָּ֑הּ וְלֹ֥א תִ֝מָּצֵ֗א בְּאֶ֣רֶץ הַחַיִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Man cannot know its price; nor is it found in the land of the living.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Man does not know its worth, and it is not found in the land of the living [(but only among those who "mortify" themselves [by toil and hunger] for it)].


###### Job 28:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:14)


תְּה֣וֹם אָ֭מַר לֹ֣א בִי־הִ֑יא וְיָ֥ם אָ֝מַ֗ר אֵ֣ין עִמָּדִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The depth says, It is not in me: and the sea says, It is not with me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The depths [i.e., those who plumb the depths for gems] say: "It is not within me," and the sea [-merchant] says: "It is not with me."


###### Job 28:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:15)


לֹא־יֻתַּ֣ן סְג֣וֹר תַּחְתֶּ֑יהָ וְלֹ֥א יִ֝שָּׁקֵ֗ל כֶּ֣סֶף מְחִירָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It cannot be gotten for gold, nor shall silver be weighed for its price.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Pure gold cannot be given for it, and silver cannot be weighed in exchange for it.


###### Job 28:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:16)


לֹא־תְ֭סֻלֶּה בְּכֶ֣תֶם אוֹפִ֑יר בְּשֹׁ֖הַם יָקָ֣ר וְסַפִּֽיר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It cannot be valued with the gold of Ofir, with the precious shoham stone, or the sapphire.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
It cannot be valued by the jewels of Ophir, or the precious onyx, or the sapphire.


###### Job 28:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:17)


לֹא־יַעַרְכֶ֣נָּה זָ֭הָב וּזְכוֹכִ֑ית וּתְמ֖וּרָתָ֣הּ כְּלִי־פָֽז׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Gold and glass cannot equal it: and the exchange of it shall not be for vessels of fine gold.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Gold and fine glass cannot be compared to it; [nor] can its exchange be made in vessels of gold.


###### Job 28:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:18)


רָאמ֣וֹת וְ֭גָבִישׁ לֹ֣א יִזָּכֵ֑ר וּמֶ֥שֶׁךְ חׇ֝כְמָ֗ה מִפְּנִינִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
No mention shall be made of coral, or of crystal: for the price of wisdom is above rubies.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Coral and crystal will not be mentioned [next to it], and the extraction of wisdom [is more precious] than pearls.


###### Job 28:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:19)


לֹֽא־יַ֭עַרְכֶנָּה פִּטְדַת־כּ֑וּשׁ בְּכֶ֥תֶם טָ֝ה֗וֹר לֹ֣א תְסֻלֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The chrysolite of Kush shall not equal it, nor shall it be valued with pure gold.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The crysolithe of Kush cannot be compared to it. It cannot be valued by pure jewels.


###### Job 28:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:20)


וְֽ֭הַחׇכְמָה מֵאַ֣יִן תָּב֑וֹא וְאֵ֥י זֶ֝֗ה מְק֣וֹם בִּינָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Whence then does wisdom come? and where is the place of understanding?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Now whence is wisdom [(Torah)] derived, and where is the place of understanding?


###### Job 28:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:21)


וְֽ֭נֶעֶלְמָה מֵעֵינֵ֣י כׇל־חָ֑י וּמֵע֖וֹף הַשָּׁמַ֣יִם נִסְתָּֽרָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Seeing it is hidden from the eyes of all living, and kept close from the birds of the sky.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
It is hidden from the eyes of all living things and concealed from the winged things [(the angels)] of heaven.


###### Job 28:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:22)


אֲבַדּ֣וֹן וָ֭מָוֶת אָ֣מְר֑וּ בְּ֝אׇזְנֵ֗ינוּ שָׁמַ֥עְנוּ שִׁמְעָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Avaddon and death say, We have heard a rumour of it with our ears.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Destruction and death [(i.e., those who mortify themselves over it (viz. 13)] have said: "With our ears we have heard its report [(i.e., that it remains only with those who toil over it)].


###### Job 28:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:23)


אֱ֭לֹהִים הֵבִ֣ין דַּרְכָּ֑הּ וְ֝ה֗וּא יָדַ֥ע אֶת־מְקוֹמָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
God understands its way, and he knows its place.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Only] G d understands its way; [only] He knows its place.


###### Job 28:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:24)


כִּי־ה֭וּא לִקְצוֹת־הָאָ֣רֶץ יַבִּ֑יט תַּ֖חַת כׇּל־הַשָּׁמַ֣יִם יִרְאֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For he looks to the ends of the earth, and sees everything under the whole heaven;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For He looks [in it (Torah)] to [establish] the ends of the earth, and He sees in it [(the needs of all the creations)] under all of the heavens [(Torah being the blueprint of the universe)].


###### Job 28:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:25)


לַעֲשׂ֣וֹת לָר֣וּחַ מִשְׁקָ֑ל וּ֝מַ֗יִם תִּכֵּ֥ן בְּמִדָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
when he makes a weight for the winds; and he weighs the waters by measure.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[He looks in it] to give the wind [its proper] weight [(relative to the land mass)] and [(likewise)] to prepare water by measure,


###### Job 28:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:26)


בַּעֲשֹׂת֣וֹ לַמָּטָ֣ר חֹ֑ק וְ֝דֶ֗רֶךְ לַחֲזִ֥יז קֹלֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When he made a decree for the rain, and a way for the lightning of the thunder:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
decreeing a statute for the rain [(relative to the land)] and [making] a path for cloud and thunder.


###### Job 28:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:27)


אָ֣ז רָ֭אָהּ וַֽיְסַפְּרָ֑הּ הֱ֝כִינָ֗הּ וְגַם־חֲקָרָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then he saw it, and declared it; he established it, yea, and searched it out.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He saw [it (and made everything by its counsel)] and He counted [(its letters and made everything through their combinations)]. He prepared it [(for the creation)] and He also searched it out.


###### Job 28:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/28:28)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר**׀** לָאָדָ֗ם הֵ֤ן יִרְאַ֣ת אֲ֭דֹנָי הִ֣יא חׇכְמָ֑ה וְס֖וּר מֵרָ֣ע בִּינָֽה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And to man he said, Behold, the fear of the Lord, that is wisdom; and to depart from evil is understanding.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He said to man: "The fear of the L rd — that is wisdom, and the turning away from evil is understanding."



## Chapter 29

###### Job 29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:1)


וַיֹּ֣סֶף אִ֭יּוֹב שְׂאֵ֥ת מְשָׁל֗וֹ וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover Iyyov continued his discourse, and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Iyyov resumed — raising his voice in parable — and he said:


###### Job 29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:2)


מִֽי־יִתְּנֵ֥נִי כְיַרְחֵי־קֶ֑דֶם כִּ֝ימֵ֗י אֱל֣וֹהַּ יִשְׁמְרֵֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Oh that I were as in months past, as in the days when God preserved me;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would that I were as in the months of yore [(of my greatness)], as in the days when G d watched over me.


###### Job 29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:3)


בְּהִלּ֣וֹ נֵ֭רוֹ עֲלֵ֣י רֹאשִׁ֑י לְ֝אוֹר֗וֹ אֵ֣לֶךְ חֹֽשֶׁךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
when his candle shone upon my head, and when by his light I walked through darkness;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When His lamp shone above my head, when I went by His light in the darkness [(that afflicted mankind)].


###### Job 29:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:4)


כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר הָ֭יִיתִי בִּימֵ֣י חׇרְפִּ֑י בְּס֥וֹד אֱ֝ל֗וֹהַּ עֲלֵ֣י אׇהֳלִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
as I was in the days of my youth, when God shielded my tent;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As I was in my early days, when [the righteous men of the generation came] to my tent to take counsel in the words of G d [(and in the "fences" thereto required by the times)].


###### Job 29:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:5)


בְּע֣וֹד שַׁ֭דַּי עִמָּדִ֑י סְבִ֖יבוֹתַ֣י נְעָרָֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
when the Almighty was yet with me, when my children were about me;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When the Almighty was still with me, and my young men [(my attendants)] all around me.


###### Job 29:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:6)


בִּרְחֹ֣ץ הֲלִיכַ֣י בְּחֵמָ֑ה וְצ֥וּר יָצ֥וּק עִ֝מָּדִ֗י פַּלְגֵי־שָֽׁמֶן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
when I washed my steps with butter, and the rock poured me out rivers of oil;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When my feet were washed in butter and a rock by my side streamed forth [(all manner of delights)] like rivulets of oil.


###### Job 29:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:7)


בְּצֵ֣אתִי שַׁ֣עַר עֲלֵי־קָ֑רֶת בָּ֝רְח֗וֹב אָכִ֥ין מוֹשָׁבִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
when I went out to the gate through the city, when I prepared my seat in the broad place!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When I went out to the gate on the eminence [(where the elders of the city used to sit)]; when I prepared my seat in the thoroughfare,


###### Job 29:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:8)


רָא֣וּנִי נְעָרִ֣ים וְנֶחְבָּ֑אוּ וִ֝ישִׁישִׁ֗ים קָ֣מוּ עָמָֽדוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The young men saw me, and hid themselves: and the aged arose, and stood up.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
the youths saw me and hid themselves, and the old men rose and remained standing [before me].


###### Job 29:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:9)


שָׂ֭רִים עָצְר֣וּ בְמִלִּ֑ים וְ֝כַ֗ף יָשִׂ֥ימוּ לְפִיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The princes refrained from talking, and laid their hand on their mouth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Princes stifled their speech [(waiting for me to begin)] and put a palm to their mouths.


###### Job 29:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:10)


קוֹל־נְגִידִ֥ים נֶחְבָּ֑אוּ וּ֝לְשׁוֹנָ֗ם לְחִכָּ֥ם דָּבֵֽקָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The voice of the nobles was hushed, and their tongue cleaved to the roof of their mouth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The voice of dignitaries was hidden, and their tongue stuck to their palate.


###### Job 29:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:11)


כִּ֤י אֹ֣זֶן שָׁ֭מְעָה וַֽתְּאַשְּׁרֵ֑נִי וְעַ֥יִן רָ֝אֲתָ֗ה וַתְּעִידֵֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When the ear heard me, then it blessed me; and when the eye saw me, it gave witness to me;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the ear that heard [my words] would commend me, and the eye that saw [everywhere my greatness and righteousness] would testify for me.


###### Job 29:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:12)


כִּֽי־אֲ֭מַלֵּט עָנִ֣י מְשַׁוֵּ֑עַ וְ֝יָת֗וֹם וְֽלֹא־עֹזֵ֥ר לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
because I delivered the poor that cried, and the fatherless, that had none to help him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For I would deliver the pauper from [his] outcries and the orphan bereft of help.


###### Job 29:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:13)


בִּרְכַּ֣ת אֹ֭בֵד עָלַ֣י תָּבֹ֑א וְלֵ֖ב אַלְמָנָ֣ה אַרְנִֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The blessing of him that was ready to perish came upon me: and I caused the widow’s heart to sing for joy.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The blessing of the lost one was bestowed upon me [(for I supported his wife and children)] and I brought joy to the widow's heart [(by allying myself with her)].


###### Job 29:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:14)


צֶ֣דֶק לָ֭בַשְׁתִּי וַיִּלְבָּשֵׁ֑נִי כִּֽמְעִ֥יל וְ֝צָנִ֗יף מִשְׁפָּטִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I put on righteousness, and my justice clothed me, as a robe and a diadem.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I clothed myself in righteousness [i.e., I pursued it], and it "clothed" me. My judgment was as ["comely" as] a cloak and a turban.


###### Job 29:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:15)


עֵינַ֣יִם הָ֭יִיתִי לַעִוֵּ֑ר וְרַגְלַ֖יִם לַפִּסֵּ֣חַ אָֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I was eyes to the blind, and feet was I to the lame.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I was the eyes of the blind and the feet of the lame.


###### Job 29:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:16)


אָ֣ב אָ֭נֹכִֽי לָאֶבְיוֹנִ֑ים וְרִ֖ב לֹא־יָדַ֣עְתִּי אֶחְקְרֵֽהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I was a father to the poor: and the cause which I knew not I searched out.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I was father to the poor, and I searched out the truth of a dispute [(the full facts of which)] I did not know.


###### Job 29:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:17)
