וָ֭אֲשַׁבְּרָה מְתַלְּע֣וֹת עַוָּ֑ל וּ֝מִשִּׁנָּ֗יו אַשְׁלִ֥יךְ טָֽרֶף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I broke the jaws of the wicked man, and plucked the prey out of his teeth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I broke the teeth of the wicked one and cast the prey from his teeth.


###### Job 29:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:18)


וָ֭אֹמַר עִם־קִנִּ֣י אֶגְוָ֑ע וְ֝כַח֗וֹל אַרְבֶּ֥ה יָמִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then I said, I shall die in my nest, and I shall multiply my days like the sand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I said [(that because of my righteousness)] I would die along with my habitation and that I would increase days like the phoenix.


###### Job 29:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:19)


שׇׁרְשִׁ֣י פָת֣וּחַ אֱלֵי־מָ֑יִם וְ֝טַ֗ל יָלִ֥ין בִּקְצִירִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My root shall be spread out to the waters, and the dew shall lie all night upon my branch.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[I said that] my root would be open to the water [(and would not die)] and that dew would rest upon my shoots.


###### Job 29:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:20)


כְּ֭בוֹדִי חָדָ֣שׁ עִמָּדִ֑י וְ֝קַשְׁתִּ֗י בְּיָדִ֥י תַחֲלִֽיף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My glory shall be fresh in me, and my bow shall be renewed in my hand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[I said that] my honor would be renewed for me; the strength of my hand [(for good)] I would [constantly] be reinvigorated.


###### Job 29:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:21)


לִֽי־שָׁמְע֥וּ וְיִחֵ֑לּוּ וְ֝יִדְּמ֗וּ לְמ֣וֹ עֲצָתִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
To me men gave ear, and waited, and kept silence at my counsel.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Men] paid heed to me and hoped [(for the realization of my words)] and they were silent [in acquiescence] at my counsel.


###### Job 29:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:22)


אַחֲרֵ֣י דְ֭בָרִי לֹ֣א יִשְׁנ֑וּ וְ֝עָלֵ֗ימוֹ תִּטֹּ֥ף מִלָּתִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
After my words they did not speak again; and my speech dropped upon them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
After my word they did not repeat [(the seeking of counsel)] and my word would drip [(like prophecy)] upon them.


###### Job 29:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:23)


וְיִחֲל֣וּ כַמָּטָ֣ר לִ֑י וּ֝פִיהֶ֗ם פָּעֲר֥וּ לְמַלְקֽוֹשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they waited for me as for the rain; and they opened their mouth wide as for the latter rain.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They waited upon my words as for rain and opened their mouths [(in prodigal speech) only] afterwards.


###### Job 29:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:24)


אֶשְׂחַ֣ק אֲ֭לֵהֶם לֹ֣א יַאֲמִ֑ינוּ וְא֥וֹר פָּ֝נַ֗י לֹ֣א יַפִּילֽוּן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If I laughed at them, they believed it not; and the light of my countenance they cast not down.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When I laughed with them they did not believe it [(because of their reverence for me)], and [(in awe of me)] they would not venture to approach the light of my countenance.


###### Job 29:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/29:25)


אֶ֥בְחַ֣ר דַּרְכָּם֮ וְאֵשֵׁ֢ב רֹ֥֫אשׁ וְ֭אֶשְׁכּוֹן כְּמֶ֣לֶךְ בַּגְּד֑וּד כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר אֲבֵלִ֣ים יְנַחֵֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I chose out their way, and sat as chief, and dwelt as a king in the army, as one that comforts the mourners.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I would choose their ways [(for them at their solicitation)] and I would sit at their head and dwell among them as a king among his troops, [and I would be to them] as one [(the leader of the company)] who consoles the mourners.



## Chapter 30

###### Job 30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:1)


וְעַתָּ֤ה**׀** שָׂ֥חֲק֣וּ עָלַי֮ צְעִירִ֥ים מִמֶּ֗נִּי לְיָ֫מִ֥ים אֲשֶׁר־מָאַ֥סְתִּי אֲבוֹתָ֑ם לָ֝שִׁ֗ית עִם־כַּלְבֵ֥י צֹאנִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But now they that are younger than I have me in derision, whose fathers I would have disdained to have set with the dogs of my flock.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now I am laughed at by those younger than I in days, whose fathers I would despise to place among the sheep dogs of my flock.


###### Job 30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:2)


גַּם־כֹּ֣חַ יְ֭דֵיהֶם לָ֣מָּה לִּ֑י עָ֝לֵ֗ימוֹ אָ֣בַד כָּֽלַח׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Indeed what could I gain from the strength of their hands, in whom the stock has perished?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Of what avail to me, too, was the strength of their hands? [(They were wicked and everything they did miscarried.)] Old age was lost upon them.


###### Job 30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:3)


בְּחֶ֥סֶר וּבְכָפָ֗ן גַּ֫לְמ֥וּד הַעֹרְקִ֥ים צִיָּ֑ה אֶ֝֗מֶשׁ שׁוֹאָ֥ה וּמְשֹׁאָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For want and famine they are solitary; they flee into the wilderness, into a gloomy waste and desolation.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In want and hunger they sat solitary. They were driven to the wasteland, [to] night, darkness, and desolation.


###### Job 30:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:4)


הַקֹּטְפִ֣ים מַלּ֣וּחַ עֲלֵי־שִׂ֑יחַ וְשֹׁ֖רֶשׁ רְתָמִ֣ים לַחְמָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They cut up mallows by the bushes, and roots of broom for warmth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They picked salted grass. The leaves of trees and the root of the broom plant was their bread.


###### Job 30:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:5)


מִן־גֵּ֥ו יְגֹרָ֑שׁוּ יָרִ֥יעוּ עָ֝לֵ֗ימוֹ כַּגַּנָּֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They are driven forth from among men, (they cry after them as after a thief;)


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They were driven from the midst [of the city] — They were shouted at like thieves —


###### Job 30:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:6)


בַּעֲר֣וּץ נְחָלִ֣ים לִשְׁכֹּ֑ן חֹרֵ֖י עָפָ֣ר וְכֵפִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to dwell in the clefts of the wadis, in holes of the earth, and in the rocks.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
to dwell in the rifts of the valleys, in the clefts of the earth and the rocks.


###### Job 30:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:7)


בֵּין־שִׂיחִ֥ים יִנְהָ֑קוּ תַּ֖חַת חָר֣וּל יְסֻפָּֽחוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Among the bushes they bray; under the nettles they huddle together.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They brayed among the trees; they gathered under the thorns.


###### Job 30:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:8)


בְּֽנֵי־נָ֭בָל גַּם־בְּנֵ֣י בְלִי־שֵׁ֑ם נִ֝כְּא֗וּ מִן־הָאָֽרֶץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They are children of churls, yea, children of base men: they are whipped out of the land.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The sons of the despised, the sons of the nameless, the broken from the earth [(whom I scorned)] —


###### Job 30:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:9)


וְ֭עַתָּה נְגִינָתָ֣ם הָיִ֑יתִי וָאֱהִ֖י לָהֶ֣ם לְמִלָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And now I am their song, yea, I am their byword.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now I have become their song; I have become a byword to them.


###### Job 30:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:10)


תִּ֭עֲבוּנִי רָ֣חֲקוּ מֶ֑נִּי וּ֝מִפָּנַ֗י לֹא־חָ֥שְׂכוּ רֹֽק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They abhor me, they flee far from me, and spare not to spit in my face.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They have despised me. They have removed themselves from me, and they have not spared spittle from my face.


###### Job 30:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:11)


כִּֽי־[יִתְרִ֣י] (יתרו) פִ֭תַּח וַיְעַנֵּ֑נִי וְ֝רֶ֗סֶן מִפָּנַ֥י שִׁלֵּֽחוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Because he has loosed my cord, and afflicted me, they have also cast off restraint before me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They have undone my bow-string [i.e., my strength] and oppressed me. They have cast away the reins [(by which they were tethered)] from me.


###### Job 30:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:12)


עַל־יָמִין֮ פִּרְחַ֢ח יָ֫ק֥וּמוּ רַגְלַ֥י שִׁלֵּ֑חוּ וַיָּסֹ֥לּוּ עָ֝לַ֗י אׇרְח֥וֹת אֵידָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Upon my right hand rise the youthful rabble; they push away my feet, and they raise up against me the ways of their destruction.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The young rabble stand on my right hand. They shove my feet aside [(to make place for themselves)].


###### Job 30:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:13)


נָֽתְס֗וּ נְֽתִיבָ֫תִ֥י לְהַוָּתִ֥י יֹעִ֑ילוּ לֹ֖א עֹזֵ֣ר לָֽמוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They mar my path, they further my calamity; they have no need for help.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They have ravaged my path [(of good)]. To taunt me causelessly was their joy.


###### Job 30:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:14)


כְּפֶ֣רֶץ רָחָ֣ב יֶאֱתָ֑יוּ תַּ֥חַת שֹׁ֝אָ֗ה הִתְגַּלְגָּֽלוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They come upon me as through a wide breach: amid the crash they roll on.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As through a wide breach they have come [(to shame me)]. Under [cover of] darkness they have emerged [against me].


###### Job 30:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:15)


הׇהְפַּ֥ךְ עָלַ֗י בַּלָּ֫ה֥וֹת תִּרְדֹּ֣ף כָּ֭רוּחַ נְדִבָתִ֑י וּ֝כְעָ֗ב עָבְרָ֥ה יְשֻׁעָתִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Terrors are turned upon me: my dignity is pursued as by the wind: and my welfare passes away as a cloud.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Terrors [(demons)] have turned upon me. They pursue my repose like evil spirits and my salvation passes like a cloud.


###### Job 30:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:16)


וְעַתָּ֗ה עָ֭לַי תִּשְׁתַּפֵּ֣ךְ נַפְשִׁ֑י יֹ֖אחֲז֣וּנִי יְמֵי־עֹֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And now my soul is poured out within me; days of affliction have taken hold of me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now [(in my affliction)] my soul spills out upon me. Days of affliction have gripped me.


###### Job 30:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:17)


לַ֗יְלָה עֲ֭צָמַי נִקַּ֣ר מֵעָלָ֑י וְ֝עֹרְקַ֗י לֹ֣א יִשְׁכָּבֽוּן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My bones are pierced in me in the night season: and my sinews take no rest.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
At night my bones are picked off me and my veins have no rest.


###### Job 30:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:18)


בְּרׇב־כֹּ֭חַ יִתְחַפֵּ֣שׂ לְבוּשִׁ֑י כְּפִ֖י כֻתׇּנְתִּ֣י יַאַזְרֵֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
By the great force of my disease is my skin changed: it binds me about as the collar of my coat.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the intensity [of my illness] my "clothing" [i.e., my encrustation] renews itself. As my collar girds [my neck], so does it [my encrustation] gird me.


###### Job 30:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:19)


הֹרָ֥נִי לַחֹ֑מֶר וָ֝אֶתְמַשֵּׁ֗ל כֶּעָפָ֥ר וָאֵֽפֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He has cast me into the mire, and I am become like dust and ashes.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
It [(my affliction)] has taught me to [take refuge in] the mud [viz. 2:8]. I have become like dust and ashes.


###### Job 30:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:20)


אֲשַׁוַּ֣ע אֵ֭לֶיךָ וְלֹ֣א תַעֲנֵ֑נִי עָ֝מַ֗דְתִּי וַתִּתְבֹּ֥נֶן בִּֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I cry to Thee, and Thou dost not answer me: I stand up, dost Thou then regard me?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When I scream to You, You do not answer me. When I stand [in silence] You study me [(to renew my afflictions)].


###### Job 30:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:21)


תֵּהָפֵ֣ךְ לְאַכְזָ֣ר לִ֑י בְּעֹ֖צֶם יָדְךָ֣ תִשְׂטְמֵֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou art become cruel to me: with Thy strong hand Thou opposest Thyself against me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You have turned cruel against me. With the might of Your hand You have brandished hatred against me.


###### Job 30:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:22)


תִּשָּׂאֵ֣נִי אֶל־ר֭וּחַ תַּרְכִּיבֵ֑נִי וּ֝תְמֹגְגֵ֗נִי (תשוה) [תּוּשִׁיָּֽה]׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou liftest me up to the wind; Thou causest me to ride upon it, and the roar of the storm dissolves me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You have born me aloft to the wind [i.e., to demons]. Weakness overwhelms me and melts me.


###### Job 30:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:23)


כִּֽי־יָ֭דַעְתִּי מָ֣וֶת תְּשִׁיבֵ֑נִי וּבֵ֖ית מוֹעֵ֣ד לְכׇל־חָֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For I know that Thou wilt bring me to death, and to the house appointed for all living.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For I knew that You would return me unto death, [(to the grave)] the destined abode of all the living.


###### Job 30:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:24)


אַ֣ךְ לֹֽא־בְ֭עִי יִשְׁלַח־יָ֑ד אִם־בְּ֝פִיד֗וֹ לָהֶ֥ן שֽׁוּעַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yet does not one in a heap of ruins stretch out his hand? and in his disaster cry for help?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But not in destruction [(unrelieved by consolation)] would He [i.e., was it His wont] to send forth [His] hand. If He broke, He would [also] salve.


###### Job 30:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:25)


אִם־לֹ֣א בָ֭כִיתִי לִקְשֵׁה־י֑וֹם עָֽגְמָ֥ה נַ֝פְשִׁ֗י לָאֶבְיֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Did not I weep for him that was in trouble? was not my soul grieved for the poor?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Did I not cry for him of the luckless day? Did my soul [not] despond over the poor


###### Job 30:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:26)


כִּ֤י ט֣וֹב קִ֭וִּיתִי וַיָּ֣בֹא רָ֑ע וַאֲיַחֲלָ֥ה לְ֝א֗וֹר וַיָּ֥בֹא אֹֽפֶל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But when I looked for good, then evil came: and when I waited for light, there came darkness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
that I should hope for good and be met with evil? [that I should hope] for light and be met with darkness?


###### Job 30:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:27)


מֵעַ֖י רֻתְּח֥וּ וְלֹא־דָ֗מּוּ קִדְּמֻ֥נִי יְמֵי־עֹֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My bowels are in turmoil, and they do not rest: days of affliction have came upon me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My innards boil and cannot be stilled. Days of affliction have accosted me.


###### Job 30:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:28)


קֹדֵ֣ר הִ֭לַּכְתִּי בְּלֹ֣א חַמָּ֑ה קַ֖מְתִּי בַקָּהָ֣ל אֲשַׁוֵּֽעַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I go mourning without the sun: I stand up, and I cry in the congregation.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I have gone about blackened, but not with the sun. I have risen in the crowd and screamed.


###### Job 30:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:29)


אָ֭ח הָיִ֣יתִי לְתַנִּ֑ים וְ֝רֵ֗עַ לִבְנ֥וֹת יַעֲנָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I am a brother to jackals, and a companion to owls.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I have been a brother [(in wailing)] to jackals and a companion to ostriches.


###### Job 30:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:30)


ע֭וֹרִי שָׁחַ֣ר מֵעָלָ֑י וְעַצְמִי־חָ֝֗רָה מִנִּי־חֹֽרֶב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My skin hangs down black from me, and my bones are burned with heat.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My skin has charred off me, and my bones are dried with parching.


###### Job 30:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/30:31)


וַיְהִ֣י לְ֭אֵבֶל כִּנֹּרִ֑י וְ֝עֻגָבִ֗י לְק֣וֹל בֹּכִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore my lyre is turned to mourning, and my pipe to the voice of those who weep.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My lyre is turned to lamentation and my pipe to the voice of mourners.



## Chapter 31

###### Job 31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:1)


בְּ֭רִית כָּרַ֣תִּי לְעֵינָ֑י וּמָ֥ה אֶ֝תְבּוֹנֵ֗ן עַל־בְּתוּלָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I have made a covenant with my eyes; why then should I look upon a virgin?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I made a covenant with my eyes [(not to gaze at a married woman)] and a virgin [(when this might cause me to lust her after she is married)].


###### Job 31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:2)


וּמֶ֤ה חֵ֣לֶק אֱל֣וֹהַּ מִמָּ֑עַל וְֽנַחֲלַ֥ת שַׁ֝דַּ֗י מִמְּרֹמִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For what would be my portion from God above, and my heritage from the Almighty on high?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But what portion [i.e., what reward did I receive for this from] G d on high, and what inheritance from the Almighty in the heavens?


###### Job 31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:3)


הֲלֹא־אֵ֥יד לְעַוָּ֑ל וְ֝נֵ֗כֶר לְפֹ֣עֲלֵי אָֽוֶן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Is not destruction due to the wicked? and a strange punishment to the workers of iniquity?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Is destruction not befitting the churl [(and not me)], and estrangement the workers of iniquity?


###### Job 31:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:4)


הֲלֹא־ה֭וּא יִרְאֶ֣ה דְרָכָ֑י וְֽכׇל־צְעָדַ֥י יִסְפּֽוֹר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Does he not see my ways, and count all my steps?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Does He not see my ways and count all my steps


###### Job 31:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:5)


אִם־הָלַ֥כְתִּי עִם־שָׁ֑וְא וַתַּ֖חַשׁ עַל־מִרְמָ֣ה רַגְלִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If I have walked with vanity, or if my foot hath hasted to deceit;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[to know] whether I have walked with vain men or whether my feet have hastened to deceit?


###### Job 31:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:6)


יִשְׁקְלֵ֥נִי בְמֹֽאזְנֵי־צֶ֑דֶק וְיֵדַ֥ע אֱ֝ל֗וֹהַּ תֻּמָּתִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
let me be weighed in an even balance, that God may know my integrity.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would that He weighed me in righteous scales, so that G d would know my innocence.


###### Job 31:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:7)


אִ֥ם־תִּטֶּ֣ה אַשֻּׁרִי֮ מִנִּ֢י הַ֫דָּ֥רֶךְ וְאַחַ֣ר עֵ֭ינַי הָלַ֣ךְ לִבִּ֑י וּ֝בְכַפַּ֗י דָּ֣בַק מֽאוּם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If my step has turned out of the way, and my heart walked after my eyes, and if anything has cleaved to my hands;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If my feet have strayed from the [proper] path, or if my heart went astray after my eyes, or if any blemish stuck to my palm,


###### Job 31:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:8)


אֶ֭זְרְעָה וְאַחֵ֣ר יֹאכֵ֑ל וְֽצֶאֱצָאַ֥י יְשֹׁרָֽשׁוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then let me sow, and let another eat; yea, let my offspring be rooted out.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
then let me sow and another eat, and let my issue be uprooted.


###### Job 31:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:9)


אִם־נִפְתָּ֣ה לִ֭בִּי עַל־אִשָּׁ֑ה וְעַל־פֶּ֖תַח רֵעִ֣י אָרָֽבְתִּי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If my heart has been deceived by a woman, or if I have laid wait at my neighbour’s door;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If my heart was enticed by a woman and I lurked at my neighbor's door [(for his wife)],


###### Job 31:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:10)


תִּטְחַ֣ן לְאַחֵ֣ר אִשְׁתִּ֑י וְ֝עָלֶ֗יהָ יִכְרְע֥וּן אֲחֵרִֽין׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then let my wife grind for another, and let others bow down upon her.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
then let my wife be "ground" by another and let others kneel over her.


###### Job 31:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:11)


כִּי־[הִ֥יא] (הוא) זִמָּ֑ה (והיא) [וְ֝ה֗וּא] עָוֺ֥ן פְּלִילִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For that would be a heinous crime; yea, it would be iniquity to be punished by the judges.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For it [(such thought)] is lewdness and is a sin deserving punishment [(by affliction)].


###### Job 31:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:12)


כִּ֤י אֵ֣שׁ הִ֭יא עַד־אֲבַדּ֣וֹן תֹּאכֵ֑ל וּֽבְכׇל־תְּב֖וּאָתִ֣י תְשָׁרֵֽשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For it is a fire that consumes to Avaddon, and would root out all my increase.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For it is a fire that consumes everything, and it would uproot all of my produce [(wherefore I shunned it)].


###### Job 31:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:13)


אִם־אֶמְאַ֗ס מִשְׁפַּ֣ט עַ֭בְדִּי וַאֲמָתִ֑י בְּ֝רִבָ֗ם עִמָּדִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If I did despise the cause of my manservant or of my maidservant, when they contended with me;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Did I despise the complaint of my man-servant or maid-servant in their dispute with me?


###### Job 31:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:14)


וּמָ֣ה אֶ֭עֱשֶׂה כִּֽי־יָק֣וּם אֵ֑ל וְכִי־יִ֝פְקֹ֗ד מָ֣ה אֲשִׁיבֶֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
what then shall I do when God rises up? and when he remembers, what shall I answer him?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[No, rather I said:] What will I do if G d rises up [against me]? And if He reckons [this sin against me], how will I answer Him?


###### Job 31:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:15)


הֲֽלֹא־בַ֭בֶּטֶן עֹשֵׂ֣נִי עָשָׂ֑הוּ וַ֝יְכֻנֶ֗נּוּ בָּרֶ֥חֶם אֶחָֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Did not he who made me in the belly make him? and did not the same one fashion us in the womb?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Did He not make him in the womb [of his mother as] He made me [in the womb of mine]? And one [G d] framed both of us] in the womb.


###### Job 31:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:16)


אִם־אֶ֭מְנַע מֵחֵ֣פֶץ דַּלִּ֑ים וְעֵינֵ֖י אַלְמָנָ֣ה אֲכַלֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If I have withheld the poor from their desire, or have caused the eyes of the widow to fail;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Have I withheld the desire of the poor? Have I made the eyes of the widow go wanting?


###### Job 31:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:17)


וְאֹכַ֣ל פִּתִּ֣י לְבַדִּ֑י וְלֹֽא־אָכַ֖ל יָת֣וֹם מִמֶּֽנָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
or have eaten my morsel myself alone, and the fatherless have not eaten thereof;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Did I eat my loaf alone and have no orphan eat of it?


###### Job 31:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:18)


כִּ֣י מִ֭נְּעוּרַי גְּדֵלַ֣נִי כְאָ֑ב וּמִבֶּ֖טֶן אִמִּ֣י אַנְחֶֽנָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
(for from my youth he was brought up with me, as with a father, and I have guided her from my mother’s womb;)


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But from my youth it [(the trait of charitableness)] has raised me as a father, and from the womb of my mother I have pursued it.


###### Job 31:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:19)


אִם־אֶרְאֶ֣ה א֭וֹבֵד מִבְּלִ֣י לְב֑וּשׁ וְאֵ֥ין כְּ֝ס֗וּת לָאֶבְיֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
if I have seen any perish for want of clothing, or any poor without covering;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If I saw a lost one without a garment, or a poor man with no covering,


###### Job 31:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:20)


אִם־לֹ֣א בֵרְכ֣וּנִי חֲלָצָ֑ו וּמִגֵּ֥ז כְּ֝בָשַׂ֗י יִתְחַמָּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
if his loins have not blessed me, and if he were not warmed with the fleece of my sheep;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
did his descendants not bless me? Was he not warmed with the shearings of my sheep?


###### Job 31:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:21)


אִם־הֲנִיפ֣וֹתִי עַל־יָת֣וֹם יָדִ֑י כִּֽי־אֶרְאֶ֥ה בַ֝שַּׁ֗עַר עֶזְרָתִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
if I have lifted up my hand against the fatherless, even though I saw my help in the gate:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If I [ever] raised my hand against the orphan, seeing my defense [i.e., that I would be acquitted] in the gate [of justice],


###### Job 31:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:22)


כְּ֭תֵפִי מִשִּׁכְמָ֣הֿ תִפּ֑וֹל וְ֝אֶזְרֹעִ֗י מִקָּנָ֥הֿ תִשָּׁבֵֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then let my arm fall from my shoulder blade, and my arm be broken from the bone.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
then let my arm fall from my shoulder and my forearm be broken in its socket.


###### Job 31:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:23)


כִּ֤י פַ֣חַד אֵ֭לַי אֵ֣יד אֵ֑ל וּ֝מִשְּׂאֵת֗וֹ לֹ֣א אוּכָֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For destruction from God was a terror to me, and by reason of his majesty I could do nothing.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For I was in fear of G d's punishment, and I was restrained by His majesty.


###### Job 31:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:24)


אִם־שַׂ֣מְתִּי זָהָ֣ב כִּסְלִ֑י וְ֝לַכֶּ֗תֶם אָמַ֥רְתִּי מִבְטַחִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If I have made gold my hope, or have said to the fine gold, Thou art my confidence;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Did I make gold my hope and regard pure gold as my defense [(in cowing the poor)]?


###### Job 31:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:25)


אִם־אֶ֭שְׂמַח כִּֽי־רַ֣ב חֵילִ֑י וְכִֽי־כַ֝בִּ֗יר מָצְאָ֥ה יָדִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
if I rejoiced because my wealth was great, and because my hand had gotten much;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Did I rejoice in the abundance of my wealth or in the vast acquisitions of my hand [(causing the poor to envy me)]?


###### Job 31:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:26)


אִם־אֶרְאֶ֣ה א֭וֹר כִּ֣י יָהֵ֑ל וְ֝יָרֵ֗חַ יָקָ֥ר הֹלֵֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
if I beheld the sun when it shone, or the moon walking in brightness;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When I saw the sun in its strength and the moon waxing in brightness,


###### Job 31:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:27)


וַיִּ֣פְתְּ בַּסֵּ֣תֶר לִבִּ֑י וַתִּשַּׁ֖ק יָדִ֣י לְפִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and my heart had been secretly enticed, or my mouth had kissed my hand:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
was my heart enticed in secret [(to regard them as deities)], and did I press my hand to my mouth [(to keep my heart from being "overheard")]?


###### Job 31:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:28)


גַּם־ה֭וּא עָוֺ֣ן פְּלִילִ֑י כִּֽי־כִחַ֖שְׁתִּי לָאֵ֣ל מִמָּֽעַל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
this also were an iniquity to be punished by the judges: for I should have betrayed the God that is above.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
That, too, would have been a sin deserving of punishment, for I would have been denying G d on high.


###### Job 31:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:29)


אִם־אֶ֭שְׂמַח בְּפִ֣יד מְשַׂנְאִ֑י וְ֝הִתְעֹרַ֗רְתִּי כִּֽי־מְצָ֥אוֹ רָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Did I rejoice at the destruction of him who hates me, or did I exult when evil found him?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Did I rejoice in the downfall of my enemy and rise against him when he was beset with ill?


###### Job 31:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:30)


וְלֹא־נָתַ֣תִּי לַחֲטֹ֣א חִכִּ֑י לִשְׁאֹ֖ל בְּאָלָ֣ה נַפְשֽׁוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Indeed I have not suffered my mouth to sin by wishing a curse to his soul.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I did not [even] allow my palate to sin [against him] by demanding his soul through an oath.


###### Job 31:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:31)


אִם־לֹ֣א אָ֭מְרוּ מְתֵ֣י אׇהֳלִ֑י מִי־יִתֵּ֥ן מִ֝בְּשָׂר֗וֹ לֹ֣א נִשְׂבָּֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Have not the men of my tent said, Oh that we had of his flesh! we cannot be satisfied?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Did the people of my tent [i.e., my servants] not say [(about me because of my "burdening" them with the duties of hospitality)]: "Would that we could eat of his flesh! We would not be sated"?


###### Job 31:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:32)


בַּ֭חוּץ לֹא־יָלִ֣ין גֵּ֑ר דְּ֝לָתַ֗י לָאֹ֥רַח אֶפְתָּֽח׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the stranger did not lodge in the street: I opened my doors to the traveller.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The stranger would never lie outside [my house]. My doors were open to the wayfarer.


###### Job 31:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:33)


אִם־כִּסִּ֣יתִי כְאָדָ֣ם פְּשָׁעָ֑י לִטְמ֖וֹן בְּחֻבִּ֣י עֲוֺנִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Have I covered my transgressions like Adam, by hiding my iniquity in my bosom?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Did I conceal my offenses as other men do, hiding my sins in secret places?


###### Job 31:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:34)


כִּ֤י אֶעֱר֨וֹץ**׀** הָ֘מ֤וֹן רַבָּ֗ה וּבוּז־מִשְׁפָּח֥וֹת יְחִתֵּ֑נִי וָ֝אֶדֹּ֗ם לֹא־אֵ֥צֵא פָֽתַח׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Did I fear the great multitude, or did the contempt of families terrify me? but I kept silence, and went not out of the door.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(In the past)] I humbled a multitude [(of the wicked, but now)] the most despised of families frightens me, and I must be still and not leave my door.


###### Job 31:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:35)


מִ֤י יִתֶּן־לִ֨י**׀** שֹׁ֘מֵ֤עַֽ לִ֗י הֶן־תָּ֭וִי שַׁדַּ֣י יַעֲנֵ֑נִי וְסֵ֥פֶר כָּ֝תַ֗ב אִ֣ישׁ רִיבִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Oh that one would hear me! here is my mark, let the Almighty answer me! and would that my adversary would pen his writ!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would that someone listened to me! Let me [i.e., a true account of me] be written down. May the Almighty grant me [this], and let my disputant [too] write [down his argument in] a book.


###### Job 31:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:36)


אִם־לֹ֣א עַל־שִׁ֭כְמִי אֶשָּׂאֶ֑נּוּ אֶעֶנְדֶ֖נּוּ עֲטָר֣וֹת לִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Surely I would take it upon my shoulder, and bind it as a crown to me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In truth, I would carry him [(the writer of my book)] on my shoulders. I would wear it [(my book)] as a crown.


###### Job 31:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:37)


מִסְפַּ֣ר צְ֭עָדַי אַגִּידֶ֑נּוּ כְּמוֹ־נָ֝גִ֗יד אֲקָֽרְבֶֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I would declare to him the number of my steps; as a prince would I go near to him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I would tell him the number of my steps and draw him close, as a dignitary.


###### Job 31:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:38)


אִם־עָ֭לַי אַדְמָתִ֣י תִזְעָ֑ק וְ֝יַ֗חַד תְּלָמֶ֥יהָ יִבְכָּיֽוּן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If my land cry against me, or its furrows complain together;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If my land cries out against me [(for not having tithed its produce)] and its furrows bewail themselves [(for having plowed with ox and ass)] together —


###### Job 31:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:39)


אִם־כֹּ֭חָהּ אָכַ֣לְתִּי בְלִי־כָ֑סֶף וְנֶ֖פֶשׁ בְּעָלֶ֣יהָ הִפָּֽחְתִּי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
if I have eaten its fruits without money, or have caused its owners to sigh:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If I have consumed its strength without pay [(for its workers)] and have aggrieved the soul of its owner [(the tenant-farmer, who works it)] —


###### Job 31:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/31:40)


תַּ֤חַת חִטָּ֨ה**׀** יֵ֥צֵא ח֗וֹחַ וְתַֽחַת־שְׂעֹרָ֥ה בׇאְשָׁ֑ה תַּ֝֗מּוּ דִּבְרֵ֥י אִיּֽוֹב׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
let thistles grow instead of wheat, and cockle instead of barley. The words of Iyyov are ended.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
then in place of wheat let thistles grow, and in place of barley, cockles. The words of Iyyov are ended.



## Chapter 32

###### Job 32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:1)


וַֽיִּשְׁבְּת֡וּ שְׁלֹ֤שֶׁת הָאֲנָשִׁ֣ים הָ֭אֵלֶּה מֵעֲנ֣וֹת אֶת־אִיּ֑וֹב כִּ֤י ה֖וּא צַדִּ֣יק בְּעֵינָֽיו׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So these three men ceased to answer Iyyov, because he was righteous in his own eyes.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then the three men ceased answering Iyyov because he was righteous in his own eyes.


###### Job 32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:2)


וַיִּ֤חַר אַ֨ף**׀** אֱלִיה֣וּא בֶן־בַּרַכְאֵ֣ל הַבּוּזִי֮ מִמִּשְׁפַּ֢חַ֫ת רָ֥ם בְּ֭אִיּוֹב חָרָ֣ה אַפּ֑וֹ עַֽל־צַדְּק֥וֹ נַ֝פְשׁ֗וֹ מֵאֱלֹהִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the wrath of Elihu the son of Barakh᾽el the Buzite, of the family of Ram burned: against Iyyov his anger burned, because he justified himself rather than God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Elihu, the son of Berachel the Buzite, from the family of Ram [(Abraham)], was angered at Iyyov. His wrath burned [against Iyyov] because he had made himself more righteous than G d.


###### Job 32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:3)


וּבִשְׁלֹ֣שֶׁת רֵעָיו֮ חָרָ֢ה אַ֫פּ֥וֹ עַ֤ל אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹא־מָצְא֣וּ מַעֲנֶ֑ה וַ֝יַּרְשִׁ֗יעוּ אֶת־אִיּֽוֹב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Also against his three friends did his anger burn, because they had found no answer, and yet had condemned Iyyov.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And his wrath burned against his three friends because they had found no answer and had [thus] condemned "Iyyov" [(euphemism for "G d")].


###### Job 32:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:4)


וֶאֱלִיה֗וּ חִכָּ֣ה אֶת־אִ֭יּוֹב בִּדְבָרִ֑ים כִּ֤י זְֽקֵנִים־הֵ֖מָּה מִמֶּ֣נּוּ לְיָמִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now Elihu had waited to speak to Iyyov because they were older than he.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Elihu had held back his words against Iyyov, for they [(the three)] were older than he in days.


###### Job 32:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:5)


וַיַּ֤רְא אֱלִיה֗וּא כִּ֘י־אֵ֤ין מַעֲנֶ֗ה בְּ֭פִי שְׁלֹ֥שֶׁת הָאֲנָשִׁ֗ים וַיִּ֥חַר אַפּֽוֹ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When Elihu saw that there was no answer in the mouth of these three men, then his anger burned.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But when Elihu saw that the three men had no answer, he was angered.


###### Job 32:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:6)


וַיַּ֤עַן**׀** אֱלִיה֖וּא בֶן־בַּרַכְאֵ֥ל הַבּוּזִ֗י וַיֹּ֫אמַ֥ר צָ֘עִ֤יר אֲנִ֣י לְ֭יָמִים וְאַתֶּ֣ם יְשִׁישִׁ֑ים עַל־כֵּ֖ן זָחַ֥לְתִּי וָאִירָ֓א**׀** מֵחַוֺּ֖ת דֵּעִ֣י אֶתְכֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Elihu the son of Barakh᾽el the Buzite answered and said, I am young, and you are very old; therefore I was afraid, and durst not declare my opinion to you.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Elihu, the son of Berachel the Buzite, answered, saying: I am young in days, and you are old. That is why I was apprehensive and feared expressing my views together with yours.


###### Job 32:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:7)


אָ֭מַרְתִּי יָמִ֣ים יְדַבֵּ֑רוּ וְרֹ֥ב שָׁ֝נִ֗ים יֹדִ֥יעוּ חׇכְמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I said, Days should speak, and multitude of years should teach wisdom.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I said [to myself]: ["Just as the passage of] days [teaches one to] speak, [so the passage of] many years [must] teach [one] wisdom."


###### Job 32:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:8)


אָ֭כֵן רוּחַ־הִ֣יא בֶאֱנ֑וֹשׁ וְנִשְׁמַ֖ת שַׁדַּ֣י תְּבִינֵֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But there is a spirit in man: and the breath of the Almighty gives them understanding.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But [I see now that] it is a spirit [(the spirit of G d)] in man [which teaches wisdom] and the soul [bequeathed them by] the Almighty [which makes] them understand.


###### Job 32:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:9)


לֹא־רַבִּ֥ים יֶחְכָּ֑מוּ וּ֝זְקֵנִ֗ים יָבִ֥ינוּ מִשְׁפָּֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The old are not always wise: nor do the aged understand judgment.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Therefore,] not many are wise, and the elderly [do not necessarily] understand judgment.


###### Job 32:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:10)


לָכֵ֣ן אָ֭מַרְתִּי שִׁמְעָה־לִּ֑י אֲחַוֶּ֖ה דֵעִ֣י אַף־אָֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore I said, Hearken to me; I also will express my opinion.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
That is why I have said: "Listen to me; I, too, will speak my mind."


###### Job 32:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:11)


הֵ֤ן הוֹחַ֨לְתִּי**׀** לְֽדִבְרֵיכֶ֗ם אָ֭זִין עַד־תְּב֥וּנֹֽתֵיכֶ֑ם עַֽד־תַּחְקְר֥וּן מִלִּֽין׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, I waited for your words; I gave ear to your reasons, whilst you searched out what to say,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I have waited for your words. I have listened until [the end of] your presentations, until [the end of] your probings of words.


###### Job 32:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:12)


וְעָ֥דֵיכֶ֗ם אֶתְבּ֫וֹנָ֥ן וְהִנֵּ֤ה אֵ֣ין לְאִיּ֣וֹב מוֹכִ֑יחַ עוֹנֶ֖ה אֲמָרָ֣יו מִכֶּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yea, I attended unto you, and, behold, there was none of you that convinced Iyyov, or that answered his words.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I have reflected upon your words, and, behold, there is no reprover of Iyyov. [None] among you answers his words.


###### Job 32:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:13)


פֶּן־תֹּ֣֭אמְרוּ מָצָ֣אנוּ חׇכְמָ֑ה אֵ֖ל יִדְּפֶ֣נּוּ לֹא־אִֽישׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Beware lest you say, We have found out wisdom: God will thrust him down, not man.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not say: "We have found wisdom [in our silence, not to provoke him further]; let G d repel him, not man" [(for you thereby detract from G d's honor)].


###### Job 32:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:14)


וְלֹא־עָרַ֣ךְ אֵלַ֣י מִלִּ֑ין וּ֝בְאִמְרֵיכֶ֗ם לֹ֣א אֲשִׁיבֶֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now he has not directed his words against me: so that I will not answer him with your speeches.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would that he had argued with me! I would not have answered him with your words.


###### Job 32:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:15)


חַ֭תּוּ לֹא־עָ֣נוּ ע֑וֹד הֶעְתִּ֖יקוּ מֵהֶ֣ם מִלִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They are amazed, they answer no more: they have left off speaking.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They [(the friends)] were broken [by Iyyov's rejoinders]. They answered no more. Words departed from them.


###### Job 32:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:16)


וְ֭הוֹחַלְתִּי כִּי־לֹ֣א יְדַבֵּ֑רוּ כִּ֥י עָ֝מְד֗וּ לֹא־עָ֥נוּ עֽוֹד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And shall I wait because they do not speak; because they stand there, and answer no more?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I waited until they would speak no more, until they were silent and answered no more.


###### Job 32:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:17)


אַעֲנֶ֣ה אַף־אֲנִ֣י חֶלְקִ֑י אֲחַוֶּ֖ה דֵעִ֣י אַף־אָֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I also will answer my part, I also will express my opinion.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Now,] I, too, will speak my part. I, too, will express my view.


###### Job 32:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:18)


כִּ֭י מָלֵ֣תִי מִלִּ֑ים הֱ֝צִיקַ֗תְנִי ר֣וּחַ בִּטְנִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For I am full of words; the spirit within me constrains me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For I am full with words. The swelling of my stomach oppresses me.


###### Job 32:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:19)


הִנֵּֽה־בִטְנִ֗י כְּיַ֥יִן לֹֽא־יִפָּתֵ֑חַ כְּאֹב֥וֹת חֲ֝דָשִׁ֗ים יִבָּקֵֽעַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, my belly is like wine which has no vent; it is ready to burst like new wineskins.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My stomach is [swelling with rejoinders] like an unopened wine [skin], like skins of new [wine], ready to burst.


###### Job 32:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:20)


אֲדַבְּרָ֥ה וְיִֽרְוַֽח־לִ֑י אֶפְתַּ֖ח שְׂפָתַ֣י וְאֶעֱנֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I will speak, that I may find relief: I will open my lips and answer.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I will speak and I will be eased; I will open my lips and my answers will come forth.


###### Job 32:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:21)


אַל־נָ֭א אֶשָּׂ֣א פְנֵי־אִ֑ישׁ וְאֶל־אָ֝דָ֗ם לֹ֣א אֲכַנֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let me not, I pray you, respect any man’s person, nor let me give flattering titles to any man.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I will not now court anyone's favor, and I will refer to no man obliquely [(but directly)].


###### Job 32:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/32:22)


כִּ֤י לֹ֣א יָדַ֣עְתִּי אֲכַנֶּ֑ה כִּ֝מְעַ֗ט יִשָּׂאֵ֥נִי עֹשֵֽׂנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For I do not know how to flatter; or else my maker would soon take me away.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For I do not know indirection. My Maker would almost remove me [(from the world for it)].



## Chapter 33

###### Job 33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:1)


וְֽאוּלָ֗ם שְׁמַֽע־נָ֣א אִיּ֣וֹב מִלָּ֑י וְֽכׇל־דְּבָרַ֥י הַאֲזִֽינָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But now, Iyyov, I pray thee, hear my speeches, and hearken to all my words.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But hear my words, Iyyov, and listen to all that I say.


###### Job 33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:2)


הִנֵּה־נָ֭א פָּתַ֣חְתִּי פִ֑י דִּבְּרָ֖ה לְשׁוֹנִ֣י בְחִכִּֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, now I have opened my mouth, my tongue has spoken in my mouth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I will now open my mouth, and my tongue will speak upon my palate.


###### Job 33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:3)


יֹשֶׁר־לִבִּ֥י אֲמָרָ֑י וְדַ֥עַת שְׂ֝פָתַ֗י בָּר֥וּר מִלֵּֽלוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My words shall be of the uprightness of my heart: and my lips shall utter knowledge in purity.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My words are [from] the uprightness of my heart, and my lips will speak clear knowledge.


###### Job 33:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:4)


רֽוּחַ־אֵ֥ל עָשָׂ֑תְנִי וְנִשְׁמַ֖ת שַׁדַּ֣י תְּחַיֵּֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The spirit of God has made me, and the breath of the Almighty has given me life.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The will of G d made me [(and I am not stronger than you)], and the breath of the Almighty will sustain me.


###### Job 33:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:5)


אִם־תּוּכַ֥ל הֲשִׁיבֵ֑נִי עֶרְכָ֥הֿ לְ֝פָנַ֗י הִתְיַצָּֽבָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If thou canst, answer me; set thy words in order before me; stand up.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If you can, answer me. Prepare your case and stand up against me.


###### Job 33:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:6)


הֵן־אֲנִ֣י כְפִ֣יךָ לָאֵ֑ל מֵ֝חֹ֗מֶר קֹרַ֥צְתִּי גַם־אָֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, I am towards God as thou art: I also am formed out of the clay.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Here am I, as you requested, to argue G d's case [(without your being confounded by Him [viz. 13:21])]. I, too, have been hewn from clay.


###### Job 33:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:7)


הִנֵּ֣ה אֵ֭מָתִי לֹ֣א תְבַעֲתֶ֑ךָּ וְ֝אַכְפִּ֗י עָלֶ֥יךָ לֹֽא־יִכְבָּֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, my terror shall not make thee afraid, nor shall my pressure be heavy upon thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Fear of me will not confound you, and my compulsion will not bear down upon you.


###### Job 33:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:8)


אַ֭ךְ אָמַ֣רְתָּ בְאׇזְנָ֑י וְק֖וֹל מִלִּ֣ין אֶשְׁמָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Surely thou hast spoken in my hearing, and I have heard the voice of thy words, saying,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But what you said came to my ears, and I heard [only] the sound [(not the senses)] of words:


###### Job 33:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:9)


זַ֥ךְ אֲנִ֗י בְּֽלִ֫י־פָ֥שַׁע חַ֥ף אָנֹכִ֑י וְלֹ֖א עָוֺ֣ן לִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I am clean without transgression, I am innocent; neither is there iniquity in me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
"I am pure, without offense; I am clean, without transgression."


###### Job 33:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:10)


הֵ֣ן תְּ֭נוּאוֹת עָלַ֣י יִמְצָ֑א יַחְשְׁבֵ֖נִי לְאוֹיֵ֣ב לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, he finds occasions against me, he counts me for his enemy.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
"He has found pretexts against me; He reckons me His foe."


###### Job 33:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:11)


יָשֵׂ֣ם בַּסַּ֣ד רַגְלָ֑י יִ֝שְׁמֹ֗ר כׇּל־אׇרְחֹתָֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He puts my feet in the stocks, he marks all my paths.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
"He has put my feet in stocks; He guards all my ways."


###### Job 33:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:12)


הֶן־זֹ֣את לֹא־צָדַ֣קְתָּ אֶעֱנֶ֑ךָּ כִּי־יִרְבֶּ֥ה אֱ֝ל֗וֹהַּ מֵאֱנֽוֹשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, in this thou art not right: I will answer thee, that God is greater than man.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Only this shall I answer you: that G d is [far] more righteous than man [(and He is not overly severe with you)].


###### Job 33:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:13)


מַ֭דּוּעַ אֵלָ֣יו רִיב֑וֹתָ כִּ֥י כׇל־דְּ֝בָרָ֗יו לֹ֣א יַעֲנֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Why dost thou strive against him, seeing that none of his words will answer you.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Why did you argue against Him that He does not answer for all of His ways?


###### Job 33:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:14)


כִּֽי־בְאַחַ֥ת יְדַבֶּר־אֵ֑ל וּ֝בִשְׁתַּ֗יִם לֹ֣א יְשׁוּרֶֽנָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Surely God speaks once, yea twice, yet man perceives it not.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For G d speaks in one way — and in a second, for him who does not see [the first].


###### Job 33:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:15)


בַּחֲל֤וֹם**׀** חֶזְי֬וֹן לַ֗יְלָה בִּנְפֹ֣ל תַּ֭רְדֵּמָה עַל־אֲנָשִׁ֑ים בִּ֝תְנוּמ֗וֹת עֲלֵ֣י מִשְׁכָּֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In a dream, in a vision of the night, when deep sleep falls upon men, in slumberings upon the bed,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In a dream, a vision of the night, when deep sleep falls upon men, or when they slumber upon their couch,


###### Job 33:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:16)


אָ֣ז יִ֭גְלֶה אֹ֣זֶן אֲנָשִׁ֑ים וּבְמֹ֖סָרָ֣ם יַחְתֹּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then he opens the ears of men, and with discipline seals their instruction,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
then He uncovers the ear of men [(as in the instance of Avimelech [viz. Genesis 20])], and in their afflictions He seals [them].


###### Job 33:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:17)


לְ֭הָסִיר אָדָ֣ם מַעֲשֶׂ֑ה וְגֵוָ֖ה מִגֶּ֣בֶר יְכַסֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that he may withdraw man from his purpose, and hide pride from man.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To remove a man from his [contemplated] deed and to conceal [i.e., to remove] haughtiness from a man [(and so spare him from death)].


###### Job 33:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:18)


יַחְשֹׂ֣ךְ נַ֭פְשׁוֹ מִנִּי־שָׁ֑חַת וְ֝חַיָּת֗וֹ מֵעֲבֹ֥ר בַּשָּֽׁלַח׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He keeps back his soul from the pit, and his life from perishing by the sword.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He [thus] saves his soul from the pit, and his life from passing [under] the sword [(of the Angel of Death)].


###### Job 33:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:19)


וְהוּכַ֣ח בְּ֭מַכְאוֹב עַל־מִשְׁכָּב֑וֹ (וריב) [וְר֖וֹב] עֲצָמָ֣יו אֵתָֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He is chastened also with pain upon his bed, and the multitude of his bones with strong pain:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He is chastised with pain upon his couch [when] most of his bones are [yet] strong.


###### Job 33:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:20)


וְזִהֲמַ֣תּוּ חַיָּת֣וֹ לָ֑חֶם וְ֝נַפְשׁ֗וֹ מַאֲכַ֥ל תַּאֲוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
so that his life abhors bread, and his soul dainty food.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then his soul abhors bread, and his spirit, [all] savory food.


###### Job 33:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:21)


יִ֣כֶל בְּשָׂר֣וֹ מֵרֹ֑אִי (ושפי) [וְשֻׁפּ֥וּ] עַ֝צְמֹתָ֗יו לֹ֣א רֻאּֽוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
His flesh is consumed away, that it cannot be seen; and his bones that were not seen stick out.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
His flesh dwindles from sight, and his bones slip [(from their sockets) until] they are no [longer] visible.


###### Job 33:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:22)


וַתִּקְרַ֣ב לַשַּׁ֣חַת נַפְשׁ֑וֹ וְ֝חַיָּת֗וֹ לַֽמְמִתִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For his soul draws near to the pit, and his life to the destroyers.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And then his spirit draws near to the pit, and his soul [draws near] to the killers [(the messengers of the Angel of Death)].


###### Job 33:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:23)


אִם־יֵ֤שׁ עָלָ֨יו**׀** מַלְאָ֗ךְ מֵלִ֗יץ אֶחָ֥ד מִנִּי־אָ֑לֶף לְהַגִּ֖יד לְאָדָ֣ם יׇשְׁרֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If there be an angel over him, an interpreter, one among a thousand, to declare to man what is right:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If there be for him one defending angel among a thousand [accusers] to speak of a man's righteousness,


###### Job 33:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:24)


וַיְחֻנֶּ֗נּוּ וַיֹּ֗אמֶר פְּ֭דָעֵהוּ מֵרֶ֥דֶת שָׁ֗חַת מָצָ֥אתִי כֹֽפֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then he is gracious to him, and says, Deliver him from going down to the pit: I have found a ransom.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
then He is gracious to him and says: "Redeem him from descending to the pit; I have found atonement."


###### Job 33:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:25)


רֻֽטְפַ֣שׁ בְּשָׂר֣וֹ מִנֹּ֑עַר יָ֝שׁ֗וּב לִימֵ֥י עֲלוּמָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
His flesh shall be smoother than a child’s: he shall return to the days of his youth:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
His flesh will cease from its convulsions. He will return to the days of his youth.


###### Job 33:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:26)


יֶעְתַּ֤ר אֶל־אֱל֨וֹהַּ**׀** וַיִּרְצֵ֗הוּ וַיַּ֣רְא פָּ֭נָיו בִּתְרוּעָ֑ה וַיָּ֥שֶׁב לֶ֝אֱנ֗וֹשׁ צִדְקָתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
he shall pray to God, and he will be favourable to him: and he shall see his face with joy: for he will render to man his righteousness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when he entreats G d and courts His favor and beholds His face with prayer, then He will return to [i.e., accord to] the man [reward for] his righteousness.


###### Job 33:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:27)


יָשֹׁ֤ר**׀** עַל־אֲנָשִׁ֗ים וַיֹּ֗אמֶר חָ֭טָאתִי וְיָשָׁ֥ר הֶעֱוֵ֗יתִי וְלֹא־שָׁ֥וָה לִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He should then assemble a row of men, and say, I have sinned, and perverted that which was right, and it profited me not.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(And when upon recovery from his illness)] he makes rows of men [(and passes through them)] saying: "I have sinned and have perverted what is just and have not profited by it,"


###### Job 33:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:28)


פָּדָ֣ה (נפשי) [נַ֭פְשׁוֹ] מֵעֲבֹ֣ר בַּשָּׁ֑חַת (וחיתי) [וְ֝חַיָּת֗וֹ] בָּא֥וֹר תִּרְאֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thus he will redeem his soul from going into the pit, and his life shall see the light.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He will save his spirit from passing to the pit, and his soul will see the light.


###### Job 33:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:29)


הֶן־כׇּל־אֵ֭לֶּה יִפְעַל־אֵ֑ל פַּעֲמַ֖יִם שָׁל֣וֹשׁ עִם־גָּֽבֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Lo, God does all these things twice or three times with a man,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All of this G d will do two or three times with a man.


###### Job 33:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:30)


לְהָשִׁ֣יב נַ֭פְשׁוֹ מִנִּי־שָׁ֑חַת לֵ֝א֗וֹר בְּא֣וֹר הַחַיִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to bring back his soul from the pit, to be enlightened with the light of the living.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To turn his soul from the pit to be illumined in the light of life.


###### Job 33:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:31)


הַקְשֵׁ֖ב אִיּ֥וֹב שְֽׁמַֽע־לִ֑י הַ֝חֲרֵ֗שׁ וְאָנֹכִ֥י אֲדַבֵּֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Mark well, O Iyyov, hearken to me: hold thy peace, and I will speak.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Listen, Iyyov, hear me. Be still and I will speak.


###### Job 33:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:32)


אִם־יֵשׁ־מִלִּ֥ין הֲשִׁיבֵ֑נִי דַּ֝בֵּ֗ר כִּֽי־חָפַ֥צְתִּי צַדְּקֶֽךָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If thou hast anything to say, answer me: speak, for I desire to justify thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If you have words, answer me; for I desire to vindicate you.


###### Job 33:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/33:33)


אִם־אַ֭יִן אַתָּ֥ה שְֽׁמַֽע־לִ֑י הַ֝חֲרֵ֗שׁ וַאֲאַלֶּפְךָ֥ חׇכְמָֽה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If not, hearken to me: hold thy peace, and I shall teach thee wisdom.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If not, then you listen to me. Be still, and I will teach you wisdom.



## Chapter 34

###### Job 34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:1)


וַיַּ֥עַן אֱלִיה֗וּא וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Furthermore, Elihu answered and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Elihu raised his voice and said:


###### Job 34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:2)


שִׁמְע֣וּ חֲכָמִ֣ים מִלָּ֑י וְ֝יֹדְעִ֗ים הַאֲזִ֥ינוּ לִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hear my words, O wise men; and give ear to me, you who have knowledge.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Listen, you wise ones, to my words; you knowing ones, give ear to me,


###### Job 34:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:3)


כִּי־אֹ֭זֶן מִלִּ֣ין תִּבְחָ֑ן וְ֝חֵ֗ךְ יִטְעַ֥ם לֶאֱכֹֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the ear tries words, as the palate tastes food.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
as the ear discriminates words, and as the palate tastes food.


###### Job 34:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:4)


מִשְׁפָּ֥ט נִבְחֲרָה־לָּ֑נוּ נֵדְעָ֖ה בֵינֵ֣ינוּ מַה־טּֽוֹב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let us choose what is right: let us know among ourselves what is good.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Let us choose a criterion for ourselves. Let us know among ourselves what is good.


###### Job 34:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:5)


כִּֽי־אָמַ֣ר אִיּ֣וֹב צָדַ֑קְתִּי וְ֝אֵ֗ל הֵסִ֥יר מִשְׁפָּטִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For Iyyov has said, I am righteous: and God has taken away my right:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For Iyyov has said: "I am just, and G d has removed my plea.


###### Job 34:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:6)


עַל־מִשְׁפָּטִ֥י אֲכַזֵּ֑ב אָנ֖וּשׁ חִצִּ֣י בְלִי־פָֽשַׁע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
notwithstanding my right I am counted a liar; my wound is incurable, though I am without transgression.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As to my judgment, I must belie [my Judge (for He has not judged me truly)]. My wound is grievous, without offense."


###### Job 34:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:7)


מִי־גֶ֥בֶר כְּאִיּ֑וֹב יִֽשְׁתֶּה־לַּ֥עַג כַּמָּֽיִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
What man is like Iyyov, who drinks up scorning like water?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Is there another man like Iyyov, who drinks mockery as [one would drink] water?


###### Job 34:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:8)


וְאָרַ֣ח לְ֭חֶבְרָה עִם־פֹּ֣עֲלֵי אָ֑וֶן וְ֝לָלֶ֗כֶת עִם־אַנְשֵׁי־רֶֽשַׁע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who goes in company with the workers of iniquity, and walks with wicked men.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who walks the path to join with workers of iniquity and to go with men of evil?


###### Job 34:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:9)


כִּֽי־אָ֭מַר לֹ֣א יִסְכׇּן־גָּ֑בֶר בִּ֝רְצֹת֗וֹ עִם־אֱלֹהִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For he has said, It profits a man nothing that he should be in accord with God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who says it profits not a man to desire [to go] with G d?


###### Job 34:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:10)


לָכֵ֤ן**׀** אַ֥נְשֵׁ֥י לֵבָ֗ב שִׁמְע֫וּ־לִ֥י חָלִ֖לָה לָאֵ֥ל מֵרֶ֗שַׁע וְשַׁדַּ֥י מֵעָֽוֶל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore hearken to me, you men of understanding: far be it from God, that he should do wickedness; and from the Almighty, that he should commit iniquity.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Therefore, men of wisdom, listen to me. Far be it from G d to do evil, and for the Almighty to do wrong.


###### Job 34:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:11)


כִּ֤י פֹ֣עַל אָ֭דָם יְשַׁלֶּם־ל֑וֹ וּֽכְאֹ֥רַח אִ֝֗ישׁ יַמְצִאֶֽנּוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the work of a man shall he pay back to him, and according to his ways will he cause to befall every man.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For [according to] a man's work He will pay him, and according to a man's way He will provide for him.


###### Job 34:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:12)


אַף־אׇמְנָ֗ם אֵ֥ל לֹֽא־יַרְשִׁ֑יעַ וְ֝שַׁדַּ֗י לֹא־יְעַוֵּ֥ת מִשְׁפָּֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yea, surely God will not do wickedly, nor will the Almighty pervert justice.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Much more so will G d do no evil and the Almighty not pervert judgment.


###### Job 34:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:13)


מִֽי־פָקַ֣ד עָלָ֣יו אָ֑רְצָה וּמִ֥י שָׂ֝֗ם תֵּבֵ֥ל כֻּלָּֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Who has given him a charge over the earth? or who has disposed the whole world?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who made Him his deputy over the earth [(and responsible to him for an accounting)]? Who [but He] made the entire world?


###### Job 34:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:14)


אִם־יָשִׂ֣ים אֵלָ֣יו לִבּ֑וֹ רוּח֥וֹ וְ֝נִשְׁמָת֗וֹ אֵלָ֥יו יֶאֱסֹֽף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If he set his heart upon man, if he gather to himself his spirit and his breath;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If He set His heart against him [(man) to destroy him], He could gather back his spirit and his soul [(just as He gave it to him)].


###### Job 34:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:15)


יִגְוַ֣ע כׇּל־בָּשָׂ֣ר יָ֑חַד וְ֝אָדָ֗ם עַל־עָפָ֥ר יָשֽׁוּב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
all flesh shall perish together, and man shall return to dust.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then all flesh would perish together, and man would return to dust.


###### Job 34:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:16)


וְאִם־בִּ֥ינָה שִׁמְעָה־זֹּ֑את הַ֝אֲזִ֗ינָה לְק֣וֹל מִלָּֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If now thou hast understanding, hear this: hearken to the voice of my words.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Now, if [you wish], understand; hear this. Listen to the voice of my words.


###### Job 34:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:17)


הַאַ֬ף שׂוֹנֵ֣א מִשְׁפָּ֣ט יַחֲב֑וֹשׁ וְאִם־צַדִּ֖יק כַּבִּ֣יר תַּרְשִֽׁיעַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Shall even one that hates right govern? and wilt thou condemn him that is most just?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will He heal even the hater of [His] justice? [(And if you say that what He has done is not justice)], would you [thus] egregiously incriminate the Righteous One?


###### Job 34:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:18)


הַאֲמֹ֣ר לְמֶ֣לֶךְ בְּלִיָּ֑עַל רָ֝שָׁ֗ע אֶל־נְדִיבִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Is it fit to say to a king, Thou art base? and to princes, You are wicked?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would one call the king "worthless," or the nobles "wicked"?


###### Job 34:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:19)


אֲשֶׁ֤ר לֹֽא־נָשָׂ֨א**׀** פְּנֵ֥י שָׂרִ֗ים וְלֹ֣א נִכַּר־שׁ֭וֹעַ לִפְנֵי־דָ֑ל כִּֽי־מַעֲשֵׂ֖ה יָדָ֣יו כֻּלָּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
How much less to him who does not respect the persons of princes, nor regards the rich more than the poor? for they all are the work of his hands.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(Would you thus speak of the King of the world)] who does not favor the face of princes, and [before whom] the lord is not recognized above the pauper, all of them being His handiwork?


###### Job 34:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:20)


רֶ֤גַע**׀** יָמֻתוּ֮ וַחֲצ֢וֹת לָ֥֫יְלָה יְגֹעֲשׁ֣וּ עָ֣ם וְיַעֲבֹ֑רוּ וְיָסִ֥ירוּ אַ֝בִּ֗יר לֹ֣א בְיָֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In a moment they die, at midnight the people are shaken, and pass away; for they are removed and spirited away without hands.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[If He so wishes,] in an instant they die, and at midnight a nation [Egypt] quakes and passes on, their strength departed, gone from [their] hand.


###### Job 34:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:21)


כִּֽי־עֵ֭ינָיו עַל־דַּרְכֵי־אִ֑ישׁ וְֽכׇל־צְעָדָ֥יו יִרְאֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For his eyes are upon the ways of man, and he sees all his goings.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For His eyes are on the ways of man, and He sees all of his steps.


###### Job 34:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:22)


אֵֽין־חֹ֭שֶׁךְ וְאֵ֣ין צַלְמָ֑וֶת לְהִסָּ֥תֶר שָׁ֝֗ם פֹּ֣עֲלֵי אָֽוֶן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There is no darkness, nor deep gloom, where the workers of iniquity may hide themselves.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There is no darkness, no shadow of death, in which the workers of iniquity can be concealed.


###### Job 34:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:23)


כִּ֤י לֹ֣א עַל־אִ֭ישׁ יָשִׂ֣ים ע֑וֹד לַהֲלֹ֥ךְ אֶל־אֵ֝֗ל בַּמִּשְׁפָּֽט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For he will not lay upon man anything more, that he should enter into judgment with God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For it is not for a man to constantly set [his heart] to contend with G d in judgment [(for it is not His way to dispute with His creations) but]


###### Job 34:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:24)


יָרֹ֣עַ כַּבִּירִ֣ים לֹא־חֵ֑קֶר וַיַּעֲמֵ֖ד אֲחֵרִ֣ים תַּחְתָּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall break in pieces mighty men without number, and set others in their stead.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He breaks the mighty beyond a trace, and establishes others in their place.


###### Job 34:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:25)


לָכֵ֗ן יַ֭כִּיר מַעְבָּדֵיהֶ֑ם וְהָ֥פַךְ לַ֝֗יְלָה וְיִדַּכָּֽאוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Surely he knows their works, and he overturns them in the night, so that they are destroyed.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For He knows their deeds [(and He requires no disputation)]. And He turns night [i.e., affliction (upon them)], and they are crushed.


###### Job 34:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:26)


תַּֽחַת־רְשָׁעִ֥ים סְפָקָ֗ם בִּמְק֥וֹם רֹאִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He strikes them as wicked men in the open sight of others;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the place of the wicked He smites them [(the men of Sodom)], in a place of onlookers [i.e., in view of all].


###### Job 34:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:27)


אֲשֶׁ֣ר עַל־כֵּ֭ן סָ֣רוּ מֵאַחֲרָ֑יו וְכׇל־דְּ֝רָכָ֗יו לֹ֣א הִשְׂכִּֽילוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
because they turned back from following him, and would not have regard to any of his ways:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Because they strayed from Him and did not know all [i.e., any one] of His ways.


###### Job 34:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:28)


לְהָבִ֣יא עָ֭לָיו צַעֲקַת־דָּ֑ל וְצַעֲקַ֖ת עֲנִיִּ֣ים יִשְׁמָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
so that they cause the cry of the poor to come to him, and he hears the cry of the afflicted.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[the men of Sodom, in their cruelty,] bringing before Him the outcry of the poor man and [causing Him] to hear the outcry of the afflicted ones.


###### Job 34:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:29)


וְה֤וּא יַשְׁקִ֨ט**׀** וּמִ֥י יַרְשִׁ֗עַ וְיַסְתֵּ֣ר פָּ֭נִים וּמִ֣י יְשׁוּרֶ֑נּוּ וְעַל־גּ֖וֹי וְעַל־אָדָ֣ם יָֽחַד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When he gives quietness, who then can condemn? and when he hides his face, who then can behold him? Whether against a nation, or against a man alike:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when He quiets [(i.e., relieves) the poor from those who oppress them)], who will wrong [them further]? And when He conceals [His face], who will see Him? [And this "quieting" and "concealing" holds for] nation and individual alike.


###### Job 34:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:30)


מִ֭מְּלֹךְ אָדָ֥ם חָנֵ֗ף מִמֹּ֥קְשֵׁי עָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that the hypocrite reign not, lest the people be ensnared.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[And He quiets the poor] from [there further] ruling [over them] a flatterer, [(who had previously ruled over them)] because of the snares [i.e., the sins] of the people. [And you should not have aspersed such a G d.]


###### Job 34:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:31)


כִּֽי־אֶל־אֵ֭ל הֶאָמַ֥ר נָשָׂ֗אתִי לֹ֣א אֶחְבֹּֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For surely it is fitting to say to God, I suffer, I will no more offend:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For to [such] a G d [it is fitting] to be said: "I have borne [and accept Your punishment and] I shall not do harm [to myself."]


###### Job 34:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:32)


בִּלְעֲדֵ֣י אֶ֭חֱזֶה אַתָּ֣ה הֹרֵ֑נִי אִֽם־עָ֥וֶל פָּ֝עַ֗לְתִּי לֹ֣א אֹסִֽיף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that which I do not see teach me: if I have done iniquity, I will do no more.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[And it further befits him to say:] "Aside from what I see [and understand of Your ways], You teach me [and] if I have done wrong, I will do so no more."


###### Job 34:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:33)


הֲֽמֵעִמְּךָ֬ יְשַׁלְּמֶ֨נָּה**׀** כִּֽי־מָאַ֗סְתָּ כִּֽי־אַתָּ֣ה תִבְחַ֣ר וְלֹא־אָ֑נִי וּֽמַה־יָּדַ֥עְתָּ דַבֵּֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Shall he recompense it according to thy mind, when thou refusest his judgment? shall he say, Thou shalt choose, and not I; and speak what thou knowst.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(Elihu speaking for G d:)] Should [I have consulted] with you as to the exaction of payment [for your sins] that you have despised [your life (viz. 7:16 and 6:9)]? Should you choose [your punishment] and not I? Speak what you know.


###### Job 34:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:34)


אַנְשֵׁ֣י לֵ֭בָב יֹ֣אמְרוּ לִ֑י וְגֶ֥בֶר חָ֝כָ֗ם שֹׁמֵ֥עַֽ לִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Men of understanding will tell me, and the wise man who hears me will say,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Elihu continues:] Men of heart will say to me and the wise man will concur with me [that]


###### Job 34:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:35)


אִ֭יּוֹב לֹא־בְדַ֣עַת יְדַבֵּ֑ר וּ֝דְבָרָ֗יו לֹ֣א בְהַשְׂכֵּֽיל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Iyyov has spoken without knowledge, and his words were without wisdom.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Iyyov does not speak with knowledge, and his words are not wise.


###### Job 34:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:36)


אָבִ֗י יִבָּחֵ֣ן אִיּ֣וֹב עַד־נֶ֑צַח עַל־תְּ֝שֻׁבֹ֗ת בְּאַנְשֵׁי־אָֽוֶן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Would that Iyyov may be tried to the end because he answers like wicked men.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
My Father, would that Iyyov were tried eternally, so that men of iniquity would repent!


###### Job 34:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/34:37)


כִּ֥י־יֹ֘סִ֤יף עַֽל־חַטָּאת֣וֹ פֶ֭שַׁע בֵּינֵ֣ינוּ יִשְׂפּ֑וֹק וְיֶ֖רֶב אֲמָרָ֣יו לָאֵֽל׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For he adds rebellion to his sin; he strikes his fist among us, and multiplies his words against God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For he compounds his sin with offense. In our presence he rants and he multiplies his words against G d.



## Chapter 35

###### Job 35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:1)


וַיַּ֥עַן אֱלִיה֗וּ וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Elihu spoke, and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Elihu raised his voice and said:


###### Job 35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:2)


הֲ֭זֹאת חָשַׁ֣בְתָּ לְמִשְׁפָּ֑ט אָ֝מַ֗רְתָּ צִדְקִ֥י מֵאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thinkst thou this to be right, that thou dost say, My righteousness is more than God’s?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do you really consider this valid — your saying: "I am more righteous than G d"?


###### Job 35:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:3)


כִּֽי־תֹ֭אמַר מַה־יִּסְכׇּן־לָ֑ךְ מָה־אֹ֝עִ֗יל מֵחַטָּאתִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For thou dost say, What advantage will it be to thee? and, What profit shall I have, more than if I had sinned?
