**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
your saying what good does it [(being upright)] do you, or: "How does [righteousness] avail me more than sinning?"


###### Job 35:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:4)


אֲ֭נִי אֲשִֽׁיבְךָ֣ מִלִּ֑ין וְֽאֶת־רֵעֶ֥יךָ עִמָּֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I will answer thee, and thy companions with thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I will answer you, and your friends [(who were silent at your words)] along with you.


###### Job 35:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:5)


הַבֵּ֣ט שָׁמַ֣יִם וּרְאֵ֑ה וְשׁ֥וּר שְׁ֝חָקִ֗ים גָּבְה֥וּ מִמֶּֽךָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Look to the heavens, and see; and behold the clouds which are higher than thou.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Look to the heavens and see, and look to the heights above you [(See how they dwarf you!)]


###### Job 35:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:6)


אִם־חָ֭טָאתָ מַה־תִּפְעׇל־בּ֑וֹ וְרַבּ֥וּ פְ֝שָׁעֶ֗יךָ מַה־תַּֽעֲשֶׂה־לּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If thou hast sinned, what doest thou against him? or if thy transgressions are multiplied, what doest thou for him?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If you sin, what can you do to Him? And if your offenses be many, what do you do to Him?


###### Job 35:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:7)


אִם־צָ֭דַקְתָּ מַה־תִּתֶּן־ל֑וֹ א֥וֹ מַה־מִּיָּדְךָ֥ יִקָּֽח׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If thou art righteous, what givest thou him? or what does he receive of thy hand?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If you are righteous, what do you give Him? Or what does He take from your hand?


###### Job 35:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:8)


לְאִישׁ־כָּמ֥וֹךָ רִשְׁעֶ֑ךָ וּלְבֶן־אָ֝דָ֗ם צִדְקָתֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thy wickedness may hurt a man as thou art; and thy righteousness may profit the son of man.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Your evil injures [only] one such as you, and [only] the son of man is benefited by your righteousness.


###### Job 35:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:9)


מֵ֭רֹב עֲשׁוּקִ֣ים יַזְעִ֑יקוּ יְשַׁוְּע֖וּ מִזְּר֣וֹעַ רַבִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
By reason of the multitude of oppressors the oppressed cry: they cry out by reason of the arm of the mighty.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Witness the many wicked ones who] because of the abundance of the oppressed ones [(their victims)] cause [men] to cry out [before Him], to cry out because of the arm of the many [(who oppress them)].


###### Job 35:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:10)


וְֽלֹא־אָמַ֗ר אַ֭יֵּה אֱל֣וֹהַּ עֹשָׂ֑י נֹתֵ֖ן זְמִר֣וֹת בַּלָּֽיְלָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But none says, Where is God my maker, who gives songs in the night;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he [(the wicked one)] did not say [(to place the fear of the L rd before him)]: "Where is the G d that made me, who cuts off [the wicked] at night [e.g., Amrafel and his cohorts, Egypt, and Sancherev],


###### Job 35:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:11)


מַ֭לְּפֵנוּ מִבַּהֲמ֣וֹת אָ֑רֶץ וּמֵע֖וֹף הַשָּׁמַ֣יִם יְחַכְּמֵֽנוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who teaches us by the beasts of the earth, and makes us wiser by the birds of the sky?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
who taught us [i.e., who ennobled us] more than the beasts of the earth and made us wiser than the birds of heaven."


###### Job 35:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:12)


שָׁ֣ם יִ֭צְעֲקוּ וְלֹ֣א יַעֲנֶ֑ה מִ֝פְּנֵ֗י גְּא֣וֹן רָעִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There, when they cry he does not answer, because of the pride of evil men.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[And yet,] there they [(the afflicted ones)] cry out because of the pride of the wicked [(their oppressors)].


###### Job 35:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:13)


אַךְ־שָׁ֭וְא לֹא־יִשְׁמַ֥ע׀אֵ֑ל וְ֝שַׁדַּ֗י לֹ֣א יְשׁוּרֶֽנָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Surely God will not hear an empty cry, nor will the Almighty regard it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But it is in vain — G d will not hear [(immediately)]; the Almighty will not see [(immediately, but He withholds His wrath)].


###### Job 35:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:14)


אַ֣ף כִּֽי־תֹ֭אמַר לֹ֣א תְשׁוּרֶ֑נּוּ דִּ֥ין לְ֝פָנָ֗יו וּתְח֥וֹלֵֽל לֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Although thou sayst, Thou wilt not regard it, yet judgment is before him; therefore trust in him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
How much more so [will He not hasten to answer you, who He himself afflicts, and who] says [i.e., who complains] that you do not see Him [(to debate with Him)]! [But], present your case before Him [(wherever He may be)] and hope to Him.


###### Job 35:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:15)


וְעַתָּ֗ה כִּי־אַ֭יִן פָּקַ֣ד אַפּ֑וֹ וְלֹֽא־יָדַ֖ע בַּפַּ֣שׁ מְאֹֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But now, because his anger does not punish, and he does not heed the multitude of the words,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, [realize] that this visitation of His wrath [upon you] is nothing [compared to what you deserve], and that He [made Himself] "unknowing" of the great proliferation [of your sins].


###### Job 35:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/35:16)


וְ֭אִיּוֹב הֶ֣בֶל יִפְצֶה־פִּ֑יהוּ בִּבְלִי־דַ֝֗עַת מִלִּ֥ין יַכְבִּֽר׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
therefore does Iyyov open his mouth in vain, and multiplies words without knowledge.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Iyyov has opened his mouth in vanity. Without knowledge has he multiplied words.



## Chapter 36

###### Job 36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:1)


וַיֹּ֥סֶף אֱלִיה֗וּא וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Elihu also proceeded, and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Elihu continued and said:


###### Job 36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:2)


כַּתַּר־לִ֣י זְ֭עֵיר וַאֲחַוֶּ֑ךָּ כִּ֤י ע֖וֹד לֶאֱל֣וֹהַּ מִלִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Suffer me a little, and I will show thee; for I have yet words to speak on God’s behalf.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Bear with me yet awhile and I will speak to you, for there are yet more words in G d's behalf.


###### Job 36:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:3)


אֶשָּׂ֣א דֵ֭עִי לְמֵרָח֑וֹק וּ֝לְפֹעֲלִ֗י אֶֽתֵּֽן־צֶֽדֶק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I will fetch my knowledge from afar, and will ascribe righteousness to my Maker.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I will lift my voice afar, and I will speak righteousness for my Maker.


###### Job 36:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:4)


כִּֽי־אׇ֭מְנָם לֹא־שֶׁ֣קֶר מִלָּ֑י תְּמִ֖ים דֵּע֣וֹת עִמָּֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For truly my words are not false: one that is perfect in knowledge is with thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For, in truth, my words are not false. [(I will now begin speaking with you)] you who think yourself whole in thought.


###### Job 36:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:5)


הֶן־אֵ֣ל כַּ֭בִּיר וְלֹ֣א יִמְאָ֑ס כַּ֝בִּ֗יר כֹּ֣חַֽ לֵֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, God is mighty, and despises not any: he is mighty in strength of wisdom.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Behold, G d is great [in wisdom and in mercy], and He will not despise [the poor man]. He is great in strength of heart [(to take revenge) and]


###### Job 36:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:6)


לֹא־יְחַיֶּ֥ה רָשָׁ֑ע וּמִשְׁפַּ֖ט עֲנִיִּ֣ים יִתֵּֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He preserves not the life of the wicked: but gives to the poor their right.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He will not keep the wicked in life and will provide the fare of the poor.


###### Job 36:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:7)


לֹֽא־יִגְרַ֥ע מִצַּדִּ֗יק עֵ֫ינָ֥יו וְאֶת־מְלָכִ֥ים לַכִּסֵּ֑א וַיֹּשִׁיבֵ֥ם לָ֝נֶ֗צַח וַיִּגְבָּֽהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He withdraws not his eyes from the righteous: but with kings on the throne he establishes them for ever, and they are exalted.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He will not remove His eyes from the righteous one until [he is raised] with kings to the throne; and He enthrones them and exalts them for all eternity.


###### Job 36:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:8)


וְאִם־אֲסוּרִ֥ים בַּזִּקִּ֑ים יִ֝לָּכְד֗וּן בְּחַבְלֵי־עֹֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if they are bound in fetters, and are held in cords of affliction;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if they are [sometimes] tied in chains or constricted in bonds of poverty,


###### Job 36:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:9)


וַיַּגֵּ֣ד לָהֶ֣ם פׇּעֳלָ֑ם וּ֝פִשְׁעֵיהֶ֗ם כִּ֣י יִתְגַּבָּֽרוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then he declares to them their work, and their transgressions when they behave arrogantly.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He [thereby] tells them [of] their deeds [i.e., their sins] and of their offenses, which have intensified.


###### Job 36:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:10)


וַיִּ֣גֶל אׇ֭זְנָם לַמּוּסָ֑ר וַ֝יֹּ֗אמֶר כִּֽי־יְשׁוּב֥וּן מֵאָֽוֶן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He opens also their ear to discipline, and commands that they return from iniquity.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He [thereby] opens their ear to chastisement and tells them to repent of wrongdoing.


###### Job 36:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:11)


אִֽם־יִשְׁמְע֗וּ וְֽיַ֫עֲבֹ֥דוּ יְכַלּ֣וּ יְמֵיהֶ֣ם בַּטּ֑וֹב וּ֝שְׁנֵיהֶ֗ם בַּנְּעִימִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If they obey and serve him, they shall spend their days in prosperity, and their years in pleasures.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If they listen and [resume] serving [G d], they will complete their days in goodness and their years in pleasantness.


###### Job 36:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:12)


וְאִם־לֹ֣א יִ֭שְׁמְעוּ בְּשֶׁ֣לַח יַעֲבֹ֑רוּ וְ֝יִגְוְע֗וּ בִּבְלִי־דָֽעַת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if they do not obey, they shall perish by the sword, and they shall die without knowledge.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if they do not listen, they will pass [from the world] by the sword, and they will perish through their lack of understanding.


###### Job 36:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:13)


וְֽחַנְפֵי־לֵ֭ב יָשִׂ֣ימוּ אָ֑ף לֹ֥א יְ֝שַׁוְּע֗וּ כִּ֣י אֲסָרָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the hypocrites in heart heap up wrath: they do not cry when he binds them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But those who pander to their desires will fume [(at their afflictions)] and will not cry out [to G d] when He afflicts them.


###### Job 36:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:14)


תָּמֹ֣ת בַּנֹּ֣עַר נַפְשָׁ֑ם וְ֝חַיָּתָ֗ם בַּקְּדֵשִֽׁים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They die in youth, and their life ends in shame.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Their soul will die in their extremity and their spirit will expire with the fornicators.


###### Job 36:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:15)


יְחַלֵּ֣ץ עָנִ֣י בְעׇנְי֑וֹ וְיִ֖גֶל בַּלַּ֣חַץ אׇזְנָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He delivers the poor by means of his affliction, and opens their ears by oppression.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He rescues the afflicted one [(from Gehinnom)] by His afflictions and opens their ear — [(saying: "Return to Me")] — by [His] pressure [upon them].


###### Job 36:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:16)


וְאַ֤ף הֲסִֽיתְךָ֨**׀** מִפִּי־צָ֗ר רַ֭חַב לֹא־מוּצָ֣ק תַּחְתֶּ֑יהָ וְנַ֥חַת שֻׁ֝לְחָנְךָ֗ מָ֣לֵא דָֽשֶׁן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He has even removed thee out of distress into a broad place, where there is no straitness; and that which is set on thy table is full of fatness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He has moved you, too, away from the narrow mouth [of Gehinnom], [and] whose bottom is broad and not constricted [(so that its smoke collect in its midst)], and what will lie upon your table [(in the world to come)] will be full of "fatness."


###### Job 36:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:17)


וְדִין־רָשָׁ֥ע מָלֵ֑אתָ דִּ֖ין וּמִשְׁפָּ֣ט יִתְמֹֽכוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But thou art full of the judgment of the wicked: judgment and justice take hold on thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if you have been filled with the afflictions of the wicked, affliction and judgment [(i.e., those very afflictions)] will protect you [from Gehinnom (and should be accepted with love)].


###### Job 36:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:18)


כִּֽי־חֵ֭מָה פֶּן־יְסִֽיתְךָ֣ בְסָ֑פֶק וְרׇב־כֹּ֝֗פֶר אַל־יַטֶּֽךָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But beware of wrath, lest he take thee away with his clenched fist: nor let the greatness of the ransom turn thee aside.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For great wrath [will] befall you] if it [(your evil inclination)] entices you [to sin] through your railings [against G d].


###### Job 36:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:19)


הֲיַעֲרֹ֣ךְ שׁ֭וּעֲךָ לֹ֣א בְצָ֑ר וְ֝כֹ֗ל מַאֲמַצֵּי־כֹֽחַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Will thy riches avail, that are without stint, or all the forces of thy strength?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would you set up an outcry so that no afflictions [befall you] and [so that you undergo] no exertions? [How, then, will your "account" be rendered up?]


###### Job 36:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:20)


אַל־תִּשְׁאַ֥ף הַלָּ֑יְלָה לַעֲל֖וֹת עַמִּ֣ים תַּחְתָּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Desire not the night, when people are cut off in their place.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[And] do not aspire [to different afflictions,] to the night [which came to Amrafel, Egypt, and Sancherev, in which] nations were removed [from the world, while they remained] in their place [(for they thereby went lost in this world and in the world to come)].


###### Job 36:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:21)


הִ֭שָּׁמֶר אַל־תֵּ֣פֶן אֶל־אָ֑וֶן כִּֽי־עַל־זֶ֝֗ה בָּחַ֥רְתָּ מֵעֹֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Take heed, regard not iniquity: for this hast thou chosen rather than affliction.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Take heed, do not turn to poverty [(i.e., Do not contend that not these afflictions but those of poverty should have been brought upon you)], for these are preferable to [those of] poverty.


###### Job 36:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:22)


הֶן־אֵ֭ל יַשְׂגִּ֣יב בְּכֹח֑וֹ מִ֖י כָמֹ֣הוּ מוֹרֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, God is exalted by his power: who teaches like him?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Behold, [because] G d is supreme in His might, who, as He does, teaches a sinner [(to repent, warning him beforehand of the afflictions he will suffer otherwise, knowing that they cannot be forestalled by man's power)]?


###### Job 36:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:23)


מִֽי־פָקַ֣ד עָלָ֣יו דַּרְכּ֑וֹ וּמִי־אָ֝מַ֗ר פָּעַ֥לְתָּ עַוְלָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Who has enjoined him his way? or who can say, Thou hast wrought iniquity?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who [ever] appointed His way for him, and who [ever] told Him: "You have done wrong"?


###### Job 36:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:24)


זְ֭כֹר כִּֽי־תַשְׂגִּ֣יא פׇעֳל֑וֹ אֲשֶׁ֖ר שֹׁרְר֣וּ אֲנָשִֽׁים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Remember that thou magnify his work, which men behold.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Remember [His ways] and you will praise His works, which men have seen.


###### Job 36:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:25)


כׇּל־אָדָ֥ם חָזוּ־ב֑וֹ אֱ֝נ֗וֹשׁ יַבִּ֥יט מֵרָחֽוֹק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Every man may see it; man may behold it afar off.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All men have beheld it [(what He has wrought)]; man has seen it from afar [(from creation until now)].


###### Job 36:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:26)


הֶן־אֵ֣ל שַׂ֭גִּיא וְלֹ֣א נֵדָ֑ע מִסְפַּ֖ר שָׁנָ֣יו וְלֹא־חֵֽקֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, God is great, and we know it not, nor can the number of his years be searched out.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
G d is exalted and we cannot know [His greatness], and the number of His years [is unfathomable] and cannot be probed [(wherefore, man must gain his knowledge from those who came before him)].


###### Job 36:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:27)


כִּ֭י יְגָרַ֣ע נִטְפֵי־מָ֑יִם יָזֹ֖קּוּ מָטָ֣ר לְאֵדֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When he draws up small drops of water, they are distilled into a stream of rain;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When He increases [the number of] drops of water, they [(the firmaments)] pour rain into His cloud.


###### Job 36:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:28)


אֲשֶֽׁר־יִזְּל֥וּ שְׁחָקִ֑ים יִ֝רְעֲפ֗וּ עֲלֵ֤י**׀** אָדָ֬ם רָֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
which the clouds do drop and pour down on man abundantly.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And sometimes the heights will drip; they will drip by virtue of a great man.


###### Job 36:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:29)


אַ֣ף אִם־יָ֭בִין מִפְרְשֵׂי־עָ֑ב תְּ֝שֻׁא֗וֹת סֻכָּתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Can anyone understand the spreadings of the clouds, or the noise of his tabernacle?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Or, will a man understand the spreading out of a cloud, the mist of His pavilion?


###### Job 36:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:30)


הֵן־פָּרַ֣שׂ עָלָ֣יו אוֹר֑וֹ וְשׇׁרְשֵׁ֖י הַיָּ֣ם כִּסָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, he spreads his light about him, and covers the roots of the sea.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He spreads His rain upon it [(the cloud)] and covers up the roots of the sea [with it].


###### Job 36:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:31)


כִּֽי־בָ֭ם יָדִ֣ין עַמִּ֑ים יִֽתֶּן־אֹ֥כֶל לְמַכְבִּֽיר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For by these he judges the peoples; he gives food in abundance.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For with them [(the clouds)] He judges nations [(by withholding rain)] and [(by releasing rain)] provides food for one who abounds [(in children)].


###### Job 36:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:32)


עַל־כַּפַּ֥יִם כִּסָּה־א֑וֹר וַיְצַ֖ו עָלֶ֣יהָ בְמַפְגִּֽיעַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He covers his hands with the lightning, and commands it where it shall strike.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
On the clouds the rain is stopped, and He commands it [to fall] through the prayer [(of the great man of the generation)].


###### Job 36:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/36:33)


יַגִּ֣יד עָלָ֣יו רֵע֑וֹ מִ֝קְנֶ֗ה אַ֣ף עַל־עוֹלֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The noise of the storm tells of it; yea the cattle also of the rising tempest.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Sometimes] its thunder presages it [(rain)], but the accession of anger [(of the L rd against the wicked)] surmounts it [(and it does not fall)].



## Chapter 37

###### Job 37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:1)


אַף־לְ֭זֹאת יֶחֱרַ֣ד לִבִּ֑י וְ֝יִתַּ֗ר מִמְּקוֹמֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
At this also my heart trembles, and is moved out of its place.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Even at this [(i.e., what follows)] my heart trembles and springs from its place:


###### Job 37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:2)


שִׁמְע֤וּ שָׁמ֣וֹעַ בְּרֹ֣גֶז קֹל֑וֹ וְ֝הֶ֗גֶה מִפִּ֥יו יֵצֵֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hear attentively the noise of his voice, and the sound that goes out of his mouth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Hearing the reverberation of His voice [(the thunder)], like the speech leaving His mouth.


###### Job 37:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:3)


תַּֽחַת־כׇּל־הַשָּׁמַ֥יִם יִשְׁרֵ֑הוּ וְ֝אוֹר֗וֹ עַל־כַּנְפ֥וֹת הָאָֽרֶץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He directs it under the whole heaven, and his lightning to the ends of the earth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Beneath all the heavens I have seen it [(the lightning)]; His light is on the ends of the earth.


###### Job 37:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:4)


אַחֲרָ֤יו**׀** יִשְׁאַג־ק֗וֹל יַ֭רְעֵם בְּק֣וֹל גְּאוֹנ֑וֹ וְלֹ֥א יְ֝עַקְּבֵ֗ם כִּֽי־יִשָּׁמַ֥ע קוֹלֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
After it a voice roars: he thunders with the voice of his excellency; and he will not stay them when his voice is heard.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
After it [(the lightning)] its [(the thunder's)] voice roars forth. He thunders in the voice of His majesty. And He will not cut them off [(the thunder and lightning which bring the rain)] if he hears his [(the intercessor's)] voice [before Him (viz. 36:32)].


###### Job 37:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:5)


יַרְעֵ֤ם אֵ֣ל בְּ֭קוֹלוֹ נִפְלָא֑וֹת עֹשֶׂ֥ה גְ֝דֹל֗וֹת וְלֹ֣א נֵדָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
God thunders marvellously with his voice; great things he does, which we cannot comprehend.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
G d thunders with His voice wondrously. He does [even] greater things that we do not know of.


###### Job 37:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:6)


כִּ֤י לַשֶּׁ֨לֶג**׀** יֹאמַ֗ר הֱוֵ֫א־אָ֥רֶץ וְגֶ֥שֶׁם מָטָ֑ר וְ֝גֶ֗שֶׁם מִטְר֥וֹת עֻזּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For he says to the snow, Be thou on the earth; likewise to the small rain, and to the great rain of his strength.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For [through these voices] He tells the snow[ to] be [upon] the earth, and rain showers, and rain showers of His strength [i.e., coming from four directions].


###### Job 37:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:7)


בְּיַד־כׇּל־אָדָ֥ם יַחְתּ֑וֹם לָ֝דַ֗עַת כׇּל־אַנְשֵׁ֥י מַעֲשֵֽׂהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He seals up the hand of every man; that all men whom He has made may know his work.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He causes the hand of every man [(on the day of his death)] to sign [a list of his transgressions] to apprise each man of his deed [(for which he is being judged)].


###### Job 37:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:8)


וַתָּב֣וֹא חַיָּ֣ה בְמוֹ־אָ֑רֶב וּבִמְע֖וֹנֹתֶ֣יהָ תִשְׁכֹּֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the beasts go into dens, and remain in their places.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(And G d has many messengers to exact His punishments:)] The beast comes into its ambush and lurks in its lair.


###### Job 37:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:9)


מִן־הַ֭חֶדֶר תָּב֣וֹא סוּפָ֑ה וּֽמִמְּזָרִ֥ים קָרָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Out of the chamber comes the storm: and cold out of the scattering winds.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
From inside [(the clouds)] the tempest comes, and from Mezarim [(a chamber)], the cold.


###### Job 37:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:10)


מִנִּשְׁמַת־אֵ֥ל יִתֶּן־קָ֑רַח וְרֹ֖חַב מַ֣יִם בְּמוּצָֽק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
By the breath of God ice is given: and the breadth of the waters is straitened.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
By the breath of G d He produces ice, and an expanse of waters [i.e., a flood], spilling [them] forth [for punishment].


###### Job 37:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:11)


אַף־בְּ֭רִי יַטְרִ֣יחַ עָ֑ב יָ֝פִ֗יץ עֲנַ֣ן אוֹרֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Also he burdens the thick cloud with an overflow: the cloud scatters lightning:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Af-Beri [(an angel)] burdens the cloud [(with rain)] and he scatters His rain cloud.


###### Job 37:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:12)


וְה֤וּא מְסִבּ֨וֹת**׀** מִתְהַפֵּ֣ךְ בְּתַחְבּוּלֹתָ֣ו לְפׇעֳלָ֑ם כֹּ֖ל אֲשֶׁ֥ר יְצַוֵּ֓ם**׀** עַל־פְּנֵ֖י תֵבֵ֣ל אָֽרְצָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and it is turned round about by his counsels: that they may do whatever he commands them upon the face of the world, his land.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he turns around in circuits by His devices to [execute] their [the rains'] purpose [(for good or for ill)], [for] whatever [purpose] He commands them, on the face of the world, earthwards.


###### Job 37:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:13)


אִם־לְשֵׁ֥בֶט אִם־לְאַרְצ֑וֹ אִם־לְ֝חֶ֗סֶד יַמְצִאֵֽהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He causes it to come, whether for correction, or for his land, or for love.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Whether for punishment [(in which instance he causes it to rain on mountains and hills, where nothing is sowed)], or for His land [(in which instance he causes it to rain "normally")], or for lovingkindness [(in which instance he causes it to rain in the optimum time and place)] — He provides it.


###### Job 37:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:14)


הַאֲזִ֣ינָה זֹּ֣את אִיּ֑וֹב עֲ֝מֹ֗ד וְהִתְבּוֹנֵ֤ן**׀** נִפְלְא֬וֹת אֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hearken to this, O Iyyov: stand still, and consider the wondrous works of God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Hear this, Iyyov, stand and reflect upon the wonders of G d.


###### Job 37:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:15)


הֲ֭תֵדַע בְּשׂוּם־אֱל֣וֹהַּ עֲלֵיהֶ֑ם וְ֝הֹפִ֗יעַ א֣וֹר עֲנָנֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Dost thou know how God lays his command on them, and causes the lightning of his cloud to shine?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do you know how G d places [the law of His covenant] upon them [(the clouds) — how they are suspended by His pronouncement], and the rain of His cloud appears?


###### Job 37:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:16)


הֲ֭תֵדַע עַל־מִפְלְשֵׂי־עָ֑ב מִ֝פְלְא֗וֹת תְּמִ֣ים דֵּעִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Dost thou know the balancings of the clouds, the wondrous works of him who is perfect in knowledge?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do you know the dimensions of a cloud? the wonders [inherent in it] of the Perfect in Knowledge?


###### Job 37:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:17)


אֲשֶׁר־בְּגָדֶ֥יךָ חַמִּ֑ים בְּהַשְׁקִ֥ט אֶ֝֗רֶץ מִדָּרֽוֹם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou whose garments are warm, when the earth is quiet because of the south wind?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Do you know] that your garments are warm because of [His] "quieting" [i.e., warming] the earth [(by the warm east wind)] against the [cold] south [wind]?


###### Job 37:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:18)


תַּרְקִ֣יעַ עִ֭מּוֹ לִשְׁחָקִ֑ים חֲ֝זָקִ֗ים כִּרְאִ֥י מוּצָֽק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Canst thou with him spread out the sky, which is strong as a molten mirror?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Did you stretch out the heavens with Him, strong as a molten river?


###### Job 37:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:19)


ה֭וֹדִיעֵנוּ מַה־נֹּ֣אמַר ל֑וֹ לֹֽא־נַ֝עֲרֹ֗ךְ מִפְּנֵי־חֹֽשֶׁךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Teach us what we shall say to him; for we cannot order our speech by reason of darkness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Inform us what we can say to Him [in our quarrel with Him]; we cannot arrange [a case against Him] because [He is shrouded in] darkness.


###### Job 37:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:20)


הַֽיְסֻפַּר־ל֭וֹ כִּ֣י אֲדַבֵּ֑ר אִֽם־אָ֥מַר אִ֝֗ישׁ כִּ֣י יְבֻלָּֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Shall it be told him that I would speak? or should a man wish to be swallowed up?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Need He be told what I shall say? If one says [something], it is [already] revealed [to Him].


###### Job 37:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:21)


וְעַתָּ֤ה**׀** לֹ֘א־רָ֤אוּ א֗וֹר בָּהִ֣יר ה֭וּא בַּשְּׁחָקִ֑ים וְר֥וּחַ עָ֝בְרָ֗ה וַֽתְּטַהֲרֵֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And now men see not the bright light which is in the clouds: but the wind passes, and cleanses them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, [(your three friends who started answering you but were unable to complete what they had begun — they are like stillborn babes, who] did not see the light [of wisdom]. The bright [rain-bearing clouds] appear in the skies, and a wind passes and scatters them!


###### Job 37:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:22)


מִ֭צָּפוֹן זָהָ֣ב יֶאֱתֶ֑ה עַל־אֱ֝ל֗וֹהַּ נ֣וֹרָא הֽוֹד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Gold comes out of the north: about God is terrible majesty.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
From the north [wind] comes the cold [i.e., the clearing of the day — without rain!] — and before G d is [the praise of men for] the awesome glory!


###### Job 37:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:23)


שַׁדַּ֣י לֹא־מְ֭צָאנֻהוּ שַׂגִּיא־כֹ֑חַ וּמִשְׁפָּ֥ט וְרֹב־צְ֝דָקָ֗ה לֹ֣א יְעַנֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Almighty, (we cannot find him out:) he is excellent in power, and in judgment, and in plenty of justice: he will not oppress.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The Almighty — we have not found Him [to be] overbearing in power [(in judging His creations)]. And He does not afflict [them with unbearable afflictions], and [He does not demand] exceeding righteousness.


###### Job 37:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/37:24)


לָ֭כֵן יְרֵא֣וּהוּ אֲנָשִׁ֑ים לֹֽא־יִ֝רְאֶ֗ה כׇּל־חַכְמֵי־לֵֽב׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Men do therefore fear him: he regards not any who are wise of heart.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Therefore, men will fear [(debating with)] Him; He does not see all the wise of heart [i.e., their "wisdom" pales before Him].



## Chapter 38

###### Job 38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:1)


וַיַּעַן־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֶת־אִ֭יּוֹב (מנהסערה) [מִ֥ן׀הַסְּעָרָ֗ה] וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the Lord answered Iyyov out of the storm wind, and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then the L rd answered Iyyov from the tempest and said:


###### Job 38:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:2)


מִ֤י זֶ֨ה**׀** מַחְשִׁ֖יךְ עֵצָ֥ה בְמִלִּ֗ין בְּֽלִי־דָֽעַת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Who is this that darkens counsel by words without knowledge?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who is it that darkens counsel with words void of knowledge?


###### Job 38:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:3)


אֱזׇר־נָ֣א כְגֶ֣בֶר חֲלָצֶ֑יךָ וְ֝אֶשְׁאָלְךָ֗ וְהוֹדִיעֵֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Gird up now thy loins like a man; for I will demand of thee, and let me know thy answer.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Gird, now, your loins like a man. I shall question you, and you answer Me.


###### Job 38:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:4)


אֵיפֹ֣ה הָ֭יִיתָ בְּיׇסְדִי־אָ֑רֶץ הַ֝גֵּ֗ד אִם־יָדַ֥עְתָּ בִינָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Where wast thou when I laid the foundations of the earth? declare, if thou hast understanding.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Where were you when I founded the earth? Answer, if you possess understanding.


###### Job 38:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:5)


מִי־שָׂ֣ם מְ֭מַדֶּיהָ כִּ֣י תֵדָ֑ע א֤וֹ מִֽי־נָטָ֖ה עָלֶ֣יהָ קָּֽו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Who determined its measurements, if thou knowst? or who has stretched the line upon it?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who established its measurements — if you know. Who stretched a plumb line over it?


###### Job 38:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:6)


עַל־מָ֭ה אֲדָנֶ֣יהָ הׇטְבָּ֑עוּ א֥וֹ מִי־יָ֝רָ֗ה אֶ֣בֶן פִּנָּתָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
whereupon are its foundations fastened? or who laid its corner stone;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
On what were its foundations sunk? Who cast its cornerstone [(in the middle of the sea, whence the entire world was founded)]?


###### Job 38:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:7)


בְּרׇן־יַ֭חַד כּ֣וֹכְבֵי בֹ֑קֶר וַ֝יָּרִ֗יעוּ כׇּל־בְּנֵ֥י אֱלֹהִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
when the morning stars sang together, and all the sons of God shouted for joy?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Where were you] when the stars of morning sang together [(at the creation)] and all the men of G d [(the angels)] shouted?


###### Job 38:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:8)


וַיָּ֣סֶךְ בִּדְלָתַ֣יִם יָ֑ם בְּ֝גִיח֗וֹ מֵרֶ֥חֶם יֵצֵֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
or who shut up the sea with doors, when it broke forth, and issued out of the womb?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [who] barred the sea with doors [(the shore sands)] when it gushed forth to leave its womb?


###### Job 38:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:9)


בְּשׂוּמִ֣י עָנָ֣ן לְבֻשׁ֑וֹ וַ֝עֲרָפֶ֗ל חֲתֻלָּתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
when I made the cloud its garment, and thick darkness a swaddling band for it,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Where were you] when I made the cloud its [(the sea's)] garment and the mist its swaddling cloth?


###### Job 38:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:10)


וָאֶשְׁבֹּ֣ר עָלָ֣יו חֻקִּ֑י וָ֝אָשִׂ֗ים בְּרִ֣יחַ וּדְלָתָֽיִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and prescribed bounds for it, and set bars and doors,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I broke My statute upon it [i.e., I placed breakers around it to contain it] and made bar and doors [for it].


###### Job 38:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:11)


וָאֹמַ֗ר עַד־פֹּ֣ה תָ֭בוֹא וְלֹ֣א תֹסִ֑יף וּפֹא־יָ֝שִׁ֗ית בִּגְא֥וֹן גַּלֶּֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and said, Hitherto shalt thou come, but no further: and here shall thy proud waves be stayed?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I said: "Until here you may come, and no further; and here you may lift your waves in pride."


###### Job 38:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:12)


הֲֽ֭מִיָּמֶיךָ צִוִּ֣יתָ בֹּ֑קֶר (ידעתה שחר) [יִדַּ֖עְתָּ הַשַּׁ֣חַר] מְקֹמֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hast thou commanded the morning since thy days began; and caused the dayspring to know its place;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Did you ever command the sun? Do you know the [resting] place of [the herald of] the morning?


###### Job 38:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:13)


לֶ֭אֱחֹז בְּכַנְפ֣וֹת הָאָ֑רֶץ וְיִנָּעֲר֖וּ רְשָׁעִ֣ים מִמֶּֽנָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that it might take hold of the ends of the earth, that the wicked might be shaken out of it?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Did you ever command one] to hold the ends of the earth [like a garment] and to shake the wicked out of it [(as I am destined to do)]?


###### Job 38:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:14)


תִּ֭תְהַפֵּךְ כְּחֹ֣מֶר חוֹתָ֑ם וְ֝יִֽתְיַצְּב֗וּ כְּמ֣וֹ לְבֽוּשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It is changed like clay under the seal; and they stand as a garment.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Do you command] that the seal [(a man's countenance)] be transformed [in the grave] like clay, and that they stand [fully] clothed [at the resurrection]?


###### Job 38:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:15)


וְיִמָּנַ֣ע מֵרְשָׁעִ֣ים אוֹרָ֑ם וּזְר֥וֹעַ רָ֝מָ֗ה תִּשָּׁבֵֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And from the wicked their light is withheld, and the high arm shall be broken.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[At that time] their light will be withheld from the wicked and the upraised arm will be broken.


###### Job 38:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:16)


הֲ֭בָאתָ עַד־נִבְכֵי־יָ֑ם וּבְחֵ֥קֶר תְּ֝ה֗וֹם הִתְהַלָּֽכְתָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hast thou entered into the springs of the sea? or hast thou walked in the recesses of the depth?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Did you reach the enclosures of the sea, and did you go around probing the deep?


###### Job 38:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:17)


הֲנִגְל֣וּ לְ֭ךָ שַׁעֲרֵי־מָ֑וֶת וְשַׁעֲרֵ֖י צַלְמָ֣וֶת תִּרְאֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
have the gates of death been opened to thee? or hast thou seen the doors of deepest darkness?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Were the gates of death [(Gehinnom)] revealed to you, and did you see the gates of the shadow of death?


###### Job 38:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:18)


הִ֭תְבֹּנַנְתָּ עַד־רַחֲבֵי־אָ֑רֶץ הַ֝גֵּ֗ד אִם־יָדַ֥עְתָּ כֻלָּֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
hast thou comprehended the expanse of the earth? declare if thou knowst it all.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Have you probed into the far reaches of the earth? Speak if you know it all.


###### Job 38:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:19)


אֵי־זֶ֣ה הַ֭דֶּרֶךְ יִשְׁכׇּן־א֑וֹר וְ֝חֹ֗שֶׁךְ אֵי־זֶ֥ה מְקֹמֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Where is the way where light dwells? and as for darkness, where is its place,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Where is the road to the dwelling of light, and where is the place of darkness


###### Job 38:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:20)


כִּ֣י תִ֭קָּחֶנּוּ אֶל־גְּבוּל֑וֹ וְכִי־תָ֝בִ֗ין נְתִיב֥וֹת בֵּיתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that thou shouldst take it to its bound, and that thou shouldst know the paths to its home?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
that you could take it [(the light)] to its [(darkness's] boundary and that you could know the paths to its house [to bring light there]?


###### Job 38:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:21)


יָ֭דַעְתָּ כִּי־אָ֣ז תִּוָּלֵ֑ד וּמִסְפַּ֖ר יָמֶ֣יךָ רַבִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou knowst it because thou wast then born, and the number of thy days is great.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(sarcastically: Surely,)] you know [all these things], for you were born then [(when I created them)] and the number of your days are many!


###### Job 38:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:22)


הֲ֭בָאתָ אֶל־אֹצְר֣וֹת שָׁ֑לֶג וְאוֹצְר֖וֹת בָּרָ֣ד תִּרְאֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hast thou entered the treasuries of the snow? or hast thou seen the treasuries of the hail,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Did you come to the treasures of snow and did you see the treasures of hail,


###### Job 38:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:23)


אֲשֶׁר־חָשַׂ֥כְתִּי לְעֶת־צָ֑ר לְי֥וֹם קְ֝רָ֗ב וּמִלְחָמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
which I have reserved against the time of trouble, against the day of battle and war?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
which I saved for a time of trouble [(with the five kings of Giveon)], for a time of battle and war [(the war of Gog and Magog)].


###### Job 38:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:24)


אֵי־זֶ֣ה הַ֭דֶּרֶךְ יֵחָ֣לֶק א֑וֹר יָפֵ֖ץ קָדִ֣ים עֲלֵי־אָֽרֶץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
By what way is the light parted, or the east wind scattered upon the earth?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
What is the way that the light [of the sun] is parted, [the way that] the [sun of the] east spreads [itself] over the earth?


###### Job 38:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:25)


מִֽי־פִלַּ֣ג לַשֶּׁ֣טֶף תְּעָלָ֑ה וְ֝דֶ֗רֶךְ לַחֲזִ֥יז קֹלֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who has divided a watercourse for the torrent of rain, or a way for the lightning of thunder;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who cut out a basin for the hair [(each hair having its own follicle)] and a path for the sounds [i.e., the thunder peals] of the rain clouds [(each peal having its own track)]


###### Job 38:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:26)


לְ֭הַמְטִיר עַל־אֶ֣רֶץ לֹא־אִ֑ישׁ מִ֝דְבָּ֗ר לֹא־אָדָ֥ם בּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to cause it to rain on a land where no man is; on the wilderness, wherein there is no man;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
to bring rain upon a land where there is no man, upon a desert, where no man dwells,


###### Job 38:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:27)


לְהַשְׂבִּ֣יעַ שֹׁ֭אָה וּמְשֹׁאָ֑ה וּ֝לְהַצְמִ֗יחַ מֹ֣צָא דֶֽשֶׁא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to satisfy the desolate and waste ground; and to cause the bud of the tender herb to spring forth?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
to sate the darkness [i.e., the drought] and the waste and to cause the source of grass to sprout?


###### Job 38:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:28)


הֲיֵשׁ־לַמָּטָ֥ר אָ֑ב א֥וֹ מִי־ה֝וֹלִ֗יד אֶגְלֵי־טָֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Has the rain a father? or who has begotten the drops of dew?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Does the rain have a father? And who begot the waves of dew?


###### Job 38:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:29)


מִבֶּ֣טֶן מִ֭י יָצָ֣א הַקָּ֑רַח וּכְפֹ֥ר שָׁ֝מַ֗יִם מִ֣י יְלָדֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
out of whose womb came the ice? and the hoar frost of heaven, who has given it birth?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
From whose stomach did the ice come forth, and who gave birth to the heavenly frost?


###### Job 38:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:30)


כָּ֭אֶבֶן מַ֣יִם יִתְחַבָּ֑אוּ וּפְנֵ֥י תְ֝ה֗וֹם יִתְלַכָּֽדוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The waters are hidden as with a stone, and the face of the deep forms a solid mass.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Like a stone, the waters hide [beneath the ice] and the [waters on] the face of the deep cling together.


###### Job 38:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:31)


הַֽ֭תְקַשֵּׁר מַעֲדַנּ֣וֹת כִּימָ֑ה אֽוֹ־מֹשְׁכ֖וֹת כְּסִ֣יל תְּפַתֵּֽחַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Canst thou bind the chains of the Pleiades, or loosen the cords of Orion?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would you tie the bonds of the Pleiades [(to keep the world from freezing)] or undo the cords of Orion [(to warm the Pleiades thereby)]?


###### Job 38:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:32)


הֲתֹצִ֣יא מַזָּר֣וֹת בְּעִתּ֑וֹ וְ֝עַ֗יִשׁ עַל־בָּנֶ֥יהָ תַנְחֵֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
canst thou bring forth Mazzarot in their season? or canst thou guide the Bear with his sons?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would you bring out the stars in their proper time and lead forth the Bear with its sons?


###### Job 38:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:33)


הֲ֭יָדַעְתָּ חֻקּ֣וֹת שָׁמָ֑יִם אִם־תָּשִׂ֖ים מִשְׁטָר֣וֹ בָאָֽרֶץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
knowst thou the ordinances of heaven? canst thou establish its dominion in the earth?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do you know the statutes of the heavens [.e., the ordinance of the stars (in producing cold and heat, winter and summer)]? Can you establish its rule [i.e., the function of each star] upon the earth?


###### Job 38:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:34)


הֲתָרִ֣ים לָעָ֣ב קוֹלֶ֑ךָ וְֽשִׁפְעַת־מַ֥יִם תְּכַסֶּֽךָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
canst thou lift up thy voice to the clouds, that abundance of waters may cover thee?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would you raise your voice to the cloud and be covered by an outpouring of water?


###### Job 38:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:35)


הַֽתְשַׁלַּ֣ח בְּרָקִ֣ים וְיֵלֵ֑כוּ וְיֹאמְר֖וּ לְךָ֣ הִנֵּֽנוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
canst thou send lightnings, that they may go, and say to thee, Here we are?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would you send out lightnings that they go forth [in the place where they have been sent to, without needing to return] to say to you: "Here we are" [i.e., we have done your bidding (the Shechinah being omnipresent)]?


###### Job 38:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:36)


מִי־שָׁ֭ת בַּטֻּח֣וֹת חׇכְמָ֑ה א֤וֹ מִֽי־נָתַ֖ן לַשֶּׂ֣כְוִי בִינָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Who has put wisdom in the inward parts? or who has given understanding to the birds?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who places wisdom in the kidneys, or who deposits understanding in the heart?


###### Job 38:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:37)


מִֽי־יְסַפֵּ֣ר שְׁחָקִ֣ים בְּחׇכְמָ֑ה וְנִבְלֵ֥י שָׁ֝מַ֗יִם מִ֣י יַשְׁכִּֽיב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who can number the clouds by wisdom? or who can refill the bottles of heaven,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who charges the heights with wisdom, and who lays down the flagons of the sky [(the clouds)]


###### Job 38:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:38)


בְּצֶ֣קֶת עָ֭פָר לַמּוּצָ֑ק וּרְגָבִ֥ים יְדֻבָּֽקוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
when the dust grows into a mass, and the clods cleave fast together?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(as I did at the creation)] when the dust was spilled out into a mold and the clods congealed [(for the creation of the world)]?


###### Job 38:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:39)


הֲתָצ֣וּד לְלָבִ֣יא טָ֑רֶף וְחַיַּ֖ת כְּפִירִ֣ים תְּמַלֵּֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Wilt thou hunt the prey for the lion? or satisfy the appetite of the young lions,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would you hunt prey for the lion or sate the spirit of the young lion?


###### Job 38:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:40)


כִּֽי־יָשֹׁ֥חוּ בַמְּעוֹנ֑וֹת יֵשְׁב֖וּ בַסֻּכָּ֣ה לְמוֹ־אָֽרֶב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
when they couch in their dens, and abide in the covert to lie in wait?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For they crouch in their lairs; they wait under cover for the ambush.


###### Job 38:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/38:41)


מִ֤י יָכִ֥ין לָעֹרֵ֗ב צֵ֫יד֥וֹ כִּֽי־יְ֭לָדָו אֶל־אֵ֣ל יְשַׁוֵּ֑עוּ יִ֝תְע֗וּ לִבְלִי־אֹֽכֶל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Who provides for the raven his provision? when his young ones cry to God, and wander for lack of food?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who prepares his prey for the raven? For his young cry out to G d [(their father forsaking them)]. They stray, without food [(and G d Himself provides for them)].



## Chapter 39

###### Job 39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:1)


הֲיָדַ֗עְתָּ עֵ֭ת לֶ֣דֶת יַעֲלֵי־סָ֑לַע חֹלֵ֖ל אַיָּל֣וֹת תִּשְׁמֹֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Knowst thou the time when the wild goats of the rock bring forth? or canst thou mark when the hinds do calve?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do you know the time that the wild mountain goats give birth? Do you wait upon the birth pangs of the hinds?


###### Job 39:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:2)


תִּסְפֹּ֣ר יְרָחִ֣ים תְּמַלֶּ֑אנָה וְ֝יָדַעְתָּ֗ עֵ֣ת לִדְתָּֽנָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
canst thou number the months that they fulfil? or knowst thou the time when they bring forth?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do you count when their months will be full? Do you know when they will give birth?


###### Job 39:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:3)


תִּ֭כְרַעְנָה יַלְדֵיהֶ֣ן תְּפַלַּ֑חְנָה חֶבְלֵיהֶ֥ם תְּשַׁלַּֽחְנָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They bow themselves, they bring forth their young ones, they send forth their offspring.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They crouch and "split out" their young [i.e., their womb splits and their young are ejected], and [at that moment] they "send forth" [i.e., they banish] their birth pangs.


###### Job 39:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:4)


יַחְלְמ֣וּ בְ֭נֵיהֶם יִרְבּ֣וּ בַבָּ֑ר יָ֝צְא֗וּ וְלֹא־שָׁ֥בוּ לָֽמוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Their young ones grow strong; they grow up with corn; they go forth, and return not to them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Their children grow strong. They grow up in the wilderness. They leave [their parents] and do not return to them.


###### Job 39:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:5)


מִי־שִׁלַּ֣ח פֶּ֣רֶא חׇפְשִׁ֑י וּמֹסְר֥וֹת עָ֝ר֗וֹד מִ֣י פִתֵּֽחַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Who has sent out the wild ass free? or who has loosed the bands of the onager?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who set the wild ass free [(resisting bridling)], and who undid the traces of the ass of the desert,


###### Job 39:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:6)


אֲשֶׁר־שַׂ֣מְתִּי עֲרָבָ֣ה בֵית֑וֹ וּֽמִשְׁכְּנוֹתָ֥יו מְלֵחָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
whose house I have made the wilderness, and the salt land his dwellings.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
whose home I made the wilderness, and whose abode, the desert wastes?


###### Job 39:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:7)


יִ֭שְׂחַק לַהֲמ֣וֹן קִרְיָ֑ה תְּשֻׁא֥וֹת נֹ֝גֵ֗שׂ לֹ֣א יִשְׁמָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He scorns the tumult of the city, nor does he regard the shouts of the driver.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He laughs at the roar of the city. He does not hear the shouts of the driver.


###### Job 39:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:8)


יְת֣וּר הָרִ֣ים מִרְעֵ֑הוּ וְאַחַ֖ר כׇּל־יָר֣וֹק יִדְרֽוֹשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The range of the mountains is his pasture, and he searches after every green thing.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He spies out the mountains for his pasture land and inquires after all greenery.


###### Job 39:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:9)


הֲיֹ֣אבֶה רֵּ֣ים עׇבְדֶ֑ךָ אִם־יָ֝לִ֗ין עַל־אֲבוּסֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Will the wild ox be willing to serve thee, or will he abide by thy crib?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will the wild ox desire to serve you? Will he frequent your manger?


###### Job 39:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:10)


הֲֽתִקְשׇׁר־רֵ֭ים בְּתֶ֣לֶם עֲבֹת֑וֹ אִם־יְשַׂדֵּ֖ד עֲמָקִ֣ים אַחֲרֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
canst thou bind the wild ox with ropes in the furrow? or will he harrow the valleys after thee?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will you tie the wild ox [by] his rope to the furrow [(of the plow)]? Will he till valleys after you?


###### Job 39:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:11)


הֲֽתִבְטַח־בּ֭וֹ כִּֽי־רַ֣ב כֹּח֑וֹ וְתַעֲזֹ֖ב אֵלָ֣יו יְגִיעֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
wilt thou trust him, because his strength is great? or wilt thou leave thy labour to him?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will you trust to him [to gather your grain] because his power is great and leave [(the fruit of)] your toil to him [(to be gathered to the house)]?


###### Job 39:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:12)


הֲתַאֲמִ֣ין בּ֭וֹ כִּֽי־[יָשִׁ֣יב] (ישוב) זַרְעֶ֑ךָ וְֽגׇרְנְךָ֥ יֶאֱסֹֽף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
wilt thou believe him, that he will bring home thy seed, and gather it into thy barn?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will you trust him to return your seed and to gather in [the grain of] your threshing floor?


###### Job 39:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:13)


כְּנַף־רְנָנִ֥ים נֶעֱלָ֑סָה אִם־אֶ֝בְרָ֗ה חֲסִידָ֥ה וְנֹצָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The wing of the ostrich waves joyously; but are they the pinions and plumage of the stork?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The joyous peacock, or the winged chasidah, or the notzah [— observe her ways]:


###### Job 39:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:14)


כִּי־תַעֲזֹ֣ב לָאָ֣רֶץ בֵּצֶ֑יהָ וְֽעַל־עָפָ֥ר תְּחַמֵּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For she leaves her eggs in the earth, and warms them in dust,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
She leaves her eggs on the ground and [goes off] to warm herself on the earth,


###### Job 39:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:15)


וַ֭תִּשְׁכַּח כִּי־רֶ֣גֶל תְּזוּרֶ֑הָ וְחַיַּ֖ת הַשָּׂדֶ֣ה תְדוּשֶֽׁהָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and forgets that the foot may crush them, or that the wild beast may break them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
forgetting that the foot [of the wayfarer] can squash them and the beast of the field crush them.


###### Job 39:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:16)


הִקְשִׁ֣יחַ בָּנֶ֣יהָ לְּלֹא־לָ֑הּ לְרִ֖יק יְגִיעָ֣הּ בְּלִי־פָֽחַד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She is hardened against her young ones, as though they were not hers: though her labour is in vain of yet she is without fear;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
She spurns her children [as if] they were not hers, not fearing that she has labored in vain [(and will lose them)].


###### Job 39:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:17)


כִּי־הִשָּׁ֣הּ אֱל֣וֹהַּ חׇכְמָ֑ה וְלֹא־חָ֥לַק לָ֝֗הּ בַּבִּינָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
because God has deprived her of wisdom, nor has he imparted to her understanding.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For G d has made her forget wisdom and has given her no share in understanding.


###### Job 39:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:18)


כָּ֭עֵת בַּמָּר֣וֹם תַּמְרִ֑יא תִּֽשְׂחַ֥ק לַ֝סּ֗וּס וּלְרֹכְבֽוֹ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
At some time she lifts up herself on high; she scorns the horse and his rider.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When she soars to the heights, she laughs at the horse and its rider [(not fearing that they will crush her eggs)].


###### Job 39:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:19)


הֲתִתֵּ֣ן לַסּ֣וּס גְּבוּרָ֑ה הֲתַלְבִּ֖ישׁ צַוָּאר֣וֹ רַעְמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hast thou given the horse strength? hast thou clothed his neck with power?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Did you give courage to the horse? Did you clothe his neck with rumbling [(the "commotion" of his mane)]?


###### Job 39:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:20)


הֲֽ֭תַרְעִישֶׁנּוּ כָּאַרְבֶּ֑ה ה֖וֹד נַחְר֣וֹ אֵימָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Dost thou make him leap like a locust? the glory of his nostrils is terrible.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Did you [rouse him] like a locust din, the "glory" of his nostrils [inspiring] fright?


###### Job 39:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:21)


יַחְפְּר֣וּ בָ֭עֵמֶק וְיָשִׂ֣ישׂ בְּכֹ֑חַ יֵ֝צֵ֗א לִקְרַאת־נָֽשֶׁק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He paws in the valley, and rejoices in his strength: he goes out to meet the battle.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They [(his riders)] spy out the valley, and he revels in [his] strength and goes out against arms.


###### Job 39:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:22)


יִשְׂחַ֣ק לְ֭פַחַד וְלֹ֣א יֵחָ֑ת וְלֹא־יָ֝שׁ֗וּב מִפְּנֵי־חָֽרֶב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He mocks at fear, and is not affrighted; nor does he turn back from the sword.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He laughs at fear and does not tremble, and he does not turn back from the sword.


###### Job 39:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:23)


עָ֭לָיו תִּרְנֶ֣ה אַשְׁפָּ֑ה לַ֖הַב חֲנִ֣ית וְכִידֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The quiver rattles against him, the glittering spear and the javelin.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The quiver [(full of rattling arrows)] "sings" upon him, the spear-blade, and the javelin.


###### Job 39:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:24)


בְּרַ֣עַשׁ וְ֭רֹגֶז יְגַמֶּא־אָ֑רֶץ וְלֹא־יַ֝אֲמִ֗ין כִּי־ק֥וֹל שׁוֹפָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He swallows the ground with fierceness and rage: nor does he believe that it is the sound of the trumpet.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
With sound and fury he laps up the land and pays no heed to the shofar's blast.


###### Job 39:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:25)


בְּדֵ֤י שֹׁפָ֨ר**׀** יֹ֘אמַ֤ר הֶאָ֗ח וּֽ֭מֵרָחוֹק יָרִ֣יחַ מִלְחָמָ֑ה רַ֥עַם שָׂ֝רִ֗ים וּתְרוּעָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He says among the trumpets, Ha, ha; and he smells the battle afar off, the thunder of the captains, and the shouting.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the midst of the shofar din he cries out [(exultingly)]: "Heach!" and he smells battle from afar — the roar of the warriors and the shofar blast.


###### Job 39:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:26)


הֲֽ֭מִבִּינָ֣תְךָ יַאֲבֶר־נֵ֑ץ יִפְרֹ֖שׂ כְּנָפָ֣ו לְתֵימָֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Does the hawk fly by thy wisdom, and stretch her wings towards the south?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Is it with your understanding that Netz [(a hawk-like angel)] sprouts wings against the south [wind (whose fury would otherwise destroy the world)]?


###### Job 39:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:27)


אִם־עַל־פִּ֭יךָ יַגְבִּ֣יהַּ נָ֑שֶׁר וְ֝כִ֗י יָרִ֥ים קִנּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
does the vulture mount up at thy command, and make her nest on high?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Is it at your word that the eagle soars aloft and that he lifts his nest [above all others]?


###### Job 39:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:28)


סֶ֣לַע יִ֭שְׁכֹּן וְיִתְלֹנָ֑ן עַ֥ל שֶׁן־סֶ֝֗לַע וּמְצוּדָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
She dwells and abides on the rock, upon the crag of the rock, and the strong place.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He dwells and abides on the rock, on the crags of rocks and towers.


###### Job 39:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:29)


מִשָּׁ֥ם חָֽפַר־אֹ֑כֶל לְ֝מֵרָח֗וֹק עֵינָ֥יו יַבִּֽיטוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
From thence she swoops down on the prey, and her eyes behold afar off.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
From there he scouts out food, his eyes ranging far.


###### Job 39:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/39:30)


וְאֶפְרֹחָ֥ו יְעַלְעוּ־דָ֑ם וּבַאֲשֶׁ֥ר חֲ֝לָלִ֗ים שָׁ֣ם הֽוּא׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Her young ones also suck up blood: and where the slain are, there is she.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And his fledglings will gulp down blood; and where carcasses are, there he is.



## Chapter 40

###### Job 40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:1)


וַיַּ֖עַן יְהֹוָ֥ה אֶת־אִיּ֗וֹב וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the Lord answered Iyyov, and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L rd answered Iyyov and said:


###### Job 40:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:2)


הֲ֭רֹב עִם־שַׁדַּ֣י יִסּ֑וֹר מוֹכִ֖יחַ אֱל֣וֹהַּ יַעֲנֶֽנָּה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Shall a reprover contend with the Almighty? he who reproaches God, let him answer it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would one aggrandize himself to argue with the Almighty? One who would debate with G d — let him answer.


###### Job 40:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:3)


וַיַּ֖עַן אִיּ֥וֹב אֶת־יְהֹוָ֗ה וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Iyyov answered the Lord, and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Iyyov answered the L rd and said:


###### Job 40:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:4)


הֵ֣ן קַ֭לֹּתִי מָ֣ה אֲשִׁיבֶ֑ךָּ יָ֝דִ֗י שַׂ֣מְתִּי לְמוֹ־פִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, I am vile; what shall I answer Thee? I lay my hand upon my mouth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I have recognized my lowliness and how can I answer You?


###### Job 40:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:5)


אַחַ֣ת דִּ֭בַּרְתִּי וְלֹ֣א אֶעֱנֶ֑ה וּ֝שְׁתַּ֗יִם וְלֹ֣א אוֹסִֽיף׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Once I have spoken; but I will not answer: yea, twice; but I will proceed no further.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I have spoken one [thing, i.e., a little], and I shall not say [more], and [to] the two [that I have spoken (viz. 13:20)] I will not add.


###### Job 40:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:6)


וַיַּעַן־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֶת־אִ֭יּוֹב (מנסערה) [מִ֥ן׀סְעָרָ֗ה] וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the Lord answered Iyyov out of the storm wind, and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L rd answered Iyyov from the tempest and said:


###### Job 40:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:7)


אֱזׇר־נָ֣א כְגֶ֣בֶר חֲלָצֶ֑יךָ אֶ֝שְׁאָלְךָ֗ וְהוֹדִיעֵֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Gird up thy loins now like a man: I will demand of thee, and declare thou unto me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Gird, now, your loins like a man. I will ask you, and you answer Me.


###### Job 40:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:8)


הַ֭אַף תָּפֵ֣ר מִשְׁפָּטִ֑י תַּ֝רְשִׁיעֵ֗נִי לְמַ֣עַן תִּצְדָּֽק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Wilt thou also disavow my judgment? wilt thou condemn me, that thou mayst be in the right?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would you also repudiate My justice, condemn Me because of your [(assumed)] righteousness?


###### Job 40:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:9)


וְאִם־זְר֖וֹעַ כָּאֵ֥ל׀לָ֑ךְ וּ֝בְק֗וֹל כָּמֹ֥הוּ תַרְעֵֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
hast thou an arm like God? or canst thou thunder with a voice like him?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do you have the arm [i.e., the power] of G d, and can you thunder in a voice like His?


###### Job 40:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:10)


עֲדֵ֥ה נָ֣א גָא֣וֹן וָגֹ֑בַהּ וְה֖וֹד וְהָדָ֣ר תִּלְבָּֽשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Deck thyself now with majesty and excellency; and array thyself with glory and beauty.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Adorn yourself now, with pride and grandeur, and clothe yourself in majesty and glory.


###### Job 40:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:11)


הָ֭פֵץ עֶבְר֣וֹת אַפֶּ֑ךָ וּרְאֵ֥ה כׇל־גֵּ֝אֶ֗ה וְהַשְׁפִּילֵֽהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Cast abroad the rage of thy wrath: and behold everyone that is proud, and abase him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Scatter wide the fury of your wrath, and see every proud one and lower him.


###### Job 40:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:12)


רְאֵ֣ה כׇל־גֵּ֭אֶה הַכְנִיעֵ֑הוּ וַהֲדֹ֖ךְ רְשָׁעִ֣ים תַּחְתָּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Look on everyone that is proud, and bring him low; and tread down the wicked in their place.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
See every proud one and humble him, and crush the wicked in their place.


###### Job 40:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:13)


טׇמְנֵ֣ם בֶּעָפָ֣ר יָ֑חַד פְּ֝נֵיהֶ֗ם חֲבֹ֣שׁ בַּטָּמֽוּן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hide them in the dust together; and bind their faces in the hiding place.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Bury them together in the dust; steep their faces in hiddenness.


###### Job 40:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:14)


וְגַם־אֲנִ֥י אוֹדֶ֑ךָּ כִּֽי־תוֹשִׁ֖עַ לְךָ֣ יְמִינֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then I will also confess to thee that thy own right hand can save thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I, too, will acknowledge you, when your right hand is your salvation.


###### Job 40:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:15)


הִנֵּה־נָ֣א בְ֭הֵמוֹת אֲשֶׁר־עָשִׂ֣יתִי עִמָּ֑ךְ חָ֝צִ֗יר כַּבָּקָ֥ר יֹאכֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold now behemot, which I made with thee; he eats grass like an ox.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Here, now, is the behemoth [(prepared for the future)] that I made with you. He eats grass like the ox.


###### Job 40:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:16)


הִנֵּה־נָ֣א כֹח֣וֹ בְמׇתְנָ֑יו וְ֝אוֹנ֗וֹ בִּשְׁרִירֵ֥י בִטְנֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Lo now, his might is in his loins, and his force is in the muscles of his belly.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
His power is in his loins [(i.e., his testicles are undescended)], and his strength is in the navel of his belly.


###### Job 40:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:17)


יַחְפֹּ֣ץ זְנָב֣וֹ כְמוֹ־אָ֑רֶז גִּידֵ֖י פַחֲדָ֣ו יְשֹׂרָֽגוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He stiffens his tail like a cedar: the sinews of his thighs are knit together.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
His tail is hard as a cedar; the sinews of his testicles are knit together.


###### Job 40:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:18)


עֲ֭צָמָיו אֲפִיקֵ֣י נְחֻשָׁ֑ה גְּ֝רָמָ֗יו כִּמְטִ֥יל בַּרְזֶֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
His bones are tubes of brass; his limbs are like bars of iron.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
His limbs are strong as copper; his bones are a load of iron.


###### Job 40:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:19)


ה֭וּא רֵאשִׁ֣ית דַּרְכֵי־אֵ֑ל הָ֝עֹשׂ֗וֹ יַגֵּ֥שׁ חַרְבּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He is the beginning of the ways of God: let him that made him bring near his sword to him!


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He is the first of the ways of G d [(the first beast created)]. His Maker [Himself] will draw His sword [(to slaughter him)].


###### Job 40:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:20)


כִּי־ב֭וּל הָרִ֣ים יִשְׂאוּ־ל֑וֹ וְֽכׇל־חַיַּ֥ת הַ֝שָּׂדֶ֗ה יְשַׂחֲקוּ־שָֽׁם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Surely the mountains bring him forth food, where all the wild beasts play.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the mountains bear food for him, and all the animals of the field disport themselves there [(where he feeds)].


###### Job 40:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:21)


תַּֽחַת־צֶאֱלִ֥ים יִשְׁכָּ֑ב בְּסֵ֖תֶר קָנֶ֣ה וּבִצָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He lies under the thorny bushes, in the cover of the reed, and fens.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will he lie under the shade of trees? in the recess of reeds or fens?


###### Job 40:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:22)


יְסֻכֻּ֣הוּ צֶאֱלִ֣ים צִֽלְל֑וֹ יְ֝סֻבּ֗וּהוּ עַרְבֵי־נָֽחַל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The thorny bushes cover him with their shadow; the willows of the brook compass him about.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Can the shade of trees cover him [as] his shade? Will the willows of the stream surround him?


###### Job 40:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:23)


הֵ֤ן יַעֲשֹׁ֣ק נָ֭הָר לֹ֣א יַחְפּ֑וֹז יִבְטַ֓ח**׀** כִּֽי־יָגִ֖יחַ יַרְדֵּ֣ן אֶל־פִּֽיהוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, he drinks up a river, and hastens not: he trusts that river will thrust some food into his mouth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He despoils the [(entire)] river [(the Euphrates) when he drinks it] and [his stomach] does not harden [(from bloating)]. He trusts the [whole] Jordan to flow into his mouth!


###### Job 40:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:24)


בְּעֵינָ֥יו יִקָּחֶ֑נּוּ בְּ֝מוֹקְשִׁ֗ים יִנְקׇב־אָֽף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Shall anyone take him with his eyes open? or pierce through his nose with a snare?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
With his eyes [(i.e., in his awe of Him)] He will take him [and] He will pierce his snout with snares.


###### Job 40:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:25)


תִּמְשֹׁ֣ךְ לִוְיָתָ֣ן בְּחַכָּ֑ה וּ֝בְחֶ֗בֶל תַּשְׁקִ֥יעַ לְשֹׁנֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Canst thou draw out livyatan with a hook? or press down his tongue with a cord?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would you draw forth Leviathan with a fishing rod? Would you pin down his tongue with a rope?


###### Job 40:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:26)


הֲתָשִׂ֣ים אַגְמֹ֣ן בְּאַפּ֑וֹ וּ֝בְח֗וֹחַ תִּקֹּ֥ב לֶחֱיֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
canst thou put a hook into his nose? or bore his jaw through with a bridle ring?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would you put a hook in his snout? Would you pierce his cheek with a lance?


###### Job 40:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:27)


הֲיַרְבֶּ֣ה אֵ֭לֶיךָ תַּחֲנוּנִ֑ים אִם־יְדַבֵּ֖ר אֵלֶ֣יךָ רַכּֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
will he make many supplications to thee? will he speak soft words to thee?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will he plead profusely with you [(to be released)]? Will he speak softly to you? [— You could not even get near him!]


###### Job 40:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:28)


הֲיִכְרֹ֣ת בְּרִ֣ית עִמָּ֑ךְ תִּ֝קָּחֶ֗נּוּ לְעֶ֣בֶד עוֹלָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
will he make a covenant with thee? to take him for thy servant for ever?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will he enter into a covenant with you, that you take him as a slave forever?


###### Job 40:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:29)


הַֽתְשַׂחֶק־בּ֭וֹ כַּצִּפּ֑וֹר וְ֝תִקְשְׁרֶ֗נּוּ לְנַעֲרוֹתֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
wilt thou play with him as with a bird? or wilt thou bind him for thy girls?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will you disport yourself with him as with a [little] bird and tie him to [the necks of] your young ones [for their amusement]?


###### Job 40:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:30)


יִכְר֣וּ עָ֭לָיו חַבָּרִ֑ים יֶ֝חֱצ֗וּהוּ בֵּ֣ין כְּֽנַעֲנִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
will the tradesmen heap up payment for him? shall they part him among the merchants?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will the enchanters [by their charms] fish for him [in the sea] and portion him out among the merchants?


###### Job 40:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:31)


הַֽתְמַלֵּ֣א בְשֻׂכּ֣וֹת עוֹר֑וֹ וּבְצִלְצַ֖ל דָּגִ֣ים רֹאשֽׁוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
canst thou fill his skin with barbed irons? or his head with fish spears?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Will you sate (your lust) by making huts of its skin and a fish-booth of its head?


###### Job 40:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/40:32)


שִׂים־עָלָ֥יו כַּפֶּ֑ךָ זְכֹ֥ר מִ֝לְחָמָ֗ה אַל־תּוֹסַֽף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Lay thy hand upon him! thou wilt no more think of fighting.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Just try to] put your hands upon him and you will no longer recall your martial prowess.



## Chapter 41

###### Job 41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:1)


הֵן־תֹּחַלְתּ֥וֹ נִכְזָ֑בָה הֲגַ֖ם אֶל־מַרְאָ֣יו יֻטָֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
