Behold, the hope of him is in vain: shall not one be cast down even at the sight of him?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
How much will one's hope [of victory] fail him if the (mere) sight of him unmans him!


###### Job 41:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:2)


לֹֽא־אַ֭כְזָר כִּ֣י יְעוּרֶ֑נּוּ וּמִ֥י ה֝֗וּא לְפָנַ֥י יִתְיַצָּֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
None is so fierce that dare stir him up: who then is able to stand before me?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
No one would be so bold as to wake him. Who, then, would stand up against Me!


###### Job 41:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:3)


מִ֣י הִ֭קְדִּימַנִי וַאֲשַׁלֵּ֑ם תַּ֖חַת כׇּל־הַשָּׁמַ֣יִם לִי־הֽוּא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Who has a claim on me from before, that I should repay him? whatever is under the whole heaven is mine.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who would approach Me [to serve Me and to heed My mitzvoth]? — I would [of a certainty] reward (him, for) everything under the heavens is Mine!


###### Job 41:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:4)


(לא) [לוֹ־]אַחֲרִ֥ישׁ בַּדָּ֑יו וּדְבַר־גְּ֝בוּר֗וֹת וְחִ֣ין עֶרְכּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I will hold my peace at him; listen to his words, the report of his power, and his graceful speech.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I will not be silent [in rewarding as well] his [the tzaddik's] sons [in their father's merit], and for [his] strength [in the performance of mitzoth] and for the fervor of his prayer.


###### Job 41:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:5)


מִֽי־גִ֭לָּה פְּנֵ֣י לְבוּשׁ֑וֹ בְּכֶ֥פֶל רִ֝סְנ֗וֹ מִ֣י יָבֽוֹא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Who can uncover the face of his garment? or who can come within his double bridle?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who would [dare approach him (the leviathan)] to reveal his outer garment [his scales]? Who would venture into the double-fold of his bit?


###### Job 41:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:6)


דַּלְתֵ֣י פָ֭נָיו מִ֣י פִתֵּ֑חַ סְבִיב֖וֹת שִׁנָּ֣יו אֵימָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who can open the doors of his face? his teeth are terrible round about.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who would open the doors of his face [(his lips)]? Around his teeth there is fear.


###### Job 41:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:7)


גַּ֭אֲוָה אֲפִיקֵ֣י מָגִנִּ֑ים סָ֝ג֗וּר חוֹתָ֥ם צָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
His scales are his pride, shut up together as with a close seal:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He exults in the strength of his scales, each sealed tightly [to its neighbor].


###### Job 41:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:8)


אֶחָ֣ד בְּאֶחָ֣ד יִגַּ֑שׁוּ וְ֝ר֗וּחַ לֹא־יָבֹ֥א בֵינֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
one is so near to another, that no air can come between them:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
One touches the other. The wind does not enter between them.


###### Job 41:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:9)


אִישׁ־בְּאָחִ֥יהוּ יְדֻבָּ֑קוּ יִ֝תְלַכְּד֗וּ וְלֹ֣א יִתְפָּרָֽדוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
they are joined one to another, they stick together, that they cannot be sundered.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
One is attached to the other. They are fixed firmly together and do not come apart.


###### Job 41:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:10)


עֲֽ֭טִישֹׁתָיו תָּ֣הֶל א֑וֹר וְ֝עֵינָ֗יו כְּעַפְעַפֵּי־שָֽׁחַר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
His sneezings flash forth light, and his eyes are like the eyelids of the morning.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
His sneezes flash light, and his eyes are like the first light of day.


###### Job 41:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:11)


מִ֭פִּיו לַפִּידִ֣ים יַהֲלֹ֑כוּ כִּיד֥וֹדֵי אֵ֝֗שׁ יִתְמַלָּֽטוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Out of his mouth go burning torches, and sparks of fire leap out.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
From his mouth torches come forth. Sparks of fire escape from it.


###### Job 41:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:12)


מִ֭נְּחִירָיו יֵצֵ֣א עָשָׁ֑ן כְּד֖וּד נָפ֣וּחַ וְאַגְמֹֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Out of his nostrils goes smoke, as out of a seething pot and burning rushes.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
From his nostrils smoke comes forth, as from a seething pot and an agmon [(a kind of pot)].


###### Job 41:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:13)


נַ֭פְשׁוֹ גֶּחָלִ֣ים תְּלַהֵ֑ט וְ֝לַ֗הַב מִפִּ֥יו יֵצֵֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
His breath kindles coals, and a flame goes out of his mouth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
His breath glows as coals and a flame goes forth from his mouth.


###### Job 41:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:14)


בְּֽ֭צַוָּארוֹ יָלִ֣ין עֹ֑ז וּ֝לְפָנָ֗יו תָּד֥וּץ דְּאָבָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In his neck abides strength, and terror dances before him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Strength resides on his throat, and his chest makes sadness rejoice.


###### Job 41:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:15)


מַפְּלֵ֣י בְשָׂר֣וֹ דָבֵ֑קוּ יָצ֥וּק עָ֝לָ֗יו בַּל־יִמּֽוֹט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The flakes of his flesh are joined together: they are firm upon him; they cannot be moved.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The folds of his flesh cling fast [as if] they were forged together, not to part.


###### Job 41:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:16)


לִ֭בּוֹ יָצ֣וּק כְּמוֹ־אָ֑בֶן וְ֝יָצ֗וּק כְּפֶ֣לַח תַּחְתִּֽית׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
His heart is as firm as a stone; yea, as hard as the nether millstone.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
His heart is forged together, as a stone, forged as the lower millstone.


###### Job 41:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:17)


מִ֭שֵּׂתוֹ יָג֣וּרוּ אֵלִ֑ים מִ֝שְּׁבָרִ֗ים יִתְחַטָּֽאוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When he raises himself up, the mighty are afraid: by reason of the crashings they are beside themselves.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Mighty ones tremble at his grandeur. The breakers break [before him].


###### Job 41:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:18)


מַשִּׂיגֵ֣הוּ חֶ֭רֶב בְּלִ֣י תָק֑וּם חֲנִ֖ית מַסָּ֣ע וְשִׁרְיָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Though the sword reaches him, it does not avail; nor the spear, the dart, or the javelin.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The sword that confronts him will not prevail — the spear, the dart, or the javelin.


###### Job 41:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:19)


יַחְשֹׁ֣ב לְתֶ֣בֶן בַּרְזֶ֑ל לְעֵ֖ץ רִקָּב֣וֹן נְחוּשָֽׁה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He esteems iron as straw, and brass as rotten wood:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He accounts iron [weapons] as straw and copper as rotted wood.


###### Job 41:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:20)


לֹא־יַבְרִיחֶ֥נּוּ בֶן־קָ֑שֶׁת לְ֝קַ֗שׁ נֶהְפְּכוּ־ל֥וֹ אַבְנֵי־קָֽלַע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
the arrow cannot make him flee: slingstones are turned with him into stubble:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The bowman will not drive him away. The stones of the sling turn into stubble against him.


###### Job 41:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:21)


כְּ֭קַשׁ נֶחְשְׁב֣וּ תוֹתָ֑ח וְ֝יִשְׂחַ֗ק לְרַ֣עַשׁ כִּידֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
darts are counted as stubble; he laughs at the shaking of a spear.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Catapult [projectiles] are accounted as stubble and he laughs at the shouts of the spear [hurlers].


###### Job 41:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:22)


תַּ֭חְתָּיו חַדּ֣וּדֵי חָ֑רֶשׂ יִרְפַּ֖ד חָר֣וּץ עֲלֵי־טִֽיט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
His underparts are like sharp potsherds: he spreads himself like a threshing sledge on the mire.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Beneath him are flashes of sun [glancing off his fins], his resting place on the sand [shining like] fine gold.


###### Job 41:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:23)


יַרְתִּ֣יחַ כַּסִּ֣יר מְצוּלָ֑ה יָ֝֗ם יָשִׂ֥ים כַּמֶּרְקָחָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He makes the deep to boil like a pot: he makes the sea like a kettle.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He boils the depths as a pot. He makes the sea like a spicery [when he moves].


###### Job 41:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:24)


אַ֭חֲרָיו יָאִ֣יר נָתִ֑יב יַחְשֹׁ֖ב תְּה֣וֹם לְשֵׂיבָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
After him he leaves a shining wake; one would think the deep to be hoary.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Behind him his wake is alight. He renders the depths as [frail as] a grey-beard.


###### Job 41:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:25)


אֵֽין־עַל־עָפָ֥ר מׇשְׁל֑וֹ הֶ֝עָשׂ֗וּ לִבְלִי־חָֽת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Upon earth there is not his like, who is made without fear.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There is not his like upon the earth, constituted without fear.


###### Job 41:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/41:26)


אֵֽת־כׇּל־גָּבֹ֥הַּ יִרְאֶ֑ה ה֝֗וּא מֶ֣לֶךְ עַל־כׇּל־בְּנֵי־שָֽׁחַץ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He beholds all high things: he is a king over all the children of pride.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All that is high he sees [as beneath him]. He is king over all the sons of pride. [(How, then, can you presume to contend with Me, who has wrought all of these awesome things?)]



## Chapter 42

###### Job 42:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:1)


וַיַּ֖עַן אִיּ֥וֹב אֶת־יְהֹוָ֗ה וַיֹּאמַֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Iyyov answered the Lord, and said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then Iyyov answered G d and said:


###### Job 42:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:2)


(ידעת) [יָ֭דַעְתִּי] כִּי־כֹ֣ל תּוּכָ֑ל וְלֹֽא־יִבָּצֵ֖ר מִמְּךָ֣ מְזִמָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I know that Thou canst do everything, and that no purpose of Thine can be thwarted.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I have [always] known that You are omnipotent, and that no thought is withheld from You.


###### Job 42:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:3)


מִ֤י זֶ֨ה**׀** מַעְלִ֥ים עֵצָ֗ה בְּֽלִ֫י־דָ֥עַת לָכֵ֣ן הִ֭גַּדְתִּי וְלֹ֣א אָבִ֑ין נִפְלָא֥וֹת מִ֝מֶּ֗נִּי וְלֹ֣א אֵדָֽע׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Who is he that hides counsel without knowledge? therefore I have uttered that which I did not understand; things too wonderful for me, which I knew not.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who would deny counsel [(and the working of wonders to the L rd)]? Therefore, I have related [(what was known to me)]. But I did not understand [certain things] which are removed from me, which I do not know.


###### Job 42:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:4)


שְֽׁמַֽע־נָ֭א וְאָנֹכִ֣י אֲדַבֵּ֑ר אֶ֝שְׁאָלְךָ֗ וְהוֹדִיעֵֽנִי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hear, I beseech Thee, and I will speak: I will demand of Thee, and declare Thou unto me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Listen, now, and I will speak. I will ask You, and You will give me knowledge.


###### Job 42:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:5)


לְשֵֽׁמַע־אֹ֥זֶן שְׁמַעְתִּ֑יךָ וְ֝עַתָּ֗ה עֵינִ֥י רָאָֽתְךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I have heard of Thee by the hearing of the ear: but now my eye sees Thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Before,] I heard of You with my ears, but now my eyes have seen You [(i.e., the Shechinah)].


###### Job 42:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:6)


עַל־כֵּ֭ן אֶמְאַ֣ס וְנִחַ֑מְתִּי עַל־עָפָ֥ר וָאֵֽפֶר׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Wherefore I abhor myself, and repent in dust and ashes.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Therefore, I despise [my life]. And I would be consoled [if I could return] to dust and ashes.


###### Job 42:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:7)


וַיְהִ֗י אַחַ֨ר דִּבֶּ֧ר יְהֹוָ֛ה אֶת־הַדְּבָרִ֥ים הָאֵ֖לֶּה אֶל־אִיּ֑וֹב וַיֹּ֨אמֶר יְהֹוָ֜ה אֶל־אֱלִיפַ֣ז הַתֵּימָנִ֗י חָרָ֨ה אַפִּ֤י בְךָ֙ וּבִשְׁנֵ֣י רֵעֶ֔יךָ כִּ֠י לֹ֣א דִבַּרְתֶּ֥ם אֵלַ֛י נְכוֹנָ֖ה כְּעַבְדִּ֥י אִיּֽוֹב׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it was so, that after the Lord had spoken these words to Iyyov, the Lord said to Elifaz the Temanite; My anger burns against thee, and against thy two friends: for you have not spoken of me the thing that is right, like my servant Iyyov.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, after G d had spoken these words to Iyyov, that the L rd said to Elifaz the Temanite: I am angry with you and with your two friends [Bildad the Shuchite and Tzofar the Na'amathite], for you did not speak rightly to Me [(i.e., you did not present a cogent claim)] as (did) my servant Iyyov. [Iyyov did not speak offensively to Him, but only plaintively (viz. Chapter 9) and in the throes of affliction, but they offended in branding Iyyov as wicked and not consoling him (as Elihu did), and taunting him even beyond his suffering.]


###### Job 42:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:8)


וְעַתָּ֡ה קְחוּ־לָכֶ֣ם שִׁבְעָֽה־פָרִים֩ וְשִׁבְעָ֨ה אֵילִ֜ים וּלְכ֣וּ**׀** אֶל־עַבְדִּ֣י אִיּ֗וֹב וְהַעֲלִיתֶ֤ם עוֹלָה֙ בַּעַדְכֶ֔ם וְאִיּ֣וֹב עַבְדִּ֔י יִתְפַּלֵּ֖ל עֲלֵיכֶ֑ם כִּ֧י אִם־פָּנָ֣יו אֶשָּׂ֗א לְבִלְתִּ֞י עֲשׂ֤וֹת עִמָּכֶם֙ נְבָלָ֔ה כִּ֠י לֹ֣א דִבַּרְתֶּ֥ם אֵלַ֛י נְכוֹנָ֖ה כְּעַבְדִּ֥י אִיּֽוֹב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore take for yourselves now seven bullocks and seven rams, and go to my servant Iyyov, and offer up for yourselves a burnt offering; and my servant Iyyov shall pray for you: for to him I will show favour in doing you no disgrace, although you have not spoken of me the thing which is right, like my servant Iyyov.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, take for yourselves seven bullocks and seven rams and go to My servant Iyyov and bring up a burnt-offering for yourselves, and Iyyov My servant will pray for you; for I will show favor to him not to do anything vile to you. For you did not speak rightly to Me as (did) My servant Iyyov.


###### Job 42:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:9)


וַיֵּלְכוּ֩ אֱלִיפַ֨ז הַתֵּימָנִ֜י וּבִלְדַּ֣ד הַשּׁוּחִ֗י צֹפַר֙ הַנַּ֣עֲמָתִ֔י וַיַּעֲשׂ֔וּ כַּאֲשֶׁ֛ר דִּבֶּ֥ר אֲלֵיהֶ֖ם יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיִּשָּׂ֥א יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶת־פְּנֵ֥י אִיּֽוֹב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So Elifaz the Temanite and Bildad the Shuĥite and Żofar the Na῾amatite went, and did as the Lord commanded them: the Lord also accepted Iyyov.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Elifaz the Temanite and Bildad the Shuchite and Tzofar the Na'amathite went, and did as the L rd had spoken to them, and the L rd showed favor to Iyyov [(and accepted his prayer for them)].


###### Job 42:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:10)


וַיהֹוָ֗ה שָׁ֚ב אֶת־[שְׁב֣וּת] (שבית) אִיּ֔וֹב בְּהִֽתְפַּֽלְל֖וֹ בְּעַ֣ד רֵעֵ֑הוּ וַיֹּ֧סֶף יְהֹוָ֛ה אֶת־כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֥ר לְאִיּ֖וֹב לְמִשְׁנֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord restored the fortunes of Iyyov, when he prayed for his friends: and the Lord gave Iyyov twice as much as he had before.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L rd returned what had been taken from Iyyov, because of his praying for his friend, and the L rd increased all that Iyyov owned to double [its former amount].


###### Job 42:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:11)


וַיָּבֹ֣אוּ אֵ֠לָ֠יו כׇּל־אֶחָ֨יו וְכׇל־אַחְיֹתָ֜יו וְכׇל־יֹדְעָ֣יו לְפָנִ֗ים וַיֹּאכְל֨וּ עִמּ֣וֹ לֶ֘חֶם֮ בְּבֵיתוֹ֒ וַיָּנֻ֤דוּ לוֹ֙ וַיְנַחֲמ֣וּ אֹת֔וֹ עַ֚ל כׇּל־הָ֣רָעָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־הֵבִ֥יא יְהֹוָ֖ה עָלָ֑יו וַיִּתְּנוּ־ל֗וֹ אִ֚ישׁ קְשִׂיטָ֣ה אֶחָ֔ת וְאִ֕ישׁ נֶ֥זֶם זָהָ֖ב אֶחָֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then all his brothers came to him, and all his sisters, and all they who had been of his acquaintance before, and did eat bread with him in his house: and they bemoaned him, and comforted him over all the evil that the Lord had brought upon him: every man also gave him a qesita, and everyone an earring of gold.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there came to him all of his brothers and all of his sisters and all of his former acquaintances [(all who had been his good friends before the afflictions)], and they ate bread with him in his house, and they nodded to him [in sympathy] and consoled him for all of the suffering that the L rd had brought upon him, and each of them gave him one kesita [(a coin)], and each, a golden nose-ring.


###### Job 42:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:12)


וַיהֹוָ֗ה בֵּרַ֛ךְ אֶת־אַחֲרִ֥ית אִיּ֖וֹב מֵרֵֽאשִׁת֑וֹ וַֽיְהִי־ל֡וֹ אַרְבָּעָה֩ עָשָׂ֨ר אֶ֜לֶף צֹ֗אן וְשֵׁ֤שֶׁת אֲלָפִים֙ גְּמַלִּ֔ים וְאֶלֶף־צֶ֥מֶד בָּקָ֖ר וְאֶ֥לֶף אֲתוֹנֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So the Lord blessed the latter end of Iyyov more than his beginning: for he had fourteen thousand sheep, and six thousand camels, and a thousand yoke of oxen, and a thousand she asses.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L rd blessed Iyyov's latter days more than [He had blessed] his beginning. And he had fourteen thousand sheep, six thousand camels, a thousand pairs of cattle, and a thousand she-asses.


###### Job 42:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:13)


וַֽיְהִי־ל֛וֹ שִׁבְעָ֥נָה בָנִ֖ים וְשָׁל֥וֹשׁ בָּנֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He had also seven sons and three daughters.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he had twice seven sons and three daughters.


###### Job 42:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:14)


וַיִּקְרָ֤א שֵׁם־הָאַחַת֙ יְמִימָ֔ה וְשֵׁ֥ם הַשֵּׁנִ֖ית קְצִיעָ֑ה וְשֵׁ֥ם הַשְּׁלִישִׁ֖ית קֶ֥רֶן הַפּֽוּךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he called the name of the first, Yemima; and the name of the second, Qeżi῾a; and the name of the third, Qeren-happukh.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He called the first Yemimah [(sun)]; the second, Ketziah [(spice)]; and the third, Keren Hapuch [("the puch [cosmetic] horn")].


###### Job 42:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:15)


וְלֹ֨א נִמְצָ֜א נָשִׁ֥ים יָפ֛וֹת כִּבְנ֥וֹת אִיּ֖וֹב בְּכׇל־הָאָ֑רֶץ וַיִּתֵּ֨ן לָהֶ֧ם אֲבִיהֶ֛ם נַחֲלָ֖ה בְּת֥וֹךְ אֲחֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And in all the land were no women found so fair as the daughters of Iyyov: and their father gave them inheritance among their brothers.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there were no women as beautiful as the daughters of Iyyov in the whole land, and their father [(because of their distinctiveness and beauty)] gave them an inheritance together with their brothers.


###### Job 42:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:16)


וַיְחִ֤י אִיּוֹב֙ אַחֲרֵי־זֹ֔את מֵאָ֥ה וְאַרְבָּעִ֖ים שָׁנָ֑ה (וירא) [וַיִּרְאֶ֗ה] אֶת־בָּנָיו֙ וְאֶת־בְּנֵ֣י בָנָ֔יו אַרְבָּעָ֖ה דֹּרֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
After this Iyyov lived a hundred and forty years, and saw his sons, and his sons’ sons, even four generations.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Iyyov lived after this a hundred and forty years, and he saw four generations of his sons and grandsons.


###### Job 42:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Job/r/42:17)


וַיָּ֣מׇת אִיּ֔וֹב זָקֵ֖ן וּשְׂבַ֥ע יָמִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So Iyyov died, being old and full of days.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Iyyov died old and sated with days [of good].
